EN: "Above all, you need to survive as many deadly situations as I have. If you make it through alive, you'll get stronger! If that's too much, I suppose you could explore labyrinths, caves, and other places where monsters appear. That should make you stronger too.<end>"
KO: "어쨌든 나처럼 수많은 수라장을 헤쳐 나가 봐. 거기서 살아남으면 실력이 붙을 거야. 그게 싫으면 미궁이나 동굴처럼 몬스터가 나오는 곳을 탐험해도 실력이 붙겠지.<end>"

## 1210201
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺、思うんだけど護衛や運送の仕事って<line-break>すごく楽な仕事なんじゃないかな。<line-break>姉ちゃん俺たち実力つけたいんだよな？<line-break>俺、楽な仕事ばかりやってると実力が<line-break>つかないと思うんだ。だから、探索とか<line-break>退治とかの仕事を中心にしていこうぜ。<end>"
EN: "I've been thinking: aren't Escort and transport jobs really easy? Sis, we want to get stronger, right? I don't think we'll improve if we keep doing nothing but easy jobs. So let's focus on exploration and extermination work.<end>"
KO: "내 생각엔 호위나 운송 일은 굉장히 쉬운 일인 것 같아. 누나, 우리 실력을 키우고 싶은 거잖아? 쉬운 일만 하면 실력이 안 늘 것 같아. 그러니까 탐색이나 퇴치 같은 일을 중심으로 하자.<end>"

## 1210202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "迷宮や洞窟を探検してみるのは<line-break>どうでしょう？<line-break>もちろん、危険はともないますよ。<line-break>しかし、危険を恐れていては、<line-break>他人に尊敬されるような冒険者に<line-break>なれないと思います。<end>"
EN: "Why not try exploring a labyrinth or cave? There will be danger, of course. But if you're afraid of danger, I don't think you can become the kind of adventurer others respect.<end>"
KO: "미궁이나 동굴을 탐험해 보는 건 어떻습니까? 물론 위험은 따릅니다. 하지만 위험을 두려워해서는 남들에게 존경받는 모험자가 될 수 없다고 생각합니다.<end>"

## 1210203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おいおい、基本は冒険者ギルドの仕事を<line-break>こなしていくことだぜ。<line-break>そうすりゃソウルも育つし実力もつく。<line-break>だが、洞窟や迷宮を探検することも<line-break>たまには必要かもしれん。<line-break>腕試しの意味も含めてな。<end>"
EN: "Come on, the main thing is to keep doing jobs for the Adventurers' Guild. That'll develop your Soul and make you stronger. Still, you may need to explore a cave or labyrinth from time to time, if only to test your skills.<end>"
KO: "이봐, 기본은 모험가 길드 일을 해내는 거야. 그러면 소울도 자라고 실력도 붙지. 하지만 가끔은 동굴이나 미궁을 탐험할 필요도 있을 거야. 실력 시험도 겸해서 말이지.<end>"

## 1210204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者ギルドの仕事をこなし、ソウルを<line-break>鍛えることが先決だ。だが、楽な仕事<line-break>ばかりでは実戦経験が不足する。<line-break>迷宮や洞窟を探検することも<line-break>たまには必要だろう。<line-break>だが、無理はするなよ。<end>"
EN: "Your first priority is to complete jobs for the Adventurers' Guild and train your Soul. But easy jobs alone won't give you enough real combat experience. You should explore labyrinths and caves from time to time as well. Just don't push yourself too far.<end>"
KO: "우선 모험자 길드의 일을 해내며 소울을 단련하는 게 먼저다. 하지만 쉬운 일만 해서는 실전 경험이 부족해진다. 때로는 미궁이나 동굴을 탐험할 필요도 있겠지. 그래도 무리는 하지 마라.<end>"

## 1210205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうじゃのう、わしが駆け出しのころは<line-break>冒険者ギルドで引き受けた仕事の合間に<line-break>迷宮や洞窟を探検しておったな。<line-break>迷宮や洞窟を制覇するたびに、少しづつ<line-break>実力がついたような気がしたものじゃ。<line-break>懐かしいのう。<end>"
EN: "Ah, yes. When I was a novice, I used to explore labyrinths and caves between the jobs I took from the Adventurers' Guild. Each time I conquered one, I felt as though I had grown a little stronger. Those were the days.<end>"
KO: "그렇구나. 내가 풋내기였을 때는 모험자 길드에서 받은 일 사이사이에 미궁이나 동굴을 탐험했지. 미궁이나 동굴을 하나씩 제패할 때마다 조금씩 실력이 붙는 느낌이었네. 그립구먼.<end>"

## 1210206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者ギルドの仕事をこなすだけじゃ<line-break>ダメみたいだね。少し自信がついて<line-break>きたら迷宮や洞窟の探検にも行こうよ。<line-break>モンスターとの戦いから戦闘の駆け引き<line-break>を習得して戦い上手になるんだ。<end>"
EN: "It seems that just completing jobs for the Adventurers' Guild isn't enough. Once we've gained a little confidence, let's go explore some labyrinths and caves too. By fighting monsters, we'll learn how to handle ourselves in combat and become better fighters.<end>"
KO: "모험자 길드 일만 해선 안 되는 모양이네. 어느 정도 자신감이 생기면 미궁이나 동굴 탐험도 가자. 몬스터와 싸우며 전투의 요령을 익혀서 싸움에 능숙해지는 거야.<end>"

## 1210207
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギルドの仕事をこなすだけが<line-break>冒険者の全てではないぞ。<line-break>君の知らない世界は、広い。<end>"
EN: "An adventurer's life is about more than completing Guild jobs. The world you have yet to see is vast.<end>"
KO: "길드 일을 해내는 것만이 모험자의 전부는 아니다. 네가 모르는 세계는 넓다.<end>"

## 1210208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたの実力なんて高貴で知的な<line-break>アタクシが興味あるわけないでしょ。<line-break>失礼しちゃうわね。<line-break>そんなに実力つけたいなら<line-break>戦闘バカのボルダンやドワーフにでも<line-break>聞いてみればいいじゃない！<end>"
EN: "Why would someone as noble and intelligent as moi care how capable you are? Honestly, how insulting. If you want to get stronger that badly, why don't you ask that battle-crazed fool Boldan, or a Dwarf?<end>"
KO: "네 실력 따위 고귀하고 지적인 내가 관심 있을 리 없잖아. 실례네. 그렇게 실력을 키우고 싶으면 전투광 볼단이나 드워프에게라도 물어봐!<end>"

## 1210209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "実力をつけるには？ボゲー<end>"
EN: "How do you get stronger, Bogee?<end>"
KO: "실력을 키우려면? 보게<end>"

## 1210210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタは冒険者ギルドのお仕事で<line-break>実力をつけたほうがいいと思うよぉ。<line-break>でもね、それだけじゃダメなのかも…<line-break>ギルドのおじさんがね、迷宮や洞窟を<line-break>探検しない冒険者は、いつまでも<line-break>駆け出しだって言うんだもん。<end>"
EN: "Lulluantha thinks you should get stronger by doing jobs for the Adventurers' Guild. But maybe that alone isn't enough... The man at the Guild says adventurers who never explore labyrinths or caves stay novices forever.<end>"
KO: "루루안타는 모험자 길드 일을 하며 실력을 키우는 게 좋다고 생각해. 하지만 그것만으론 안 될지도 몰라…. 길드 아저씨가 미궁이나 동굴을 탐험하지 않는 모험자는 언제까지나 초보라고 했거든.<end>"

## 1210211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>洞窟と迷宮だ。<end>"
EN: "... Caves and labyrinths.<end>"
KO: "………………. 동굴과 미궁이다.<end>"

## 1210212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どんな状況下でも生き残ることが<line-break>できる実力と冷静な判断力を<line-break>身につけることだ。<line-break>そのためには冒険者ギルドで仕事を<line-break>もらい、それを確実にこなしていけ。<line-break>少しずつ実力をつけるのだ。<end>"
EN: "Develop the ability and calm judgment needed to survive under any circumstances. To do that, take jobs from the Adventurers' Guild and complete them without fail. Build your strength little by little.<end>"
KO: "어떤 상황에서도 살아남을 실력과 냉정한 판단력을 익혀라. 그러려면 모험가 길드에서 일을 받아 확실히 해내라. 조금씩 실력을 쌓는 거다.<end>"

## 1210213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "護衛や運送の仕事ばかりしていると<line-break>なかなか実力がつかないよ。<line-break>探索や退治の仕事を中心にするんだ。<line-break>特に迷宮や洞窟に行くような仕事が<line-break>実力をつけるには、もってこいだよ。<line-break>ぼくのアドバイスって役に立つでしょ。<end>"
EN: "You won't improve much if you only do Escort and transport jobs. Focus on exploration and extermination work. Jobs that send you into labyrinths or caves are especially good for building your abilities. My advice is useful, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "호위나 운송 일만 하면 좀처럼 실력이 늘지 않아. 탐색이나 퇴치 일을 중심으로 해. 특히 미궁이나 동굴에 가는 일이 실력 키우기엔 딱 좋아. 내 조언 도움 되지?<end>"

## 1210214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "平均点ほどの者が言いそうなことだ。<line-break>つまり君は、良くも悪くも人並み。<line-break>わかるかね？<line-break>群集から抜け出たいと思うなら、<line-break>皆の行かぬところへ行き、<line-break>困難な仕事を成功させることだ。<end>"
EN: "That's precisely what one would expect from someone of merely average caliber. In other words, for better or worse, you're unexceptional. Do you understand? If you wish to rise above the crowd, go where others will not and succeed at difficult jobs.<end>"
KO: "평균점 정도인 사람이 할 법한 말이군. 즉 자네는 좋든 나쁘든 평범한 사람이라는 뜻이야. 알겠나? 군중에서 벗어나고 싶다면 남들이 가지 않는 곳으로 가서 어려운 일을 성공시켜야 하네.<end>"

## 1210215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "中級以上の冒険者は町から離れ、<line-break>洞窟や迷宮にも踏み入って<line-break>実力を磨いています。<line-break>行くきっかけは依頼としてギルドで<line-break>得ることができますが、相応の<line-break>危険も伴うことをお忘れなく。<end>"
EN: "Adventurers of intermediate rank and above leave the towns behind and venture into caves and labyrinths to hone their skills. The Guild can provide jobs that give you a reason to go, but don't forget that such places pose dangers to match.<end>"
KO: "중급 이상의 모험가들은 마을을 떠나 동굴이나 미궁에도 들어가 실력을 닦습니다. 길드 의뢰가 그런 곳에 갈 계기를 주기도 하지만 그만한 위험이 따른다는 점은 잊지 마십시오.<end>"

## 1210216
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私たちも、昔は苦労したんだよ。<line-break>何回も危ない目に会ったしね。<line-break>そのおかげで、今の私があるの！<end>"
EN: "We had a hard time in the old days too. We found ourselves in danger over and over. And that's what made me who I am today!<end>"
KO: "우리도 옛날엔 고생했어. 몇 번이나 위험한 일을 겪었고. 그 덕분에 지금의 내가 있는 거야!<end>"

## 1210217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほどな、荷物配達などは、<line-break>もういい加減、物足りなくなってきた<line-break>というわけじゃ。<line-break>ならば町の外にも目を向けよ。<line-break>迷宮や遺跡、森や洞窟や湖や…<line-break>何ならわしが案内してやってもいいぞ。<end>"
EN: "I see. So parcel deliveries and the like are no longer enough to satisfy you. Then cast your eyes beyond the town: labyrinths, ruins, forests, caves, lakes... Why, I could even guide you myself, if you like.<end>"
KO: "그렇군, 짐 배달 같은 건 이제 슬슬 시시해졌다는 거군. 그렇다면 마을 밖으로도 눈을 돌려라. 미궁이나 유적, 숲이나 동굴이나 호수…. 원한다면 내가 안내해 줘도 좋다.<end>"

## 1210218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういうことで悩むようになれば、<line-break>冒険者として軌道に乗ってきた<line-break>証拠だと言えるだろうな。<line-break>今までは町と町をつなぐような<line-break>仕事が多かったが、洞窟や迷宮にも<line-break>足を伸ばしてみるといい。<end>"
EN: "The fact that you've begun worrying about such things is proof that you're finding your feet as an adventurer. Most of your work until now has taken you from one town to another, but you should try venturing into caves and labyrinths as well.<end>"
KO: "그런 문제로 고민하기 시작했다면 모험자로서 궤도에 올랐다는 증거라고 할 수 있겠군. 지금까지는 마을과 마을을 잇는 일이 많았지만 동굴이나 미궁에도 발을 뻗어보는 게 좋아.<end>"

## 1210220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "実力をつけたいなら<line-break>洞窟や迷宮に行って、<line-break>モンスターたちを相手にすること。<line-break>ギルドでそれ系の仕事をこなしていけば<line-break>すぐ実力がつくはずよ。<line-break>例えば、退治や探索なんかね。<end>"
EN: "If you want to get stronger, go to caves and labyrinths and take on the monsters there. Keep doing that sort of job for the Guild, and you should improve in no time. Extermination and exploration jobs, for example.<end>"
KO: "실력을 키우고 싶으면 동굴이나 미궁에 가서 몬스터들을 상대해. 길드에서 그런 계열 일을 계속 해내면 금방 실력이 붙을 거야. 예를 들면 퇴치나 탐색 같은 거지.<end>"

## 1210222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっぱり実戦が一番だと思います！<line-break>よく言うじゃないですか？<line-break>百回の練習より一回の本番って。<line-break>ギルドの仕事でモンスターと戦うような<line-break>仕事をこなしていけば、<line-break>自然と実力がつくはずです。<end>"
EN: "I really do think actual combat is best! You know what they say: one real battle is worth a hundred practice sessions. If you keep taking Guild jobs that have you fighting monsters, your abilities should improve naturally.<end>"
KO: "역시 실전이 최고라고 생각해요! 흔히 말하잖아요? 백 번 연습보다 한 번 실전이라고. 길드 일로 몬스터와 싸우는 일을 계속 하면 자연스럽게 실력이 붙을 거예요.<end>"

## 1220000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（ディンガル帝国の朱雀将軍になった<line-break>　アンギルダンから、自分の副将に<line-break>　なるようにとの手紙をもらった）<line-break>（アンギルダンの頼みを聞いて、<line-break>　副将になるためにエンシャントの城へ<line-break>　行こうか、それとも断りに…）<end>"
EN: "(Angeerdan, now the Vermilion Bird General of the Dyneskal Empire, sent a letter asking me to become his deputy.) (Should I accept Angeerdan's request and go to the castle in Ancient to become his deputy, or go there to decline...?)<end>"
KO: "(딩갈 제국의 주작장군이 된 앙길단에게서 자신의 부장이 되어 달라는 편지를 받았다.) (앙길단의 부탁을 받아 부장이 되기 위해 엔샨트 성으로 갈까, 아니면 거절하러 갈까…)<end>"

## 1220001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "朱雀将軍から自分の副将になるよう<line-break>誘われたって本当なの！？<line-break>それってすごいことよ。<line-break>うらやましいな…。<line-break>もちろん誘いを受けるんでしょ。<line-break>こんなチャンスは二度とないわよ。<end>"
EN: "Is it true the Vermilion Bird General asked you to become his deputy!? That's incredible. I'm so jealous... Of course you're going to accept. You'll never get another chance like this.<end>"
KO: "주작장군이 자기 부장이 되라고 권했다는 게 정말이야!? 그거 대단한 일이야. 부럽다…. 당연히 받아들일 거지? 이런 기회는 두 번 다시 없어.<end>"

## 1220003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者から朱雀軍の副将になるなんて<line-break>かなりの強運の持ち主かも。<line-break>この若さで副将に出世ということは…<line-break>数年後にはディンガル皇帝とか…。<line-break>これは玉のこしねらいでアタックする<line-break>しかないかも…。<end>"
EN: "Going from adventurer to deputy commander of the Vermilion Bird Army... You must be incredibly lucky. If you've risen to deputy commander at such a young age, then in a few years you might be Emperor of Dyneskal... Maybe I should make a move and marry into power...<end>"
KO: "모험가에서 주작군 부장이라니 상당히 운이 좋은 사람인가 봐. 이 나이에 부장까지 출세했다면… 몇 년 뒤엔 딘갈 황제라든가…. 이건 신분 상승을 노리고 들이대야 할지도….<end>"

## 1220004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダンがレターをくレター？<line-break>なんで俺じゃなくてお前に来るんだよ！<line-break>どう考えても俺の方が強いのによ。<line-break>ところで、アンギルダンって誰だ？<line-break>何！　騎士だってぇ！<line-break>急いでエンシャントに行かナイト！<end>"
EN: "Angeerdan sent you a letter? Why'd it come to you instead of me!? I'm obviously stronger than you. By the way, who's Angeerdan? What!? He's a knight? Then we'd better not wait all night―hurry to Ancient!<end>"
KO: "앙길단이 레터를 줬다고? 왜 나 말고 너한테 오는 거야! 아무리 생각해도 내가 더 강한데. 그런데 앙길단이 누구지? 뭐! 기사라고! 빨리 엔샨트로 가야 나이트!<end>"

## 1220005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダン？<line-break>もしかして真紅のプレートアーマーを<line-break>身につけたお爺さんのこと？<line-break>へ～、あの人ってすごい人だったのね。<line-break>実はボク、リベルダムの酒場で<line-break>ごちそうになったことがあるんだ。<end>"
EN: "Angeerdan? You mean the old man in crimson plate armor? Huh, so he was someone important. He actually treated me to a meal at the tavern in Liberdam once.<end>"
KO: "앙길단? 혹시 진홍색 플레이트 아머를 입은 할아버지 말이야? 헤에~ 그 사람이 대단한 사람이었구나. 사실 나, 리벨덤 술집에서 얻어먹은 적 있어.<end>"

## 1220006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダンがあなたを副将に招く？<line-break>…それは良かったわね。せいぜい<line-break>戦死しないように気をつけるといいわ。<end>"
EN: "Angeerdan invited you to be his deputy? ...How nice for you. Do try not to get killed in battle.<end>"
KO: "앙길단이 당신을 부장으로 부른다고? …잘됐네. 기왕이면 전사하지 않게 조심하는 게 좋겠어.<end>"

## 1220007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "帝国軍朱雀将軍の副将に、あなたが？<line-break>それはすごい出世ですねえ。<line-break>…え、受けるかどうか迷ってる？<line-break>存分、考え、悩むといいでしょう。<line-break>それだけの価値があることです。<end>"
EN: "You, deputy to the imperial army's Vermilion Bird General? That is quite a promotion. ...What? You're unsure whether to accept? Take all the time you need to think and agonize over it. A decision like this deserves no less.<end>"
KO: "제국군 주작장군의 부장으로 당신이요? 대단한 출세네요. …뭐, 받을지 말지 고민 중이라고요? 충분히 생각하고 고민하세요. 그럴 가치가 있는 일이니까요.<end>"

## 1220008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギのおっさんに認められるなんて、<line-break>やるじゃん、<value:$28>。<line-break><line-break>帝国軍に入ったら、このカルラさんも<line-break>色々教えてあげるよ～。<line-break>手取り足取り、なんてね。<end>"
EN: "Not bad, <value:$28>. You even earned old Angie's approval. If you join the imperial army, Karula can teach you all sorts of things. Hand over hand, every step of the way... Just kidding.<end>"
KO: "안기 아저씨에게 인정받다니 제법인데, <value:$28>. 제국군에 들어가면 이 카를라도 이것저것 알려 줄게~. 손잡고 발잡고, 뭐 그런 식으로.<end>"

## 1220009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃん、アンギルダンの手紙には<line-break>なんて書いてあったのか教えてくれよ。<line-break>えっ、見せてくれるって。どれどれ…<line-break>…すごいよ姉ちゃん！<line-break>アンギルダンに副将を頼まれるなんて。<line-break>もちろんエンシャントへ行くんだよな。<end>"
EN: "Sis, tell me what Angeerdan's Letter said. What, you'll let me see it? Let's have a look... Wow, Sis! Angeerdan asked you to be his deputy! You're going to Ancient, right?<end>"
KO: "누나, 앙길단의 편지에 뭐라고 적혀 있었는지 알려 줘. 뭐, 보여 준다고? 어디 보자…. 대단해 누나! 앙길단이 부장을 맡아 달라니. 당연히 엔샨트로 갈 거지?<end>"

## 1220010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一介の冒険者から副将を選ぶとは。<line-break>アンギルダンも思い切ったことを。<line-break>さすがは歴戦の老将ということかしら。<end>"
EN: "Choosing a mere adventurer as his deputy... That was a bold decision, even for Angeerdan. I suppose that is what one should expect from a veteran general.<end>"
KO: "일개 모험가 중에서 부장을 고르다니. 앙길단도 과감한 결정을 했군. 역시 백전노장이라는 건가.<end>"

## 1220011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダンのとっつぁんも人を<line-break>見る目があるねー。俺も同じ立場なら<line-break>お前さんを副将にするだろうな。<line-break>どうする？　エンシャントへ行くかい。<line-break>俺に遠慮する必要はこれっぽっちも<line-break>ないぜ。自分の思うようにするといい。<end>"
EN: "Old Angeerdan has a good eye for people. If I were in his position, I'd make you my deputy too. So, what will you do? Go to Ancient? Don't hold back on my account―not even a little. Do whatever you think is right.<end>"
KO: "앙길단 아저씨도 사람 보는 눈이 있네. 나라도 네가 그 자리에 있다면 부장으로 삼을 거야. 어쩔래? 엔샨트로 갈래? 나한테 눈치 볼 필요 전혀 없어. 네 생각대로 해.<end>"

## 1220012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダンが自分の副将に<line-break>お前を選んだのか…。<line-break>大胆な男だな。<line-break>どうする？　誘いを受けるなら<line-break>迷わずエンシャントへ向かえ。<end>"
EN: "Angeerdan chose you as his deputy...? He's a bold man. What will you do? If you're accepting his offer, head straight for Ancient.<end>"
KO: "앙길단이 자기 부장으로 널 골랐나…. 대담한 남자군. 어쩔 거지? 제안을 받아들일 거라면 망설이지 말고 엔샨트로 가라.<end>"

## 1220013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんと！　アンギルダン殿がお主を<line-break>自分の副将として取り立ててくれると<line-break>言うのか。それはすごいことじゃな。<line-break>それで、お主はどうするつもりじゃ。<line-break>アンギルダン殿はお主が来るのを<line-break>今や遅しと待っておるのじゃぞ。<end>"
EN: "What! Lord Angeerdan means to elevate you to the position of his deputy? That is remarkable indeed. So, what do you intend to do? Lord Angeerdan is waiting impatiently for you to arrive.<end>"
KO: "뭐라고! 앙길단 님이 너를 자신의 부장으로 삼겠다고 한단 말인가. 대단한 일이군. 그래서 넌 어떻게 할 생각이지? 앙길단 님은 네가 오기만을 손꼽아 기다리고 있을 텐데.<end>"

## 1220014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダンさんは、自分の副将に<line-break>なるよう誘ってくれたんでしょ。<line-break><value:$28>どうするの？<line-break>アンギルダンさんは、エンシャントで<line-break><value:$28>のことを待っている<line-break>と思うよ。<end>"
EN: "Angeerdan asked you to become his deputy, right? What will you do, <value:$28>? I think Angeerdan is waiting for <value:$28> in Ancient.<end>"
KO: "앙길단 씨가 자기 부장이 되라고 권한 거지? <value:$28>, 어떻게 할 거야? 앙길단 씨는 엔샨트에서 <value:$28>를 기다리고 있을 것 같아.<end>"

## 1220016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふーん、あんた戦争に行くつもりなの。<line-break>アンギルダンの副将になるっていうのは<line-break>そういうことなんでしょ。<line-break>アタクシは遠慮させてもらうわ。<line-break>高貴で優雅なエルフのアタクシは人間の<line-break>醜い争いになど巻き込まれたくないの。<end>"
EN: "Oh? So you're planning to go to war. That's what becoming Angeerdan's deputy means, isn't it? Count me out. I am a noble and elegant Elf, and I have no desire to be dragged into the ugly conflicts of humans.<end>"
KO: "흐음, 너 전쟁에 갈 생각이구나. 앙길단의 부장이 된다는 건 그런 뜻이잖아. 나는 사양할래. 고귀하고 우아한 엘프인 내가 인간의 추한 싸움에 휘말리고 싶진 않거든.<end>"

## 1220017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダン殿は騎士ですぞ。<line-break>兄弟子、ぐずぐすしていてはダメです。<line-break>急いでエンシャントへ行かナイト！<line-break>………………。<line-break>使い古されたネタで申し訳ありませぬ。<end>"
EN: "Lord Angeerdan is a knight, Senior Disciple. This is no time to dawdle. Hurry to Ancient―don't wait all knight! ............... Forgive me for resorting to such a tired joke.<end>"
KO: "앙길단 님은 기사이십니다. 사형, 꾸물대면 안 됩니다. 서둘러 엔샨트로 가야 나이트! ………………. 낡은 개그라 죄송합니다.<end>"

## 1220018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダンのお爺ちゃんは<line-break>ディンガル帝国の朱雀将軍に<line-break>なったんだよねぇ。<line-break>ルルアンタ、副将には興味ないけど<line-break>アンギルダンのお爺ちゃんに<line-break>会いたくなってきちゃった。<end>"
EN: "Grandpa Angeerdan became the Dyneskal Empire's Vermilion Bird General, right? Lulluantha isn't interested in being a deputy, but now I want to see Grandpa Angeerdan.<end>"
KO: "앙길단 할아버지는 딘갈 제국의 주작장군이 됐지. 릴비는 부장 자리엔 관심 없지만 앙길단 할아버지는 만나고 싶어졌어.<end>"

## 1220019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>アンギルダンの願いを聞いてやれ。<end>"
EN: "............... Grant Angeerdan's request.<end>"
KO: "………………. 앙길단의 소원을 들어줘라.<end>"

## 1220020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前がアンギルダン、つまり、<line-break>ディンガル帝国朱雀将軍の副将に？<line-break>関係ない。誰であろうと、な。<end>"
EN: "You? Deputy to Angeerdan―the Dyneskal Empire's Vermilion Bird General? It makes no difference. No matter who you are.<end>"
KO: "네가 앙길단, 그러니까 딘갈 제국 주작장군의 부장이 된다고? 상관없어. 누구든 말이지.<end>"

## 1220021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダンの副将ってことは<line-break>ディンガル帝国朱雀将軍の副将だよね。<line-break>冒険者から副将なんて大出世だよ。<line-break>だんだん英雄伝説っぽくなってきた！<line-break>これはもう、その話にのるしかないね。<line-break>すぐにエンシャントの城へ行かないと！<end>"
EN: "Being Angeerdan's deputy means being deputy to the Dyneskal Empire's Vermilion Bird General, right? Going from adventurer to deputy commander is an enormous promotion. This is starting to feel like a heroic legend! You simply have to accept. Hurry to the castle in Ancient!<end>"
KO: "앙길단의 부장이라면 딘갈 제국 주작장군의 부장이란 거지? 모험가에서 부장이라니 엄청난 출세야. 점점 영웅전설 같아지는데! 이건 받아들일 수밖에 없어. 당장 엔샨트 성으로 가자!<end>"

## 1220022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "朱雀将軍が君を副将に選んだか。<line-break>君の素質を見抜くとは、<line-break>さすがアンギルダンは目ざといな。<end>"
EN: "So the Vermilion Bird General chose you as his deputy. Angeerdan was sharp enough to recognize your potential. Impressive.<end>"
KO: "주작장군이 너를 부장으로 골랐나. 네 자질을 알아보다니 역시 앙길단은 눈이 빠르군.<end>"

## 1220023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おめでたく思います。…が、<line-break>そのようなことを誰彼かまわず<line-break>触れ回って、危険はないのですか？<end>"
EN: "I am happy for you. However... is it safe to go around telling everyone about such a thing?<end>"
KO: "축하할 일이라고 생각합니다. …하지만 그런 일을 아무에게나 떠벌리고 다녀도 위험하지 않습니까?<end>"

## 1220024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガッカリだよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>それを受けちゃったら、<line-break>私たち、敵同士になるんだよ！？<end>"
EN: "I'm disappointed, <value:$28>. If you accept, we'll become enemies!<end>"
KO: "실망이야, <value:$28>. 그걸 받아 버리면 우리 서로 적이 되는 거야!?<end>"

## 1220026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>が、朱雀将軍の<line-break>副将に大抜擢されるとは…。<line-break><line-break>祝ってやりたいが、今一度よく<line-break>考えて決断するといい。その出世は、<line-break>戦場に通じているのだからな。<end>"
EN: "To think <value:$28> would be chosen for the great honor of serving as the Vermilion Bird General's deputy... I want to congratulate you, but think it over once more before deciding. That promotion leads directly to the battlefield.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>가 주작장군의 부장으로 대발탁되다니…. 축하해 주고 싶지만 다시 한번 잘 생각하고 결정해라. 그 출세는 전장으로 이어지는 길이니까.<end>"

## 1220027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのアンギルダンから<line-break>副将になってくれと頼まれるなんて、<line-break>腕を上げたな、<value:$28>。<line-break>しかし、そうなると<line-break>ロストールを攻めることになるのか…。<end>"
EN: "For Angeerdan himself to ask you to be his deputy... You've come a long way, <value:$28>. But would that mean attacking Rostorl...?<end>"
KO: "그 앙길단에게 부장이 되어 달라는 부탁을 받다니 실력이 늘었구나, <value:$28>. 하지만 그러면 로스톨을 공격하게 되는 건가….<end>"

## 1220028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "朱雀将軍アンギルタンの副将に？<line-break>彼の目にとまるなんて、さすがね。<end>"
EN: "Deputy to Vermilion Bird General Angeerdan? Impressive, catching his attention like that.<end>"
KO: "주작장군 안길단의 부장으로? 그의 눈에 들다니 역시 대단하네.<end>"

## 1220030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガル帝国の朱雀軍の副将に<line-break>なるんですか？<line-break>　<line-break>ロストールを<value:$28>さんが<line-break>攻めるってことですよね？<line-break>いえ、なんでもないです…。<end>"
EN: "You're becoming a deputy commander in the Dyneskal Empire's Vermilion Bird Army? That means <value:$28> will attack Rostorl, doesn't it? No, it's nothing...<end>"
KO: "딘갈 제국 주작군의 부장이 되시는 건가요? 그러면 <value:$28> 씨가 로스톨을 공격한다는 거죠? 아뇨, 아무것도 아니에요….<end>"

## 1220032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（エンシャントの南東にある賢者の森。<line-break>　そこには運命に選ばれた人間のみが<line-break>　入れる屋敷があるらしい）<line-break>（冒険者ギルドの主人が言うには、<line-break>　未来の勇者を自称するガルドランは<line-break>　その猫屋敷へ向かったようだ）<end>"
EN: "(Southeast of Ancient lies the Sage's Forest. It is said to contain a manor that only humans chosen by destiny may enter.) (According to the master of the Adventurers' Guild, Girldlan, the self-proclaimed future Hero, has set out for that Cat Manor.)<end>"
KO: "(엔샨트 남동쪽에 있는 현자의 숲. 그곳에는 운명에 선택된 인간만 들어갈 수 있는 저택이 있다고 한다.) (모험자 길드 주인의 말로는 미래의 용사를 자칭하는 갈드란이 그 고양이 저택으로 향한 모양이다.)<end>"

## 1220033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の聞いた話によると、ガルドランは<line-break>賢者の森へ向かったらしいわ。<line-break>猫屋敷に通れるつもりなのね。<end>"
EN: "From what I've heard, Girldlan has gone to the Sage's Forest. He must think he can gain entry to Cat Manor.<end>"
KO: "내가 들은 바로는 갈드란은 현자의 숲으로 향한 모양이야. 고양이 저택에 들어갈 수 있다고 생각하나 봐.<end>"

## 1220034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あやつ、賢者の森へ行ったそうじゃ。<line-break>運命に選ばれた者のみ通れるという<line-break>賢者殿に会えるつもりなのじゃなあ。<end>"
EN: "I hear that fellow went to the Sage's Forest. He must think the Sage, who receives only those chosen by destiny, will meet with him.<end>"
KO: "그 녀석, 현자의 숲으로 갔다더군. 운명에 선택받은 자만 지나갈 수 있다는 현자를 만날 수 있다고 생각하는 모양이야.<end>"

## 1220035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガルドラン様が賢者の森へ行ったという<line-break>ことはですね～、運命に選ばれた者だ<line-break>っていう自覚があるからです！<line-break>賢者様が会ってくれなかったら、<line-break>少しは落ち着きが出るんでしょうか？<line-break>そうなるといいですね～！　きゃっ！<end>"
EN: "Lord Girldlan went to the Sage's Forest because he considers himself one of those chosen by destiny! If the Sage refuses to see him, do you think he might calm down a little? I certainly hope so! Eek!<end>"
KO: "갈드란 님이 현자의 숲에 갔다는 건요~ 자신이 운명에 선택된 자라는 자각이 있기 때문이에요! 현자님이 만나 주지 않으면 조금은 얌전해질까요? 그러면 좋겠네요~! 꺅!<end>"

## 1220036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガルドランの野郎は、賢者の森へ<line-break>行ったらしいぜ！　くっそ～！<line-break>俺より先に猫遊びするつもりだな！<end>"
EN: "That bastard Girldlan went to the Sage's Forest! Damn it! He's going to play with the cats before I do!<end>"
KO: "갈드란 녀석은 현자의 숲으로 간 모양이야! 젠장~! 나보다 먼저 고양이랑 놀 생각이군!<end>"

## 1220037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボクが聞いたところによるとね、<line-break>ガルドランは賢者の森へ行ったって！<line-break>猫屋敷まで踏み込むつもりなのかな？<end>"
EN: "From what I heard, Girldlan went to the Sage's Forest! I wonder if he plans to venture all the way into Cat Manor.<end>"
KO: "내가 듣기로 갈드란은 현자의 숲으로 갔대! 고양이 저택까지 들어갈 생각일까?<end>"

## 1220038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガルドラン？<line-break>どこへ行ったかということ以前に、<line-break>それが誰だったかが思い出せない…。<end>"
EN: "Girldlan? Never mind where he went―I can't even remember who he was...<end>"
KO: "갈드란? 어디로 갔는지를 떠나서, 그게 누구였는지도 기억이 안 나….<end>"

## 1220039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、私は黙っていましょうかね。<line-break>人を選ぶのは、運命、ですから…。<line-break>ふふふっ。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps I should keep quiet. After all, destiny is what chooses people... Heh heh.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 저는 잠자코 있기로 하죠. 사람을 고르는 건 운명이니까요…. 후후훗.<end>"

## 1220040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、あのうるっさいヤツね。<line-break>酒場ではどうやら賢者の森へ行くって<line-break>叫んでたらしいけど…？<end>"
EN: "Oh, that obnoxiously loud fellow. Apparently he was shouting in the tavern about going to the Sage's Forest...?<end>"
KO: "아, 그 시끄러운 녀석 말이지. 술집에서 현자의 숲에 가겠다고 소리쳤다던데…?<end>"

## 1220041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大変だ、姉ちゃん！<line-break>ガルドランの奴、賢者の森の猫屋敷へ<line-break>向かったらしいんだ！<line-break>姉ちゃんや俺を差し置いて、<line-break>ガルドランが賢者様に会っちゃったら<line-break>俺、たまんなく悔しいぜ！<end>"
EN: "This is bad, Sis! That Girldlan fellow is heading for Cat Manor in the Sage's Forest! If Girldlan meets the Sage before you or me, I'll be beside myself with frustration!<end>"
KO: "큰일이야, 누나! 갈드란 녀석, 현자의 숲 고양이 저택으로 향했다는 모양이야! 누나랑 나를 제쳐 두고 갈드란이 현자를 만나 버리면 나 진짜 분해서 못 견딜 거야!<end>"

## 1220042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガルドラン？　勇者を名乗る冒険者？<line-break>冒険者であるあなたを差し置いて、<line-break>私がその行方を知るはずがないわ。<end>"
EN: "Girldlan? The adventurer who calls himself a Hero? If you, an adventurer, do not know where he went, how could I possibly know?<end>"
KO: "갈드란? 용사를 자칭하는 모험가? 모험가인 당신도 모르는데 내가 그 행방을 알 리 없죠.<end>"

## 1220043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、ガルドランだろ？<line-break>酒場でな、賢者の森へ行くって<line-break>声高に叫んでたみたいだぜ？<line-break>運命に選ばれた者だなんて平気で<line-break>言えるんだからな。いやはや、<line-break>恐れ入っちまうぜ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, Girldlan? Apparently he was loudly proclaiming in the tavern that he was going to the Sage's Forest. Anyone who can casually call himself chosen by destiny is truly something else. I'm almost impressed.<end>"
KO: "아, 갈드란 말이지? 술집에서 현자의 숲에 간다고 큰소리쳤던 모양이야. 자기가 운명에 선택받은 사람이라고 태연히 말하니 원, 대단하다니까.<end>"

## 1220044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガルドランは賢者の森へ行った。<line-break>猫屋敷まで通るつもりなのだろうが、<line-break>まず十割方、不可能だろうな。<end>"
EN: "Girldlan went to the Sage's Forest. He probably intends to make it as far as Cat Manor, but there is virtually no chance of that.<end>"
KO: "갈드란은 현자의 숲으로 갔다. 고양이 저택까지 가려는 모양이지만 십중팔구 불가능하겠지.<end>"

## 1220045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、あの勇者気取りはな、<line-break>賢者の森へと行ったらしいぞ。<line-break>エンシャントの南東じゃ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, that would-be Hero apparently went to the Sage's Forest. It lies southeast of Ancient.<end>"
KO: "오오, 그 용사 행세하던 녀석 말이냐. 현자의 숲으로 갔다더군. 엔샨트 남동쪽이다.<end>"

## 1220046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガルドランは賢者の森にある<line-break>猫屋敷へ向かったらしいね。<line-break>僕たちもいそいで追いかけようよ。<end>"
EN: "Apparently Girldlan has headed for Cat Manor in the Sage's Forest. Let's hurry after him.<end>"
KO: "갈드란은 현자의 숲에 있는 고양이 저택으로 향한 모양이야. 우리도 서둘러 뒤쫓자.<end>"

## 1220047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガルドランは賢者の森へ向かった。<line-break>猫屋敷の賢者に会うつもりらしいな。<line-break>会えるかどうかは運命次第だが…。<end>"
EN: "Girldlan went to the Sage's Forest. Apparently he means to meet the Sage of Cat Manor. Whether he succeeds is up to destiny...<end>"
KO: "갈드란은 현자의 숲으로 향했다. 고양이 저택의 현자를 만나려는 모양이군. 만날 수 있을지는 운명에 달렸지만….<end>"

## 1220048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガルドラン？　冒険者？<line-break>よして頂戴。そんな下賎なことは、<line-break>アタクシより酒場の親父の専門だわ。<line-break>"
EN: "Girldlan? An adventurer? Spare me. Such lowly matters are the tavern keeper's concern, not mine."
KO: "갈드란? 모험자? 그만둬. 그런 천한 일은 나보다 술집 주인장의 전문 분야라고."

## 1220049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エラーメッセージです。<end>"
EN: "This is an error message.<end>"
KO: "오류 메시지입니다.<end>"

## 1220050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタ、知ってるよぉ。<line-break>ガルドランは賢者の森目指して<line-break>駆けてったんだよぉ！<end>"
EN: "Lulluantha knows! Girldlan went running toward the Sage's Forest!<end>"
KO: "루루안타 알고 있어. 갈드란은 현자의 숲을 향해 달려갔어!<end>"

## 1220051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…賢者の森だ。<line-break>勝手に行くといいだろう。<end>"
EN: "...The Sage's Forest. Go there if you want.<end>"
KO: "…현자의 숲이다. 마음대로 가면 되겠지.<end>"

## 1220052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガルドラン？<line-break>冒険者の行く場所など、知らん。<line-break>酒場か、宿屋ではないのか…。<end>"
EN: "Girldlan? I know nothing of the places adventurers frequent. Perhaps a tavern or an inn...<end>"
KO: "갈드란? 모험자가 가는 곳 따윈 모른다. 술집이나 여관에 있지 않겠나….<end>"

## 1220053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガルドランは、エンシャント南東の<line-break>賢者の森に向かったらしいよ！<line-break><line-break>猫屋敷の賢者様に会うつもりだとは<line-break>思うけど、運命は味方について<line-break>くれるのかな～？<end>"
EN: "Apparently Girldlan headed for the Sage's Forest southeast of Ancient! I imagine he means to meet the Sage of Cat Manor, but I wonder if destiny will favor him.<end>"
KO: "갈드란은 엔샨트 남동쪽 현자의 숲으로 갔대! 고양이 저택의 현자님을 만나려는 것 같긴 한데, 운명이 편을 들어 줄까~?<end>"

## 1220054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賢者の森へと向かったらしいな。<line-break>猫屋敷の賢者が会ってくれると<line-break>考えているらしいが…。<end>"
EN: "Apparently he headed for the Sage's Forest. He seems to think the Sage of Cat Manor will meet with him...<end>"
KO: "현자의 숲으로 향한 모양이군. 고양이 저택의 현자가 만나 줄 거라고 생각하는 것 같지만….<end>"

## 1220055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その者なら、賢者の森へ行ったと<line-break>聞きましたよ。猫屋敷の賢者様に<line-break>会えると考えてのことじゃ？<end>"
EN: "I heard that person went to the Sage's Forest. Presumably in the belief that the Sage of Cat Manor would grant an audience?<end>"
KO: "그 사람이라면 현자의 숲으로 갔다고 들었습니다. 고양이 저택의 현자님을 만날 수 있다고 생각한 게 아닐까요?<end>"

## 1220056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガルドラン…？<line-break>ヴィア、そんな人、<line-break>知らないよ。<end>"
EN: "Girldlan...? Via doesn't know anyone like that.<end>"
KO: "갈드란…? 비아, 그런 사람 난 몰라.<end>"

## 1220057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自称未来の勇者が、<line-break>おのれの素質を試しに行ったか。<line-break><line-break>やめておけばいいものを。<line-break>賢者の森はそんなに甘くはない。<line-break>立ち直れなくなるぞ。<end>"
EN: "So the self-proclaimed future Hero has gone to test his potential. He should have left well enough alone. The Sage's Forest is not so forgiving. This may crush him beyond recovery.<end>"
KO: "자칭 미래의 용자가 자기 재능을 시험하러 갔나. 그만두면 좋을 것을. 현자의 숲은 만만한 곳이 아니다. 다시 일어서지 못하게 될 수도 있어.<end>"

## 1220058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガルドランというと…<line-break>ああ、あの、未来の勇者などと<line-break>自分で叫んでいた男か。<line-break>賢者の森へと向かったと聞いたが、<line-break>賢者のいる猫屋敷までたどり着くのは<line-break>おそらく無理だろうな。<end>"
EN: "Girldlan...? Ah, the man who went around proclaiming himself the future Hero. I heard he headed for the Sage's Forest, but he will probably never reach Cat Manor, where the Sage resides.<end>"
KO: "갈드란이라면… 아, 미래의 용사라고 스스로 떠들던 그 남자인가. 현자의 숲으로 향했다는 이야기는 들었지만 현자가 있는 고양이 저택까지 가는 건 아마 무리겠지.<end>"

## 1220060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自称勇者様なら賢者の森に向かった<line-break>らしいわ。自分が運命に選ばれた者って<line-break>思い込んでるのね。<end>"
EN: "Apparently our self-proclaimed Hero went to the Sage's Forest. He really believes he was chosen by destiny.<end>"
KO: "자칭 용사님이라면 현자의 숲으로 갔다더라. 자기가 운명에 선택받은 사람이라고 굳게 믿고 있나 봐.<end>"

## 1220062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガルドランさんは、賢者の森へ<line-break>向かったみたいです。運命に選ばれた<line-break>人しか猫屋敷に入れないのに。<line-break>ま、まさかガルドランさんが運命に<line-break>選ばれた人なんでしょうか！？<end>"
EN: "It seems Girldlan went to the Sage's Forest, even though only those chosen by destiny can enter Cat Manor. C-could Girldlan actually be one of the chosen!?<end>"
KO: "갈드란 씨는 현자의 숲으로 간 모양이에요. 운명에 선택받은 사람만 고양이 저택에 들어갈 수 있는데. 서, 설마 갈드란 씨가 운명에 선택받은 사람인 걸까요!?<end>"

## 1220064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（ジラークのアルノートゥン攻めを<line-break>　手伝う約束をした）<line-break><line-break>（準備ができたらアキュリュースへ<line-break>　行かなければ…）<end>"
EN: "(I promised to help Zsilak attack Alnortun.) (Once preparations are complete, I must go to Aqyurius...)<end>"
KO: "(지라크의 알노툰 공격을 돕기로 약속했다) (준비가 되면 아큐류스로 가야 해…)<end>"

## 1220065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークのアルノートゥン攻めを<line-break>手伝うのよね。準備ができたら<line-break>アキュリュースへ行きましょ。<end>"
EN: "We're helping Zsilak attack Alnortun, remember? Once we're ready, let's go to Aqyurius.<end>"
KO: "지라크의 알노툰 공격을 돕는 거지. 준비되면 아큐류스로 가자.<end>"

## 1220066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークのアルノートゥン攻めを<line-break>手伝うのじゃな。準備ができたら<line-break>アキュリュースへ行くとしようぞ。<end>"
EN: "We are to help Zsilak attack Alnortun. Once preparations are complete, let us go to Aqyurius.<end>"
KO: "지라크의 알노툰 공격을 돕는 거로군. 준비가 되면 아큐류스로 가자꾸나.<end>"

## 1220067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ！<line-break>ジラークさんと一緒に戦うって<line-break>言ってませんでしたか？<end>"
EN: "What! Didn't you say you were going to fight alongside Zsilak?<end>"
KO: "엣! 지라크 씨와 함께 싸우겠다고 하지 않았나요?<end>"

## 1220068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークがあんなに真剣に頼んでいた<line-break>のを忘れたのかよ！<line-break>記憶力のない奴だな。<end>"
EN: "Have you forgotten how earnestly Zsilak pleaded with you? Your memory is terrible.<end>"
KO: "지라크가 그렇게 진지하게 부탁했던 걸 잊었냐! 기억력 없는 녀석이네.<end>"

## 1220069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークと一緒にアルノートゥンを<line-break>攻める約束をしていたよね。<line-break>もう準備は終わったの？<end>"
EN: "You promised to attack Alnortun with Zsilak, remember? Have you finished preparing?<end>"
KO: "지라크와 함께 알노툰을 공격하기로 했지. 준비는 다 끝났어?<end>"

## 1220070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークに従ってアルノートゥンを<line-break>攻めるという話ではなかったの？<line-break>…うかつね。<end>"
EN: "Weren't you going to follow Zsilak into the attack on Alnortun? ...How careless.<end>"
KO: "지라크를 따라 알노툰을 공격하기로 한 거 아니었어? …방심했네.<end>"

## 1220071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークさんが聞いたら泣きますよ。<line-break>あなたに仲間になって欲しくて<line-break>一生懸命頼んでいたのですから。<end>"
EN: "Zsilak would weep if he heard that. He pleaded so earnestly because he wanted you to join him.<end>"
KO: "지라크 씨가 들으면 울겠네요. 당신이 동료가 되어 주길 바라서 그렇게 열심히 부탁했는데.<end>"

## 1220072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あらら～、それ聞いたら、<line-break>ジラークはガックリくるわよ？<line-break>あんたに相当期待してたからね。<end>"
EN: "Oh dear. Zsilak would be crushed if he heard that. He had such high hopes for you.<end>"
KO: "어머~ 그 얘기 들으면 지라크가 크게 낙담할걸? 너한테 꽤 기대하고 있었으니까.<end>"

## 1220073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃんしっかりしてくれよ。<line-break>ジラークと一緒にアルノートゥンを<line-break>攻めるんだろ。<line-break>準備ができたらアキュリュースへ<line-break>来てくれって頼まれたんだろ。<end>"
EN: "Get it together, Sis. You're attacking Alnortun with Zsilak, remember? He asked you to come to Aqyurius once you were ready.<end>"
KO: "누나, 정신 차려. 지라크와 함께 알노툰을 공격하는 거잖아. 준비되면 아큐류스로 오라고 부탁받았잖아.<end>"

## 1220074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークのアルノートゥン攻めに<line-break>傭兵として参加するのよ。<line-break>アキュリュースで合流よ。<end>"
EN: "You're joining Zsilak's attack on Alnortun as a mercenary. You are to rendezvous in Aqyurius.<end>"
KO: "지라크의 알노툰 공격에 용병으로 참가하는 거야. 아큐류스에서 합류해.<end>"

## 1220075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラーク側につくんだったな。<line-break>アルノートゥン攻めの用意は<line-break>できたのかい？<line-break>用意ができたなら、<line-break>アキュリュースへ行くとしようや。<end>"
EN: "You were going to side with Zsilak. Are you ready for the attack on Alnortun? If so, let's head to Aqyurius.<end>"
KO: "지라크 쪽에 붙기로 했었지. 알노툰 공격 준비는 끝났나? 준비됐으면 아큐류스로 가자고.<end>"

## 1220076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークのアルノートゥン攻めに<line-break>参加するのだったな。<end>"
EN: "You were going to join Zsilak's attack on Alnortun.<end>"
KO: "지라크의 알노툰 공격에 참가하는 거였지.<end>"

## 1220077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークのアルノートゥン攻めに<line-break>参加するのであろう。<line-break>しっかりせんかい。<end>"
EN: "You are joining Zsilak's attack on Alnortun, are you not? Pull yourself together.<end>"
KO: "지라크의 알노툰 공격에 참가할 거잖아. 정신 차려.<end>"

## 1220078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>もジラーク将軍の<line-break>アルノートゥン攻めに<line-break>参加してくれるんだよね。<line-break>僕もコーンスが差別されない世界を<line-break>つくるために全力で戦うよ。<line-break>さあ！　アキュリュースへ行こうよ。<end>"
EN: "You're joining General Zsilak's attack on Alnortun too, right, <value:$28>? I'll fight with all my strength to create a world where the Konsu aren't discriminated against. Come on! Let's go to Aqyurius.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>도 지라크 장군의 알노툰 공략에 참가해 줄 거지. 나도 콘스가 차별받지 않는 세상을 만들기 위해 전력으로 싸울게. 자! 아큐류스로 가자.<end>"

## 1220080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "高貴で気品あふれるエルフのアタクシが<line-break>どうしてあんたの雇い主を覚えなくちゃ<line-break>いけないのよ。失礼するわねぇ。<end>"
EN: "Why should a noble and refined Elf like me have to remember who employs you? Now, if you'll excuse me.<end>"
KO: "고귀하고 기품 넘치는 엘프인 내가 왜 네 고용주까지 기억해야 하는데. 실례할게.<end>"

## 1220081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よー、平和ですな傭兵さん…<line-break>拙者にはダジャレの才能がないので<line-break>あろうか…否！　くじけませんぞ！<end>"
EN: "Ah, all is mercenarily peaceful, I see... Could it be that I have no talent for puns? No! I shall not be discouraged!<end>"
KO: "안녕하십니까, 평화롭군요 용병님… 소인에게는 말장난 재능이 없는 걸까요… 아니! 꺾이지 않겠습니다!<end>"

## 1220082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークさんと一緒にアルノートゥンを<line-break>攻めるんだよぉ。<line-break>でもねルルアンタ、戦争は嫌いなの。<line-break>だから、本当は<value:$28>にも<line-break>ジラークさんにも戦わなくていい方法を<line-break>考えて欲しいなぁ…。<end>"
EN: "We're going to attack Alnortun with Zsilak. But Lulluantha hates war. So I really wish <value:$28> and Zsilak would find a way for neither of you to have to fight...<end>"
KO: "지라크 씨와 함께 알노툰을 공격하는 거야. 하지만 릴비는 전쟁 싫어. 그러니까 사실은 <value:$28>도 지라크 씨도 싸우지 않아도 되는 방법을 생각해 줬으면 좋겠어….<end>"

## 1220083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>ジラークと共に、アルノートゥンを<line-break>攻めると約束したのではないのか。<end>"
EN: "............... Did you not promise to attack Alnortun alongside Zsilak?<end>"
KO: "………………. 지라크와 함께 알노툰을 공격하기로 약속하지 않았나.<end>"

## 1220084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなことも忘れたのか。<line-break>ジラークのアルノートゥン攻めも、<line-break>先行き不安だな。<end>"
EN: "You forgot even that? Zsilak's attack on Alnortun does not bode well.<end>"
KO: "그런 것도 잊었나. 지라크의 알노툰 공략도 앞날이 불안하군.<end>"

## 1220085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラーク将軍が、あんなに一生懸命<line-break>頼んできたのを忘れちゃったの？<line-break>ジラーク将軍が聞いたら泣くよぉ。<line-break>ふふふ…、冗談、冗談。<line-break><value:$28>は忙しいからね。<line-break>たま～に物忘れするのも仕方ないよ。<end>"
EN: "You forgot how earnestly General Zsilak pleaded with you? He'll cry if he hears about this. Hee hee... Just kidding. <value:$28> is very busy, so forgetting things once in a while can't be helped.<end>"
KO: "지라크 장군이 그렇게 열심히 부탁한 걸 잊어버렸어? 지라크 장군이 들으면 울겠다. 후후후… 농담이야, 농담. <value:$28>는 바쁘니까 가끔 깜빡하는 것도 어쩔 수 없지.<end>"

## 1220086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君が参加するのは、ジラークの<line-break>アルノートゥン攻めだったはずだ。<line-break>帝国の面汚しになってくれるなよ。<end>"
EN: "You are supposed to be joining Zsilak's attack on Alnortun. Do not disgrace the Empire.<end>"
KO: "네가 참가하는 건 지라크의 알노툰 공격이었을 텐데. 제국의 수치가 되지는 마라.<end>"

## 1220087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークに依頼されたのは<line-break>アルノートゥンの攻撃ですが、<line-break>合流地はアキュリュースです。<end>"
EN: "Zsilak asked you to attack Alnortun, but the rendezvous point is Aqyurius.<end>"
KO: "지라크에게 받은 의뢰는 알노툰 공격이지만 합류 장소는 아큐류스입니다.<end>"

## 1220088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークは、アキュリュースに行けって<line-break>言ったんだよね。<line-break>あっ、気をつけてね。<end>"
EN: "Zsilak told you to go to Aqyurius, right? Oh, and be careful.<end>"
KO: "지라크는 아큐류스로 가라고 했지. 아, 조심해.<end>"

## 1220090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークが頼んできたのは、<line-break>アルノートゥン攻めだったはずだ。<line-break><line-break>準備が整い次第、アキュリュースに<line-break>来てくれとのことだった。<end>"
EN: "Zsilak asked you to help attack Alnortun. He said to come to Aqyurius as soon as your preparations were complete.<end>"
KO: "지라크가 부탁한 건 알노툰 공략이었을 거야. 준비가 되는 대로 아큐류스에 와 달라고 했지.<end>"

## 1220091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おい、しっかりするんだ。<line-break>ジラークと共にアルノートゥンを<line-break>攻めると決めただろ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, pull yourself together. You decided to attack Alnortun alongside Zsilak.<end>"
KO: "어이, 정신 차려. 지라크와 함께 알노툰을 공격하기로 했잖아.<end>"

## 1220092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークと一緒に<line-break>アルノートゥンを攻めるんでしょ。<line-break>さあ、アキュリュースに行くわよ。<end>"
EN: "You're attacking Alnortun with Zsilak, remember? Come on, we're going to Aqyurius.<end>"
KO: "지라크와 함께 알노툰을 공격하는 거지. 자, 아큐류스로 가자.<end>"

## 1220093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークと共にアルノートゥンを<line-break>攻めるとのことでしたが。<end>"
EN: "I was told you would attack Alnortun alongside Zsilak.<end>"
KO: "지라크와 함께 알노툰을 공격한다고 했습니다만.<end>"

## 1220094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークさんに怒られちゃいますよ。<line-break>一緒にアルノートゥンを攻めるって<line-break>約束していたじゃないですか。<line-break>でもできれば戦争なんてしてほしく<line-break>ないです。人がいっぱい死んじゃうのは<line-break>つらいですから。<end>"
EN: "Zsilak will be angry with you. You promised to attack Alnortun together, remember? But if possible, I wish you wouldn't go to war at all. It's painful when so many people die.<end>"
KO: "지라크 씨에게 혼날 거예요. 같이 알노툰을 공격하기로 약속했잖아요. 하지만 가능하면 전쟁 같은 건 하지 않았으면 해요. 사람이 많이 죽는 건 괴로우니까요.<end>"

## 1220095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークと<line-break>アルノートゥンを攻めると<line-break>約束してたんじゃないのかい？<end>"
EN: "Didn't you promise to attack Alnortun with Zsilak?<end>"
KO: "지라크와 함께 알노툰을 공격하기로 약속한 거 아니었어?<end>"

## 1220096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（エルズの冒険者ギルドで、<line-break>　風泣きの洞窟に現れる怪物を<line-break>　倒してくれと頼まれたな）<line-break>（怪物を倒したら、<line-break>　忘れずに報告しないと…）<end>"
EN: "(At the Adventurers' Guild in Elz, I accepted a request to slay the monster appearing in Wind-Wailing Cave.) (Once it is defeated, I must remember to report back...)<end>"
KO: "(엘즈 모험자 길드에서 바람우는 동굴에 나타나는 괴물을 쓰러뜨려 달라는 의뢰를 받았지. 괴물을 쓰러뜨리면 잊지 말고 보고해야 해…)<end>"

## 1220097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、エルズの冒険者ギルドで<line-break>引き受けた怪物退治の仕事ね。<line-break>そいつは風泣きの洞窟に現れるのよね。<line-break>エルズの人たちも、洞窟に入れなくて<line-break>困っているみたいだし、急ぎましょ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, the monster-slaying job you accepted at the Adventurers' Guild in Elz. The creature appears in Wind-Wailing Cave, right? The people of Elz are having trouble because they cannot enter the cave, so let's hurry.<end>"
KO: "아, 엘즈 모험자 길드에서 맡은 괴물 퇴치 일이네. 그 녀석은 바람우는 동굴에 나타나지. 엘즈 사람들도 동굴에 못 들어가서 곤란한 모양이니 서두르자.<end>"

## 1220098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "風泣きの洞窟に現れるという怪物を<line-break>退治するのであったな。<line-break>準備ができたら出発しようぞ。<line-break>見事、怪物を倒し冒険者ギルドへ<line-break>報告すれば、エルズの人々も安心して<line-break>コロル石を拾いに行けるようになるな。<end>"
EN: "We are to slay the monster said to appear in Wind-Wailing Cave. Let us depart once preparations are complete. If we defeat it and report back to the Adventurers' Guild, the people of Elz will be able to gather Korol Stone without fear.<end>"
KO: "바람우는 동굴에 나타난다는 괴물을 퇴치하는 일이었지. 준비가 되면 출발하자. 멋지게 괴물을 쓰러뜨리고 모험자 길드에 보고하면 엘즈 사람들도 안심하고 코롤석을 주우러 갈 수 있겠구나.<end>"

## 1220099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たしか、風泣きの洞窟に出る<line-break>怪物を退治するお仕事でしたよね。<line-break>どんな怪物なのかな？<end>"
EN: "The job was to slay the monster in Wind-Wailing Cave, wasn't it? I wonder what kind of monster it is.<end>"
KO: "분명 바람 우는 동굴에 나타나는 괴물을 퇴치하는 일이었죠. 어떤 괴물일까?<end>"

## 1220100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ものすごい怪物だって言うじゃないか。<line-break>よし、行こうぜ！　風泣きの洞窟によ！<line-break>俺の実力を見せてやるぜ！！<end>"
EN: "They say it's one incredible monster. All right, let's go―to Wind-Wailing Cave! I'll show you what I can do!<end>"
KO: "엄청난 괴물이라며. 좋아, 가자! 바람 우는 동굴로! 내 실력을 보여 주겠어!!<end>"

## 1220101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボクらが怪物を退治しないと、<line-break>エルズの人たちはコロル石を<line-break>採掘できなくて困るんだ。<line-break>できるだけ早く怪物を退治して、<line-break>町の人たちを安心させてあげたいよ。<line-break>キミもそう思うよね？<end>"
EN: "Unless we defeat the monster, the people of Elz cannot mine Korol Stone. I want to slay it as soon as possible and put the townspeople's minds at ease. You feel the same, right?<end>"
KO: "우리가 괴물을 퇴치하지 않으면 엘즈 사람들은 코롤석을 채굴할 수 없어서 곤란해. 최대한 빨리 괴물을 퇴치해서 마을 사람들을 안심시켜 주고 싶어. 너도 그렇게 생각하지?<end>"

## 1220102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "風泣きの洞窟での怪物退治の依頼よ。<line-break>コロル石が採掘できず、エルズの<line-break>住民が困り果てているらしいわ。<end>"
EN: "The request is to slay the monster in Wind-Wailing Cave. Apparently the people of Elz are desperate because they cannot mine Korol Stone.<end>"
KO: "바람우는 동굴의 괴물 퇴치 의뢰야. 코롤석을 채굴할 수 없어서 엘즈 주민들이 몹시 곤란해하고 있대.<end>"

## 1220103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "風泣きの洞窟での怪物退治です。<line-break>どうやらかなりの大物らしいですが、<line-break>エルズの皆さんの嘆願もあります。<line-break>がんばってくださいね、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Your task is to slay the monster in Wind-Wailing Cave. It seems to be quite formidable, but the people of Elz have pleaded for help. Do your best, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "바람우는 동굴의 괴물 퇴치입니다. 꽤 거물인 모양이지만 엘즈 사람들의 탄원도 있습니다. 힘내 주세요, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1220104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "風泣きの洞窟に出るってヤツね。<line-break>とんでもないのがいるらしいって？<line-break>大丈夫、カルラさんがついてるって！<end>"
EN: "Oh, the thing that appears in Wind-Wailing Cave. They say it's something truly awful? Don't worry―the great Karula is with you!<end>"
KO: "바람 우는 동굴에 나온다는 그 녀석이지. 엄청난 게 있다던데? 괜찮아, 카를라 씨가 함께니까!<end>"

## 1220105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃん、この仕事がんばろうな。<line-break>エルズの人たちは、俺たちを頼って<line-break>いるんだものな。<line-break>風泣きの洞窟の怪物を退治して<line-break>冒険者ギルドに報告すれば、<line-break>町の人たちも喜ぶだろうね。<end>"
EN: "Let's do our best on this job, Sis. The people of Elz are counting on us. If we slay the monster in Wind-Wailing Cave and report back to the Adventurers' Guild, the townspeople will be delighted.<end>"
KO: "누나, 이 일 힘내자. 엘즈 사람들은 우리를 믿고 있잖아. 풍곡의 동굴 괴물을 퇴치하고 모험자 길드에 보고하면 마을 사람들도 기뻐할 거야.<end>"

## 1220106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "風泣きの洞窟での怪物退治。<line-break>コロル石採掘所で生計を立てている<line-break>エルズの住人たちの悲願よ。<end>"
EN: "Slay the monster in Wind-Wailing Cave. It is the dearest wish of the people of Elz, whose livelihoods depend on the Korol Stone mine.<end>"
KO: "바람 우는 동굴의 괴물 퇴치. 코롤석 채굴장으로 생계를 잇는 엘즈 주민들의 간절한 소원이야.<end>"

## 1220107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "風泣きの洞窟にものすごい怪物が<line-break>現れるもんで、エルズの人らは<line-break>コロル石を採掘できなくて困っている。<line-break>その怪物を退治するのが、<line-break>お前さんのお仕事ってわけだ。<line-break>退治したらギルドに報告するんだぜ。<end>"
EN: "A fearsome monster has been appearing in Wind-Wailing Cave, leaving the people of Elz unable to mine Korol Stone. Your job is to slay it. Once it's dead, report back to the Guild.<end>"
KO: "풍곡의 동굴에 엄청난 괴물이 나타나서 엘즈 사람들은 코롤석을 채굴하지 못해 곤란해하고 있어. 그 괴물을 퇴치하는 게 네 일이야. 퇴치했으면 길드에 보고하라고.<end>"

## 1220108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "風泣きの洞窟に現れる怪物か…。<line-break>気になる事がある。<line-break>付き合ってやってもいいぞ。<end>"
EN: "The monster appearing in Wind-Wailing Cave... Something about it concerns me. I suppose I could accompany you.<end>"
KO: "바람우는 동굴에 나타나는 괴물인가…. 신경 쓰이는 게 있어. 함께 가 줘도 좋다.<end>"

## 1220109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルズの人々を安心させるためにも、<line-break>ものすごい怪物とやらを退治せねばな。<line-break>場所は風泣きの洞窟じゃったな？<line-break>こうして話す時間も惜しいわい。<line-break>さっさと退治して、冒険者ギルドに<line-break>報告するのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "We must slay this so-called fearsome monster and put the people of Elz at ease. It was in Wind-Wailing Cave, yes? We have no time to waste talking. Slay it at once and report back to the Adventurers' Guild.<end>"
KO: "엘즈 사람들을 안심시키기 위해서라도 그 엄청난 괴물이라는 놈을 퇴치해야겠구나. 장소는 바람우는 동굴이었지? 이렇게 이야기할 시간도 아깝다. 얼른 퇴치하고 모험자 길드에 보고하자꾸나.<end>"

## 1220110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルズのギルドで引き受けた仕事だね。<line-break>たしか、風泣きの洞窟に現れる怪物を<line-break>退治する仕事だよ。<line-break>どんな強敵かわからないから、<line-break>万全の準備をして出かけなくちゃね。<end>"
EN: "It's the job we accepted at the Guild in Elz. We have to slay the monster that appears in Wind-Wailing Cave. We don't know how powerful it is, so we'll need to prepare thoroughly before setting out.<end>"
KO: "엘즈 길드에서 맡은 일이네. 분명 바람우는 동굴에 나타나는 괴물을 퇴치하는 일이야. 어떤 강적인지 모르니 만반의 준비를 하고 가야 해.<end>"

## 1220111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "風泣きの洞窟に出没する怪物か…。<line-break>気にかかることがある。<line-break>用心してくれ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "The monster haunting Wind-Wailing Cave... Something about it troubles me. Be careful, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "바람우는 동굴에 나타나는 괴물인가…. 신경 쓰이는 게 있어. 조심해, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1220112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "高貴なエルフのアタクシが、<line-break>怪物退治に興味があるわけないでしょ。<line-break>くだらない質問をしないで欲しいわ！<end>"
EN: "Why would a noble Elf like me have any interest in slaying monsters? Stop asking such ridiculous questions!<end>"
KO: "고귀한 엘프인 이 몸이 괴물 퇴치에 관심 있을 리 없잖아. 시시한 질문은 하지 말아 줘!<end>"

## 1220113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "怪物かい？　ぶっ飛ばしてやろう。<line-break>かい？、ぶっと…<line-break>かいぶつ…。<line-break>兄弟子、いかがですかな？<end>"
EN: "A monster, you say? We'll cave it in! Cave it... cave... Senior Disciple, what do you think?<end>"
KO: "괴물이라고? 날려 버리자. 괴… 날려… 괴물…. 사형, 어떠십니까?<end>"

## 1220114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "怪物は風泣きの洞窟にいるんだよぉ。<line-break>はやく退治して、エルズの人たちに<line-break>安心して欲しいなぁ。<line-break>それから怪物を退治したら、<line-break>忘れずに報告しなきゃダメだよ。<end>"
EN: "The monster is in Wind-Wailing Cave. I want to defeat it quickly so the people of Elz can stop worrying. And once it's dead, don't forget to report back.<end>"
KO: "괴물은 바람우는 동굴에 있어. 빨리 퇴치해서 엘즈 사람들이 안심했으면 좋겠어. 그리고 괴물을 쓰러뜨리면 잊지 말고 보고해야 해.<end>"

## 1220115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>風泣きの洞窟に現れる怪物を倒すなど<line-break>お前にとっては造作もないことだ。<line-break>さっさと片付けてしまえ。<end>"
EN: "............... Defeating the monster in Wind-Wailing Cave should be child's play for you. Get it over with.<end>"
KO: "………………. 바람울음 동굴에 나타나는 괴물을 쓰러뜨리는 건 네게 어려운 일도 아니야. 얼른 해치워 버려.<end>"

## 1220116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "話ではかなりの大物だということだが<line-break>臆病な村人たちの言は信用できん。<line-break>風泣きの洞窟へ行くぞ。<end>"
EN: "They say it is quite formidable, but the words of cowardly villagers cannot be trusted. We are going to Wind-Wailing Cave.<end>"
KO: "말로는 상당한 거물이라지만 겁쟁이 마을 사람들의 말은 믿을 수 없다. 바람우는 동굴로 간다.<end>"

## 1220117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ものすごい怪物は風泣きの洞窟に<line-break>現れるって言うよ。<line-break>どんなのか興味あるよねぇ？<line-break>ねえねえ、早く行こうよ。<line-break>ギルドの主人もエルズの人たちも<line-break><value:$28>の報告を待ってるよ。<end>"
EN: "They say the fearsome monster appears in Wind-Wailing Cave. Aren't you curious what it's like? Come on, let's hurry! The Guild master and the people of Elz are waiting for <value:$28> to report back.<end>"
KO: "엄청난 괴물이 풍곡의 동굴에 나타난다더라. 어떤 놈인지 궁금하지? 있잖아, 빨리 가자. 길드 주인도 엘즈 사람들도 <value:$28>의 보고를 기다리고 있어.<end>"

## 1220118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "風泣きの洞窟は大陸中でも重要な<line-break>コロル石の産地だ。モンスター<line-break>ごときに占拠させるわけにはいかん。<end>"
EN: "Wind-Wailing Cave is one of the continent's most important sources of Korol Stone. We cannot let a mere monster occupy it.<end>"
KO: "바람 우는 동굴은 대륙에서도 중요한 코롤석 산지다. 몬스터 따위에게 점거시킬 수는 없다.<end>"

## 1220119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "風泣きの洞窟にいるという怪物は<line-break>かなり強大だと聞きました。<line-break>準備はもうお済みですか？<end>"
EN: "I heard the monster in Wind-Wailing Cave is extremely powerful. Have you finished preparing?<end>"
KO: "바람 우는 동굴에 있다는 괴물은 상당히 강하다고 들었습니다. 준비는 다 마치셨나요?<end>"

## 1220120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "風泣きの洞窟にいる怪物は、<line-break>とってもすごいっていったよ。<line-break>頑張らないとね！<end>"
EN: "They said the monster in Wind-Wailing Cave is really incredible. We'll have to do our best!<end>"
KO: "바람우는 동굴에 있는 괴물은 엄청나다고 했어. 힘내야겠네!<end>"

## 1220121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "風泣きの洞窟の怪物というのは、<line-break>聞く限り、かなりの大物らしいな。<line-break>これはもしや、イズキヤルの…。<line-break>ぜひこの目で確かめたい。<line-break>必ずわしも連れて行ってくれよ。<end>"
EN: "From what I have heard, the monster in Wind-Wailing Cave is quite formidable. Could it perhaps be one of Izkiyal's...? I must see it with my own eyes. Be sure to take me with you.<end>"
KO: "바람우는 동굴의 괴물이라는 건 듣기로 상당한 거물인 모양이군. 혹시 이즈키얄의…. 꼭 내 눈으로 확인하고 싶다. 반드시 나도 데려가 다오.<end>"

## 1220122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "風泣きの洞窟の怪物を退治してくれ<line-break>というのが、エルズの人たちの<line-break>悲願だ。<line-break>大切なコロル石が採れなくては<line-break>彼らは生活していけない。<line-break>頑張ろう、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "The people of Elz desperately want the monster in Wind-Wailing Cave slain. Without their precious Korol Stone, they cannot make a living. Let us do our best, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "바람우는 동굴의 괴물을 퇴치해 달라는 건 엘즈 사람들의 숙원이야. 소중한 코롤석을 캐지 못하면 그들은 살아갈 수 없어. 힘내자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1220123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルズの人たちが<line-break>コロル石を取れなくて困ってるらしい。<line-break>　<line-break>風泣きの洞窟の化け物を倒して<line-break>皆を助けるぞ。<end>"
EN: "Apparently the people of Elz are suffering because they cannot gather Korol Stone. We'll defeat the beast in Wind-Wailing Cave and help everyone.<end>"
KO: "엘즈 사람들이 코롤석을 캐지 못해 곤란하다더군. 바람울음 동굴의 괴물을 쓰러뜨리고 모두를 돕자.<end>"

## 1220124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "風泣きの洞窟で怪物退治ね。<line-break>結構手ごわいらしいから<line-break>気をつけて行きましょ。<end>"
EN: "We're slaying a monster in Wind-Wailing Cave. It is supposed to be fairly tough, so let's be careful.<end>"
KO: "바람우는 동굴에서 괴물 퇴치라. 꽤 강한 모양이니 조심해서 가자.<end>"

## 1220126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ものすごい怪物が<line-break>風泣きの洞窟にいるらしいですよ。<line-break>頼まれたのは、その怪物の退治です。<line-break>エルズの人たちは<line-break>コロル石が採掘できなくて困ってます。<line-break>助けてあげましょう！<end>"
EN: "Apparently there is a fearsome monster in Wind-Wailing Cave. We were asked to slay it. The people of Elz are suffering because they cannot mine Korol Stone. Let's help them!<end>"
KO: "엄청난 괴물이 바람 우는 동굴에 있대요. 부탁받은 건 그 괴물 퇴치예요. 엘즈 사람들은 코롤석을 채굴하지 못해서 곤란해하고 있어요. 도와주자고요!<end>"

## 1220128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（エンシャントの冒険者ギルドで、<line-break>　女魔道士に、乙女の鏡に現れる<line-break>　魔物を倒すよう依頼されたな）<line-break>（魔物を倒したら、<line-break>　忘れずに報告しないと…）<end>"
EN: "(At the Ancient Adventurers' Guild, a female mage asked me to slay the monster appearing at Maiden's Mirror.) (Once it is defeated, I must remember to report back...)<end>"
KO: "(엔샨트의 모험자 길드에서 여마도사에게 소녀의 거울에 나타나는 마물을 쓰러뜨려 달라는 의뢰를 받았지.) (마물을 쓰러뜨리면 잊지 말고 보고해야 해…)<end>"

## 1220129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントのギルドで頼まれた、<line-break>乙女の鏡に現れる魔物を倒して<line-break>欲しいっていう依頼のことね。<line-break>報酬額も聞かずに依頼を受けちゃう<line-break>なんて…。<line-break>女魔道士の色香に惑わされたのかしら？<end>"
EN: "Oh, the request you received at the Guild in Ancient to slay the monster appearing at Maiden's Mirror. You accepted without even asking about the reward... Did the female mage's charms cloud your judgment?<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 길드에서 부탁받은, 처녀의 거울에 나타나는 마물을 쓰러뜨려 달라는 의뢰 말이지. 보수도 안 듣고 맡다니…. 여마도사의 색기에 넘어간 거 아냐?<end>"

## 1220130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、エンシャントのギルドで<line-break>アカデミーの女魔道士に頼まれた<line-break>仕事のことだな。<line-break>依頼内容は乙女の鏡に現れる魔物を<line-break>退治して欲しいというものだったな。<line-break>お主、忘れてしまったのか？<end>"
EN: "Ah, the job that female mage from the Academy gave you at the Guild in Ancient. She asked you to slay the monster appearing at Maiden's Mirror. Have you forgotten?<end>"
KO: "오, 엔샨트 길드에서 아카데미의 여마도사에게 부탁받은 일이군. 의뢰 내용은 처녀의 거울에 나타나는 마물을 퇴치해 달라는 것이었지. 자네, 잊어버렸나?<end>"

## 1220131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様に魔物退治を<line-break>依頼した女魔道士って魔道アカデミーの<line-break>関係者じゃないですよね？<line-break>ユーリスは退学だ！<line-break>なんて言ってないですよね？<end>"
EN: "The female mage who asked <value:$28> to slay the monster isn't connected to the Magic Academy, is she? She didn't say, “Julls is expelled!” or anything like that, did she?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님에게 마물 퇴치를 의뢰한 여마도사는 마도 아카데미 관계자가 아니죠? 유리스는 퇴학이다! 같은 말은 안 했죠?<end>"

## 1220132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "乙女の鏡とかいう湖に出る魔物を<line-break>倒してくれっていう依頼だろ。<line-break>もう、準備はできたのか？<line-break>他のヤツに倒されちまったら困るだろ。<line-break>さっさと退治してしまおうぜ。<line-break>報酬は、ほーしゅーないのか？<end>"
EN: "It's the request to slay the monster appearing in that lake called Maiden's Mirror, right? Are you ready? We can't let someone else defeat it first. Let's hurry and finish it off. And we'd better get our just re-ward!<end>"
KO: "처녀의 거울이라는 호수에 나타나는 마물을 쓰러뜨리는 의뢰지. 준비는 끝났어? 다른 녀석이 먼저 잡으면 곤란하잖아. 얼른 퇴치하자. 보수는… 보수 없는 건 아니겠지?<end>"

## 1220133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボクの知る限りだと、<line-break>エンシャントの北にある乙女の鏡に、<line-break>退治を依頼された魔物が出るんだよね。<line-break>魔物を退治したら、<line-break>エンシャントのギルドに行って、<line-break>女魔道士に報告するのを忘れないでよ。<end>"
EN: "As far as I know, the monster you were asked to slay appears at Maiden's Mirror, north of Ancient. Once it's dead, don't forget to go to the Guild in Ancient and report to the female mage.<end>"
KO: "내가 알기로는 엔샨트 북쪽 처녀의 거울에 퇴치 의뢰를 받은 마물이 나타나. 마물을 쓰러뜨리면 엔샨트 길드에 가서 여자 마도사에게 보고하는 것도 잊지 마.<end>"

## 1220134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その依頼主、果たして信用に足りる<line-break>人物かしら？　都合よく魔物退治だけ<line-break>させるつもりだったら、どうするの？<end>"
EN: "Can that client really be trusted? What will you do if all she wants is to use you to slay the monster?<end>"
KO: "그 의뢰인, 정말 믿을 만한 사람일까? 자기 편한 대로 마물 퇴치만 시키려는 거면 어떡할 거야?<end>"

## 1220135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "乙女の鏡の魔物退治でしたか。<line-break>それで、その女魔道士が誰なのか、<line-break>ちゃんと調べたのですか？<line-break>…いや、お人よしは、あなたの<line-break>いいところだと思いますよ。<line-break>気をつけて行って来てください。<end>"
EN: "It was the monster at Maiden's Mirror, wasn't it? Did you properly investigate who that female mage is? ...No, I do think your trusting nature is one of your better qualities. Take care out there.<end>"
KO: "처녀의 거울 마물 퇴치였습니까. 그런데 그 여자 마도사가 누군지 제대로 알아봤나요? …아니, 남 좋은 게 당신 장점이라고 생각합니다. 조심해서 다녀오세요.<end>"

## 1220136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "乙女の鏡で魔物退治、ってやつね。<line-break>軽～く引き受けちゃったのは、<line-break>お得意な仕事内容だから？<end>"
EN: "Oh, the monster-slaying job at Maiden's Mirror. Did you accept it so casually because it's your specialty?<end>"
KO: "처녀의 거울에서 마물 퇴치라는 거지. 가볍게 맡은 건 네가 잘하는 종류의 일이어서야?<end>"

## 1220137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃんがエンシャントのギルドで<line-break>女魔道士に依頼された魔物退治の<line-break>ことかい？<line-break>乙女の鏡に出るっていう魔物を<line-break>退治すれば依頼達成なんだろ。<line-break>まさか、姉ちゃん忘れてたのか？<end>"
EN: "You mean the monster-slaying job that female mage gave you at the Guild in Ancient, Sis? The request is complete once you slay the monster appearing at Maiden's Mirror, right? Don't tell me you forgot.<end>"
KO: "누나가 엔샨트 길드에서 여자 마도사에게 받은 마물 퇴치 의뢰 말이지? 처녀의 거울에 나타난다는 마물을 쓰러뜨리면 끝나는 거잖아. 설마 누나, 잊은 거야?<end>"

## 1220138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "乙女の鏡に出没する魔物退治。<line-break>依頼内容自体だけ見れば、<line-break>ごくありふれたものだわ。<end>"
EN: "Slaying the monster that appears at Maiden's Mirror. Judging by the request alone, it is perfectly ordinary.<end>"
KO: "소녀의 거울에 출몰하는 마물 퇴치. 의뢰 내용만 보면 아주 흔한 일이야.<end>"

## 1220139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、乙女の鏡に現れる魔物を<line-break>退治してくれって依頼の話ね。<line-break>なんだ？　怖じ気づいたのかい。<line-break>お前さんの評判を聞いて、<line-break>わざわざ依頼してきたっていうのに<line-break>裏切るわけにもいかんわな。<end>"
EN: "Oh, the request to slay the monster appearing at Maiden's Mirror. What, have you lost your nerve? The client sought you out after hearing of your reputation. You can hardly betray that trust.<end>"
KO: "아, 처녀의 거울에 나타나는 마물을 퇴치해 달라는 의뢰 말이지. 왜, 겁먹은 거냐? 네 평판을 듣고 일부러 의뢰해 왔는데 배신할 수도 없잖아.<end>"

## 1220140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "乙女の鏡に現れる魔物か…。<line-break>気になる事がある。<line-break>付き合ってやってもいいぞ。<end>"
EN: "The monster appearing at Maiden's Mirror... Something about it concerns me. I suppose I could accompany you.<end>"
KO: "소녀의 거울에 나타나는 마물인가…. 마음에 걸리는 게 있다. 같이 가 줘도 좋다.<end>"

## 1220141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "乙女の鏡に現れる魔物じゃと…。<line-break>ふん、恐れることはないわ。<line-break>わしが、ついておるのじゃからのう。<line-break>さっさと退治して、その女魔道士を<line-break>喜ばせてやるとよいわい。<end>"
EN: "A monster at Maiden's Mirror...? Hmph, there is nothing to fear. I shall be with you. Hurry and slay it, and make that female mage happy.<end>"
KO: "소녀의 거울에 나타나는 마물이라…. 흥, 두려워할 것 없다. 내가 함께하니까 말이지. 얼른 해치워서 그 여자 마도사를 기쁘게 해 주게.<end>"

## 1220142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "乙女の鏡に現れる魔物を退治して<line-break>欲しいってお願いだったね。<line-break>特に期限はなかったと思うよ。<line-break>わざわざ<value:$28>を指名して<line-break>きたんだから責任重大だね。<line-break>失敗は許されないよ。<end>"
EN: "The request was to slay the monster appearing at Maiden's Mirror. I don't think there was any particular deadline. The client specifically requested <value:$28>, so this is a serious responsibility. Failure is not an option.<end>"
KO: "처녀의 거울에 나타나는 마물을 퇴치해 달라는 부탁이었지. 특별한 기한은 없었던 것 같아. 일부러 <value:$28>을 지명해서 부탁한 거니까 책임이 막중하네. 실패는 용납되지 않아.<end>"

## 1220143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "乙女の鏡に現れる魔物退治か。<line-break>依頼主が何者かはともかく、<line-break>油断しないようにな。<end>"
EN: "Slaying the monster appearing at Maiden's Mirror, is it? Whatever the client's identity, do not let your guard down.<end>"
KO: "소녀의 거울에 나타나는 마물 퇴치인가. 의뢰인이 누군지는 둘째 치고 방심하지 마.<end>"

## 1220144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "高貴なエルフのアタクシが、<line-break>ど～して、女魔道士のお願いを<line-break>聞かなくちゃいけないわけぇ？<line-break>魔道士なら、魔物退治なんて<line-break>自分でやればいいんだわ！<end>"
EN: "Why should a noble Elf like me grant a female mage's request? If she's a mage, she can slay the monster herself!<end>"
KO: "고귀한 엘프인 내가 왜 여자 마도사의 부탁을 들어줘야 하는 건데? 마도사라면 마물 퇴치쯤은 자기가 하면 되잖아!<end>"

## 1220145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "兄弟子！<line-break>女魔道士の色香に<line-break>まどうわされもうしたな！<line-break>魔道と惑うのシャレ…<line-break>いや、もう何も語りますまい。<line-break>兄弟子の顔が物語っておるゆえ。<end>"
EN: "Senior Disciple! That female mage's charms have utterly bewitched you! A mage making you amazed... No, I shall say no more. Your face says it all.<end>"
KO: "사형! 여자 마도사의 미색에 홀리셨군요! 마도와 홀리다의 말장난… 아니, 이제 아무 말도 하지 않겠습니다. 사형의 얼굴이 다 말해 주는군요.<end>"

## 1220146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔道士のお姉さんのお願い事？<line-break>魔物退治のことかなぁ？<line-break>それだったらルルアンタ知ってるよ。<line-break>魔物は乙女の鏡に現れて、<line-break>とっても強そうなんだって。<line-break><value:$28>、勝てる自信ある？<end>"
EN: "A favor for the lady mage? Do you mean slaying the monster? Lulluantha knows about that. They say it appears at Maiden's Mirror and looks really strong. Are you sure you can beat it, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "마도사 누나의 부탁? 마물 퇴치 말인가? 그거라면 릴비도 알아. 마물은 처녀의 거울에 나타나고 엄청 강해 보인대. <value:$28>, 이길 자신 있어?<end>"

## 1220147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>乙女の鏡の魔物か…。<line-break>俺を倒したお前の実力なら<line-break>簡単な仕事だな。<end>"
EN: "............... The monster at Maiden's Mirror... With the strength you showed in defeating me, it should be an easy job.<end>"
KO: "………………. 처녀의 거울의 마물인가…. 나를 쓰러뜨린 네 실력이라면 쉬운 일이겠군.<end>"

## 1220148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "依頼を受けるのはかまわないが、<line-break>その女魔道士は信用の置ける<line-break>人物なのか？<line-break>乙女の鏡の魔物を倒させて、<line-break>あとは知らぬ存ぜぬだったら<line-break>どうするつもりだ。<end>"
EN: "Accepting the request is fine, but can that female mage be trusted? What will you do if she has you slay the monster at Maiden's Mirror and then disclaims all responsibility?<end>"
KO: "의뢰를 받는 건 상관없지만 그 여자 마도사는 믿을 만한 인물인가? 소녀의 거울의 마물을 쓰러뜨리게 하고 나 몰라라 하면 어쩔 셈이지?<end>"

## 1220149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "乙女の鏡に魔物が出るのは<line-break>あんまり、きれいじゃないね。<line-break><value:$28>もイヤだよねぇ？<line-break>だったら、さっさとやっつけちゃおう。<line-break>だいじょうぶ、だいじょうぶ、<line-break><value:$28>なら楽勝だよ。<end>"
EN: "Having a monster at Maiden's Mirror isn't very pretty. You don't like it either, do you, <value:$28>? Then let's hurry and beat it. Don't worry, don't worry. It'll be easy for <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "처녀의 거울에 마물이 나오는 건 별로 예쁘지 않네. <value:$28>도 싫지? 그럼 얼른 해치워 버리자. 괜찮아, 괜찮아. <value:$28>라면 식은 죽 먹기야.<end>"

## 1220150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "怪物退治は君の十八番ではないか。<line-break>速やかに乙女の鏡へ向かいたまえ。<end>"
EN: "Slaying monsters is your specialty, is it not? Proceed to Maiden's Mirror at once.<end>"
KO: "괴물 퇴치는 자네 특기 아닌가. 얼른 소녀의 거울로 가게.<end>"

## 1220151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "乙女の鏡に出没する魔物退治が<line-break>依頼の内容ですが、ギルドを<line-break>通していない依頼なだけに、<line-break>解決後、その女魔道士を<line-break>逃がさないのが重要です。<end>"
EN: "The request is to slay the monster appearing at Maiden's Mirror. Since it did not come through the Guild, it is important not to let the female mage get away afterward.<end>"
KO: "소녀의 거울에 출몰하는 마물 퇴치가 의뢰 내용이지만 길드를 통하지 않은 의뢰인 만큼 해결 뒤 그 여자 마도사를 놓치지 않는 게 중요합니다.<end>"

## 1220152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "乙女の鏡にいる魔物退治だって。<line-break>お礼、ちゃんともらえるのかな？<end>"
EN: "They want you to slay the monster at Maiden's Mirror. I wonder if you'll actually receive a reward.<end>"
KO: "소녀의 거울에 있는 마물 퇴치래. 보수는 제대로 받을 수 있을까?<end>"

## 1220153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギルドを通していないだけに、<line-break>乙女の鏡に出没する魔物については<line-break>情報が少ないな。<line-break>しかし、これももしかすると…。<line-break>足手まといにはならん。<line-break>わしも連れて行ってくれい。<end>"
EN: "Since the request did not come through the Guild, there is little information about the monster appearing at Maiden's Mirror. But perhaps this too is... I will not be a burden. Take me with you.<end>"
KO: "길드를 통하지 않은 의뢰라 처녀의 거울에 나타나는 마물에 대한 정보가 적군. 하지만 이것도 어쩌면…. 짐이 되진 않겠다. 나도 데려가 주게.<end>"

## 1220154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "乙女の鏡に出没する魔物退治が<line-break>依頼だが…これはギルドを通した<line-break>仕事ではない。<line-break>報酬がもらえなくても自暴自棄を<line-break>起こすんじゃないぞ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "The request is to slay the monster appearing at Maiden's Mirror, but it did not come through the Guild. Don't do anything desperate if you receive no reward, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "소녀의 거울에 나타나는 마물을 퇴치하는 의뢰지만… 길드를 통한 일은 아니야. 보수를 못 받더라도 자포자기하진 마, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1220155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "乙女の鏡に現れる化け物退治か。<line-break>お前の噂を聞いて依頼してきたんだ。<line-break>失敗は許されないぞ。<end>"
EN: "So you're slaying the beast appearing at Maiden's Mirror. The client sought you out after hearing the rumors about you. Failure is not permitted.<end>"
KO: "처녀의 거울에 나타나는 괴물 퇴치인가. 네 소문을 듣고 의뢰해 온 거다. 실패는 용납되지 않아.<end>"

## 1220156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの依頼、引き受けたのね。<line-break>なんだか信用していいのか<line-break>わからない依頼主だったけど…。<line-break>もしかして、色香に負けたとか…？<line-break>って冗談よ。早く乙女の鏡に<line-break>魔物を倒しに行きましょ。<end>"
EN: "So you accepted that request. The client didn't seem entirely trustworthy... Did her charms get the better of you? I'm joking. Let's hurry to Maiden's Mirror and slay the monster.<end>"
KO: "그 의뢰 받아들였구나. 왠지 믿어도 되는지 모르겠는 의뢰인이었는데…. 혹시 색기에 넘어간 거야…? 농담이야. 빨리 처녀의 거울에 나타나는 마물을 쓰러뜨리러 가자.<end>"

## 1220158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "乙女の鏡の魔物退治ですよね。<line-break>なんだか強いって噂ですけど、<line-break>きっと大丈夫ですよね。<line-break>だって私には<value:$28>さんが…<line-break>あ、いえ、な…なんでもないです。<end>"
EN: "We're slaying the monster at Maiden's Mirror, right? Rumor says it's strong, but I'm sure everything will be fine. After all, I have <value:$28>... Oh, n-no, it's nothing.<end>"
KO: "소녀의 거울에 나타나는 마물 퇴치죠. 꽤 강하다는 소문이지만, 분명 괜찮을 거예요. 왜냐하면 제겐 <value:$28> 님이…아, 아니, 아… 아무것도 아니에요.<end>"

## 1220160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（アミラルの冒険者ギルドで、<line-break>　幻惑の洞窟に現れる獣を<line-break>　退治するよう頼まれたな）<line-break>（獣を倒したら、<line-break>　忘れずに報告しないと…）<end>"
EN: "(At the Adventurers' Guild in Amiral, I accepted a request to slay the beast appearing in Bewildering Cave.) (Once it is defeated, I must remember to report back...)<end>"
KO: "(아미랄의 모험자 길드에서 환혹의 동굴에 나타나는 짐승을 퇴치해 달라는 부탁을 받았지.) (짐승을 쓰러뜨리면 잊지 말고 보고해야 해….)<end>"

## 1220161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたってほんとにお人好しよね。<line-break>他人のために、正体不明の獣を倒す<line-break>約束をしちゃうなんて。<line-break>でも、そこがいいところなのかもね。<line-break>獣が出るのは幻惑の洞窟だったかしら？<line-break>依頼主のためにも急ぎましょ。<end>"
EN: "You really are too kind. You promised to slay an unknown beast for someone else's sake. But perhaps that's one of your better qualities. The beast appears in Bewildering Cave, doesn't it? Let's hurry, for the client's sake.<end>"
KO: "당신 정말 사람이 좋다니까. 남을 위해 정체불명의 짐승을 쓰러뜨리겠다고 약속하다니. 하지만 그런 점이 장점일지도 모르겠네. 짐승이 나오는 곳이 환혹의 동굴이었지? 의뢰주를 위해서라도 서두르자.<end>"

## 1220162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "近頃、幻惑の洞窟に住み着いた<line-break>獣を退治するのだな。<line-break>わしの準備はできておるぞ。<line-break>そいつを倒して、アミラルの者が<line-break>安心してコロル石を採取できるように<line-break>してやろうではないか。<end>"
EN: "We are to slay the beast that recently settled in Bewildering Cave. I am ready. Let us defeat it so the people of Amiral can gather Korol Stone without fear.<end>"
KO: "요즘 환혹의 동굴에 자리 잡은 짐승을 퇴치하는 거구먼. 나는 준비됐네. 그놈을 쓰러뜨려 아미랄 사람들이 안심하고 코롤석을 채굴할 수 있게 해 주자고.<end>"

## 1220163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幻惑の洞窟に出るんですよね、その獣。<line-break>洞窟はアミラルの近くにあるし…。<line-break>アミラルっていい思い出がないんです。<line-break>あんまり行きたくないな…。<end>"
EN: "That beast appears in Bewildering Cave, right? And the cave is near Amiral... I don't have good memories of Amiral. I really don't want to go back...<end>"
KO: "환혹의 동굴에 나오는 거죠, 그 짐승. 동굴은 아미랄 근처고…. 아미랄에는 좋은 기억이 없어요. 별로 가고 싶지 않네….<end>"

## 1220164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかってる、何も言うなって。<line-break>ひとりで獣退治に行く度胸がないから、<line-break>一緒に来てくれって言いたいわけだ。<line-break>引き受けた仕事は、やり遂げないと<line-break>恥ずかしいもんな。<line-break>よし、幻惑の洞窟に行こうぜ。<end>"
EN: "I know, you don't have to say anything. You lack the nerve to go slay the beast alone, so you want me to come. It would be embarrassing not to finish a job you've accepted. All right, let's go to Bewildering Cave.<end>"
KO: "알아, 아무 말 하지 마. 혼자 짐승을 잡으러 갈 배짱이 없으니 같이 가 달라는 거잖아. 맡은 일을 끝내지 못하면 창피하지. 좋아, 환혹의 동굴로 가자.<end>"

## 1220165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その獣は、幻惑の洞窟に<line-break>住み着いてるみたいだよ。<line-break>アミラルの人たちも困っているみたい。<line-break>キミなら簡単に退治できるよね。<line-break>早く依頼してきた冒険者や町の人を<line-break>安心させてあげたいな。<end>"
EN: "It seems the beast has settled in Bewildering Cave. The people of Amiral are having trouble too. You can defeat it easily, right? I want to put the adventurer who made the request and the townspeople at ease as soon as possible.<end>"
KO: "그 짐승은 환혹의 동굴에 자리 잡은 모양이야. 아미랄 사람들도 곤란한 것 같아. 너라면 간단히 퇴치할 수 있지? 의뢰한 모험가와 마을 사람들을 빨리 안심시켜 주고 싶네.<end>"

## 1220166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幻惑の洞窟に住み着いた獣を<line-break>倒すというのが依頼だったはず。<line-break>洞窟は、アミラルの東にあるわ。<end>"
EN: "The request was to slay the beast that settled in Bewildering Cave. The cave lies east of Amiral.<end>"
KO: "환혹의 동굴에 자리 잡은 짐승을 쓰러뜨리는 게 의뢰였을 거야. 동굴은 아미랄 동쪽에 있어.<end>"

## 1220167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幻惑の洞窟に不穏な獣が住み着いて<line-break>いるようですね。アミラルの人たちが<line-break>あなたの戦いに期待していますよ。<end>"
EN: "It seems an ominous beast has settled in Bewildering Cave. The people of Amiral are counting on you.<end>"
KO: "환혹의 동굴에 불길한 짐승이 자리 잡은 모양이네요. 아미랄 사람들이 당신의 활약을 기대하고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1220168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "またモンスター退治を引き受けたの？<line-break>ホント、そういうの好きよね。<end>"
EN: "You accepted another monster-slaying job? You really do love that sort of thing.<end>"
KO: "또 몬스터 퇴치를 맡은 거야? 정말 그런 거 좋아하네.<end>"

## 1220169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その獣が出る幻惑の洞窟は、<line-break>アミラルの東にあるんだぜ。<line-break>姉ちゃん知ってたか？<line-break>姉ちゃんアミラル行くんなら、<line-break>俺もつれてってくれよな。<line-break>あそこの料理はうまいんだぜー。<end>"
EN: "Bewildering Cave, where the beast appears, lies east of Amiral. Did you know that, Sis? If you're going to Amiral, take me with you. The food there is delicious!<end>"
KO: "그 짐승이 나온다는 환혹의 동굴은 아미랄 동쪽에 있어. 누나, 알고 있었어? 누나가 아미랄에 간다면 나도 데려가 줘. 거기 음식이 맛있거든.<end>"

## 1220170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アミラルの東にある幻惑の洞窟に<line-break>不穏な獣が出没しているらしい。<line-break>それを倒せという依頼よ。<end>"
EN: "An ominous beast is said to be roaming Bewildering Cave, east of Amiral. Your request is to slay it.<end>"
KO: "아미랄 동쪽 환혹의 동굴에 불온한 짐승이 나타나는 모양이야. 그걸 쓰러뜨리라는 의뢰지.<end>"

## 1220171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しっかし、お前さんに泣きついた<line-break>冒険者も、うかつな奴だねぇ。<line-break>仕事は自分の実力で選ぶものだろうに。<line-break>で、その獣は幻惑の洞窟にいるんだな。<line-break>さっさと退治して、噂のうかつ君を<line-break>安心させてやろうや。<end>"
