EN: "Still, that adventurer who came crying to you was careless. People should choose jobs suited to their own abilities. So the beast is in Bewildering Cave? Let's slay it quickly and reassure our famously careless friend.<end>"
KO: "그나저나 자네한테 매달린 그 모험자도 참 덤벙대는 녀석이구먼. 일은 자기 실력에 맞게 골라야 하는 법인데. 그래서 그 짐승은 환혹의 동굴에 있다는 거지? 얼른 퇴치해서 그 덤벙이 녀석을 안심시켜 주자고.<end>"

## 1220172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幻惑の洞窟に住み着いた獣か…。<line-break>気になる事がある。<line-break>付き合ってやってもいいぞ。<end>"
EN: "The beast that settled in Bewildering Cave... Something about it concerns me. I suppose I could accompany you.<end>"
KO: "환혹의 동굴에 자리 잡은 짐승인가…. 신경 쓰이는 게 있어. 함께 가 줘도 좋다.<end>"

## 1220173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ものすごい獣とな？<line-break>ふむ、戦ってみる価値は<line-break>ありそうじゃな。<line-break>お主の準備ができたなら、<line-break>幻惑の洞窟に行くとしようぞ。<end>"
EN: "A fearsome beast, you say? Hmm. It sounds worth fighting. Once you are ready, let us go to Bewildering Cave.<end>"
KO: "엄청난 짐승이라…. 흐음, 싸워 볼 가치는 있겠군. 준비가 되면 환혹의 동굴로 가자.<end>"

## 1220174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "困っている後輩を助けてあげるのは<line-break>先輩の役目だって、<line-break>お爺ちゃんも言っていたよ。<line-break>幻惑の洞窟に住み着いた獣を倒して、<line-break>後輩冒険者を助けてあげようね。<line-break>準備ができたら出発しよう。<end>"
EN: "Grandpa always said it is a senior's duty to help a junior in trouble. Let's slay the beast that settled in Bewildering Cave and help that junior adventurer. We can leave once preparations are complete.<end>"
KO: "곤란한 후배를 돕는 건 선배의 역할이라고 할아버지도 말씀하셨어. 환혹의 동굴에 자리 잡은 짐승을 쓰러뜨려 후배 모험가를 도와주자. 준비되면 출발하자.<end>"

## 1220175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幻惑の洞窟に現れる獣退治か。<line-break>どんな獣が出てくるものか…<line-break>ともかく、油断しないようにな。<end>"
EN: "So we are slaying the beast appearing in Bewildering Cave. Who knows what sort of creature it may be... In any case, do not let your guard down.<end>"
KO: "환혹의 동굴에 나타나는 짐승 퇴치인가. 어떤 짐승이 나올지…. 아무튼 방심하지 마라.<end>"

## 1220176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "高貴なエルフのアタクシが、<line-break>ど～して名前も知らない冒険者の<line-break>ために獣退治をしなくちゃならないの！<line-break>洞窟の中で泥まみれになって<line-break>戦うのなんて、泥臭いドワーフにでも<line-break>やらせればいいんだわ！<end>"
EN: "Why should a noble Elf like me slay a beast for some adventurer whose name I don't even know? Leave the muddy work of fighting in a cave to some grubby Dwarf!<end>"
KO: "고귀한 엘프인 내가 왜 이름도 모르는 모험가를 위해 짐승을 잡아야 하는데! 동굴에서 진흙투성이가 되어 싸우는 일은 흙냄새 나는 드워프에게나 시키면 되잖아!<end>"

## 1220177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ものすごい獣は、化け物！<line-break>化け物は、のけ者！<line-break>拙者は道化者！<line-break>兄弟子！　どうですかぁあああ！<line-break>会心の３連発はっ！<end>"
EN: "A fearsome beast is a monster! A monster is an outcast! And I am a jester! Senior Disciple! What do you thiiink? Three brilliant hits in a row!<end>"
KO: "엄청난 짐승은 괴물! 괴물은 외톨이! 소인은 광대! 사형! 어떻습니까아아아! 회심의 3연발은!<end>"

## 1220178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ものすごい獣って、<line-break>どのくらいすごいのぉ？<line-break>大きな牙がはえてたりするのかなぁ。<line-break>自分が強いからって油断はだめだよ。<line-break>幻惑の洞窟に行くときは、<line-break>きちんと準備してね。<end>"
EN: "Just how fearsome is this fearsome beast? Maybe it has enormous fangs. Don't get careless just because you're strong. Prepare properly before going to Bewildering Cave.<end>"
KO: "엄청난 짐승이라는데 얼마나 대단한 걸까? 큰 송곳니라도 나 있으려나. 네가 강하다고 방심하면 안 돼. 환혹의 동굴에 갈 때는 제대로 준비해.<end>"

## 1220179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>追いつめられた獣は手強い。<line-break>油断するな。<end>"
EN: "............... A cornered beast is dangerous. Do not let your guard down.<end>"
KO: "………………. 궁지에 몰린 짐승은 강하다. 방심하지 마라.<end>"

## 1220180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幻惑の洞窟に潜んでいる獣退治？<line-break>またそんな依頼を引き受けて来たのか？<line-break><line-break>…あまりにも情報が少なすぎる。<line-break>軽はずみに引き受けてばかりいると、<line-break>寿命を縮めかねんぞ。<end>"
EN: "Slaying the beast lurking in Bewildering Cave? You accepted another request like that? ...There is far too little information. If you keep accepting jobs so recklessly, you may shorten your life.<end>"
KO: "환혹의 동굴에 숨어 있는 짐승 퇴치? 또 그런 의뢰를 받아 온 거냐? …정보가 너무 부족해. 생각 없이 계속 받아들이면 수명을 줄일 수도 있어.<end>"

## 1220181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その獣は幻惑の洞窟のコロル石が<line-break>取れる所にいるんだよね？<line-break>それなら洞窟の一番奥だよ。<line-break>コロル石がいろんな色で輝いて<line-break>きれいな場所なんだぁ。<end>"
EN: "The beast is near the Korol Stone deposits in Bewildering Cave, right? Then it must be at the very back. It's a beautiful place, with Korol Stone shining in all sorts of colors.<end>"
KO: "그 짐승은 환혹의 동굴에서 코롤석을 캐는 곳에 있는 거지? 그렇다면 동굴 가장 안쪽이야. 코롤석이 여러 색으로 빛나서 아름다운 곳이야.<end>"

## 1220182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幻惑の洞窟は内部がとても狭くなって<line-break>いたはずだ。ましてや、<line-break>敵の獣の正体は一切不明…。<line-break>不意を突かれれば、君といえど簡単に<line-break>命を失うことになるかもしれない。<line-break>アミラルの人々を、失望させるなよ。<end>"
EN: "The passages inside Bewildering Cave are extremely narrow, if I recall. Worse, we know nothing at all about the beast. If it catches you by surprise, even you might easily lose your life. Do not disappoint the people of Amiral.<end>"
KO: "환혹의 동굴은 내부가 매우 좁았을 거다. 게다가 적 짐승의 정체는 전혀 알 수 없지…. 기습당하면 자네라도 쉽게 목숨을 잃을 수 있다. 아미랄 사람들을 실망시키지 마라.<end>"

## 1220183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幻惑の洞窟に潜む獣退治というのが、<line-break><value:$28>様が今回<line-break>お引き受けになった依頼です。<line-break>洞窟最深部のコロル石の採掘所あたりに<line-break>その獣は出没するらしいのですが、<line-break>残念ながら、詳細は一切不明です。<end>"
EN: "The request <value:$28> has accepted is to slay the beast lurking in Bewildering Cave. It apparently appears near the Korol Stone mine at the cave's deepest point, but unfortunately, no further details are known.<end>"
KO: "환혹의 동굴에 숨어 있는 짐승을 퇴치하는 것이 이번에 <value:$28> 님이 맡으신 의뢰입니다. 동굴 최심부의 코롤석 채굴장 부근에 나타난다지만 안타깝게도 자세한 정보는 전혀 없습니다.<end>"

## 1220184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幻惑の洞窟の奥に、<line-break>ものすごい獣が出るんだって。<line-break>ものすごいらしいよ。<end>"
EN: "They say a fearsome beast appears deep inside Bewildering Cave. Apparently it really is fearsome.<end>"
KO: "환혹의 동굴 깊은 곳에 엄청난 짐승이 나온대. 정말 엄청나다나 봐.<end>"

## 1220185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしに一種特殊な勘があると言ったら、<line-break><value:$28>、おぬしは<line-break>それを信じてくれるか？<line-break>…幻惑の洞窟の、凶暴かつ巨大な獣。<line-break>それが怒りとともに発している悲しみを<line-break>わしはふと直感した気がするのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "If I told you I possessed a certain special intuition, would you believe me, <value:$28>? That huge, ferocious beast in Bewildering Cave... I feel I somehow sensed the sorrow it gives off along with its rage.<end>"
KO: "내게 조금 특별한 감이 있다고 하면, <value:$28>, 자네는 믿어 주겠나? …환혹의 동굴에 있는 흉포하고 거대한 짐승. 그놈이 분노와 함께 뿜어내는 슬픔을 문득 직감한 것 같네.<end>"

## 1220186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近、この手の依頼が<line-break>増えているような気がしないか？<line-break>冒険者としては腕が鳴るところだが…<line-break>敵も生命あるものだと意識すると、<line-break>罪を自覚してしまいそうになる。<line-break>せめて…今回も、祈りを捧げよう。<end>"
EN: "Does it not feel as though requests like this have become more common lately? As an adventurer, the challenge excites me... But when I remember that our enemies are living beings too, I begin to feel the weight of sin. At the very least... let us offer a prayer again this time.<end>"
KO: "최근 이런 의뢰가 늘어난 것 같지 않나? 모험자로서는 솜씨를 발휘할 기회지만… 적도 살아 있는 존재라고 생각하면 죄를 자각하게 될 것 같군. 적어도… 이번에도 기도를 올리자.<end>"

## 1220187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前はなんにでも首を突っ込むんだな。<line-break>俺もほっとくことができない性分だから<line-break>気持ちはわかるけどな。<line-break>引き受けたからには、手を抜くなよ。<line-break>獣のいる幻惑の洞窟へ向かうぞ。<end>"
EN: "You stick your nose into everything. I understand, though―I'm not the sort who can ignore trouble either. Since you accepted the job, don't hold back. We're heading to Bewildering Cave, where the beast is.<end>"
KO: "넌 뭐든 참견하는구나. 나도 내버려 두질 못하는 성격이라 마음은 알지만. 맡았으면 대충하지 마. 짐승이 있는 환혹의 동굴로 가자.<end>"

## 1220188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>って人がよすぎよ。<line-break>あんな依頼引き受けちゃうなんて、<line-break>ほんと、お人好しね。<line-break>あの冒険者もプライドが無いのかしら。<line-break>ま、いいわ。幻惑の洞窟にいる獣を<line-break>倒しに行きましょ。<end>"
EN: "You're far too kind, <value:$28>. Accepting a request like that―you really are a soft touch. Doesn't that adventurer have any pride? Oh well. Let's go slay the beast in Bewildering Cave.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>는 너무 사람이 좋아. 그런 의뢰를 맡다니 정말 착해 빠졌네. 그 모험가도 자존심이 없는 건가. 뭐, 됐어. 환혹의 동굴에 있는 짐승을 쓰러뜨리러 가자.<end>"

## 1220190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さんってすごいです。<line-break>今日会った人のために、<line-break>危険な仕事を引き受けるだなんて…。<line-break>そういうところが私…<line-break>あ、いえ、なんでもないんです。<line-break>幻惑の洞窟に急ぎましょう！<end>"
EN: "You're amazing, <value:$28>. Taking on a dangerous job for someone you only met today... That part of you makes me... Oh, n-no, it's nothing. Let's hurry to Bewildering Cave!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨는 대단해요. 오늘 처음 만난 사람을 위해 위험한 일을 맡다니…. 그런 점이 저는… 아, 아니에요, 아무것도 아니에요. 환혹의 동굴로 서둘러요!<end>"

## 1220192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（ロセンの冒険者ギルドで、<line-break>　火山岩地帯にいる魔物を倒すよう<line-break>　依頼されたな）<line-break>（魔物を倒したら、<line-break>　忘れずに報告しないと…）<end>"
EN: "(At the Adventurers' Guild in Rocen, I accepted a request to slay the monster in the Volcanic Rock Zone.) (Once it is defeated, I must remember to report back...)<end>"
KO: "(로센 모험자 길드에서 화산암 지대의 마물을 쓰러뜨리라는 의뢰를 받았지.) (마물을 쓰러뜨리면 잊지 말고 보고해야 해…)<end>"

## 1220193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神官の話だと、その魔物は<line-break>とてつもない闇の力を持っている<line-break>らしいじゃない。<line-break>自分の実力を過信して挑めば、<line-break>命を落とすわよ。<end>"
EN: "According to the priest, that monster possesses tremendous dark power. If you challenge it out of overconfidence in your abilities, it will cost you your life.<end>"
KO: "신관 말로는 그 마물이 엄청난 어둠의 힘을 지녔다던데. 자기 실력을 과신하고 덤비면 목숨을 잃을 거야.<end>"

## 1220194
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こたびの魔物は、とてつもない闇の力を<line-break>持っておるというではないか。<line-break>お主、魔物を倒す自信はあるのか？<line-break>火山岩地帯は火属性のモンスターが<line-break>多く出るところだ。<line-break>風属性の武器が欲しいものだがな。<end>"
EN: "They say this monster possesses tremendous dark power. Are you confident you can defeat it? The Volcanic Rock Zone is crawling with Fire-element monsters. A Wind-element weapon would come in handy.<end>"
KO: "이번 마물은 엄청난 어둠의 힘을 지녔다지 않느냐. 자네, 그 마물을 쓰러뜨릴 자신이 있나? 화산암 지대는 불 속성 몬스터가 많이 나오는 곳이다. 바람 속성 무기가 있으면 좋겠군.<end>"

## 1220196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の魔物がどうしたよ！<line-break>俺たちにかかりゃ敵じゃないぜ。<line-break>さっさと火山岩地帯へ行くぞ。<line-break>まさか、闇の魔物だからって<line-break>やみるつもりじゃないだろうな！<end>"
EN: "Who cares if it's a dark monster? It's no match for us. Let's get to the Volcanic Rock Zone already. You're not going to let a little darkness make you turn tail, are you?<end>"
KO: "어둠의 마물이 어쨌다고! 우리한테는 상대도 안 돼. 얼른 화산암 지대로 가자. 설마 어둠의 마물이라고 그만둘 건 아니겠지!<end>"

## 1220197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "キミがロセンで引き受けた仕事だね。<line-break>ギルドに依頼してきた神官の話だと、<line-break>闇の力を持った魔物なんでしょ。<line-break>どう？　やっつける自信あるの？<line-break>ボク…キミにはあまり無茶を<line-break>しないで欲しいな。<end>"
EN: "This is the job you took in Rocen, right? The priest who brought the request to the Guild said the monster possesses dark power. So? Think you can beat it? I...don't want you taking too many risks.<end>"
KO: "네가 로센에서 맡은 일이구나. 길드에 의뢰한 신관 말로는 어둠의 힘을 지닌 마물이라며. 어때? 쓰러뜨릴 자신 있어? 난… 네가 너무 무리하지 않았으면 좋겠어.<end>"

## 1220198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガルは何をしている？<line-break>闇の魔物が出没していると言うのに、<line-break>その退治を冒険者ごときに任すつもり？<line-break><value:$28>が通りかかったのは、<line-break>幸いだったとしか言い様がないわ。<line-break>火山岩地帯へ向かうわよ！<end>"
EN: "What is Dyneskal doing? A dark monster is on the loose, yet its extermination is being left to a mere adventurer? It was extraordinarily fortunate that <value:$28> happened to pass by. We're heading for the Volcanic Rock Zone!<end>"
KO: "딩갈은 뭘 하고 있는 거지? 어둠의 마물이 나타난다는데 그 퇴치를 고작 모험자에게 맡길 셈이야? <value:$28>이 마침 지나간 건 정말 다행이라고밖에 할 수 없네. 화산암 지대로 가자!<end>"

## 1220199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロセンの神官殿の話では、<line-break>火山岩地帯の魔物は、<line-break>闇の力を持つらしいですねえ。<line-break>なかなか手ごわいかもしれません。<line-break>心してかかった方が良さそうですよ。<end>"
EN: "According to the priest from Rocen, the monster in the Volcanic Rock Zone possesses dark power. It may prove quite formidable. You'd best take it seriously.<end>"
KO: "로센의 신관님 말씀으로는 화산암 지대의 마물이 어둠의 힘을 지닌 모양입니다. 상당히 강할지도 모르니 각오하는 게 좋겠어요.<end>"

## 1220200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "火山岩地帯に、闇の魔物ねえ。<line-break>行く前に、装備を再確認した方が<line-break>いいかもよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>ちょ～っとややこしいけどさ、<line-break>その辺しっかりできる冒険者って、<line-break>カッコいいよ、やっぱさ。<end>"
EN: "A dark monster in the Volcanic Rock Zone, huh? You might want to double-check your equipment before you go, <value:$28>. It's kind of a pain, but an adventurer who takes care of that stuff properly is pretty cool, you know?<end>"
KO: "화산암 지대에 어둠의 마물이라…. 가기 전에 장비를 다시 확인하는 게 좋을지도 몰라, <value:$28>. 좀 복잡하긴 해도 그런 걸 제대로 챙기는 모험자가 역시 멋지잖아.<end>"

## 1220201
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃん、火山岩地帯はロセンの<line-break>南東にあるんだぜ。<line-break>知ってたか？<line-break>魔物を倒したあとの報告は、<line-break>ロセンの冒険者ギルドに行くんだぜ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, miss, the Volcanic Rock Zone is southeast of Rocen. Did you know that? Once you've defeated the monster, report back to the Adventurers' Guild in Rocen.<end>"
KO: "누나, 화산암 지대는 로센 남동쪽이야. 알고 있었어? 마물을 쓰러뜨린 뒤 보고는 로센 모험자 길드로 가야 해.<end>"

## 1220202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "火山岩地帯に出没している魔物が、<line-break>闇の力を持っている、ですって…？<line-break><line-break>…知らなかった。<line-break><value:$28>、私からも、<line-break>どうか退治をお願いするわ。<end>"
EN: "The monster appearing in the Volcanic Rock Zone has dark power...? I...didn't know. Please, <value:$28>. I too ask you to defeat it.<end>"
KO: "화산암 지대에 출몰하는 마물이, 어둠의 힘을 지니고 있다고…?…몰랐어.<value:$28>, 나도 부탁할게, 부디 퇴치해 줘.<end>"

## 1220203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王の覚醒が原因かどうかわからんが、<line-break>闇の力を持った魔物が封印を解かれて<line-break>うろつくことが多くなっている。<line-break>今回のも、そのたぐいかもしれんな。<line-break>お前さんなら問題ないと思うが、<line-break>油断するなよ。<end>"
EN: "I don't know whether the Dragon King's Awakening is to blame, but more and more monsters with dark power have been breaking free of their seals and roaming about. This one may be another of that sort. I don't expect you'll have any trouble, but don't let your guard down.<end>"
KO: "용왕의 각성이 원인인지는 모르겠지만, 어둠의 힘을 지닌 마물이 봉인에서 풀려 돌아다니는 일이 늘고 있다. 이번 것도 그런 부류일지 모르지. 자네라면 문제없겠지만 방심하지 마라.<end>"

## 1220204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の力を持つ魔物か…。<line-break>気になる事がある。<line-break>付き合ってやってもいいぞ。<end>"
EN: "A monster with dark power... There's something I want to look into. I suppose I could come along.<end>"
KO: "어둠의 힘을 지닌 마물인가…. 신경 쓰이는 게 있어. 같이 가 줘도 되겠지.<end>"

## 1220205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とてつもない闇の力を持つ魔物とな？<line-break>火山岩地帯に、そのような魔物が<line-break>おるとは初耳じゃぞ。<line-break>火山岩地帯には火草の種を求めて<line-break>冒険者が集まるぞ。<line-break>被害者が出る前に退治せねばな。<end>"
EN: "A monster with tremendous dark power, you say? This is the first I've heard of such a monster in the Volcanic Rock Zone. Adventurers gather there in search of Firegrass Seeds. We must defeat it before anyone comes to harm.<end>"
KO: "엄청난 어둠의 힘을 가진 마물이라고? 화산암 지대에 그런 마물이 있다니 처음 듣는구나. 화산암 지대에는 불꽃풀 씨앗을 찾는 모험가가 모인다. 피해자가 나오기 전에 퇴치해야겠군.<end>"

## 1220206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロセンの冒険者ギルドで引き受けた<line-break>仕事のことだね。<line-break>たしか、依頼者は神官だったかな。<line-break>神官の話だと魔物からは、<line-break>ものすごい闇の力が感じられたそう<line-break>だから、用心しないとね。<end>"
EN: "This is about the job you took at the Adventurers' Guild in Rocen, right? If I remember correctly, the client was a priest. The priest apparently sensed tremendous dark power coming from the monster, so we'd better be careful.<end>"
KO: "로센 모험자 길드에서 맡은 일이네. 의뢰자는 아마 신관이었지. 신관 말로는 마물에게서 엄청난 어둠의 힘이 느껴졌대. 조심해야겠어.<end>"

## 1220207
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一言助言を言わせてもらえるなら、<line-break>火属性の敵には風属性、闇属性には<line-break>聖属性の攻撃が有効だ。<line-break>知恵と機転もまた我々の武器。<line-break>…そういうことだな。<end>"
EN: "If I may offer one piece of advice: Wind-element attacks are effective against Fire-element enemies, while Holy-element attacks are effective against Dark-element enemies. Wisdom and resourcefulness are also weapons at our disposal. ...That's the idea.<end>"
KO: "한마디 조언하자면 불 속성 적에게는 바람 속성, 어둠 속성에는 성 속성 공격이 유효하다. 지혜와 재치 또한 우리의 무기지. …그런 뜻이다.<end>"

## 1220208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何度も言うけどアタクシは、<line-break>高貴で優雅で知性あふれる<line-break>エルフなのよ、エ・ル・フ。<line-break>そのアタクシがど～して<line-break>山登りなんてしないと<line-break>いけないのよ！<end>"
EN: "How many times must I say it? I am a noble, graceful, brilliant elf. An e-l-f! So why in the world must someone like me go climbing mountains?!<end>"
KO: "몇 번이나 말하지만 난 고귀하고 우아하고 지성 넘치는 엘프야, 엘·프. 그런 내가 왜 산을 타야 하는 건데!<end>"

## 1220209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すさまじい闇の力を持つ魔物ですか…。<line-break>ま～ものにもよりますが、<line-break>闇だけにやみといたほうがいいですぞ。<end>"
EN: "A monster with tremendous dark power, you say...? Well, it depends on the monster, but with darkness like that, perhaps you'd best call it a night.<end>"
KO: "엄청난 어둠의 힘을 가진 마물입니까…. 뭐, 마물 나름이지만 어둠인 만큼 그만두는 게 좋을지도 모르겠군요.<end>"

## 1220210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今度は火山岩地帯に行くの？<line-break><value:$28>が有名になったから、<line-break>いろんな人が頼ってくるね。<line-break>たくさんお仕事して疲れたら、<line-break>無理しないで休んでね。<end>"
EN: "Are you going to the Volcanic Rock Zone this time? Now that you're famous, <value:$28>, all sorts of people come asking for help. If all that work wears you out, don't push yourself. Be sure to rest.<end>"
KO: "이번엔 화산암 지대로 가는 거야? <value:$28>가 유명해져서 여러 사람이 부탁하네. 일 많이 해서 피곤하면 무리하지 말고 쉬어.<end>"

## 1220211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>戦いは生か死だ。<line-break>気を抜いた者が死ぬ。<end>"
EN: "……………… Battle is life or death. Those who let their guard down die.<end>"
KO: "………………. 싸움은 삶 아니면 죽음이다. 방심한 자가 죽는다.<end>"

## 1220212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者ギルドに泣きついてくるとは…<line-break>闇を払うのこそ、神官最大の仕事では<line-break>なかったのか？<line-break>ふん…それだけ世は<line-break>怠慢に満ちているということか。<line-break>嘆かわしい限りだな。<end>"
EN: "To think a priest would go crying to the Adventurers' Guild... Was driving back the darkness not a priest's greatest duty? Hmph. Is this how thoroughly the world has succumbed to indolence? How deplorable.<end>"
KO: "모험가 길드에 울며 매달리다니…. 어둠을 몰아내는 것이야말로 신관의 가장 큰 일이 아니었나? 흥… 그만큼 세상에 나태함이 넘친다는 건가. 개탄스럽군.<end>"

## 1220213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "火山岩地帯って、渦巻きの岩が<line-break>あるところだね。<line-break>昔から気になってたんだぁ。<line-break>ボクのカンだと、あそこが怪しいよ。<line-break>あーゆーとこに魔人とか闇の力を<line-break>持った魔物とかが集まるものなのさ。<end>"
EN: "The Volcanic Rock Zone is where those spiral-shaped rocks are, right? I've been curious about that place for ages. My instincts tell me there's something suspicious about it. Places like that always attract fiends and monsters with dark powers.<end>"
KO: "화산암 지대라면 소용돌이 바위가 있는 곳이지. 예전부터 신경 쓰였어. 내 감으로는 거기가 수상해. 그런 곳에 마인이나 어둠의 힘을 지닌 마물이 모이는 법이거든.<end>"

## 1220214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王の覚醒以来、闇の魔物の<line-break>出現を耳にする機会が<line-break>明らかに増えてきている。<line-break>変革の時代が到来したという<line-break>証なのか…。<end>"
EN: "Since the Dragon King's Awakening, reports of dark monsters have clearly become more frequent. Could this be proof that an age of upheaval has arrived...?<end>"
KO: "용왕이 깨어난 뒤로 어둠의 마물이 나타났다는 이야기를 확실히 자주 듣게 되었어. 변혁의 시대가 왔다는 증거인가….<end>"

## 1220215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "火山岩地帯に現れるという魔物は、<line-break>ロセンの神官の現によれば、<line-break>闇の力を持つもののようです。<line-break>こちらも闇属性に耐えうるような<line-break>防備を調えておかねば、<line-break>危険かもしれません。<end>"
EN: "According to the priest from Rocen, the monster said to appear in the Volcanic Rock Zone seems to possess dark power. It may be dangerous unless we equip ourselves to withstand the Dark element as well.<end>"
KO: "화산암 지대에 나타난다는 마물은 로센 신관이 본 바에 따르면 어둠의 힘을 지닌 모양입니다. 우리도 어둠 속성에 견딜 수 있는 방비를 갖추지 않으면 위험할지 모릅니다.<end>"

## 1220216
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "火山岩の近くにいる魔物は、<line-break>闇の力を持つって言ったよ。<line-break>気をつけないとね。<end>"
EN: "The monster near the volcanic rocks was said to have dark power. We'd better be careful.<end>"
KO: "화산암 근처에 있는 마물은 어둠의 힘을 지녔다고 했어. 조심해야겠네.<end>"

## 1220217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神官ともあろう人間が、闇の力を持つ<line-break>魔物退治を他人任せにするとは…！<line-break>…ま、叫んでおっても仕方ない。<line-break>魔物の方も、おざなりされるよりは<line-break>手厚く弔われた方が幸せに決まっとる。<line-break>火山岩地帯へ行こうぞ、<end>"
EN: "For a priest to leave the slaying of a monster with dark power to someone else...! Still, there is no point in shouting about it. The monster will surely be happier receiving a proper burial than being treated as an afterthought. Let us go to the Volcanic Rock Zone.<end>"
KO: "신관이라는 자가 어둠의 힘을 지닌 마물 퇴치를 남에게 맡기다니…! …뭐, 소리쳐 봐야 소용없지. 마물도 방치되는 것보단 제대로 장례를 치러 주는 편이 행복하겠지. 화산암 지대로 가자.<end>"

## 1220218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者として名が知れたことで<line-break>多くの人々に頼られるように<line-break>なったのは誇るべきだ。<line-break>彼らの期待に答えるためにも、<line-break>視野は広く持たないといけないぞ。<end>"
EN: "You should be proud that your renown as an adventurer has led so many people to rely on you. To live up to their expectations, you must keep a broad perspective.<end>"
KO: "모험자로 이름이 알려져 많은 사람에게 의지받게 된 건 자랑할 일이다. 그 기대에 답하려면 시야를 넓게 가져야 해.<end>"

## 1220219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神官の話だと、闇の力を持つ魔物は<line-break>火山岩地帯にいるらしいな。<line-break>　<line-break>今回は何か危険な匂いがする。<line-break>いつも以上に気を引き締めていくぞ。<end>"
EN: "According to the priest, the monster with dark power is in the Volcanic Rock Zone. Something about this job smells dangerous. We'll need to stay even more alert than usual.<end>"
KO: "신관 말로는 어둠의 힘을 가진 마물이 화산암 지대에 있는 모양이야. 이번엔 뭔가 위험한 냄새가 난다. 평소보다 더 긴장하고 가자.<end>"

## 1220220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうやら今回の魔物退治は<line-break>少し危険みたいね。<line-break>　<line-break>万全の態勢で火山岩地帯に<line-break>向かいましょ。<end>"
EN: "It sounds like hunting this monster may be a little dangerous. Let's make sure we're fully prepared before heading to the Volcanic Rock Zone.<end>"
KO: "이번 마물 토벌은 조금 위험한 모양이네. 만반의 준비를 하고 화산암 지대로 가자.<end>"

## 1220222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec122-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今回の依頼って危険じゃないですか？<line-break>だってすごい闇の力を持ってるって<line-break>神官の方がおっしゃってましたし…。<line-break>…そうですよね。それでも行くのが<line-break><value:$28>さんですよね。<line-break>私もほんの少しですけど力になります。<end>"
EN: "Isn't this request dangerous? The priest said the monster has tremendous dark power... Yes, I know. You'd go anyway, wouldn't you, <value:$28>? I may not be much help, but I'll do what little I can.<end>"
KO: "이번 의뢰, 위험하지 않나요? 신관님이 엄청난 어둠의 힘을 가졌다고 하셨잖아요…. …그렇죠. 그래도 가는 게 <value:$28>씨죠. 저도 아주 조금이지만 힘이 될게요.<end>"

## 1230000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（ロストールの冒険者ギルドで青年に、<line-break>　城塞都市跡にいる魔物を<line-break>　倒してくれと頼まれた）<line-break>（彼の仲間は、その魔物に殺された<line-break>　らしい）<end>"
EN: "(At the Adventurers' Guild in Rostorl, a young man asked you to defeat a monster in the Fortress City Ruins.) (Apparently, that monster killed his companions.)<end>"
KO: "(로스톨 모험자 길드에서 청년에게 성채도시 터의 마물을 쓰러뜨려 달라는 부탁을 받았다.) (그의 동료는 그 마물에게 살해당한 모양이다.)<end>"

## 1230001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたに仇討ちを依頼してきた青年の<line-break>仲間を殺した魔物は、<line-break>城塞都市跡にいるのよ。<line-break>駆け出しとはいえ冒険者を何人も<line-break>殺している魔物よ。<line-break>油断しないでね。<end>"
EN: "The monster that killed the companions of the young man who asked you to avenge them is in the Fortress City Ruins. Novices though they were, it has killed several adventurers. Don't let your guard down.<end>"
KO: "당신에게 복수를 의뢰한 청년의 동료들을 죽인 마물은 성새도시 터에 있어. 풋내기라 해도 모험자를 몇이나 죽인 녀석이야. 방심하지 마.<end>"

## 1230002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その青年は、仲間の仇討ちのために、<line-break>自分のケガも省みず有り金をはたいて、<line-break>お主を雇ったのだな？<line-break>よし！　その仇討ち、わしも手伝う。<line-break>共に魔物とやらを成敗しようぞ。<line-break>城塞都市跡に出発じゃ。<end>"
EN: "So that young man spent every coin he had to hire you to avenge his companions, despite his own injuries? Very well! I shall help you avenge them. Together, we shall punish this monster. Let us depart for the Fortress City Ruins!<end>"
KO: "그 청년은 동료의 원수를 갚기 위해 자기 상처도 돌보지 않고 가진 돈을 털어 자네를 고용한 거군? 좋아! 그 복수, 나도 돕지. 함께 그 마물을 처단하자. 성채도시 유적으로 출발이다.<end>"

## 1230003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仇討ちなんて物騒ですね。<line-break>でも、時代錯誤な<line-break>男のロマンを感じます。<line-break><value:$28>様、<line-break>がんばってくださいね。<line-break>私、応援してますから。<end>"
EN: "Vengeance is such a violent business. Still, it has the air of some old-fashioned male fantasy. Do your best, <value:$28>. I'll be cheering you on.<end>"
KO: "복수라니 무시무시하네요. 하지만 시대착오적인 남자의 로망이 느껴져요. <value:$28> 님, 힘내세요. 저, 응원할게요.<end>"

## 1230004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はいつでも出発できるぜ。<line-break>凶暴な魔物だろうが、何だろうが、<line-break>俺の鉄拳で叩きつぶしてやる！<end>"
EN: "I'm ready to leave whenever you are. Savage monster or whatever else it may be, I'll smash it flat with my iron fist!<end>"
KO: "난 언제든 출발할 수 있어. 흉폭한 마물이든 뭐든 내 주먹으로 때려부숴 주지!<end>"

## 1230005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールで会った<line-break>冒険者との約束を守るために、<line-break>城塞都市跡にいる魔物を倒すんだね。<line-break>相手は、冒険者を何人も殺している<line-break>凶暴な魔物だよ。<line-break>絶対に油断しちゃダメだからね。<end>"
EN: "So you're going to defeat the monster in the Fortress City Ruins and keep your promise to the adventurer you met in Rostorl. It's a savage beast that's already killed several adventurers. You absolutely must not let your guard down.<end>"
KO: "로스톨에서 만난 모험가와의 약속을 지키려고 성채도시 유적의 마물을 쓰러뜨리는 거구나. 상대는 모험가를 여럿 죽인 흉폭한 마물이야. 절대 방심하면 안 돼.<end>"

## 1230006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仇討ちなのね…。<line-break>行くかどうかは、あなたが決めて。<line-break>私はあなたの意志に従うわ。<end>"
EN: "So this is about revenge... Whether we go is up to you. I'll abide by your decision.<end>"
KO: "원수 갚기구나…. 갈지 말지는 네가 결정해. 나는 네 뜻을 따를게.<end>"

## 1230007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きっと、若すぎるあまり、<line-break>勇み足が過ぎたのでしょうね。<line-break>痛ましいことです。<line-break>城塞都市跡へ行くのなら、<line-break>せめてあなたは万全を期しなさい。<line-break>それが彼らのためでもあります。<end>"
EN: "They were surely so young that they rushed in too recklessly. How tragic. If you go to the Fortress City Ruins, at least make every possible preparation. That would be for their sake as well.<end>"
KO: "분명 너무 젊어서 지나치게 앞서 나갔던 거겠죠. 안타까운 일입니다. 성새도시 터로 간다면 적어도 당신은 만반의 준비를 하세요. 그것이 그들을 위한 일이기도 합니다.<end>"

## 1230008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仲間がもう死んじゃってるなら、<line-break><value:$28>が魔物を倒しても<line-break>何の得にもならないのにねぇ。<line-break>それでも仇討ちを依頼するんだから、<line-break>人の心って、不思議なもんだわ。<end>"
EN: "With his companions already dead, <value:$28> gains nothing by defeating the monster. Yet he still asks for revenge. The human heart is a strange thing.<end>"
KO: "동료가 이미 죽었다면 <value:$28>가 마물을 쓰러뜨려도 아무 이득이 없을 텐데. 그래도 복수를 의뢰하니 사람 마음은 참 신기해.<end>"

## 1230009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールで受けた依頼だろ？<line-break>魔物に仲間を殺された冒険者の<line-break>仇を討ってやるんだよな。<line-break>姉ちゃんは、その魔物に勝つ自信が<line-break>あるから引き受けたんだろ？<line-break>まさか、そいつに同情したのか！<end>"
EN: "This is the job you took in Rostorl, right? You're avenging the adventurer whose companions were killed by a monster. You accepted because you're confident you can beat it, right, sis? Don't tell me you felt sorry for the guy!<end>"
KO: "로스톨에서 받은 의뢰지? 마물에게 동료를 잃은 모험가의 원수를 갚는 거잖아. 누나는 그 마물을 이길 자신이 있어서 맡은 거지? 설마 그 녀석이 불쌍해서 그런 건 아니지!<end>"

## 1230010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私には、その若者の気持ちが<line-break>痛いほどよくわかるわ。<line-break><line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>あなたが行かないのであれば、<line-break>私が城塞都市跡へ行くわ。<end>"
EN: "I understand that young man's feelings all too painfully. <value:$28>, if you won't go, then I will go to the Fortress City Ruins myself.<end>"
KO: "나는 그 젊은이의 마음을 뼈저리게 이해해. <value:$28>, 네가 가지 않는다면 내가 성채도시 유적으로 갈게.<end>"

## 1230011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんは今や、駆け出しの<line-break>冒険者たちの目標だ。<line-break>やつらの期待を裏切らないためにも、<line-break>今回の仇討ちは成功させろよ。<line-break>城塞都市のモンスターは強い。<line-break>油断はするな。<end>"
EN: "You've become a role model for novice adventurers. To live up to their expectations, make sure this quest for vengeance succeeds. The monster in the Fortress City Ruins is strong. Don't let your guard down.<end>"
KO: "너는 이제 풋내기 모험가들의 목표다. 녀석들의 기대를 배신하지 않으려면 이번 원수 갚기는 성공시켜라. 성새도시의 몬스터는 강하다. 방심하지 마.<end>"

## 1230012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "城塞都市跡に現れる魔物か…。<line-break>気になる事がある。<line-break>付き合ってやってもいいぞ。<end>"
EN: "A monster appearing in the Fortress City Ruins... Something about that concerns me. I suppose I could accompany you.<end>"
KO: "성채도시 터에 나타나는 마물인가…. 신경 쓰이는 게 있어. 같이 가 줘도 되겠지.<end>"

## 1230013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お主ほど有名になってしまうと、<line-break>他の冒険者の手本となるように<line-break>行動せねばいかんぞ。<line-break>城塞都市跡に現れる魔物とやらも、<line-break>ほっとけば犠牲者が増える一方じゃ。<line-break>ここは、お主が倒すしかないぞ。<end>"
EN: "Now that you have become so famous, you must conduct yourself as an example to other adventurers. If this monster in the Fortress City Ruins is left alone, the number of victims will only grow. You have no choice but to defeat it.<end>"
KO: "자네 정도로 유명해졌다면 다른 모험가의 본보기가 되도록 행동해야 한다. 성채도시 유적의 마물도 내버려 두면 희생자만 늘어날 거야. 여기선 자네가 쓰러뜨릴 수밖에 없어.<end>"

## 1230014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールの冒険者の間でも、<line-break>城塞都市跡の魔物は問題に<line-break>なっているみたいだよ。<line-break>ほっとけば新たな犠牲者が出るかも<line-break>しれないし、僕たちで何とかしようよ。<end>"
EN: "It sounds like the monster in the Fortress City Ruins has become a problem for Rostorl's adventurers too. If we leave it alone, there may be more victims. Let's do something about it ourselves.<end>"
KO: "로스톨 모험자들 사이에서도 성채도시 터의 마물은 문제가 되는 모양이야. 내버려 두면 새 희생자가 나올지도 모르니 우리가 어떻게든 해 보자.<end>"

## 1230015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "死んだ仲間の仇討ちか…。<line-break>無念を晴らしてやりたい気持ちは<line-break>私にもよくわかるが、<line-break>君まで犠牲になってはならない。<line-break>城塞都市跡に行くのなら、<line-break>万全を期すべきだ。<end>"
EN: "Avenging fallen companions... I understand the desire to avenge them, but you must not become another victim. If you go to the Fortress City Ruins, you should make every possible preparation.<end>"
KO: "죽은 동료의 복수인가…. 원한을 풀어 주고 싶은 마음은 나도 잘 알지만, 너까지 희생돼선 안 된다. 성채도시 폐허에 갈 거라면 만전을 기해야 한다.<end>"

## 1230016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仇討ちなんて、<line-break>ほ～んと人間って野蛮よね。<line-break>だから戦争なんて起こすんだわ。<line-break>高貴で優雅で知性あふれる<line-break>アタクシたちを見習って欲しいものね。<end>"
EN: "Revenge? Humans truly are so barbaric. That's why they start wars. They should learn from us: noble, graceful, and brimming with intellect.<end>"
KO: "복수라니, 정말 인간은 야만적이네. 그러니까 전쟁 같은 걸 일으키는 거야. 고귀하고 우아하고 지적인 우리를 본받았으면 좋겠어.<end>"

## 1230017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あだー！　ウッ！　ちぃいいい！<line-break>仇討ちに失敗してケガをしたでござる。<line-break><line-break>少し冗長でござったな。<line-break>拙者、さらに精進する所存。<line-break>兄弟子もがんばってくだされ。<end>"
EN: "Avenge―ow! Ugh! Gaaah! I failed to avenge them and was injured. That was a little long-winded. I intend to train even harder. Do your best as well, senior disciple.<end>"
KO: "아야! 윽! 으으으! 원수 갚기에 실패해서 다쳤소이다. 조금 장황했소이다. 소인은 더욱 정진할 생각이오. 사형도 힘내 주시오.<end>"

## 1230018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールの酒場で教えてもらったの。<line-break>城塞都市跡に凶暴な魔物が住み着いて<line-break>冒険者を襲っているんだってぇ。<line-break><value:$28>が倒してって<line-break>頼まれたのも、きっとその魔物だよ。<end>"
EN: "I heard about it at a tavern in Rostorl. A vicious monster has settled in the Fortress City Ruins and is attacking adventurers. I bet it's the same monster <value:$28> was asked to defeat.<end>"
KO: "로스톨 술집에서 들었어. 성채도시 터에 흉폭한 마물이 자리 잡고 모험자를 습격한대. <value:$28>에게 쓰러뜨려 달라고 한 것도 분명 그 마물이야.<end>"

## 1230019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>相手の力量を見誤れば、死ぬ。<line-break>忘れるな。<end>"
EN: "........... Misjudge your opponent's strength and you die. Never forget that.<end>"
KO: "………………. 상대의 실력을 잘못 보면 죽는다. 잊지 마라.<end>"

## 1230020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無力がために、次々仲間たちが<line-break>殺されていく…。<line-break>さぞ無念だったことだろうな。<line-break>しかし、仇討ちを他人に頼むと<line-break>なると、話は別だ。その者の命まで<line-break>捧げることとなったら、どうする。<end>"
EN: "To be powerless as one companion after another is killed... The frustration must have been unbearable. But asking someone else to take revenge is another matter. What if it costs that person a life as well?<end>"
KO: "무력해서 동료들이 차례차례 죽어 가는 걸 봤겠지…. 얼마나 원통했을까. 하지만 복수를 남에게 맡기는 건 다른 이야기다. 그 사람의 목숨까지 바치게 된다면 어떻게 할 거지?<end>"

## 1230021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "城塞都市跡で次々に冒険者を襲っている<line-break>魔物はね、キマイラみたいに頭が２個<line-break>あって、しっぽがヘビなんだって。<line-break>それからねロストールの冒険者たちは、<line-break><value:$28>が魔物を倒してくれる<line-break>のを待ち望んでるみたいだよ。<end>"
EN: "They say the monster attacking adventurers in the Fortress City Ruins has two heads like a Chimera and a snake for a tail. And it sounds like the adventurers of Rostorl are eagerly waiting for <value:$28> to defeat it.<end>"
KO: "성새도시 유적에서 모험자들을 계속 습격하는 마물은 키마이라처럼 머리가 두 개고 꼬리가 뱀이래. 그리고 로스톨 모험자들은 <value:$28>이 그 마물을 쓰러뜨려 주길 기다리고 있는 모양이야.<end>"

## 1230022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "駆け出しの身で城塞都市跡に行くとは<line-break>軽率さゆえ身を滅ぼしたと<line-break>言われても仕方がない。<line-break>しかし、彼らのおかげで魔物の<line-break>危険さがわかったというわけだ。<line-break>放っておくわけにはいかないな。<end>"
EN: "They were mere novices when they went to the Fortress City Ruins, so no one could deny that their recklessness cost them their lives. But thanks to them, we now know how dangerous the monster is. We cannot leave it alone.<end>"
KO: "초보인데 성채도시 유적에 갔으니 경솔함 때문에 목숨을 잃었다고 해도 할 말이 없지. 하지만 그들 덕분에 마물의 위험성을 알게 됐다는 거야. 내버려 둘 순 없겠군.<end>"

## 1230023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "町で聞いた噂が確かならば、<line-break>城塞都市跡に現れる魔物は、<line-break>かなり強大なもののようです。<line-break>仇討ちはともかく、このまま<line-break>放っておくのは、犠牲者を増やす<line-break>ことにつながるかもしれません。<end>"
EN: "If the rumors we heard in town are true, the monster appearing in the Fortress City Ruins is extremely powerful. Revenge aside, leaving it alone may lead to more victims.<end>"
KO: "마을에서 들은 소문이 맞다면 성채도시 터에 나타나는 마물은 상당히 강한 모양입니다. 복수는 둘째 치고 이대로 방치하면 희생자가 더 늘어날지도 모릅니다.<end>"

## 1230024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの人たちのかたきを討ちに行くのね？<line-break>頑張ろう、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "We're going to avenge those people, right? Let's do our best, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그 사람들 원수를 갚으러 가는 거지? 힘내자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1230025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…まこと、けしからん。<line-break>道理知らずの冒険者気取りが、<line-break>むざむざ落命なぞしおって！<line-break>城塞都市跡へ向かおう。<line-break>魔物は、獲物ではないのじゃ…。<end>"
EN: "...Truly disgraceful. Those ignorant would-be adventurers went and threw their lives away! Let us head to the Fortress City Ruins. A monster is no mere quarry...<end>"
KO: "…정말 괘씸하군. 도리도 모르는 모험자 흉내쟁이가 허무하게 목숨을 잃다니! 성채도시 터로 가자. 마물은 사냥감이 아니니라….<end>"

## 1230026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの若者、さぞ無念だったろうな。<line-break>仲間が犠牲になっていくのを目の前に<line-break>どうもできなかったとは…。<line-break><value:$28>に出会えたのは<line-break>せめてもの幸いだっただろう。<line-break>これで仇が討たれるのだからな。<end>"
EN: "That young man must have felt utterly helpless. To watch his companions fall and be unable to do anything... Meeting <value:$28> was at least one stroke of good fortune. Now they will be avenged.<end>"
KO: "그 청년, 정말 원통했겠군. 동료들이 희생되는 걸 눈앞에서 보고도 아무것도 할 수 없었다니…. 그나마 <value:$28>를 만난 게 다행이겠지. 이제 원수를 갚을 수 있으니까.<end>"

## 1230027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "依頼してきた男の仲間を殺した魔物は、<line-break>城塞都市跡にいるそうだ。<line-break><line-break>あいつの無念を晴らすために、<line-break>行くぞ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "The monster that killed the client's companions is apparently in the Fortress City Ruins. Let's go, <value:$28>, and lay his regrets to rest.<end>"
KO: "의뢰한 남자의 동료를 죽인 마물은 성새도시터에 있는 모양이야. 그의 한을 풀어 주러 가자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1230028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの冒険者もかわいそうね…。<line-break>とにかく仇を討ってあげましょ。<line-break>魔物は城塞都市跡にいるわ。<line-break>でもね、相手の力を見誤るっていうのは<line-break>命に関わることなのよ。<line-break>肝に銘じておいて、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I feel sorry for that adventurer too... In any case, let's avenge his companions. The monster is in the Fortress City Ruins. But misjudging an opponent's strength can cost you your life. Take that to heart, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그 모험자도 불쌍하네…. 아무튼 원수를 갚아주자. 마물은 성채도시 터에 있어. 하지만 상대의 힘을 잘못 판단하는 건 목숨이 걸린 일이야. 명심해 둬, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1230030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔物に仲間たちを殺された…。<line-break>その気持ち、よくわかります。<line-break><line-break>死んでいった彼の仲間たちのためにも、<line-break>城塞都市跡の魔物を倒しましょう。<end>"
EN: "To have your companions killed by a monster... I know that pain well. For the sake of the companions he lost, let us defeat the monster in the Fortress City Ruins.<end>"
KO: "마물에게 동료들을 잃었군요… 그 마음, 잘 압니다. 죽은 동료들을 위해서라도 성채도시 터의 마물을 쓰러뜨리죠.<end>"

## 1230032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（テラネの冒険者ギルドで、<line-break>　紺碧の洞窟を荒らす怪物の退治を<line-break>　依頼された）<end>"
EN: "(At the Adventurers' Guild in Terane, we were hired to defeat the monster ravaging Azure Cave.)<end>"
KO: "(테라네의 모험자 길드에서 감청의 동굴을 어지럽히는 괴물 퇴치를 의뢰받았다)<end>"

## 1230033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "テラネで依頼された仕事ね。<line-break>紺碧の洞窟のコロル石は、<line-break>ディンガル帝国の収入源のひとつよ。<line-break>この仕事のできしだいでは、<line-break>私たちの働きが帝国に認められて<line-break>騎士になれる…なんて無理か。<end>"
EN: "This is the job we took in Terane. The Korol Stone in Azure Cave is one of the Dyneskal Empire's sources of revenue. If we handle this job well, perhaps the Empire will recognize our service and make us knights... No, I suppose that's impossible.<end>"
KO: "테라네에서 의뢰받은 일이네. 푸른 동굴의 코롤석은 딘갈 제국의 수입원 중 하나야. 이 일을 잘 해내면 우리 활약을 제국이 인정해서 기사가 될 수도… 역시 무리인가.<end>"

## 1230034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "５０００ギアとは、<line-break>帝国のコロル石採掘部門も<line-break>ずいぶんとケチじゃな。<line-break>紺碧の洞窟は、お主が依頼を受けた<line-break>テラネの北にある。<line-break>準備ができたら出発しようぞ。<end>"
EN: "Five thousand Gea? The Empire's Korol Stone mining division is rather stingy. Azure Cave lies north of Terane, where you accepted the job. Let us depart once you are ready.<end>"
KO: "5000기어라니, 제국의 코롤석 채굴 부문도 꽤 짜구나. 감벽의 동굴은 네가 의뢰를 받은 테라네 북쪽에 있다. 준비되면 출발하자꾸나.<end>"

## 1230035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その怪物をやっつけるだけで<line-break>５０００ギアも、もらえるんですか！？<line-break>す、すごいです…。<line-break>冒険者って本当にもうかるんですね。<line-break>私、応援してますから、<line-break>がんばってくださいね。<end>"
EN: "You get five thousand Gea just for defeating that monster?! Th-That's incredible... Adventurers really do make a lot of money. I'll be cheering you on, so do your best!<end>"
KO: "그 괴물 하나 쓰러뜨리는 것만으로 5000기어나 받는다고요!? 대, 대단해요…. 모험가는 정말 돈을 많이 버는군요. 응원하고 있을 테니 힘내세요.<end>"

## 1230036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この怪物退治は、ほんとうに<line-break>テラネの冒険者ギルドで引き受けた<line-break>仕事なんだな！<line-break>よっしゃぁああ！<line-break>故郷に錦を飾るチャンス到来。<line-break>親父！　おふくろ！　見ててくれよ！<end>"
EN: "You really took this monster-hunting job at the Adventurers' Guild in Terane, right? All riiight! My chance to return home in glory has finally come. Dad! Mom! Watch me!<end>"
KO: "이 괴물 퇴치가 정말 테라네 모험가 길드에서 받은 일이란 말이지! 좋아아아! 고향에 금의환향할 기회가 왔다. 아버지! 어머니! 지켜봐 줘!<end>"

## 1230037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "テラネのギルドで依頼されて、<line-break>紺碧の洞窟へ怪物退治に行くんでしょ。<line-break>ふふふ、ボクには何でもお見通しさ。<line-break>報酬が５０００ギアだってこともね。<line-break>今回の報酬でボクの装備品を鍛えて<line-break>欲しいなぁ…なんてダメ？<end>"
EN: "The Guild in Terane hired you to hunt a monster in Azure Cave, right? Heh heh, I know everything. Even that the reward is five thousand Gea. Maybe you could use the money to improve my equipment... No?<end>"
KO: "테라네 길드에서 의뢰받아서 감청의 동굴로 괴물 퇴치하러 가는 거지? 후후후, 나한텐 다 보인다니까. 보수가 5000기어라는 것도 말이야. 이번 보수로 내 장비도 강화해 줬으면 하는데… 안 될까?<end>"

## 1230038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "テラネで依頼されたのは、<line-break>紺碧の洞窟での怪物退治よ。<line-break><line-break>さすがに紺碧の洞窟まで行くとなると<line-break>辺境という気がするわね。<end>"
EN: "The job you took in Terane is to hunt a monster in Azure Cave. Going all the way to Azure Cave really does feel like venturing into the frontier.<end>"
KO: "테라네에서 의뢰받은 건 감청색 동굴의 괴물 퇴치야. 감청색 동굴까지 가려니 확실히 변방이라는 느낌이 드네.<end>"

## 1230039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確か、テラネで依頼されたのは、<line-break>紺碧の洞窟の怪物退治だった<line-break>はずですね。<end>"
EN: "If I remember correctly, the job you took in Terane was to hunt the monster in Azure Cave.<end>"
KO: "분명 테라네에서 받은 의뢰는 남빛 동굴의 괴물 퇴치였을 겁니다.<end>"

## 1230040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今度は紺碧の洞窟で怪物退治？<line-break>報酬が高いのはいいけど、<line-break>ずいぶん遠くまで行くのねぇ～。<end>"
EN: "Now we're hunting a monster in Azure Cave? A big reward is nice, but we're going an awfully long way.<end>"
KO: "이번엔 감청의 동굴에서 괴물 퇴치야? 보수가 높은 건 좋지만 꽤 멀리까지 가네~.<end>"

## 1230041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃん聞いたぜ！<line-break>テラネで引き受けた怪物退治の仕事は、<line-break>成功報酬が５０００ギアなんだって！<line-break>俺、冒険者やっててよかったよ。<line-break>もちろん畑仕事も好きだけど、<line-break>それじゃ大金は手に入らないからな。<end>"
EN: "Sis, I heard! The monster-hunting job you took in Terane pays five thousand Gea if you succeed! I'm glad I became an adventurer. I like farming too, of course, but it won't make me rich.<end>"
KO: "누나, 들었어! 테라네에서 맡은 괴물 퇴치 일은 성공 보수가 5000기어래! 나 모험자 하길 잘했어. 물론 밭일도 좋아하지만 그걸로는 큰돈을 벌 수 없으니까.<end>"

## 1230042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "紺碧の洞窟といえば、コロル石の<line-break>採掘場がある。帝国側からしても<line-break>無関係の場所ではないわ。<end>"
EN: "Azure Cave contains a Korol Stone mine. The Empire has a stake in the place too.<end>"
KO: "감청의 동굴이라면 코롤석 채굴장이 있다. 제국 입장에서도 무관한 장소는 아니지.<end>"

## 1230043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "普通は帝国の依頼した仕事なんて、<line-break>腕利きの冒険者じゃないと<line-break>冒険者ギルドも紹介しないもんだ。<line-break>それが依頼されたってんだから、<line-break>お前さんも腕利き冒険者として、<line-break>世間に認められてるってことだぜ。<end>"
EN: "Normally, the Adventurers' Guild only refers jobs from the Empire to highly skilled adventurers. Since they offered it to you, that means people now recognize you as a skilled adventurer too.<end>"
KO: "보통 제국이 의뢰한 일은 실력 있는 모험자가 아니면 모험자 길드도 소개해 주지 않아. 그런데 네게 의뢰가 왔다는 건 너도 실력 있는 모험자로 세상에서 인정받고 있다는 뜻이야.<end>"

## 1230044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "紺碧の洞窟の怪物か…。<line-break>気になる事がある。<line-break>付き合ってやってもいいぞ。<end>"
EN: "The monster in Azure Cave... Something about that concerns me. I suppose I could accompany you.<end>"
KO: "감청의 동굴 괴물인가…. 신경 쓰이는 게 있어. 같이 가 줘도 되겠지.<end>"

## 1230045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "帝国の騎士団もふがいないのう。<line-break>怪物退治など、自分たちで<line-break>やればよかろうに。<line-break>それとも、そのような事に<line-break>騎士団を出せない事情がある<line-break>ということじゃろうか。<end>"
EN: "The Empire's knights are a sorry lot. Surely they could hunt the monster themselves. Or is there some reason they cannot deploy the knights for such a task?<end>"
KO: "제국 기사단도 한심하군. 괴물 퇴치쯤은 자기들이 하면 될 텐데. 아니면 그런 일에 기사단을 보낼 수 없는 사정이라도 있는 걸까.<end>"

## 1230046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "テラネの町は冒険者に冷たいから、<line-break>あまり冒険者が集まらないんだ。<line-break>ギルドの主人も困っていたと思うよ。<line-break>だから、もしも僕たちがこの仕事を<line-break>失敗したら、他にやってくれる人は<line-break>いないよ。<end>"
EN: "Terane isn't friendly to adventurers, so not many gather there. I think the Guildmaster was having trouble because of that. If we fail this job, there may be no one else willing to do it.<end>"
KO: "테라네는 모험가에게 냉정해서 모험가가 별로 모이지 않아. 길드 주인도 곤란해하고 있을 거야. 그러니 우리가 이 일을 실패하면 대신 해 줄 사람은 없을 거야.<end>"

## 1230047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "テラネのギルドで依頼されたのは<line-break>紺碧の洞窟の怪物退治だったな。<line-break>気を抜かず、解決しよう。<end>"
EN: "The job we took at the Guild in Terane was to hunt the monster in Azure Cave. Let us stay alert and see it through.<end>"
KO: "테라네 길드에서 받은 의뢰는 감청의 동굴 괴물 퇴치였지. 방심하지 말고 해결하자.<end>"

## 1230048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま～た飽きもせず怪物退治ぃ！<line-break>いつになったら冒険のすばらしさを<line-break>教えてくれるわけぇ？<line-break>高貴で知的なアタクシでも、<line-break>がまんの限界ってものがあるわよ！<end>"
EN: "Hunting monsters again? Don't you ever get tired of it?! When are you going to show me the wonders of adventure? Even a noble and intellectual lady such as myself has limits to her patience!<end>"
KO: "또 질리지도 않고 괴물 퇴치야!? 언제쯤 모험의 멋짐을 알려 줄 건데? 고귀하고 지적인 나도 참는 데 한계가 있어!<end>"

## 1230049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "紺碧の怪物を貝でぶつ！<line-break>略して、今回ぶつ！<line-break>かかってこんかい！<line-break>おそまつさまでござる。<end>"
EN: "I'll conk the Azure monster with a conch! In short: conch-k it! Come and get conked! Thank you, thank you.<end>"
KO: "감청의 괴물을 조개로 후려친다! 줄여서 이번에도 친다! 덤벼라! 변변찮았소이다.<end>"

## 1230050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "紺碧の洞窟の怪物を退治したら、<line-break><value:$28>、テラネのギルドへ<line-break>報告に行くんでしょ。<line-break>テラネのボンガさんはいい人だよぉ。<line-break>ルルアンタ、お菓子をもらっちゃった。<line-break>また今度遊びに行くんだぁ。<end>"
EN: "After defeating the monster in Azure Cave, you're going to report back to the Guild in Terane, right, <value:$28>? Bonga in Terane is really nice. He gave Lulluantha some sweets! I'm going back to visit again sometime.<end>"
KO: "감청의 동굴 괴물을 퇴치하면 <value:$28>, 테라네 길드에 보고하러 가는 거지? 테라네의 본가 씨는 좋은 사람이야아. 루루안타, 과자도 받았어. 다음에 또 놀러 갈 거야아.<end>"

## 1230051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>興味がない。<end>"
EN: "........... I have no interest.<end>"
KO: "………………. 관심 없어.<end>"

## 1230052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "紺碧の洞窟も、ディンガルの<line-break>鉱物採掘場のひとつだと聞いた。<line-break><line-break>ならば、わざわざ俺たちが<line-break>出るまでもなかろう？<end>"
EN: "I heard Azure Cave is one of Dyneskal's mineral mines. If so, surely there is no need for us to intervene.<end>"
KO: "감청색 동굴도 딘갈의 광물 채굴장 중 하나라고 들었다. 그렇다면 굳이 우리가 나설 필요는 없겠지?<end>"

## 1230053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "紺碧の洞窟の地底湖ってきれいだよね。<line-break>きれいなものも見られて、報酬も<line-break>もらえるなんて一石二鳥だよ。<line-break>ボクはもう、行く気満々だからぁ。<line-break>いつでも出発できるよっ！<line-break>さあ、しゅっぱーつ！<end>"
EN: "The underground lake in Azure Cave is beautiful, isn't it? We get to see something lovely and earn a reward too―the best of both worlds! I'm already raring to go. I can leave anytime! Come on, let's gooo!<end>"
KO: "감청의 동굴 지저호는 예쁘지. 예쁜 것도 보고 보수도 받고, 일석이조야. 나는 이미 갈 마음 만만이니까 언제든 출발할 수 있어! 자, 출발~!<end>"

## 1230054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "報告によると紺碧の洞窟の魔物は<line-break>かなり手ごわい相手らしい。<line-break>君がしくじるとは思えんが…。<line-break>用心はしておいた方がよかろう。<end>"
EN: "According to the reports, the monster in Azure Cave is a formidable opponent. I do not expect you to fail, but caution would still be wise.<end>"
KO: "보고에 따르면 감청색 동굴의 마물은 꽤 강한 상대라고 한다. 네가 실수할 거라 생각하진 않지만…. 조심하는 게 좋겠지.<end>"

## 1230055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "紺碧の洞窟にはディンガルの<line-break>鉱物資源採掘場があります。<line-break><line-break>そこを取り戻したいというのなら、<line-break>帝国が動くべきではないでしょうか？<line-break>どうお考えです、<value:$28>様？<end>"
EN: "Azure Cave contains one of Dyneskal's mineral extraction sites. If the Empire wants to reclaim it, should the Empire not take action itself? What do you think, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "감청의 동굴에는 딩갈의 광물자원 채굴장이 있습니다. 그곳을 되찾고 싶다면 제국이 움직여야 하지 않을까요? 어떻게 생각하십니까, <value:$28> 님?<end>"

## 1230056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "紺碧の洞窟に行くんだよね。<line-break>でも、ディンガルの土地でしょ？<line-break>どうして冒険者にやらせるのかな？<end>"
EN: "We're going to Azure Cave, right? But that's Dyneskal territory, isn't it? Why are they making adventurers handle it?<end>"
KO: "감청의 동굴에 가는 거지? 하지만 딘갈 땅이잖아? 왜 모험자에게 시키는 걸까?<end>"

## 1230057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "美しい地底湖とコロル石産出で知られる<line-break>紺碧の洞窟にも魔物が出現したとな。<line-break>闇の勢いは依然増している…。<line-break>封士と呼ばれた身、<line-break>いざ、紺碧の洞窟へ行くぞ！<end>"
EN: "So a monster has appeared even in Azure Cave, famed for its beautiful underground lake and Korol Stone. The darkness continues to gather strength... As one once called a Sealer, I must answer the call. To Azure Cave!<end>"
KO: "아름다운 지하호와 코롤석 산지로 알려진 감청의 동굴에도 마물이 나타났다더군. 어둠의 기세는 여전히 커지고 있어… 봉사라 불렸던 몸, 자, 감청의 동굴로 간다!<end>"

## 1230058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "紺碧の洞窟が次の目的地になるとは、<line-break>私たちもずいぶんと遠くへやってきた<line-break>ものだな。<line-break>岬の先は全て無限とも思える大海、<line-break>まさに最果ての地という感じだ。<end>"
EN: "To think Azure Cave would be our next destination. We truly have come a long way. Beyond the cape lies nothing but a vast sea that seems infinite. It truly feels like the end of the world.<end>"
KO: "감청의 동굴이 다음 목적지가 되다니 우리도 참 멀리 왔군. 곶 너머는 끝이 없을 듯한 대해. 정말 세상의 끝 같은 곳이야.<end>"

## 1230059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "紺碧の洞窟はテラネの北にある。<line-break>おそらく強力な相手だ、気を抜くな。<end>"
EN: "Azure Cave is north of Terane. Our opponent is probably powerful, so stay alert.<end>"
KO: "남빛 동굴은 테라네 북쪽에 있어. 아마 강한 상대일 거야. 방심하지 마.<end>"

## 1230060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "報酬が高ければ高いほど<line-break>危険なのよ、わかってるわよね？<line-break>気を引き締めてかかるのよ。<end>"
EN: "The greater the reward, the greater the danger. You understand that, don't you? Keep your wits about you.<end>"
KO: "보수가 높으면 높을수록 위험한 거야. 알고 있지? 정신 바짝 차리고 덤벼.<end>"

## 1230062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "紺碧の洞窟で怪物を倒す依頼です。<line-break>報酬は、なんと５０００ギア！？<line-break>たくさんもらえるんですね。<end>"
EN: "The job is to defeat a monster in Azure Cave. And the reward is five thousand Gea?! That's a lot of money.<end>"
KO: "감청의 동굴에서 괴물을 쓰러뜨리는 의뢰예요. 보수는 무려 5000기어!? 많이 받는군요.<end>"

## 1230064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（ウルカーンのギルドで会った男の子に<line-break>　鳳凰山へ鳳凰を探しに行ってくれと<line-break>　頼まれてしまった）<end>"
EN: "(A boy we met at the Guild in Ulkarne asked us to search Phoenix Mountain for a phoenix.)<end>"
KO: "(울칸 길드에서 만난 남자아이에게 봉황산에 봉황을 찾으러 가 달라는 부탁을 받았다.)<end>"

## 1230065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "鳳凰山に鳳凰を探しに行くって、本気？<line-break>あなたに鳳凰探しを頼んだ子が、<line-break>嘘を言ってるかもしれないのに…。<line-break>ほんと、お人好しよね。<line-break>そこがあなたのいいところ<line-break>なのかもしれないけど。<end>"
EN: "Are you seriously going to Phoenix Mountain to look for a phoenix? The child who asked you may have been lying... You really are too kind for your own good. Though perhaps that's one of your better qualities.<end>"
KO: "봉황산에 봉황을 찾으러 간다고, 진심이야? 부탁한 아이가 거짓말했을지도 모르는데…. 정말 사람 좋다니까. 그게 네 좋은 점인지도 모르지만.<end>"

## 1230066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "鳳凰山に鳳凰探しとな？<line-break>ハハハハハハ…。<line-break>おもしろい！　わしも手伝うぞ。<line-break>子どもの願いを聞いてやることも、<line-break>勇者としては大切な仕事だからな。<end>"
EN: "Search Phoenix Mountain for a phoenix, you say? Ha ha ha ha ha! Fascinating! I shall help too. Granting a child's wish is also an important duty for a hero.<end>"
KO: "봉황산에서 봉황 찾기라고? 하하하하하… 재미있군! 나도 돕겠다. 아이의 소원을 들어주는 것도 용자로서 중요한 일이니까.<end>"

## 1230067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界的に有名な冒険者、<line-break><value:$28>様に<line-break>１０ギアで仕事を頼むなんて…。<line-break>ほんっとに子どもって迷惑ですよね。<line-break>でも、そんな子どものわがままにも<line-break>つきあってあげるなんて立派ですね。<end>"
EN: "Imagine asking the world-famous adventurer <value:$28> to do a job for ten Gea... Children truly are such a nuisance. Still, it is admirable of you to indulge such a selfish request.<end>"
KO: "세계적으로 유명한 모험자 <value:$28>님께 10기어짜리 일을 부탁하다니…. 정말 아이들은 민폐라니까요. 그래도 그런 아이의 억지까지 받아 주시다니 훌륭하시네요.<end>"

## 1230068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "鳳凰山に鳳凰を探しに行くのか！<line-break>おっもしれえじゃないか。<line-break>俺も行きたいぜ。<line-break>ところで、<line-break>鳳凰を見つけたらどうすんだ？<line-break>羽でも抜いて帰るのか？<end>"
EN: "You're going to Phoenix Mountain to look for a phoenix? That sounds great! I want to come too. By the way, what'll you do if you find one? Pluck out a feather and bring it back?<end>"
KO: "봉황산에 봉황을 찾으러 가는 거야? 재밌잖아. 나도 가고 싶어. 그런데 봉황을 찾으면 뭘 할 건데? 깃털이라도 뽑아 올 거야?<end>"

## 1230069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボクは、その子の話を信じるよ。<line-break>キミだって信じているから、<line-break>鳳凰探しを引き受けたんでしょ。<line-break>その子が嘘つきじゃないってことを<line-break>証明するためにも、<line-break>ぜったいに鳳凰を見つけようね。<end>"
EN: "I believe that child's story. You accepted the search because you believe it too, right? Let's make sure we find the phoenix and prove that child isn't a liar.<end>"
KO: "난 그 아이 말을 믿어. 너도 믿으니까 봉황 찾기를 맡은 거잖아. 그 아이가 거짓말쟁이가 아니라는 걸 증명하기 위해서라도 반드시 봉황을 찾자.<end>"

## 1230070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "鳳凰を見たという子供の言葉を<line-break>信じるとは、<value:$28>も<line-break>いいところがあるわね。<line-break>ギルドでは一蹴されていたけど、<line-break>腹黒い大人たちに比べれば、<line-break>子供はよほど信頼できるわ。<end>"
EN: "So you believe the child who claims to have seen a phoenix. You do have some good qualities, <value:$28>. The Guild dismissed the story outright, but children are far more trustworthy than scheming adults.<end>"
KO: "봉황을 봤다는 아이 말을 믿다니, <value:$28>도 괜찮은 데가 있네. 길드에선 일축당했지만 속 검은 어른들보단 아이가 훨씬 믿을 만하지.<end>"

## 1230071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "子供の言うことを聞いてあげるとは<line-break><value:$28>もなかなか<line-break>余裕が出てきましたね。<line-break>行ってあげるといいでしょう。<line-break>その子が業を煮やして、自分で<line-break>確かめに走らないうちにね。<end>"
EN: "Indulging a child's request shows that you have gained some composure, <value:$28>. You should go, before the child loses patience and runs off to investigate alone.<end>"
KO: "아이 말을 들어주다니 <value:$28>도 꽤 여유가 생겼군요. 가 주는 게 좋겠어요. 그 아이가 답답해서 직접 확인하러 달려가기 전에요.<end>"

## 1230072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へぇ～、<value:$28>の<line-break>意外な一面発見！　って感じねえ。<line-break>感心しちゃうわ。<line-break>じゃ、いっちょ、鳳凰山まで<line-break>行きましょうか、勇者様。<line-break>夢あるチビッ子のために！<end>"
EN: "Well, well, an unexpected side of <value:$28>! I'm impressed. All right, let's take a trip to Phoenix Mountain, mighty Hero―for the sake of a little dreamer!<end>"
KO: "헤에~ <value:$28>의 의외의 면을 발견한 느낌이네. 감탄했어. 그럼 봉황산까지 가 볼까, 용사님. 꿈 많은 꼬마를 위해서!<end>"

## 1230073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすが、姉ちゃんはひと味違うよ。<line-break>世界中捜したって子どもの依頼を<line-break>受ける冒険者は姉ちゃんだけだぜ。<line-break>俺、姉ちゃんの弟だって誇りに思う。<line-break>俺も手伝うからさ、あの子のためにも<line-break>鳳凰を見つけような！<end>"
EN: "I knew you were special, sis. Search the whole world and you wouldn't find another adventurer willing to accept a child's request. I'm proud to be your little brother. I'll help too, so let's find that phoenix for the kid!<end>"
KO: "역시 누나는 남다르다니까. 세상을 다 뒤져도 아이의 의뢰를 받아 주는 모험자는 누나뿐일 거야. 나, 누나의 동생인 게 자랑스러워. 나도 도울 테니까 그 아이를 위해서라도 봉황을 찾자!<end>"

## 1230074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "周囲は何と言ったか知らないけれど、<line-break>私は、<value:$28>の判断は<line-break>正しいと思うわ。<line-break>鳳凰山にもモンスターはいる。<line-break>そんなところへ子供を行かせる<line-break>わけにはいかないもの…。<end>"
EN: "I don't know what everyone else said, but I think you made the right decision, <value:$28>. There are monsters on Phoenix Mountain too. We cannot let a child go to such a place...<end>"
KO: "주변에서 뭐라고 했는지는 모르겠지만, 난 <value:$28>의 판단이 옳았다고 생각해. 봉황산에도 몬스터는 있어. 그런 곳에 아이를 보낼 순 없잖아….<end>"

## 1230075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウルカーンのギルドの主人に聞いたぜ。<line-break>お前さん、鳳凰山に鳳凰を<line-break>探しに行く依頼を受けたんだろ？<line-break>ギルドの主人は信じてなかったが、<line-break>鳳凰山に鳳凰が降り立つって伝説は<line-break>本当にあるんだ。信じていいと思うぜ。<end>"
EN: "I heard from the Guildmaster in Ulkarne. You took a job to search Phoenix Mountain for a phoenix, right? The Guildmaster didn't believe it, but there really is a legend that a phoenix descends upon Phoenix Mountain. I think you can believe the boy.<end>"
KO: "울칸 길드 주인에게 들었어. 너 봉황산에서 봉황을 찾는 의뢰를 받았다며? 길드 주인은 믿지 않던데, 봉황산에 봉황이 내려온다는 전설은 진짜야. 믿어도 될 거라고 봐.<end>"

## 1230076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "鳳凰山に鳳凰を探しにいくのか…。<line-break>気になる事がある。<line-break>付き合ってやってもいいぞ。<end>"
EN: "You're going to Phoenix Mountain to search for a phoenix... Something about that concerns me. I suppose I could accompany you.<end>"
KO: "봉황산에 봉황을 찾으러 가는 건가…. 신경 쓰이는 게 있어. 함께 가 줘도 좋다.<end>"

## 1230077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "鳳凰山に鳳凰を探しに行くとな？<line-break>わはははは…、これは傑作じゃ。<line-break>わしも、ぜひ参加したいぞ。<line-break>もちろん冗談で付き合うのではないぞ。<line-break>お主が、その子を信じておるからこそ、<line-break>わしも信じておるのじゃからな。<end>"
EN: "You mean to search Phoenix Mountain for a phoenix? Wah ha ha ha! What a marvelous idea. I insist on joining you. And I am not coming along as a joke. I believe because you believe that child.<end>"
KO: "봉황산에 봉황을 찾으러 간다고? 와하하하… 이거 걸작이군. 나도 꼭 참가하고 싶다. 물론 농담 삼아 따라가는 건 아니야. 네가 그 아이를 믿으니 나도 믿는 거다.<end>"

## 1230078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "鳳凰探し？<line-break>ああ、ウルカーンのギルドで、<line-break>男の子に頼まれた仕事だね。<line-break>その子の言うとおり、鳳凰山で鳳凰を<line-break>見つけられればいいんだけど…。<line-break>もし、いなかったらどうしようか？<end>"
EN: "A phoenix hunt? Ah, the job that boy gave you at the Guild in Ulkarne. I hope we can find a phoenix on Phoenix Mountain, just as he said... But what will we do if there isn't one?<end>"
KO: "봉황 찾기? 아, 울칸 길드에서 남자아이가 부탁한 일이네. 그 아이 말대로 봉황산에서 봉황을 찾으면 좋겠지만…. 만약 없다면 어떡하지?<end>"

## 1230079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "鳳凰山には鳳凰が降り立つという<line-break>伝説が現にあるらしい。<line-break><line-break>その第一発見者が子供だというだけで、<line-break>鳳凰自体は本当にいるのかもしれない。<line-break>私としても、興味はあるな。<end>"
EN: "Apparently there really is a legend that a phoenix descends upon Phoenix Mountain. The mere fact that a child was the first to spot it does not mean the phoenix itself is imaginary. I admit, I am interested.<end>"
KO: "봉황산에는 봉황이 내려온다는 전설이 실제로 있는 모양이야. 최초 발견자가 아이라는 이유만으로, 봉황 자체는 정말 있을지도 몰라. 나도 흥미가 있군.<end>"

## 1230080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "鳳凰山に鳳凰を探しに行くぅ？<line-break>アナタ、もしかしてバカなの？<line-break>あの山に鳳凰がいるわけないでしょ！<line-break>山の名前が鳳凰山だからって<line-break>鳳凰がいる証拠にはならないのよ！<line-break>くだらなくって付き合ってられないわ。<end>"
EN: "You're going to Phoenix Mountain to look for a phoenix? Are you an idiot? There's no way a phoenix lives on that mountain! Calling it Phoenix Mountain doesn't prove there's a phoenix there! This is too ridiculous for me to bother with.<end>"
KO: "봉황산에 봉황을 찾으러 간다고오? 당신 혹시 바보야? 그 산에 봉황이 있을 리 없잖아! 산 이름이 봉황산이라고 봉황이 있다는 증거는 아니거든! 시시해서 못 어울려 주겠어.<end>"

## 1230081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーおー<line-break>鳳凰でござるか。<line-break>ほおーっておけばいいでござるよ。<line-break>ふふふふ…、<line-break>拙者もダジャレのコツがつかめて<line-break>きましたぞ。<end>"
EN: "A fee-nix, you say? I say we should nix the whole affair. Heh heh heh... I am finally getting the hang of puns.<end>"
KO: "호오오— 봉황이란 말씀이오? 그냥 내버려 두면 되겠소이다. 후후후후… 소인도 이제 말장난의 요령을 잡은 듯하오.<end>"

## 1230082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "鳳凰山に鳳凰を探しに行くの？<line-break>ルルアンタも連れてって欲しいなあ。<line-break>だって鳳凰、見たことないんだもん。<line-break>もしダメなら捕まえてきてね。<line-break>約束だよ、ね、<value:$28>。<line-break>ルルアンタのお願いだよ。<end>"
EN: "Are you going to Phoenix Mountain to look for a phoenix? Lulluantha wants to come too! I've never seen a phoenix before. If I can't come, catch it and bring it back, okay? Promise me, <value:$28>. It's Lulluantha's special request.<end>"
KO: "봉황산에 봉황을 찾으러 가는 거야? 루루안타도 데려가 줬으면 좋겠다아. 봉황은 본 적이 없거든. 안 되면 잡아 와 줘. 약속이야, 알았지 <value:$28>? 루루안타의 부탁이야.<end>"

## 1230083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>興味がない。<end>"
EN: "........... I have no interest.<end>"
KO: "………………. 관심 없어.<end>"

## 1230084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "鳳凰山に鳳凰がいるというのか。<line-break>子供の言葉は信用できるとは言えんが、<line-break>おもしろい話ではあるな。<end>"
EN: "A phoenix on Phoenix Mountain? I cannot say a child's word is reliable, but it is an intriguing story.<end>"
KO: "봉황산에 봉황이 있다고? 아이 말을 믿을 수 있다고는 할 수 없지만 재미있는 이야기이긴 하군.<end>"

## 1230085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へぇ～、その子は鳳凰を見たのかぁ。<line-break>いいなー、いいなー、ボクも見たいな。<line-break>鳳凰探し、楽しそうだなぁー。<line-break>ねえねえ、<value:$28>～。<line-break>ボクも鳳凰探しに連れていってよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, that child saw a phoenix? Lucky! I want to see one too. Searching for a phoenix sounds like fun! Hey, <value:$28>―take me along on the phoenix hunt!<end>"
KO: "헤에, 그 아이가 봉황을 봤구나. 좋겠다, 나도 보고 싶어. 봉황 찾기, 재밌겠다. 있지, <value:$28>~. 나도 봉황 찾으러 데려가 줘.<end>"

## 1230086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "鳳凰が鳳凰山に降り立つということが<line-break>我が帝国に対して何を意味するのか、<line-break>そのことが気になる。<line-break>良い知らせであればいいが…。<end>"
EN: "I wonder what a phoenix descending upon Phoenix Mountain would mean for our Empire. I hope it is a good omen...<end>"
KO: "봉황이 봉황산에 내려온다는 게 우리 제국에 어떤 의미인지 신경 쓰이는군. 좋은 소식이면 좋겠지만….<end>"

## 1230087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その子の言うことによれば、<line-break>鳳凰山に本物の鳳凰が<line-break>降り立ったというのですね？<line-break><value:$28>様は<line-break>お優しいのですね。<end>"
EN: "According to that child, a real phoenix descended upon Phoenix Mountain? You are very kind, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그 아이 말로는 봉황산에 진짜 봉황이 내려앉았다는 거군요? <value:$28>님은 상냥하시네요.<end>"

## 1230088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "鳳凰山の鳳凰、ヴィアも見てみたいな！<line-break>絶対ヴィアも連れてってよ、<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Via wants to see the phoenix on Phoenix Mountain too! You have to take Via with you, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "봉황산의 봉황, 비아도 보고 싶어! 꼭 비아도 데려가 줘, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1230089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "鳳凰山の鳳凰じゃと？<line-break>ううむ、もしやそれが本物であれば<line-break>とてつもない瑞兆のはずなのじゃが…<line-break><value:$28>、頼む。<line-break>わしも連れて行ってくれい。<line-break>事実を見極めたいのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "A phoenix on Phoenix Mountain? Hmm... If it is genuine, it should be an extraordinary omen of good fortune. Please, <value:$28>, take me with you. I wish to ascertain the truth.<end>"
KO: "봉황산의 봉황이라고? 으음, 만약 그것이 진짜라면 엄청난 길조일 텐데…. <value:$28>, 부탁하네. 나도 데려가 주게. 사실인지 직접 확인하고 싶네.<end>"

## 1230090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "鳳凰が降り立つという伝説は、<line-break>ウルカーンに古く伝えられている<line-break>ものらしい。<line-break>それが子供にまで受け継がれている。<line-break>私としては、どうか鳳凰に<line-break>実在してほしいと思うのだが…。<end>"
EN: "The legend of a descending phoenix has apparently been passed down in Ulkarne since ancient times. It has even survived among the children. For my part, I sincerely hope the phoenix truly exists...<end>"
KO: "봉황이 내려온다는 전설은 울칸에 오래전부터 전해져 온 것 같군. 그 이야기가 아이들에게까지 이어지고 있다. 나로서는 부디 봉황이 실제로 존재했으면 좋겠는데….<end>"

## 1230091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はあの子の言葉を信じる。<line-break>お前も信じているんだろ、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>さあ鳳凰を探しに鳳凰山に行くぞ。<line-break>あの子を嘘つきにしないためにな。<end>"
EN: "I believe that child. You do too, don't you, <value:$28>? Come, let's go to Phoenix Mountain and find the phoenix. We won't let that child be branded a liar.<end>"
KO: "난 그 아이의 말을 믿어. 너도 믿고 있잖아, <value:$28>. 자, 봉황을 찾으러 봉황산에 가자. 그 아이를 거짓말쟁이로 만들지 않기 위해서.<end>"

## 1230092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は信じるのね。<line-break>鳳凰山に鳳凰がいるってあの子の言葉。<line-break>そうよね、あなたってそういう人よね。<line-break>それに付き合うあたしも、<line-break>そうとうお人好しなのかもね。<end>"
EN: "You believe the child's claim that a phoenix is on Phoenix Mountain, don't you, <value:$28>? Of course you do. That's just the kind of person you are. And perhaps I'm quite the soft touch myself for going along with it.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>는 믿는구나. 봉황산에 봉황이 있다는 그 아이의 말을. 그래, 넌 그런 사람이었지. 거기에 어울리는 나도 꽤나 사람이 좋은가 봐.<end>"

## 1230094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "優しいですね、<value:$28>さん。<line-break>あの子の依頼を引き受けるなんて。<line-break>信じているんですね、あの子の言葉を。<end>"
EN: "You're very kind, <value:$28>, accepting that child's request. You believe what the child said, don't you?<end>"
KO: "상냥하시네요, <value:$28>님. 그 아이의 의뢰를 맡다니. 그 아이의 말을 믿고 계시는군요.<end>"

## 1230096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（エステルに竜骨の砂漠で落とした<line-break>　首飾りを探してくるよう<line-break>　頼まれた）<line-break>（彼女に借りた水晶が導いてくれると<line-break>　言うが本当だろうか…。彼女は、<line-break>　リベルダムのギルドで待っている）<end>"
EN: "(Esther asked you to find a necklace she lost in the Dragonbone Desert.) (She says the crystal she lent you will guide you, but can that really be true? She is waiting at the Guild in Liberdam.)<end>"
KO: "(에스텔에게 용골의 사막에서 잃어버린 목걸이를 찾아 달라는 부탁을 받았다.) (그녀에게 빌린 수정이 이끌어 준다고 하지만 정말일까…. 그녀는 리벨덤 길드에서 기다리고 있다.)<end>"

## 1230097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自分がなくした物を、<line-break>あなたに探しにいかせるなんて<line-break>エステルってどういう娘なの！<line-break>竜骨の砂漠は広いのよ。<line-break>彼女に借りた水晶の力で本当に<line-break>見つけられるのかしら？<end>"
EN: "What kind of girl is Esther, sending you to find something she lost? The Dragonbone Desert is vast. Can the power of the crystal she lent you really lead us to it?<end>"
KO: "자기가 잃어버린 걸 당신에게 찾으러 가게 하다니 에스텔이란 애는 대체 뭐야! 용골의 사막은 넓다고. 그녀에게 빌린 수정의 힘으로 정말 찾을 수 있을까?<end>"

## 1230098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なくした首飾りと、<line-break>今、お主が持っておる水晶が<line-break>引き合うというのじゃな。<line-break>うむ、竜骨の砂漠に行ってみようぞ。<line-break>エステルとやらを信じてな。<end>"
EN: "So the lost necklace and the crystal you now carry are drawn to one another. Very well, let us venture into the Dragonbone Desert and put our faith in this Esther.<end>"
KO: "잃어버린 목걸이와 지금 네가 가진 수정이 서로 끌린다는 거구나. 음, 용골의 사막에 가 보자. 에스텔이라는 아이를 믿고 말이다.<end>"

## 1230099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜骨の砂漠は広いですよ。<line-break>暑いし、迷うし、モンスターは出るし、<line-break>ぜんっぜんいいことないです。<line-break>しかも、ボランティアなんて…。<line-break><value:$28>様はやさしすぎます！<line-break>今後は、きっぱりと断ってくださいね。<end>"
EN: "The Dragonbone Desert is huge. It's hot, you get lost, and there are monsters―there's nothing good about it! And you're doing this for free... You're far too kind, <value:$28>! From now on, you must refuse requests like this firmly.<end>"
KO: "용골의 사막은 넓어요. 덥고, 길도 잃고, 몬스터도 나오고, 정말 좋은 게 하나도 없어요. 게다가 자원봉사라니…. <value:$28>님은 너무 착하세요! 앞으로는 딱 잘라 거절해 주세요.<end>"

## 1230100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんなにでっかい竜骨の砂漠から<line-break>小さな首飾りを探すのかよ！<line-break>お前もお人好しだな…。<line-break>いくら、その水晶と引き合うったって<line-break>あてになんねえぜ。<line-break>うざい仕事だな。<end>"
EN: "You're searching that enormous Dragonbone Desert for one tiny necklace? You're too nice for your own good... Even if the necklace and crystal attract each other, I wouldn't count on it. What an annoying job.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 큰 용골의 사막에서 작은 목걸이를 찾는다고!? 너도 참 착해 빠졌군…. 그 수정과 서로 끌린다지만 믿을 만한지도 모르겠고. 귀찮은 일이네.<end>"

## 1230102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その水晶が首飾りと反応する？<line-break>それなら、エステル自らが<line-break>竜骨の砂漠へ行けばいいわ。<line-break>どうしてあなたが行く必要があるの？<end>"
EN: "The crystal reacts to the necklace? Then Esther should go to the Dragonbone Desert herself. Why do you need to go?<end>"
KO: "그 수정이 목걸이에 반응한다고? 그렇다면 에스텔이 직접 용골 사막으로 가면 되잖아. 왜 당신이 갈 필요가 있어?<end>"

## 1230103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なるほど。<line-break>しかし、そんな便利な水晶があるなら<line-break>自分で行けばいいと思うのですがね。<line-break>きっと、何が何でも、<line-break><value:$28>に依頼したかった<line-break>ということなんでしょうね。ふふっ。<end>"
EN: "...I see. Still, with such a convenient crystal, one would think she could go herself. I suppose she was determined to have <value:$28> do it, no matter what. Heh heh.<end>"
KO: "…그렇군요. 하지만 그런 편리한 수정이 있다면 직접 가면 될 것 같은데요. 분명 무슨 일이 있어도 <value:$28>에게 의뢰하고 싶었던 거겠죠. 후후.<end>"

## 1230104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "めんどくさそーだけど、ちょっと<line-break>ロマンチックなところが、<line-break>あんたの心を動かしたのね～。<line-break>わかるわ、その気持ち。<line-break>化け物退治ばっかりじゃなくって、<line-break>たまにはこういうのも楽しいわ。<end>"
EN: "It sounds like a nuisance, but the touch of romance stirred your heart, didn't it? I understand. It might be fun to do something like this for once instead of always hunting monsters.<end>"
KO: "귀찮아 보이긴 하지만 조금 로맨틱한 점이 네 마음을 움직였구나~. 그 기분 알아. 괴물 퇴치만 하는 것보다 가끔은 이런 것도 재미있지.<end>"

## 1230105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃん本気かよ！<line-break>竜骨の砂漠の広さは知ってるだろ？<line-break>首飾りなんて見つかりっこないよ！<line-break>まったく姉ちゃんのお人好しも<line-break>相当なもんだけど、エステルってのも<line-break>あつかましいな。<end>"
EN: "Are you serious, sis? You know how vast the Dragonbone Desert is! We'll never find a necklace in there! You're far too nice for your own good, but this Esther has some nerve too.<end>"
KO: "누나 진심이야! 용골의 사막이 얼마나 넓은지 알잖아? 목걸이 같은 걸 찾을 수 있을 리 없어! 누나도 상당한 호인이지만 에스텔이라는 사람도 참 뻔뻔하네.<end>"

## 1230106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜骨の砂漠で落とした首飾りを<line-break>探して来い？　…そんな些細な<line-break>依頼まで引き受けるのね。<line-break>冒険者は何でも屋とは違うとは<line-break>思っていたけれど、<line-break>線引きは難しそうね。<end>"
EN: "Find a necklace lost in the Dragonbone Desert? You even take on trivial errands like that. I thought adventurers were different from ordinary errand runners, but perhaps the line is difficult to draw.<end>"
KO: "용골의 사막에서 잃어버린 목걸이를 찾아오라고? …그런 사소한 의뢰까지 맡는구나. 모험자가 심부름꾼은 아니라고 생각했는데 경계가 애매하네.<end>"

## 1230107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へ～、その水晶が首飾りと<line-break>引き合うわけかい。<line-break>なるほど、魔道王国時代の技術だな。<line-break>お前さんの人徳ってやつかな。<line-break>色々な人間が集まってくる。<line-break>エステルってのも普通じゃなさそうだ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, so the crystal and necklace attract each other. I see―technology from the age of the Magic Kingdom. Perhaps it is your strength of character. All sorts of people are drawn to you. This Esther does not seem ordinary either.<end>"
KO: "헤에, 그 수정이 목걸이와 서로 끌어당기는 건가. 역시 마도왕국 시대 기술이군. 네 인덕 덕분인가. 여러 사람이 모여들어. 에스텔이라는 사람도 평범하진 않아 보여.<end>"

## 1230108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前は、広大な砂漠のなかから<line-break>ちっぽけな首飾りを探すと<line-break>いうのか？<line-break>…あきれたお人好しだ。<end>"
EN: "You mean to search a vast desert for one tiny necklace? ...You are astonishingly softhearted.<end>"
KO: "넌 이 광대한 사막에서 조그만 목걸이를 찾겠다는 거냐? …기가 막힐 만큼 착한 사람이군.<end>"

## 1230109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜骨の砂漠は、同じような景色が<line-break>続くので迷いやすいところじゃ。<line-break>目印のつけようもないしのう。<line-break>エステルとやらに借りた、<line-break>その水晶の力を信じるしかないのう。<line-break>しかし、なんで自分で探さんのじゃ？<end>"
EN: "The Dragonbone Desert is easy to get lost in because the scenery all looks the same. There is no way to mark the route, either. We have no choice but to trust the power of that crystal Esther lent you. But why does she not search for it herself?<end>"
KO: "용골의 사막은 비슷한 풍경이 계속돼 길을 잃기 쉬운 곳이야. 표식을 세울 곳도 없고. 에스텔에게 빌린 그 수정의 힘을 믿는 수밖에 없겠군. 그런데 왜 자기가 직접 찾지 않는 거지?<end>"

## 1230110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "首飾りと水晶が引き合うのなら、<line-break>エステルが自分で探してもいいと<line-break>おもうけど…。<line-break>どうしても<value:$28>に<line-break>やってもらわなきゃダメな理由が<line-break>あるのかな？<end>"
EN: "If the necklace and crystal attract each other, Esther could search for it herself... Is there some reason it absolutely has to be <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "목걸이와 수정이 서로 끌린다면 에스텔이 직접 찾아도 될 것 같은데…. 꼭 <value:$28>가 해 줘야 하는 이유가 있나?<end>"

## 1230111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜骨の砂漠に落とした首飾りと<line-break>その水晶が引き合うのか。<line-break>しかし、砂漠は広いぞ…。<end>"
EN: "So the necklace lost in the Dragonbone Desert and that crystal attract each other. Even so, the desert is vast...<end>"
KO: "용골의 사막에 떨어뜨린 목걸이와 그 수정이 서로 이끌린다는 건가. 하지만 사막은 넓어….<end>"

## 1230112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くだらない！　くだらない！<line-break>くだらないわー！<line-break>砂漠で首飾りを探すですって！<line-break>高貴で優雅で知性あふれるエルフの<line-break>アタクシが、砂まみれになりながら<line-break>他人の落とし物を探すわけないでしょ！<end>"
EN: "Ridiculous! Ridiculous! Absolutely ridiculous! Searching a desert for a necklace? Do you honestly think a noble, graceful, and brilliant Elf such as myself would get covered in sand looking for something another person lost?<end>"
KO: "시시해! 시시해! 너무 시시하잖아! 사막에서 목걸이를 찾는다고!? 고귀하고 우아하고 지성 넘치는 엘프인 이 몸이 모래투성이가 되면서 남의 잃어버린 물건을 찾을 리 없잖아!<end>"

## 1230113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "砂漠でサバをさばく！<line-break>エステルの首飾りを<line-break>え！　捨てる？<line-break>猛暑の砂漠をも涼しくする<line-break>拙者のダジャレ、<line-break>いかかでござるかな？<end>"
EN: "Slice mackerel in the desert! Esther's necklace―eh, toss it? How are my puns? Enough to cool even the scorching desert?<end>"
KO: "사막에서 고등어를 손질한다! 에스텔의 목걸이를 어! 버린다고? 폭염의 사막도 시원하게 할 소인의 말장난, 어떠신가?<end>"

## 1230114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜骨の砂漠に行くの？<line-break>あそこはと～っても広いから、<line-break>迷っちゃうし探すの大変だよぉ。<line-break>エステルさんに借りた水晶がないと<line-break>絶対に見つからないもん。<line-break>水晶、なくしちゃダメだからね。<end>"
EN: "You're going to the Dragonbone Desert? It's so enormous that you'll get lost, and searching will be really hard. You'll never find the necklace without the crystal Esther lent you. Make sure you don't lose it!<end>"
KO: "용골의 사막에 가는 거야? 거긴 엄청 넓어서 길을 잃기 쉽고 찾기도 힘들어. 에스텔 씨에게 빌린 수정이 없으면 절대 못 찾아. 수정 잃어버리면 안 돼.<end>"

## 1230115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>興味がない。<end>"
EN: "........... I have no interest.<end>"
KO: "………………. 관심 없어.<end>"

## 1230116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "砂漠で落とした首飾りを探す…<line-break>不可能の比喩かなにかか、それは？<line-break>全く、付き合いきれんな。<end>"
EN: "Searching a desert for a lost necklace... Is that some sort of metaphor for the impossible? Honestly, I cannot be bothered.<end>"
KO: "사막에서 떨어뜨린 목걸이를 찾는다…. 불가능하다는 비유라도 되는 건가? 정말 못 맞춰 주겠군.<end>"

## 1230117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その水晶と首飾りが<line-break>引き合うんだぁ。<line-break>不思議で、いい感じだね。<line-break>離ればなれになっても引き合い<line-break>やがて巡り会う２人…<line-break>まるで恋人みたいだね。<end>"
EN: "So the crystal and necklace are drawn to each other. That's mysterious and rather lovely. Two people drawn together and reunited after being separated... They're just like lovers.<end>"
KO: "그 수정과 목걸이가 서로 끌리는구나. 신기하고 느낌 좋네. 떨어져 있어도 서로 끌려 결국 다시 만나는 두 사람… 꼭 연인 같아.<end>"

## 1230118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜骨の砂漠に落とした首飾りを<line-break>探しに行く？　その水晶が<line-break>首飾りと引き合うというのかね？<line-break>いったい、どんな仕掛けだろうか。<line-break>ひょっとするとこれは…魔道王国の<line-break>失われた技術のひとつかもしれん。<end>"
EN: "You are going to find a necklace lost in the Dragonbone Desert? This crystal is drawn to the necklace? I wonder how it works. Perhaps this is one of the lost technologies of the Magic Kingdom...<end>"
KO: "용골의 사막에 떨어뜨린 목걸이를 찾으러 간다고? 그 수정이 목걸이와 서로 끌어당긴다는 건가? 대체 어떤 장치일까. 어쩌면 이것은… 마도왕국의 잃어버린 기술 중 하나일지도 몰라.<end>"

## 1230119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの広い竜骨の砂漠で首飾りを<line-break>探すだなんて…きっと、誰も依頼を<line-break>受けてくれなかったのでしょう。<line-break>首飾りと引き合うという水晶まで<line-break>渡されてしまっては、<line-break>行かないわけには行きませんね。<end>"
EN: "Searching that vast Dragonbone Desert for a necklace... I imagine no one else would accept the request. And after being entrusted with a crystal that is drawn to the necklace, you can hardly refuse to go.<end>"
KO: "그 넓은 용골 사막에서 목걸이를 찾는다니…. 분명 아무도 의뢰를 맡아 주지 않았겠죠. 목걸이와 끌어당긴다는 수정까지 받았으니 가지 않을 수도 없겠네요.<end>"

## 1230120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へー、水晶が首飾りと引き合うんだ。<line-break>そんな面白い依頼なら、<line-break>ヴィアも連れて行ってよ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, the crystal and necklace attract each other? If the job is that interesting, take Via along too!<end>"
KO: "헤에, 수정이 목걸이와 서로 끌어당기는구나. 그런 재미있는 의뢰라면 비아도 데려가 줘!<end>"

## 1230121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec123-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
