jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "解放軍の本部に密書を届けるだと？<line-break>また、くだらない仕事をやる羽目に<line-break>なったようだな。<end>"
EN: "Deliver a Secret Letter to the Liberation Army headquarters? It seems we've been saddled with another worthless job.<end>"
KO: "해방군 본부에 밀서를 전달한다고? 또 시시한 일을 맡게 된 모양이군.<end>"

## 1240077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイスから預かっておる密書を<line-break>解放軍の本部へ届ければよいのじゃな。<line-break>では、さっそく出かけようではないか。<end>"

## 1240078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "All you have to do is deliver this Secret Letter to the Liberation Army headquarters. That should be easy for <value:$28>, right?<end>"
KO: "이 밀서를 해방군 본부에 전하면 돼. <value:$28>이라면 간단하지?<end>"

## 1240080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "高貴で優雅なエルフのアタクシが、<line-break>うさんくさい解放軍の本部なんて<line-break>知るわけないでしょ！<end>"
EN: "How could a noble and graceful Elf like me possibly know where the headquarters of that shady Liberation Army is?!<end>"

## 1240081

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1240084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイスから依頼されたのは、<line-break>解放軍本部へ密書を届けることだ。<line-break>そつなくこなせ。<end>"
EN: "Khryuseis asked you to deliver the Secret Letter to Liberation Army Headquarters. See it done without incident.<end>"
KO: "크류세이스에게 의뢰받은 건 해방군 본부로 밀서를 전달하는 일이다. 빈틈없이 해내라.<end>"

## 1240085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ！　<value:$28>殿の<line-break>使命は、解放軍本部に密書を<line-break>届けることであります！<line-break>わたくしレルラ＝ロントンも<line-break><value:$28>殿を手助けするため<line-break>同行させていただきます…な～んてね。<end>"
EN: "Aye! <value:$28>'s mission is to deliver the Secret Letter to Liberation Army Headquarters! I, Relra-Lonton, shall accompany <value:$28> and provide assistance... Just kidding.<end>"
KO: "예! <value:$28> 님의 임무는 해방군 본부에 밀서를 전달하는 것입니다! 저 렐라=론톤도 <value:$28> 님을 돕기 위해 동행하겠습니다… 라고 해 보고 싶었지.<end>"

## 1240087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "解放軍本部に密書を運ぶのが<line-break>クリュセイスの依頼です。<line-break>速やかに終わらせてしまいましょう。<line-break><end>"
EN: "Khryuseis has asked us to carry the Secret Letter to Liberation Army Headquarters. Let us finish the task promptly.<end>"
KO: "해방군 본부에 밀서를 운반하는 것이 크류세이스의 의뢰입니다. 신속히 끝내죠.<end>"

## 1240088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "解放軍本部に密書を運ぶのが<line-break>クリュセイスの頼みだよ。<line-break>期限が短いから、急がないとね。<end>"
EN: "Khryuseis wants us to carry the Secret Letter to Liberation Army Headquarters. The deadline is tight, so we'd better hurry.<end>"
KO: "해방군 본부에 밀서를 전달하는 게 크류세이스의 부탁이야. 기한이 짧으니 서둘러야 해.<end>"

## 1240089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "解放軍本部に密書を運ぶのじゃ。<line-break>わしも、おぬしほどの頃には、<line-break>こう、危ない仕事をいろいろとな…<line-break>聞いておるか、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "We must carry the Secret Letter to Liberation Army Headquarters. When I was your age, I had all manner of dangerous jobs myself... Are you listening, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "해방군 본부에 밀서를 운반하는 게야. 나도 네 나이 때는 이런 위험한 일을 이것저것… 듣고 있느냐, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1240090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "解放軍本部に密書を運ぶのだ。<line-break>クリュセイスが指定した期限は<line-break>短い。急ぎ、出発しよう。<end>"
EN: "We must carry the Secret Letter to Liberation Army Headquarters. Khryuseis has set a tight deadline. Let us depart at once.<end>"
KO: "해방군 본부에 밀서를 운반한다. 크류세이스가 정한 기한은 짧아. 서둘러 출발하자.<end>"

## 1240091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "手紙を解放軍本部に届けるのが<line-break>今回の仕事だ。<line-break>さっさと届けてしまおう。<end>"
EN: "Our job this time is to deliver the letter to Liberation Army Headquarters. Let's get it there and be done with it.<end>"
KO: "편지를 해방군 본부에 전하는 게 이번 일이야. 얼른 전해 버리자.<end>"

## 1240092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイスって信用できないわ。<line-break>何か私たちに隠してる気がするのよね。<line-break>女のカンってやつ？<line-break>とりあえず、この手紙は<line-break>解放軍本部に届けましょ。<end>"
EN: "I don't trust Khryuseis. I feel as though she's hiding something from us. Call it woman's intuition. For now, let's deliver this letter to Liberation Army Headquarters.<end>"
KO: "크류세이스는 믿을 수 없어. 뭔가 우리에게 숨기는 것 같단 말이지. 여자의 감이라는 건가? 일단 이 편지는 해방군 본부에 전하자.<end>"

## 1240094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイスさんの手紙を<line-break>解放軍本部に運ぶのが今回の仕事です。<line-break>期限も短いですし急ぎましょう。<end>"
EN: "Our job this time is to carry Khryuseis's letter to Liberation Army Headquarters. The deadline is tight, so let's hurry.<end>"
KO: "크류세이스 씨의 편지를 해방군 본부로 옮기는 게 이번 일이에요. 기한도 짧으니 서두르죠.<end>"

## 1240096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（今度は、この密書をロセンにある<line-break>　解放軍の支部に届けないといけない。<line-break>　寄り道している暇はないな）<end>"
EN: "This time, we have to deliver the Secret Letter to the Liberation Army branch in Rocen. There's no time for detours.<end>"
KO: "(이번엔 이 밀서를 로센에 있는 해방군 지부로 전달해야 한다. 한눈팔 시간은 없어.)<end>"

## 1240097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今度は、この密書を解放軍の<line-break>ロセン支部に届けるのね。<line-break>船で行った方が早いけど船賃あるの？<end>"
EN: "This time, we're delivering the Secret Letter to the Liberation Army's Rocen branch. Going by ship would be faster, but do we have the fare?<end>"
KO: "이번엔 이 밀서를 해방군 로센 지부에 전달하는 거네. 배로 가는 게 빠르겠지만 뱃삯은 있어?<end>"

## 1240098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お次はロセンの解放軍支部じゃぞ。<line-break>わしが解放軍にいることを知れば、<line-break>カルラもおどろくじゃろうな。<end>"
EN: "Next is the Liberation Army branch in Rocen. Karula would be surprised to learn that I am with the Liberation Army.<end>"
KO: "다음은 로센의 해방군 지부로 가는 게야. 내가 해방군에 있는 걸 알면 카를라도 놀라겠지.<end>"

## 1240099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一度ならず二度までも密書の配達を<line-break><value:$28>様に命令するなんて！<line-break>そんな密書、海に捨てちゃいましょう。<end>"
EN: "How dare they order you to deliver a Secret Letter not once, but twice, <value:$28>! Let's throw it into the sea.<end>"
KO: "한 번도 아니고 두 번이나 밀서 배달을 <value:$28>님께 명령하다니! 그런 밀서는 바다에 버려 버려요.<end>"

## 1240100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本部の次はロセン支部かよ。<line-break>そのうち大陸中をたらいまわしに<line-break>されるんじゃないか？<end>"
EN: "After headquarters, it's the Rocen branch? At this rate, they'll send us running all over the continent.<end>"
KO: "본부 다음은 로센 지부냐. 이러다 대륙 전체를 뺑뺑이 돌게 되는 거 아냐?<end>"

## 1240101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "次は解放軍ロセン支部に届けるんだよ。<line-break>キミは頼りにされてるんだから、<line-break>文句言わずにがんばらないとね。<end>"
EN: "Next, you have to deliver it to the Rocen Branch of the Liberation Army. People are counting on you, so quit complaining and do your best.<end>"
KO: "다음은 해방군 로센 지부에 전달하는 거야. 넌 신뢰받고 있으니 불평하지 말고 힘내야 해.<end>"

## 1240102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "次の目的地はロセン。<line-break>そこに解放軍の支部があるらしいわ。<line-break>早いところ、片付けるわよ。<end>"
EN: "Our next destination is Rocen. Apparently, the Liberation Army has a branch there. Let's get this over with.<end>"
KO: "다음 목적지는 로센. 거기에 해방군 지부가 있대. 빨리 끝내 버리자.<end>"

## 1240103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "次なる目的地はロセンにある<line-break>解放軍の支部ですね。<line-break>寄り道している暇はなさそうです。<end>"
EN: "Our next destination is the Liberation Army branch in Rocen. It seems we have no time for detours.<end>"
KO: "다음 목적지는 로센에 있는 해방군 지부군요. 딴길로 샐 여유는 없어 보입니다.<end>"

## 1240105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃん、次は解放軍ロセン支部だぜ。<line-break>船で行くのがいいと思うけど、<line-break>船賃あったかな？<end>"
EN: "Sis, the Rocen Branch of the Liberation Army is next. I think we should go by ship, but do we have enough for the fare?<end>"
KO: "누나, 다음은 해방군 로센 지부야. 배로 가는 게 좋을 것 같은데 배삯은 있나?<end>"

## 1240106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "次はロセンの支部が目的地ね。<line-break>私たちの知らない水面下で、<line-break>彼らは活動を続けていたのね…。<end>"
EN: "Our next destination is the Rocen branch. So they have continued operating behind the scenes without our knowledge...<end>"
KO: "다음 목적지는 로센 지부네. 우리가 모르는 물밑에서 그들은 계속 활동하고 있었던 거구나….<end>"

## 1240107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんの次の任務は、<line-break>この密書を解放軍のロセン支部に<line-break>届けることだぜ。<end>"
EN: "Your next mission is to deliver this Secret Letter to the Liberation Army's Rocen branch.<end>"
KO: "자네의 다음 임무는 이 밀서를 해방군 로센 지부에 전달하는 거야.<end>"

## 1240108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "次は解放軍のロセン支部だ。<line-break>密書など、さっさと運んでしまえ。<line-break>無駄な時間を過ごす余裕はないのだぞ。<end>"
EN: "Next is the Liberation Army's Rocen branch. Deliver that Secret Letter and be done with it. We cannot afford to waste time.<end>"
KO: "다음은 해방군 로센 지부다. 밀서 따위는 얼른 운반해 버려. 쓸데없이 시간을 보낼 여유는 없다.<end>"

## 1240109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今度は解放軍のロセン支部とは…。<line-break>解放軍もそうとうな人手不足と見える。<line-break>まったくもって人使いがあらいのう。<end>"
EN: "Now they want us to go to the Liberation Army's Rocen branch... The Liberation Army must be badly short-handed. They truly work people to the bone.<end>"
KO: "이번엔 해방군 로센 지부라니…. 해방군도 상당히 일손이 부족한 모양이군. 정말 사람을 거칠게 부리는구나.<end>"

## 1240110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "次は解放軍ロセン支部だね。<line-break>大事な密書だから、素早く確実に<line-break>届けなくちゃいけないよ。<end>"
EN: "The Rocen Branch of the Liberation Army is next. This is an important Secret Letter, so we need to deliver it quickly and safely.<end>"
KO: "다음은 해방군 로센 지부네. 중요한 밀서니까 빠르고 확실하게 전달해야 해.<end>"

## 1240112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何度も何度も言わせないでよ！<line-break>高貴なエルフのアタクシは、解放軍の<line-break>ロセン支部なんて知らないわよ！<end>"
EN: "Don't make me say it again and again! A noble Elf like me knows nothing about the Liberation Army's Rocen branch!<end>"
KO: "몇 번이나 말하게 하지 마! 고귀한 엘프인 이 몸은 해방군 로센 지부 같은 건 모른다고!<end>"

## 1240113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1240116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "次はロセンの解放軍支部か。<line-break>こんなつまらん仕事は<line-break>早く片付けるに限るな。<end>"
EN: "The Liberation Army branch in Rocen is next? A dull job like this is best finished quickly.<end>"
KO: "다음은 로센 해방군 지부인가. 이런 시시한 일은 빨리 끝내는 게 상책이지.<end>"

## 1240117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "解放軍って人使いあらいんだねぇ。<line-break>今度は解放軍ロセン支部に届けろ<line-break>だなんてひどいよね。<line-break>このあとも雑用をやらせる気なんだよ。<line-break>ねーねー<value:$28>、<line-break>早く幹部に出世してよ、ね。<end>"
EN: "The Liberation Army really works people hard. Telling us to deliver it to the Rocen Branch of the Liberation Army now is just cruel. They'll keep giving us odd jobs after this too. Hey, <value:$28>, hurry up and get promoted to an officer, okay?<end>"
KO: "해방군은 사람을 너무 부려 먹네. 이번엔 해방군 로센 지부로 가져다주라니 너무하잖아. 이 뒤에도 잡일 시킬 생각일 거야. 있잖아 <value:$28>, 빨리 간부로 출세해 줘, 응?<end>"

## 1240119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "次はロセンの解放軍支部です。<line-break>世間への流出を防ぐために<line-break>回覧するというわけですね。<end>"
EN: "Next is the Liberation Army branch in Rocen. So the letter is being circulated internally to keep it from leaking to the public.<end>"
KO: "다음은 로센의 해방군 지부입니다. 세상에 유출되는 걸 막으려고 회람하는 거군요.<end>"

## 1240120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロセンに行かなくちゃね。<line-break>解放軍の支部があるんだって。<line-break>寄り道してる場合じゃないよ。<end>"
EN: "We have to go to Rocen. Apparently, there's a Liberation Army branch there. This is no time for detours.<end>"
KO: "로센에 가야 해. 해방군 지부가 있대. 딴 데 들를 때가 아니야.<end>"

## 1240121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "次の目的地はロセンの解放軍支部だった<line-break>はずじゃ。支部まで作っておるとは、<line-break>なかなか組織力があるのかもしれんな。<end>"
EN: "Our next destination should be the Liberation Army branch in Rocen. Having branches of its own suggests the organization may be quite capable.<end>"
KO: "다음 목적지는 로센의 해방군 지부였을 거다. 지부까지 만들어 놓았다니 꽤 조직력이 있는 모양이구나.<end>"

## 1240122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロセンの解放軍支部が次の目的地だ。<line-break>クリュセイスも、若いのに<line-break>人使いが荒いな。<end>"
EN: "Our next destination is the Liberation Army branch in Rocen. Khryuseis may be young, but she certainly works people hard.<end>"
KO: "로센의 해방군 지부가 다음 목적지다. 크류세이스도 어린데 사람을 꽤 험하게 부리는군.<end>"

## 1240123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今度は解放軍ロセン支部か。<line-break>船で行ったほうが早いかな。<end>"
EN: "The Rocen Branch of the Liberation Army is next? I wonder if going by ship would be faster.<end>"
KO: "이번엔 해방군 로센 지부인가. 배로 가는 게 빠르려나.<end>"

## 1240124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今度は解放軍ロセン支部ね。<line-break>船を使えば陸路より速いはずよ。<line-break>こんな雑用さっさと終わらせましょ。<end>"
EN: "The Rocen Branch of the Liberation Army is next. A ship should be faster than going overland. Let's get this menial task over with.<end>"
KO: "이번엔 해방군 로센 지부네. 배를 쓰면 육로보다 빠를 거야. 이런 잡일은 얼른 끝내자.<end>"

## 1240126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "次はロセンにある解放軍の支部です。<line-break>時間もあまりないみたいですし、<line-break>急いで届けちゃいましょう。<end>"
EN: "Next is the Liberation Army branch in Rocen. It seems we don't have much time, so let's hurry and deliver it.<end>"
KO: "다음은 로센에 있는 해방군 지부예요. 시간도 별로 없는 모양이니 빨리 전해 버리죠.<end>"

## 1240128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（この密書をクリュセイスに届ければ<line-break>　今回の任務は達成かな？<line-break>　急いでクロイス邸へ行かないと…）<end>"
EN: "Will this mission be complete once I deliver the Secret Letter to Khryuseis? I'd better hurry to the Krois residence...<end>"
KO: "(이 밀서를 크류세이스에게 전하면 이번 임무는 끝인가? 서둘러 크로이스 저택으로 가야 해…)<end>"

## 1240129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この密書をクリュセイスに返せば、<line-break>今回の任務は完了ね。<line-break>あと、一息よ。<end>"
EN: "This mission will be complete once we return the Secret Letter to Khryuseis. We're almost there.<end>"
KO: "이 밀서를 크류세이스에게 돌려주면 이번 임무는 완료야. 이제 조금만 더 가면 돼.<end>"

## 1240130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、最後はクリュセイスに、<line-break>密書を返せば任務達成じゃな。<line-break>さっさと終わらせてしまおうぞ。<end>"
EN: "Now, all that remains is to return the Secret Letter to Khryuseis, and the mission will be complete. Let us get it over with.<end>"
KO: "자, 마지막으로 크류세이스에게 밀서를 돌려주면 임무 완료구먼. 얼른 끝내자고.<end>"

## 1240131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんですって！<line-break>結局クリュセイスに返すんですか！<line-break>やっぱりこれ、捨てちゃいましょう。<end>"
EN: "What?! We're returning it to Khryuseis in the end?! We really should just throw it away.<end>"
KO: "뭐라고요! 결국 크류세이스에게 돌려주는 겁니까! 역시 이거 버려 버리죠.<end>"

## 1240132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あとはクリュセイスんとこに、<line-break>密書を返しに行けばいいんだな。<line-break>よし！　がんばろうぜ。<end>"
EN: "All that's left is to go to Khryuseis and return the Secret Letter. All right! Let's do this.<end>"
KO: "이제 크류세이스한테 밀서를 돌려주러 가면 되는 거지. 좋아! 힘내자.<end>"

## 1240133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイスに密書を返せば、<line-break>キミの今回の任務は終了だね。<line-break>さあ、クロイス邸へ急ごう！<end>"
EN: "Your mission will be over once we return the Secret Letter to Khryuseis. Come on, let's hurry to the Krois residence!<end>"
KO: "크류세이스에게 밀서를 돌려주면 이번 임무는 끝이네. 자, 크로이스 저택으로 서두르자!<end>"

## 1240134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これで最後ね。<line-break>クリュセイスの元まで密書を<line-break>持って帰るのよ。<end>"
EN: "This is the last step. Take the Secret Letter back to Khryuseis.<end>"
KO: "이걸로 마지막이네. 크류세이스에게 밀서를 가지고 돌아가.<end>"

## 1240135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "若いのに用心深いですね、<line-break>クリュセイスも。<line-break>リベルダムへ戻りましょう。<end>"
EN: "Khryuseis is cautious despite her youth. Let us return to Liberdam.<end>"
KO: "젊은데도 신중하군요, 크류세이스도. 리벨덤으로 돌아갑시다.<end>"

## 1240137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃん、最後はクロイス邸に<line-break>届けろって命令らしいな。<line-break>船を使ってさっさと行こうぜ。<end>"
EN: "Sis, apparently our last order is to deliver it to the Krois residence. Let's take a ship and get there quickly.<end>"
KO: "누나, 마지막은 크로이스 저택에 전달하라는 명령인가 봐. 배 타고 얼른 가자.<end>"

## 1240138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最後はクロイス邸に持ち帰るのね。<line-break>骨は折れるけれど、念入りでなければ、<line-break>密書とは呼べないわ。<end>"
EN: "Finally, we take it back to the Krois residence. It may be a lot of trouble, but a Secret Letter calls for thorough precautions.<end>"
KO: "마지막에는 크로이스 저택으로 다시 가져가는 거네. 고생스럽지만 이 정도로 철저해야 밀서라고 할 수 있지.<end>"

## 1240139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ～て、最後の届け先はクロイス邸だ。<line-break>ここまでやっといて期限切れなんて<line-break>ことにならないように急がないとな。<end>"
EN: "Well, our final destination is the Krois residence. We'd better hurry so the deadline doesn't expire after we've come this far.<end>"
KO: "자, 마지막 배달지는 크로이스 저택이다. 여기까지 해 놓고 기한을 넘기지 않도록 서둘러야겠군.<end>"

## 1240140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最後は、クロイス邸だ。<line-break>さっさと終わらせて<line-break>冒険に戻るぞ。<end>"
EN: "The Krois residence is the final destination. Let's finish this and get back to adventuring.<end>"
KO: "마지막은 크로이스 저택이다. 빨리 끝내고 모험으로 돌아가자.<end>"

## 1240141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど、最後はクリュセイスに<line-break>返すことで情報伝達の確認と<line-break>密書の確実な処分を行うわけじゃな。<end>"
EN: "I see. Returning it to Khryuseis at the end both confirms that the information was conveyed and ensures that the Secret Letter is disposed of properly.<end>"
KO: "그렇군, 마지막에 크류세이스에게 돌려줘 정보 전달 여부를 확인하고 밀서도 확실히 처분하는 거군.<end>"

## 1240142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いよいよこれで最後だね。<line-break>この密書をクロイス邸に<line-break>持って帰れば任務完了なんだ。<end>"
EN: "This is finally the last step. The mission will be complete once we take this Secret Letter back to the Krois residence.<end>"
KO: "드디어 이걸로 마지막이네. 이 밀서를 크로이스 저택으로 가져가면 임무 완료야.<end>"

## 1240144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう知らない知らない！<line-break>クロイス邸だろうが好きに行ったらぁ、<line-break>アタクシにはぜ～んぜん関係ないわっ！<end>"
EN: "I don't care anymore! Go to the Krois residence or wherever you like. It has absolutely nothing to do with me!<end>"
KO: "몰라 몰라! 크로이스 저택이든 어디든 마음대로 가버려, 나랑은 전~혀 상관없으니까!<end>"

## 1240145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "密書の配達ボゲー<line-break>密書の配達ボゲー<line-break>密書の配達ボゲー<line-break>密書の配達ボゲー<line-break>密書の配達ボゲー<line-break>密書の配達ボゲー<end>"
EN: "Secret Letter delivery, Bogee! Secret Letter delivery, Bogee! Secret Letter delivery, Bogee! Secret Letter delivery, Bogee! Secret Letter delivery, Bogee! Secret Letter delivery, Bogee!<end>"
KO: "밀서 배달 보게 밀서 배달 보게 밀서 배달 보게 밀서 배달 보게 밀서 배달 보게 밀서 배달 보게<end>"

## 1240146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>の最終任務は、<line-break>リベルダムにあるクロイス邸に<line-break>密書を届けることだよぉ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>'s final mission is to deliver the Secret Letter to the Krois residence in Liberdam.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>의 마지막 임무는 리벨덤의 크로이스 저택에 밀서를 전달하는 거야.<end>"

## 1240147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>クリュセイスに届けろ。<end>"
EN: "………………. Deliver it to Khryuseis.<end>"
KO: "………………. 크류세이스에게 전해라.<end>"

## 1240148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイスは、お前がちゃんと<line-break>支部へ行ったか確認するつもりだ。<line-break>さっさと終わらせるぞ。<end>"
EN: "Khryuseis intends to confirm that you actually visited the branch. Let's get this over with.<end>"
KO: "크류세이스는 네가 제대로 지부에 갔는지 확인할 생각이야. 빨리 끝내자.<end>"

## 1240149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最後のお届け先はクロイス邸かぁ。<line-break>ねえ<value:$28>、ここまできて<line-break>期限切れになるようなヘマしないでよ。<end>"
EN: "So the final delivery is to the Krois residence. Listen, <value:$28>, don't make some stupid blunder and miss the deadline after coming this far.<end>"
KO: "마지막 배달지는 크로이스 저택인가. 저기 <value:$28>, 여기까지 와서 기한을 넘기는 실수는 하지 마.<end>"

## 1240151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最後の目的地はクロイス邸です。<line-break>クリュセイス自ら、最終確認を<line-break>行うつもりなのでしょう。<end>"
EN: "Our final destination is the Krois residence. Khryuseis presumably intends to perform the final check herself.<end>"
KO: "마지막 목적지는 크로이스 저택입니다. 크류세이스가 직접 최종 확인을 할 생각인 모양입니다.<end>"

## 1240152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これで最後だよ。お屋敷で<line-break>クリュセイスのチェックしてもらえば<line-break>依頼はおしまいだよ。<end>"
EN: "This is the last step. Once Khryuseis checks it at the residence, the job will be over.<end>"
KO: "이제 마지막이야. 저택에서 크류세이스의 확인을 받으면 의뢰는 끝나.<end>"

## 1240153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たらい回しじゃ…と思ったら、<line-break>クロイス邸へと帰るのか。<line-break>やっと肩の荷が下りるわい。<end>"
EN: "I thought we were just being sent from place to place... but now we're returning to the Krois residence. At last, we'll have this burden off our shoulders.<end>"
KO: "이리저리 돌리는군… 싶었는데 크로이스 저택으로 돌아가는 건가. 드디어 짐을 내려놓겠구나.<end>"

## 1240154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最後はクロイス邸へ戻れと言うのだな。<line-break>ここで期限切れになれば、<line-break>全てが水の泡だ。急いで行こう。<end>"
EN: "So our last instruction is to return to the Krois residence. If the deadline expires now, all our work will have been for nothing. Let us hurry.<end>"
KO: "마지막은 크로이스 저택으로 돌아가라는 거군. 여기서 기한이 지나면 전부 물거품이다. 서두르자.<end>"

## 1240155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最後はクリュセイスに返す…。 <line-break>情報の流出を防ぐべく<line-break>よくよく考えての回覧か。<line-break>なかなかの切れ者だな、 <line-break>クリュセイスは…。<end>"
EN: "So it goes back to Khryuseis in the end... The whole circulation route was carefully planned to prevent the information from leaking. Khryuseis is quite shrewd...<end>"
KO: "마지막은 크류세이스에게 돌려준다…. 정보 유출을 막으려고 잘 생각해서 돌려본 건가. 꽤 머리가 좋군, 크류세이스는….<end>"

## 1240156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これでやっとおしまいね。<line-break>期限が切れないうちに急いで<line-break>クリュセイスに密書を渡さないとね。<end>"
EN: "At last, this is the end. We need to hurry and give the Secret Letter to Khryuseis before the deadline expires.<end>"
KO: "이제야 끝이네. 기한이 지나기 전에 서둘러 크류세이스에게 밀서를 건네야 해.<end>"

## 1240158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あとはクリュセイスさんに手紙を返せば<line-break>おしまいです。<line-break><line-break>なんだかいろんなところに届けて<line-break>疲れちゃいましたね。<end>"
EN: "All that remains is to return the letter to Khryuseis. Delivering it to so many different places has been rather tiring, hasn't it?<end>"
KO: "이제 크류세이스 씨에게 편지를 돌려주면 끝이에요. 여기저기 배달하느라 꽤 지쳤네요.<end>"

## 1240160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（邪竜アズラゴーザが封じられている<line-break>　邪竜の断層から、強い妖気が<line-break>　放たれているらしい）<line-break>（調査のためオルファウスを<line-break>　連れていくことになった。<line-break>　嫌な予感がする。急ごう…）<end>"
EN: "A powerful evil aura is apparently emanating from the Evil Dragon Rift, where the Evil Dragon Azuragoza is sealed. I'll be taking Orphaus there to investigate. I have a bad feeling about this. I should hurry...<end>"
KO: "(사룡 아즈라고자가 봉인된 사룡의 단층에서 강한 요기가 뿜어져 나오는 모양이다.) (조사를 위해 오르파우스를 데려가게 됐다. 불길한 예감이 든다. 서두르자….)<end>"

## 1240161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスを連れて<line-break>邪竜の断層へ行けばいいのね。<line-break>急ぎましょ、嫌な予感がするの。<end>"
EN: "We need to take Orphaus to the Evil Dragon Rift. Let's hurry. I have a bad feeling about this.<end>"
KO: "오르파우스를 데리고 사룡의 단층으로 가면 되는 거네. 서두르자, 안 좋은 예감이 들어.<end>"

## 1240162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウス殿が邪竜の断層から<line-break>強い妖気を感じるというのじゃな。<line-break>では、急がねばいかんな。<line-break>もしや、邪竜アズラゴーザの封印が<line-break>弱まっているのかもしれん。<end>"
EN: "Orphaus senses a powerful evil aura coming from the Evil Dragon Rift, does he? Then we must hurry. Perhaps the seal on the Evil Dragon Azuragoza is weakening.<end>"
KO: "오르파우스 공이 사룡의 단층에서 강한 요기를 느낀다고 했지. 그럼 서둘러야겠군. 혹시 사룡 아즈라고자의 봉인이 약해지고 있는 걸지도 몰라.<end>"

## 1240163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>オルファウス様が邪竜の断層へ<line-break>連れていって欲しいそうですよ。<line-break>断層から強い妖気を感じるらしいです。<line-break>もちろん私は、ぜんぜんまったく<line-break>感じませんけど。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, it seems Orphaus wants you to take him to the Evil Dragon Rift. Apparently, he senses a powerful evil aura coming from the rift. I, of course, feel absolutely nothing at all.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님, 오르파우스 님이 사룡의 단층으로 데려가 달라고 하시네요. 단층에서 강한 요기를 느끼신대요. 물론 저는 전혀 아무것도 못 느끼지만요.<end>"

## 1240164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスの話は嘘っぽいのに<line-break>限って本当のことだからな。<line-break>今回のは本当くさいぜ。<line-break>邪竜アズラゴーザなんて知らないが、<line-break>そいつが復活しちまうと<line-break>ヤバイ事になるくらいはわかるぜ。<end>"
EN: "Whenever Orphaus says something that sounds like a lie, it turns out to be true. This time, it sounds true. I don't know anything about an Evil Dragon called Azuragoza, but even I understand that its revival would be bad news.<end>"
KO: "오르파우스 얘기는 거짓말 같은 것만 진짜니까. 이번 건 진짜 같아. 사룡 아즈라고자 따윈 모르지만 그게 부활하면 큰일 난다는 정도는 알겠어.<end>"

## 1240165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボクもオルファウスさんの言う<line-break>妖気みたいなものは感じるよ。<line-break>急いだほうがいいかもしれない…。<line-break>邪竜の断層で何かが起こりつつ<line-break>あるのは間違いないと思う。<end>"
EN: "I can feel something like the evil aura Orphaus mentioned too. We may need to hurry... I'm certain something is beginning to happen at the Evil Dragon Rift.<end>"
KO: "나도 올파우스 씨가 말한 요기 같은 건 느껴져. 서두르는 게 좋을지도 몰라… 사룡의 단층에서 뭔가 일어나고 있는 건 틀림없어.<end>"

## 1240166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アズラゴーザが封じられている<line-break>邪竜の断層から、強い妖気が<line-break>放たれているわ。<line-break>オルファウスに同行して<line-break>その真相を確かめるのよ。<end>"
EN: "A powerful evil aura is emanating from the Evil Dragon Rift, where Azuragoza is sealed. Accompany Orphaus and uncover the truth.<end>"
KO: "아즈라고자가 봉인된 사룡의 단층에서 강한 요기가 뿜어져 나오고 있어. 오르파우스와 동행해서 진상을 확인해.<end>"

## 1240167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪竜の断層へ急いでください！<line-break>お願いします<value:$28>。<line-break>時間がないんです。<end>"
EN: "Hurry to the Evil Dragon Rift! Please, <value:$28>. There is no time.<end>"
KO: "사룡의 단층으로 서둘러 주세요! 부탁해요, <value:$28>. 시간이 없어요.<end>"

## 1240168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたしには邪竜アズラゴーザだの<line-break>妖気だのってサッパリわかんないけど<line-break>とりあえず行ってみれば？<line-break>邪竜が復活したりすると、<line-break>世界中大騒ぎになって困るじゃない。<end>"
EN: "I don't understand any of this talk about an Evil Dragon called Azuragoza or an evil aura, but why not go and see? If the Evil Dragon comes back, the whole world will be in an uproar, and that would be a nuisance.<end>"
KO: "난 사룡 아즈라고자니 요기니 하는 건 잘 모르겠지만, 일단 가 보면 어때? 사룡이 부활하면 세상이 난리가 나서 곤란하잖아.<end>"

## 1240169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺、妖気とか全然わかんないけど<line-break>オルファウスの話、信じるぜ。<line-break>姉ちゃんだってそうだろ？<line-break>邪竜の断層でとんでもない事が<line-break>起こっているかもしれないんだ。<line-break>急ごうぜ、姉ちゃん！<end>"
EN: "I don't understand evil auras at all, but I believe Orphaus. You do too, don't you, Sis? Something terrible may be happening at the Evil Dragon Rift. Let's hurry, Sis!<end>"
KO: "난 요기 같은 건 전혀 모르지만 오르파우스의 말은 믿어. 누나도 그렇지? 사룡의 단층에서 엄청난 일이 벌어지고 있을지도 몰라. 서두르자, 누나!<end>"

## 1240170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私もオルファウス同様、<line-break>邪竜の断層から強い妖気を感じます。<line-break>調べてみる必要はあると思うわ。<line-break>もし、邪竜アズラゴーザの封印が<line-break>弱まっているのだとしたら、<line-break>なんとしても封印し直さないと…。<end>"
EN: "Like Orphaus, I sense a powerful evil aura coming from the Evil Dragon Rift. I think we need to investigate. If the seal on the Evil Dragon Azuragoza is weakening, we must reseal it at any cost...<end>"
KO: "나도 오르파우스와 마찬가지로 사룡의 단층에서 강한 요기를 느껴. 조사할 필요가 있다고 생각해. 만약 사룡 아즈라고자의 봉인이 약해지고 있다면 반드시 다시 봉인해야 해….<end>"

## 1240172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪竜の断層から強い妖気か…<line-break>そうだな、オルファウスを連れて<line-break>調査に行ってみるべきだ。<line-break>あの男は、ただ者ではない。<line-break>俺たちの知らない何かを<line-break>つかんでいるのかもしれん。<end>"
EN: "A powerful evil aura from the Evil Dragon Rift... Yes, we should take Orphaus there and investigate. He is no ordinary man. Perhaps he has learned something we do not know.<end>"
KO: "사룡의 단층에서 강한 요기라…. 그래, 오르파우스를 데리고 조사하러 가 봐야겠어. 그 남자는 보통 사람이 아니야. 우리가 모르는 뭔가를 알고 있을지도 몰라.<end>"

## 1240173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウス殿を連れて<line-break>邪竜の断層へ調査に行くのじゃな。<line-break>それなら、わしも連れていけい。<line-break>邪竜アズラゴーザとやらを<line-break>見ることができるかもしれんからな。<end>"
EN: "So we are taking Orphaus to investigate the Evil Dragon Rift. Then take me along as well. I may get to see this Evil Dragon called Azuragoza.<end>"
KO: "올파우스 님을 데리고 사룡의 단층을 조사하러 가는 거구나. 그렇다면 나도 데려가게. 사룡 아즈라고자라는 녀석을 볼 수 있을지도 모르니.<end>"

## 1240174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスさんが、邪竜の断層から<line-break>強い妖気を感じるらしいんだ。<line-break><value:$28>も聞いたんでしょ？<line-break>今のオルファウスさんだと、<line-break>邪竜の断層まで行けないからね。<line-break>僕らが連れていってあげないと。<end>"
EN: "Apparently, Orphaus senses a powerful evil aura coming from the Evil Dragon Rift. You heard about it too, didn't you, <value:$28>? Orphaus can't reach the rift in his present condition. We'll have to take him there.<end>"
KO: "오르파우스 씨가 사룡의 단층에서 강한 요기를 느끼는 모양이야. <value:$28>도 들었지? 지금 오르파우스 씨는 사룡의 단층까지 갈 수 없으니까 우리가 데려가 줘야 해.<end>"

## 1240175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪竜の断層から強い妖気が…。<line-break>封じられたアズラゴーザが<line-break>覚醒したのでなければ良いが…。<end>"
EN: "A powerful evil aura is coming from the Evil Dragon Rift... Let us hope the sealed Azuragoza has not awakened...<end>"
KO: "사룡의 단층에서 강한 요기가…. 봉인된 아즈라고자가 깨어난 게 아니면 좋겠는데….<end>"

## 1240176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪竜の断層ってアズラゴーザが<line-break>封印されている場所でしょ。<line-break>もちろん、知っているわ。<line-break>アタクシは高貴で知性あふれる<line-break>エルフなんですから。<end>"
EN: "The Evil Dragon Rift is where Azuragoza is sealed, isn't it? Of course I knew that. I am a noble and intelligent Elf, after all.<end>"
KO: "사룡의 단층은 아즈라고자가 봉인된 곳이잖아. 물론 알고 있어. 난 고귀하고 지성 넘치는 엘프니까.<end>"

## 1240177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪竜の断層へボゲー<line-break>邪竜の断層へボゲー<line-break>邪竜の断層へボゲー<line-break>邪竜の断層へボゲー<line-break>邪竜の断層へボゲー<line-break>邪竜の断層へボゲー<end>"
EN: "To the Evil Dragon Rift, Bogee! To the Evil Dragon Rift, Bogee! To the Evil Dragon Rift, Bogee! To the Evil Dragon Rift, Bogee! To the Evil Dragon Rift, Bogee! To the Evil Dragon Rift, Bogee!<end>"
KO: "사룡의 단층으로 보게 사룡의 단층으로 보게 사룡의 단층으로 보게 사룡의 단층으로 보게 사룡의 단층으로 보게 사룡의 단층으로 보게<end>"

## 1240178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスさんがね、<line-break>邪竜の断層に連れていって<line-break>欲しいみたいだよぉ。<line-break>お願い、<value:$28>。<line-break>オルファウスさんを<line-break>連れていってあげてよぉ。<end>"
EN: "It seems Orphaus wants to be taken to the Evil Dragon Rift. Please, <value:$28>. Take Orphaus there.<end>"
KO: "오르파우스 씨가 말이야, 사룡의 단층에 데려가 달래. 부탁이야, <value:$28>. 오르파우스 씨를 데려가 줘.<end>"

## 1240179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>邪竜の断層へ行け。<line-break>何かが起ころうとしている。<end>"
EN: "………………. Go to the Evil Dragon Rift. Something is about to happen.<end>"
KO: "………………. 사룡의 단층으로 가라. 무언가 일어나려 하고 있다.<end>"

## 1240180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスの頼みだ、聞いてやれ。<line-break>邪竜の断層で何が起きているのか、<line-break>調べておくのも悪くないだろう。<line-break>ここで何もしないで、<line-break>問題が起きてから後悔するのは<line-break>つまらんからな。<end>"
EN: "Orphaus has asked for your help. Do as he asks. It would be wise to investigate what is happening at the Evil Dragon Rift. Doing nothing now and regretting it once trouble starts would be pointless.<end>"
KO: "오르파우스의 부탁이다. 들어줘. 사룡의 단층에서 무슨 일이 일어나는지 조사해 두는 것도 나쁘지 않겠지. 아무것도 안 하고 있다가 문제가 생긴 뒤 후회하는 건 시시하니까.<end>"

## 1240181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪竜の断層にはねぇ、<line-break>アズラゴーザが眠っているんだ。<line-break>もし、邪竜が復活したら大騒ぎだよ。<line-break>そうなるとボクも困るから、<line-break><value:$28>、がんばってよね。<line-break>世界の平和を守るんだ！<end>"
EN: "Azuragoza is sleeping in the Evil Dragon Rift. If the Evil Dragon revives, it will cause an enormous uproar. That would be a problem for me too, so do your best, <value:$28>. Protect world peace!<end>"
KO: "사룡의 단층에는 아즈라고자가 잠들어 있어. 만약 사룡이 부활하면 큰일이야. 그러면 나도 곤란하니까 <value:$28>, 힘내. 세계의 평화를 지키는 거야!<end>"

## 1240182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウス殿の言葉に間違いは<line-break>ないと私は思うが、邪竜の復活だけは<line-break>信じたくはないものだ。<line-break>早急にこの目で確かめなくては。<end>"
EN: "I believe Orphaus is correct, but I do not want to believe that the Evil Dragon is reviving. We must see the truth for ourselves at once.<end>"
KO: "오르파우스 공의 말에 틀림은 없다고 생각하지만, 사룡의 부활만큼은 믿고 싶지 않군. 서둘러 내 눈으로 확인해야겠어.<end>"

## 1240183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賢者様のおっしゃることに<line-break>従いましょう。断層に封じられし<line-break>邪竜が目覚めたのだとすれば…<line-break>到底、私たちだけの手に負える<line-break>ものではありません。<end>"
EN: "Let us follow the sage's counsel. If the Evil Dragon sealed in the rift has awakened... it is far beyond our ability to handle alone.<end>"
KO: "현자님의 말씀을 따르죠. 단층에 봉인된 사룡이 깨어난 것이라면… 도저히 우리만으로 감당할 수 없습니다.<end>"

## 1240184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賢者オルファウス様と<line-break>ご一緒できるなんてすごいよ！<line-break>もう邪竜だってこわくないよね！<end>"
EN: "It's amazing that we get to travel with the sage Orphaus! We don't have to be afraid of the Evil Dragon anymore, right?<end>"
KO: "현자 오르파우스 님과 함께 갈 수 있다니 대단해! 이제 사룡도 무섭지 않겠지!<end>"

## 1240185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪竜の断層から強い妖気とな…。<line-break>気のせいと思いたいが、<line-break>このイオンズにも嫌な予感がしよる。<line-break>邪竜の復活だけは阻止せねば！<line-break>ゆくぞ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "A powerful evil aura from the Evil Dragon Rift... I wish it were only imagination, but even Ions has a bad feeling about this. We must prevent the Evil Dragon's revival! Let us go, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "사룡의 단층에서 강한 요기가 느껴진다고…. 기분 탓이고 싶지만 이 이온즈도 불길한 예감이 든다. 사룡의 부활만은 막아야 한다! 가자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1240186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪竜の断層には、その名の通り<line-break>アズラゴーザという邪竜が封じられて<line-break>いると聞く。<line-break>それが妖気の根源とは考えたくないが<line-break>賢者様の急ぎようはただ事では<line-break>なさそうだな…。<end>"
EN: "True to its name, the Evil Dragon Rift is said to hold an Evil Dragon called Azuragoza. I do not want to believe it is the source of the evil aura, but the sage's urgency suggests this is no ordinary matter...<end>"
KO: "사룡의 단층에는 이름 그대로 아즈라고자라는 사룡이 봉인되어 있다고 들었어. 그게 요기의 근원이라고 생각하고 싶진 않지만 현자님의 서두르는 모습은 보통 일이 아닌 것 같군….<end>"

## 1240187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "強い妖気が邪竜の断層から…。<line-break>オルファウスのあの様子からして<line-break>一分一秒を争う事態らしい。<line-break>邪竜の復活は阻止しなければ。<line-break>オルファウスを連れて、急ぐぞ！<end>"
EN: "A powerful evil aura is coming from the Evil Dragon Rift... Judging by Orphaus's manner, every second counts. We must prevent the Evil Dragon's revival. Take Orphaus and hurry!<end>"
KO: "강한 요기가 사룡의 단층에서… 올파우스의 저 모습을 보면 일분일초를 다투는 사태인 것 같아. 사룡의 부활은 막아야 해. 올파우스를 데리고 서두르자!<end>"

## 1240188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この妖気は、邪竜の断層からね。<line-break>もし邪竜が復活したら<line-break>大変なことになるわ。<line-break>急いでオルファウスを連れて<line-break>調査に向かったほうがいいわね。<end>"
EN: "This evil aura is coming from the Evil Dragon Rift. If the Evil Dragon revives, we'll be in terrible trouble. We'd better take Orphaus and hurry there to investigate.<end>"
KO: "이 요기는 사룡의 단층에서 오는 거야. 사룡이 부활하면 큰일이야. 서둘러 오르파우스를 데리고 조사하러 가는 게 좋겠어.<end>"

## 1240189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪竜の断層から、<line-break>強い妖気を感じます。<line-break><line-break>封印されたアズラゴーザが<line-break>覚醒したのかもしれません。<end>"
EN: "I sense a powerful evil aura coming from the Evil Dragon Rift. The sealed Azuragoza may have awakened.<end>"
KO: "사룡의 단층에서, 강한 요기를 느낍니다. 봉인된 아즈라고자가 깨어났을지도 모릅니다.<end>"

## 1240190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は何も感じませんけど、<line-break>オルファウスさんがそう言うなら<line-break>急がないといけないですね。<line-break>邪竜アズラゴーザが目覚めないうちに<line-break>オルファウスさんを邪竜の断層に<line-break>連れていきましょう。<end>"
EN: "I don't sense anything, but if Orphaus says so, we must hurry. Let's take Orphaus to the Evil Dragon Rift before the Evil Dragon Azuragoza awakens.<end>"
KO: "저는 아무것도 못 느끼지만 오르파우스 씨가 그렇게 말한다면 서둘러야겠네요. 사룡 아즈라고자가 깨어나기 전에 오르파우스 씨를 사룡의 단층으로 데려가죠.<end>"

## 1240191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この気配は<line-break>邪竜の断層に封印されている<line-break>アズラゴーザのものだ。<line-break>覚醒は時間の問題だ。<line-break>復活したら手に負えなくなる。<line-break>止めるなら今しかない。<end>"
EN: "This is Azuragoza's presence, coming from where it is sealed in the Evil Dragon Rift. Its awakening is only a matter of time. Once it revives, it will be beyond our control. If we are to stop it, now is our only chance.<end>"
KO: "이 기척은 사룡의 단층에 봉인된 아즈라고자의 것이다. 각성은 시간문제다. 부활하면 감당할 수 없게 된다. 막으려면 지금뿐이야.<end>"

## 1240192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（オルファウスが、古の樹海で<line-break>　世界の成り立ちを話してくれる。<line-break>　古の樹海へ急ごう）<end>"
EN: "Orphaus will tell us how the world came to be in the Ancient Forest. Let's hurry there.<end>"
KO: "(오르파우스가 고대의 수해에서 세계가 어떻게 생겨났는지 이야기해 준다. 고대의 수해로 서두르자.)<end>"

## 1240193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "古の樹海へ行きましょう。<line-break>オルファウスが世界の成り立ちを<line-break>教えてくれるらしいわ。<end>"
EN: "Let's go to the Ancient Forest. Apparently, Orphaus will explain how the world came to be.<end>"
KO: "고대의 수해로 가자. 오르파우스가 세계가 어떻게 생겨났는지 알려 준대.<end>"

## 1240194
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウス殿が世界の成り立ちを<line-break>教えてくれるそうじゃ。<line-break>古の樹海へ急ぐとしよう。<end>"
EN: "Orphaus is going to explain how the world came to be. Let us hurry to the Ancient Forest.<end>"
KO: "오르파우스 님이 세계가 어떻게 만들어졌는지 알려 주신다더군. 고대의 수해로 서두르자.<end>"

## 1240195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウス様が、古の樹海で<line-break>世界の成り立ちを教えてくれる<line-break>そうです！　楽しみかも！<end>"
EN: "Orphaus is going to explain how the world came to be in the Ancient Forest! I might actually be looking forward to this!<end>"
KO: "오르파우스 님이 고대의 수해에서 세계가 어떻게 생겨났는지 알려 준대요! 기대될지도!<end>"

## 1240196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "古の樹海で、オルファウスが<line-break>世界の成り立ちを教えてくれる<line-break>らしい。行こうぜ！<end>"
EN: "Apparently, Orphaus will explain how the world came to be in the Ancient Forest. Let's go!<end>"
KO: "태고의 수해에서 오르파우스가 세계의 성립을 알려 준대. 가자!<end>"

## 1240197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "古の樹海で、オルファウス様が<line-break>世界の成り立ちについて教えて<line-break>くれるんだ！　行こう！<end>"
EN: "Orphaus is going to explain how the world came to be in the Ancient Forest! Let's go!<end>"
KO: "고대의 수해에서 오르파우스 님이 세계의 성립에 대해 알려 주신대! 가자!<end>"

## 1240198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスは、古の樹海で<line-break>世界の成り立ちについて教えて<line-break>くれるそうよ。行くわよ！<end>"
EN: "Orphaus is going to explain how the world came to be in the Ancient Forest. Let's go!<end>"
KO: "오르파우스는 고대의 수해에서 세계의 성립에 대해 알려 준대. 가자!<end>"

## 1240199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "古の樹海へ急いでください。<line-break>そこで世界の成り立ちについて<line-break>お話しましょう。<end>"
EN: "Please hurry to the Ancient Forest. There, I shall tell you how the world came to be.<end>"
KO: "고대의 수해로 서둘러 와 주세요. 그곳에서 세계가 어떻게 만들어졌는지 이야기하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1240200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスは古の樹海で<line-break>世界の成り立ちについて話して<line-break>くれるらしいわよ。<end>"
EN: "Apparently, Orphaus will tell us how the world came to be in the Ancient Forest.<end>"
KO: "오르파우스는 고대의 수해에서 세계가 어떻게 만들어졌는지 이야기해 줄 거래.<end>"

## 1240201
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃん、オルファウスさんは、<line-break>古の樹海で、世界の成り立ちを<line-break>話してくれるって言ったんだぜ！<end>"
EN: "Sis, Orphaus said he'd tell us how the world came to be in the Ancient Forest!<end>"
KO: "누나, 오르파우스 씨가 고대의 수해에서 세계가 어떻게 생겨났는지 이야기해 준다고 했잖아!<end>"

## 1240202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "古の樹海へ行くのよ。<line-break>オルファウス様がそこで世界の<line-break>成り立ちについてお話してくださるわ。<end>"
EN: "We're going to the Ancient Forest. Orphaus will tell us there how the world came to be.<end>"
KO: "고대의 수해로 가는 거야. 오르파우스 님이 그곳에서 세계의 성립에 대해 이야기해 주실 거야.<end>"

## 1240203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "古の樹海へ急ぐとしようぜ。<line-break>オルファウスはそこで世界の成り立ちに<line-break>ついて話してくれるらしい。<end>"
EN: "Let's hurry to the Ancient Forest. Apparently, Orphaus will tell us how the world came to be once we're there.<end>"
KO: "고대의 수해로 서두르자고. 오르파우스가 거기서 세계의 성립에 대해 이야기해 준다는군.<end>"

## 1240204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "古の樹海へ行け。世界の成り立ちに関わる<line-break>という、オルファウスの話には<line-break>俺も興味がある。<end>"
EN: "Go to the Ancient Forest. I too am interested in what Orphaus has to say about how the world came to be.<end>"
KO: "고대의 수해로 가. 세계의 성립과 관련 있다는 오르파우스의 이야기는 나도 흥미가 있어.<end>"

## 1240205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウス殿は、古の樹海で、<line-break>世界の成り立ちについて語って<line-break>くださるそうじゃな。<end>"
EN: "Orphaus is going to tell us how the world came to be in the Ancient Forest.<end>"
KO: "오르파우스 님께서 고대의 수해에서 세계의 성립에 대해 말씀해 주신다고 했지.<end>"

## 1240206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "古の樹海へ急ごう。オルファウスさんは<line-break>そこで世界の成り立ちについて話して<line-break>くれるって言ったよね！<end>"
EN: "Let's hurry to the Ancient Forest. Orphaus said he'd tell us how the world came to be once we're there, remember?<end>"
KO: "고대의 수해로 서두르자. 오르파우스 씨가 거기서 세계의 기원에 대해 이야기해 준다고 했지!<end>"

## 1240207
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスは、古の樹海で<line-break>世界の成り立ちについて語るつもり<line-break>らしいな。…急ぐのだ。<end>"
EN: "It seems Orphaus intends to speak about the origins of the world in the Ancient Forest. We must hurry.<end>"
KO: "오르파우스는 고대의 수해에서 세계의 성립에 대해 이야기할 생각인 모양이군. …서둘러라.<end>"

## 1240208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界の成り立ちよ！　賢者様は、<line-break>古の樹海でそれをお話しくださるのよ！<line-break>やっと、ちょっと面白くなってきたわ！<end>"
EN: "The origins of the world! The Sage is going to tell us all about it in the Ancient Forest! Finally, things are getting a little interesting!<end>"
KO: "세계의 성립 말이야! 현자님이 고대 수해에서 그 이야기를 해 주신대! 드디어 좀 재미있어지기 시작했네!<end>"

## 1240209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "古の樹海へボゲー<line-break>古の樹海へボゲー<line-break>古の樹海へボゲー<line-break>古の樹海へボゲー<line-break>古の樹海へボゲー<line-break>古の樹海へボゲー<end>"

## 1240210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "古の樹海へ行くんだよぉ。賢者様が、<line-break>そこで世界の成り立ちについて<line-break>おはなししてくれるんだってぇ。<end>"

## 1240211

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1240212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1240213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "古の樹海へ急ごう！　賢者様が<line-break>世界の成り立ちを語ってくれるんだ。<line-break>壮大だね！　ワクワクするよ！<end>"

## 1240214

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1240215

## 1240217

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1240218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1240219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1240220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "古の樹海でオルファウスが<line-break>世界の成り立ちについて<line-break>語ってくれるらしいわ。<line-break>急いでオルファウスを連れて<line-break>向かったほうがいいわね。<end>"

## 1240221
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "古の樹海で、オルファウスが<line-break>世界の成り立ちを<line-break>教えてくれるそうです。<end>"

KO: "고대의 수해에서 올파우스가 세계의 성립을 알려 준다고 합니다.<end>"

## 1240222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスさんが世界の成り立ちを<line-break>古の樹海で教えてくれるそうですよ。<line-break>すごいですね、世界の成り立ちなんて。<end>"

## 1240223
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec124-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスが世界の成り立ちを<line-break>古の樹海で語るつもりらしい。<line-break><line-break><value:$28>はこの話を、<line-break>聞いておいたほうがいいね。<end>"
EN: "It seems Orphaus intends to speak about the origins of the world in the Ancient Forest. <value:$28>, you should hear what he has to say.<end>"

## 1250001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（ディンガル帝国の玄武将軍になった<line-break>　ザギヴから、手紙をもらった）<line-break><line-break>（エンシャントの政庁へ出向けという<line-break>　内容だ。…行った方がいいのかな？）<end>"
EN: "(A letter arrived from Zagiv, now the Black Tortoise General of the Dyneskal Empire.) (It says to report to the Ancient Government Hall. ...Should I go?)<end>"
KO: "(딘갈 제국의 현무장군이 된 자기브에게 편지를 받았다.) (엔샨트 정청으로 나오라는 내용이다. …가는 편이 좋을까?)<end>"

## 1250002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "玄武将軍からの名指しの依頼！？<line-break>それってチャンスよ！<line-break>もちろん受けるんでしょっ？<line-break>うらやましいわ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "A personal request from the Black Tortoise General!? This is your big chance! You're going to accept, of course, right? I'm so jealous, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "현무장군이 직접 지명한 의뢰!? 그건 기회야! 당연히 받을 거지? 부럽다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1250003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガルの内務統括殿とはいろいろと<line-break>あってな、ワシは参加できん。<line-break>すまぬな、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I have a complicated history with Dyneskal's Director of Internal Affairs, so I cannot take part. Forgive me, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "딩갈의 내무 총괄과는 여러 일이 있어서 나는 참가할 수 없다. 미안하군, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1250004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴさんからお仕事頼まれるなんて<line-break>かなりすごいかも…。<line-break><line-break>お金をたくさんもらえたり、<line-break>とんとん拍子に出世できたり、<line-break>世界を支配できるようになるかも！？<end>"
EN: "Getting a job from Zagiv is kind of a big deal... Maybe you'll get loads of money, fly up the ranks, or even end up ruling the world!<end>"
KO: "자기브 씨한테 일을 부탁받다니 꽤 대단한 걸지도…. 돈도 많이 받고, 승진도 척척 하고, 세계까지 지배할 수 있게 될지도!?<end>"

## 1250005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺をさしおいて、なんでお前が<line-break>名指しの依頼を受けるんだよ。<line-break>ちっ、面白くねぇなぁ！<line-break>さっさとエンシャントの政庁に<line-break>行っちまえってんだ。<end>"
EN: "Why do you get a personal request instead of me? Tch. I don't like this one bit! Just hurry up and get over to the Ancient Government Hall already.<end>"
KO: "나를 제쳐 두고 왜 네가 지명 의뢰를 받는 거야. 쳇, 재미없네! 빨리 엔샨트 정청으로 가 버리라고.<end>"

## 1250006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "将軍からの依頼かぁ。!<line-break>どんな依頼なんだろうね？<line-break><line-break>早くエンシャントの政庁に行って<line-break>聞いてみたいよ。もちろん、<line-break>ボクもつれてってくれるんだよね？<end>"
EN: "A request from the general, huh? I wonder what it is. I want to hurry to the Ancient Government Hall and find out. You're taking me with you, of course... right?<end>"
KO: "장군의 의뢰인가아. 어떤 의뢰일까? 빨리 엔샨트 정청에 가서 들어 보고 싶어. 물론 나도 데려가 줄 거지?<end>"

## 1250007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴから手紙？<line-break>珍しいこともあるものね。<line-break><line-break>何か裏があるかもしれないけど、<line-break>エンシャントへ行くかどうかは、<line-break>あなたに任せるわ。<end>"
EN: "A letter from Zagiv? That's unusual. There may be more to it than meets the eye, but whether you go to Ancient is up to you.<end>"
KO: "자기브에게서 편지? 별일이네. 뭔가 속셈이 있을지도 모르지만 엔샨트에 갈지는 네게 맡길게.<end>"

## 1250008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴさんがあなたにお手紙を？<line-break>ほう、それはそれは…。<line-break><line-break>彼女の力になってあげてくださいね。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Zagiv sent you a letter? Well, well... Please do what you can to help her, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "자기브 씨가 당신에게 편지를요? 오, 그건…. 그녀에게 힘이 되어 주세요, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1250009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近は色々危ないからねー。<line-break><value:$28>も<line-break>気をつけてないとヤバイかもよ？<line-break>それでも行く？<line-break>まっ、そういうの、嫌いじゃないけどね。<end>"
EN: "Things have been dangerous lately. You could be in real trouble if you aren't careful, <value:$28>. Still going? Well, I can't say I dislike that sort of thing.<end>"
KO: "요즘은 여러모로 위험하니까. <value:$28>도 조심하지 않으면 위험할지도 몰라? 그래도 갈 거야? 뭐, 그런 거 싫진 않지만.<end>"

## 1250010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ね、姉ちゃん、俺、感動してる…。<line-break>玄武将軍にまで名声が<line-break>伝わってるなんてよ！<line-break>もちろん依頼内容、聞きに行くだろ？<line-break>場所は、エンシャントの政庁だぜ。<end>"
EN: "Sis, I'm moved... To think your fame has reached even the Black Tortoise General! Of course you're going to hear what the request is, right? The place is the Ancient Government Hall.<end>"
KO: "누나, 나 감동했어…. 현무장군한테까지 명성이 전해졌다니! 당연히 의뢰 내용을 들으러 갈 거지? 장소는 엔샨트 정청이야.<end>"

## 1250011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この手紙は私が出したものよ。<line-break>当然、来るでしょう？<end>"
EN: "I sent that letter. Naturally, you'll come, won't you?<end>"
KO: "이 편지는 내가 보낸 거야. 당연히 오겠지?<end>"

## 1250012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんも有名人になったなぁ。<line-break>人から頼りにされるってのは<line-break>けっこういい気分だろ？<line-break>とりあえず、エンシャントの政庁に<line-break>行ってみたらどうだ？<end>"
EN: "You've become quite the celebrity. Feels pretty good to have people counting on you, doesn't it? Why not start by visiting the Ancient Government Hall?<end>"
KO: "너도 유명인이 됐구나. 사람들에게 의지받는 것도 꽤 기분 좋지? 일단 엔샨트 정청에 가 보는 게 어때?<end>"

## 1250013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前に名指しの依頼か…。<line-break>ザギヴとて、将軍のひとり。油断しないことだな。<end>"
EN: "A personal request for you... Zagiv is still one of the generals. Don't let your guard down.<end>"
KO: "네 이름을 지목한 의뢰인가…. 자기브도 장군 중 하나다. 방심하지 마라.<end>"

## 1250014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "玄武将軍からの呼び出しか。<line-break>たしか、エンシャントの政庁に<line-break>行くんじゃったな。<end>"
EN: "A summons from the Black Tortoise General, eh? If I remember correctly, you need to go to the Ancient Government Hall.<end>"
KO: "현무장군의 호출인가. 분명 엔샨트 정청으로 가는 거였지.<end>"

## 1250015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴさんから手紙がきたんだね。<line-break>エンシャントの政庁に行って<line-break>詳しい話を聞いてみようよ。<end>"
EN: "So you got a letter from Zagiv. Let's go to the Ancient Government Hall and hear the details.<end>"
KO: "자기브 씨에게서 편지가 왔네. 엔샨트 정청으로 가서 자세한 이야기를 들어 보자.<end>"

## 1250017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴから手紙がきた？<line-break>高貴で優雅で知性あふれる<line-break>エルフのアタクシがど～して<line-break>そんなつまんない話を<line-break>聞かなきゃならないのよ！<line-break><line-break>ま、アタクシには関係のない話だし<line-break>好きにすればいいじゃない。<end>"
EN: "A letter from Zagiv? Why should I, a noble, graceful, brilliant Elf, have to listen to such a boooring story!? Well, it has nothing to do with me. Do whatever you like.<end>"
KO: "자기브에게서 편지가 왔다고? 고귀하고 우아하며 지성 넘치는 엘프인 내가 왜 그런 시시한 얘기를 들어야 하는 거야! 뭐, 나와는 상관없는 일이니 마음대로 해.<end>"

## 1250018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1250019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
