## 1250021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "音に聞こえし玄武将軍ザギヴも、<line-break>案外ぬるいものだな。<line-break><line-break>フッ…まあ、<line-break>ディンガルの内情を探るのも<line-break>悪くはないのかもしれん。<end>"
EN: "So even the renowned Black Tortoise General Zagiv is softer than I expected. Hmph... Still, it might not be a bad idea to investigate Dyneskal's internal affairs.<end>"
KO: "명성 높은 현무장군 자기브도 의외로 물렁하군. 흥… 뭐, 딘갈의 속사정을 살피는 것도 나쁘진 않겠지.<end>"

## 1250022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "玄武将軍からの依頼が来たって？<line-break>ぼくの広報活動が実ったんだね！<line-break><line-break>さあ、エンシャントの政庁へ行って、<line-break>話を聞こうよ！<end>"
EN: "You got a request from the Black Tortoise General? My publicity campaign finally paid off! Come on, let's go hear her out at the Ancient Government Hall!<end>"
KO: "현무장군에게서 의뢰가 왔다고? 내 홍보 활동이 결실을 맺었네! 자, 엔샨트 정청으로 가서 이야기를 들어 보자!<end>"

## 1250023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "全ては予言どおりの道をゆく、か…。<line-break>フフ、いや、だだの独り言だ。<line-break>君が気にする必要はない。<end>"
EN: "So all things follow the path foretold... Heh. No, merely talking to myself. You needn't concern yourself.<end>"
KO: "모든 것은 예언대로 흘러간다, 인가…. 후후, 아니, 그냥 혼잣말이야. 네가 신경 쓸 필요 없어.<end>"

## 1250024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様。<line-break>帝国中枢部とカルラには意見の対立が<line-break>あるようです。<line-break>この依頼、危険かもしれません。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, there appears to be a disagreement between Karula and the Empire's central leadership. This request may be dangerous.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님. 제국 중추부와 카를라 사이에 의견 충돌이 있는 것 같습니다. 이번 의뢰, 위험할지도 모릅니다.<end>"

## 1250025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こっそり教えるけどね、ザギヴは<line-break>東の方を見に出かけるらしいの。<line-break> <line-break><value:$28>には、護衛を<line-break>お願いするつもりなんじゃないかな？<end>"
EN: "Just between us, it seems Zagiv is going east to investigate. I think she may intend to ask you to escort her, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "몰래 알려 주는 건데, 자기브는 동쪽을 보러 나갈 모양이야. <value:$28>에게 호위를 부탁하려는 거 아닐까?<end>"

## 1250026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう、玄武将軍として聞こえし<line-break>ザギヴから手紙とな…。<line-break> <line-break>わしがどうこう言うもんでもない。<line-break><value:$28>、行くか行かぬか、<line-break>おぬし自身で判断するべきじゃ。<end>"
EN: "A letter from Zagiv, the renowned Black Tortoise General... It is not for me to tell you what to do. You must decide for yourself whether to go, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "호오, 현무장군으로 이름 높은 자기브에게서 편지라…. 내가 이래라저래라 할 일은 아니지. <value:$28>, 갈지 말지는 네가 직접 판단해야 한다.<end>"

## 1250027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "玄武将軍ザギヴからの手紙か…。<line-break>エンシャントの政庁に出向けと？<line-break><line-break>すべてを疑えとは言えないが、<line-break>十分気をつけるんだぞ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "A letter from Black Tortoise General Zagiv... She wants you to report to the Ancient Government Hall? I won't tell you to suspect everything, but be very careful, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "현무장군 자기브의 편지인가…. 엔샨트 정청으로 오라고? 모든 걸 의심하라고 할 순 없지만 충분히 조심해, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1250028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴから名指しの依頼か。<line-break>どんな依頼なのか気になるな。<line-break>政庁に詳しい話を聞きに行くか。<end>"
EN: "A personal request from Zagiv, huh? I wonder what it is. Shall we go to the Government Hall and hear the details?<end>"
KO: "자기브가 지명한 의뢰인가. 어떤 의뢰인지 궁금하군. 정청에 자세한 이야기를 들으러 가 볼까.<end>"

## 1250029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すごいわね、<line-break>玄武将軍じきじきの依頼だなんて！<line-break>あなたも有名になったのね。<line-break>でもザギヴは結構なやり手らしいし<line-break>油断は禁物よ。<end>"
EN: "Impressive! A request from the Black Tortoise General herself! You've become famous too. But Zagiv is apparently quite formidable, so don't let your guard down.<end>"
KO: "대단하네. 현무장군이 직접 의뢰하다니! 너도 유명해졌구나. 그래도 자기브는 꽤 유능하다니 방심하면 안 돼.<end>"

## 1250030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴからの手紙には、<line-break>エンシャント政庁に来てほしい、と<line-break>書かれていたと記憶しています。<end>"
EN: "As I recall, Zagiv's letter asked you to come to the Ancient Government Hall.<end>"
KO: "자기브의 편지에는 엔샨트 정청으로 와 달라고 적혀 있었던 것으로 기억합니다.<end>"

## 1250031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さんって<line-break>偉い人にも名前が知られてるんですね。<line-break>自分のことじゃないけど誇らしいです！<line-break>でもいったい何の用でしょうか？<end>"
EN: "Even important people know your name, <value:$28>. It's not about me, but I'm proud! What could they possibly want, though?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님은 높은 분들에게도 이름이 알려져 있군요. 제 일도 아닌데 자랑스러워요! 그런데 대체 무슨 용무일까요?<end>"

## 1250032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "玄武将軍ザギヴからの依頼か。<line-break>君の思うように行動すればいい。<line-break>どうせ君はこの依頼を受けるんだろ？<line-break>でも彼女には油断しないほうがいい。<line-break>彼女の内には闇が潜んでいるんだ。<end>"
EN: "A request from Black Tortoise General Zagiv. Act as you see fit. You'll accept it anyway, won't you? But you should be wary of her. Darkness lurks within her.<end>"
KO: "현무장군 자기브의 의뢰인가. 네 생각대로 행동하면 돼. 어차피 넌 이 의뢰를 받을 거잖아? 하지만 그녀에게는 방심하지 않는 게 좋아. 그녀 안에는 어둠이 숨어 있어.<end>"

## 1250033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（ゼネテスが処刑の危機に…。<line-break>　急いで謁見の間に行かなければ…。）<line-break> <end>"
EN: "(Xenetes is in danger of being executed... I have to hurry to the audience chamber...)<end>"
KO: "(제네테스가 처형될 위기다…. 서둘러 알현실로 가야 한다….)<end>"

## 1250034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "謁見の間でゼネテスが処刑される…。<line-break>時間はもうあまりない。<line-break>間に合うかしら…。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes will be executed in the audience chamber... There isn't much time left. I wonder if we'll make it...<end>"
KO: "알현실에서 제네테스가 처형된다…. 시간이 이제 별로 없어. 늦지 않을까…<end>"

## 1250035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "剣狼と恐れられたゼネテスの命運も<line-break>ついについえるのか…？<line-break>いや、奴ならば、あるいは…。<end>"
EN: "Has fate finally caught up with Xenetes, the dreaded Sword Wolf...? No. Knowing him, perhaps...<end>"
KO: "검랑이라 두려움받던 제네테스의 운명도 마침내 끝나는 건가…? 아니, 그라면 어쩌면….<end>"

## 1250036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスさんを助けに行くんですね～。<line-break>自らの危険もかえりみず…<line-break>きゃっ、かなりかっこいいかも！<end>"
EN: "You're going to save Xenetes? Braving danger yourself... Oh! That's actually pretty cool!<end>"
KO: "제네테스 씨를 구하러 가는 거군요~. 자기 위험도 돌보지 않고…. 꺅, 꽤 멋질지도!<end>"

## 1250037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "処刑されるゼネテスを<line-break>謁見の間に助けに行くんだ！<line-break>燃えるぜ！　うぉぉぉ！<end>"
EN: "We're going to the audience chamber to save Xenetes from execution! Now this gets my blood pumping! Raaaah!<end>"
KO: "처형당할 제네테스를 알현실로 구하러 가는 거야! 불타오른다! 우오오오!<end>"

## 1250038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスが処刑されちゃうなんて…！<line-break>謁見の間に行って、<line-break>やめさせなくちゃいけないよ！<end>"
EN: "Xenetes is going to be executed...! We have to go to the audience chamber and stop it!<end>"
KO: "제네테스가 처형당하다니…! 알현실로 가서 그만두게 해야 해!<end>"

## 1250039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "権力に目がくらんだ人間の行動とは、<line-break>かくも醜いものなのか…。<line-break>謁見の間へ急ぐわよ！<end>"
EN: "Can people blinded by power truly act so hideously...? Let's hurry to the audience chamber!<end>"
KO: "권력에 눈먼 인간의 행동이 이렇게까지 추할 수 있다니…. 알현실로 서두르자!<end>"

## 1250040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスが処刑される…。<line-break>彼の命はあなたにゆだねられた。<line-break>慎重かつ迅速に行動するのですよ。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes is going to be executed... His life is in your hands. Act swiftly, but with care.<end>"
KO: "제네테스가 처형된다…. 그의 목숨은 당신에게 달렸습니다. 신중하면서도 신속하게 행동하세요.<end>"

## 1250041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へー、あのゼネテスがねえ。<line-break>ま、これが戦争ってもんよねー。<line-break>助けたいなら、謁見の間よん。<end>"
EN: "Huh, Xenetes of all people. Well, that's war for you. If you want to save him, head to the audience chamber.<end>"
KO: "헤에, 그 제네테스가 말이지. 뭐, 이게 전쟁이라는 거겠지. 돕고 싶으면 알현실로 가.<end>"

## 1250042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃん！<line-break>とりあえず謁見の間へ行くぞ！<line-break>姉ちゃんなら敵の百人や二百人っ！<line-break>くうっ～。<line-break>ぶたなくてもいいじゃんかよ。<end>"
EN: "Sis! Let's get to the audience chamber! You could take a hundred or two hundred enemies easy! Ow... You didn't have to hit me.<end>"
KO: "누나! 일단 알현실로 가자! 누나라면 적 백 명이나 이백 명쯤! 아야~. 때리지 않아도 되잖아.<end>"

## 1250043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の気配がする…。<line-break>ロストールもまた、<line-break>闇にむしばまれている…。<end>"
EN: "I sense darkness... Rostorl, too, is being consumed by it...<end>"
KO: "어둠의 기운이 난다…. 로스톨도 다시 어둠에 잠식되고 있어….<end>"

## 1250045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "グズグズして俺の足を引っ張るな。<line-break>ゼネテスを助けたくはないのか？<line-break>…フン、謁見の間に行くぞ。<end>"
EN: "Stop dawdling and holding me back. Don't you want to save Xenetes? Hmph. We're going to the audience chamber.<end>"
KO: "꾸물대면서 내 발목 잡지 마. 제네테스를 구하고 싶지 않은 거냐? …흥, 알현실로 간다.<end>"

## 1250046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんと、あのゼネテスまでもが<line-break>捕われてしまうとは…！<line-break>謁見の間へ急ぎ、助けるのじゃ！<line-break>立ちふさがる命知らずのあしらいは、<line-break>このデルガドに任せてもらおう！<line-break>それでは、いざ！<end>"
EN: "To think even Xenetes has been captured...! Hurry to the audience chamber and rescue him! Leave any reckless fools who stand in our way to Dergado! Now, onward!<end>"
KO: "뭐라고, 제네테스마저 붙잡히다니…! 알현실로 서둘러 구해야 한다! 앞을 막는 목숨 아까운 줄 모르는 녀석들은 이 델가도에게 맡겨라! 그럼, 가자!<end>"

## 1250047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "早く行こう、<value:$28>！<line-break>ゼネテスが処刑されたら、<line-break>ロストールはどうなるかわからない。<end>"
EN: "Hurry, <value:$28>! If Xenetes is executed, who knows what will become of Rostorl?<end>"
KO: "빨리 가자, <value:$28>! 제네테스가 처형되면 로스톨이 어떻게 될지 몰라.<end>"

## 1250049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "高貴で優雅なエルフのアタクシを<line-break>ド下等生物同士の醜い争いに<line-break>巻き込まないでくれる！？<line-break>アタクシは高貴で優雅でハイソで<line-break>知性あふれるエ・ル・フなのよ！<line-break>あなた、ほんとにわかってるの！？<end>"
EN: "Would you kindly stop dragging a noble and graceful Elf like me into an ugly conflict between utterly inferior creatures!? I am a noble, graceful, high-class, brilliant E-L-F! Do you truly understand!?<end>"
KO: "고귀하고 우아한 엘프인 이 몸을 저질 하등생물들끼리의 추악한 싸움에 끌어들이지 말아 줄래!? 이 몸은 고귀하고 우아하고 상류층이고 지성이 넘치는 엘·프라고! 당신, 정말 알고 있는 거야!?<end>"

## 1250050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス救出ボゲー<line-break>ゼネテス救出ボゲー<line-break>ゼネテス救出ボゲー<line-break>ゼネテス救出ボゲー<line-break>ゼネテス救出ボゲー<line-break>ゼネテス救出ボゲー<end>"
EN: "Rescue Xenetes, Bogee! Rescue Xenetes, Bogee! Rescue Xenetes, Bogee! Rescue Xenetes, Bogee! Rescue Xenetes, Bogee! Rescue Xenetes, Bogee!<end>"
KO: "제네테스 구출 보게 제네테스 구출 보게 제네테스 구출 보게 제네테스 구출 보게 제네테스 구출 보게 제네테스 구출 보게<end>"

## 1250051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスが死んじゃうなんて、<line-break>ルルアンタ、がまんできないよぉ！<line-break>えっけんの間に行こうよぉ！<end>"
EN: "Lulluantha can't bear the thought of Xenetes dying! Let's go to the audience chamber!<end>"
KO: "제네테스가 죽는다니 루루안타는 못 참아! 알현실로 가자아!<end>"

## 1250052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>…ゼネテスを救うのか？<end>"
EN: "... Are you going to save Xenetes?<end>"
KO: "………………. …제네테스를 구할 건가?<end>"

## 1250053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クーデターを起こしたのは私だ。<line-break>貴様も私の元へ来るがいい…。<end>"
EN: "I am the one who staged the coup. You, too, may come to me...<end>"
KO: "쿠데타를 일으킨 건 나다. 네놈도 내게 오너라….<end>"

## 1250054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "迫り来る魔の手！<line-break>危機に陥る奇蹟の名将！<line-break>駆けつける我らがヒーロー！<line-break>ほんとに英雄伝説っぽくなってきた！<line-break>こんな現場に居合わせることが<line-break>できるだなんて、夢のようさ！<end>"
EN: "The hand of evil closes in! The miracle-working commander is in peril! Our hero rushes to the rescue! This is really starting to feel like a heroic legend! It's a dream come true to witness something like this firsthand!<end>"
KO: "다가오는 마수! 위기에 빠진 기적의 명장! 달려오는 우리의 영웅! 정말 영웅전설 같아졌어! 이런 현장에 함께 있을 수 있다니 꿈만 같아!<end>"

## 1250055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスはお前の友ではないのか？<line-break>もっとも、友のために命を捨てる…<line-break>それが賢いとは、私は言わんがな。<end>"
EN: "Is Xenetes not your friend? Not that I would call throwing away your life for a friend... wise.<end>"
KO: "제네테스는 네 친구가 아닌가? 물론 친구를 위해 목숨을 버리는 게 현명하다고는 말하지 않겠지만.<end>"

## 1250056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様。<line-break>王女様の安否はお任せください。<line-break>お早く謁見の間へ！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, leave the princess's safety to us. Hurry to the audience chamber!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님. 공주님의 안전은 저희에게 맡겨 주세요. 어서 알현실로!<end>"

## 1250057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王女様は、私たちが守る！<line-break>ゼネテスを助けてあげてよ、<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "We'll protect the princess! Please save Xenetes, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "공주님은 우리가 지킬게! 제네테스를 도와줘, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1250058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスが処刑されてしまう…。<line-break>姫の期待に応える気があるなら、<line-break>急がなくては！<end>"
EN: "Xenetes is about to be executed... If you mean to live up to the princess's hopes, you must hurry!<end>"
KO: "제네테스가 처형당하고 말 거야…. 공주의 기대에 부응할 생각이라면 서둘러야 해!<end>"

## 1250059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "モタモタしている暇はない。<line-break>ゼネテスの命運はつきようとしている。<line-break>謁見の間へ行くぞ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "There's no time to waste. Xenetes's life is about to end. We're going to the audience chamber, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "꾸물거릴 시간이 없다. 제네테스의 운명은 끝나 가고 있다. 알현실로 간다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1250060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ！　まさかまたクーデターが…！<line-break>ゼネテスは王宮の謁見の間だ。<line-break><value:$28>、急ぐぞ！<end>"
EN: "Damn! Not another coup...! Xenetes is in the palace audience chamber. Hurry, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "큭! 설마 또 쿠데타가…! 제네테스는 왕궁의 알현실에 있다. <value:$28>, 서두르자!<end>"

## 1250061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "デジャワの変を思い出すわ。<line-break>あの時はエリス様の知謀で事なきを得た<line-break>けど、今回はそうじゃないみたいね。<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>早くゼネテスを助け出さないと。<line-break>謁見の間に行きましょ。<end>"
EN: "This reminds me of the Tejava Incident. Lady Eris's wisdom and strategy kept matters from getting out of hand then, but this time seems different. We have to rescue Xenetes quickly, <value:$28>. Let's go to the audience chamber.<end>"
KO: "데자와의 변이 생각나네. 그때는 에리스 님의 지략으로 무사히 넘겼지만 이번엔 아닌 것 같아. <value:$28>, 빨리 제네테스를 구해야 해. 알현실로 가자.<end>"

## 1250062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスを助けるのですか。<line-break>ならば急いだほうがよいでしょう。<line-break>彼は謁見の間です。<end>"
EN: "You're going to save Xenetes? Then you should hurry. He's in the audience chamber.<end>"
KO: "제네테스를 구할 생각입니까. 그렇다면 서두르는 게 좋겠군요. 그는 알현실에 있습니다.<end>"

## 1250063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのゼネテスさんが死刑だなんて！<line-break>うちのお店に何度も来てくれたし<line-break>とても気さくで楽しい人なのに。<line-break><value:$28>さん、お願いです。<line-break>ゼネテスさんを助けてください！<line-break>謁見の間へ、早く！<end>"
EN: "I can't believe Xenetes has been sentenced to death! He's visited our shop many times, and he's such a friendly, entertaining man. Please, <value:$28>, save him! Hurry to the audience chamber!<end>"
KO: "그 제네테스 씨가 사형이라니! 우리 가게에도 몇 번이나 와 줬고 정말 붙임성 좋고 재미있는 분인데. <value:$28> 씨, 부탁이에요. 제네테스 씨를 구해 주세요! 알현실로 빨리!<end>"

## 1250064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスなんて<line-break>どうなっても構わないよ。<line-break><line-break>けど、彼が殺されたら<line-break>君が悲しい思いをするかもしれないね。<line-break>仕方ない、謁見の間に行こう。<end>"
EN: "I don't care what happens to Xenetes. But you might be sad if he's killed. Fine, let's go to the audience chamber.<end>"
KO: "제네테스 따위 어떻게 돼도 상관없어. 하지만 그가 죽으면 네가 슬퍼할지도 모르겠네. 어쩔 수 없지, 알현실로 가자.<end>"

## 1250065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（妖術宰相ゾフォルを倒す。<line-break>　闇の勢力の拡大を止めなければ…）<end>"
EN: "(Defeat Zofor, the Sorcerous Chancellor. The spread of the forces of darkness must be stopped...)<end>"
KO: "(요술재상 조포르를 쓰러뜨린다. 어둠의 세력이 확대되는 걸 막아야 해…)<end>"

## 1250066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の者の帝国…。<line-break>ディンガルの行く末が<line-break>そんなものになるだなんて…。<line-break>これまで先陣切って戦ってきたけど、<line-break>私、怖いわ…。<end>"
EN: "An empire of dark beings... To think that's what awaits Dyneskal... I've always fought at the front, but I'm afraid...<end>"
KO: "어둠의 자들의 제국…. 딘갈의 앞날이 그런 것이 된다니…. 지금까지 앞장서 싸워 왔지만 나, 무서워….<end>"

## 1250067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かつてのディンガルの首都が、<line-break>今や…。<line-break><line-break>ゾフォルめから取り戻さねばならぬ。<line-break>ワシら人の手で…！<end>"
EN: "Dyneskal's former capital, reduced to this... We must wrest it back from Zofor with human hands!<end>"
KO: "과거 딩갈의 수도가 이제는…. 조포르에게서 되찾아야 한다. 우리 인간의 손으로…!<end>"

## 1250068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "死の町には闇の精霊たちが<line-break>群れているわ。<line-break>気をつけて、<value:$28>様…。<line-break>きゃ！　今のちょっと、<line-break>大魔道士っぽかったかも！？<end>"
EN: "Dark spirits swarm through this city of death. Be careful, <value:$28>... Oh! Did I sound a little like an Archmage just now?<end>"
KO: "죽음의 도시에는 어둠의 정령들이 몰려 있어요. 조심하세요, <value:$28>님…. 꺄! 방금 좀 대마도사 같았을지도!?<end>"

## 1250069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冗談言ってる場合じゃねえ！<line-break>ここで尻込みしてちゃ、<line-break>世界のお先は真っ暗だぜ！<line-break>敵が、闇の者だけにな！！<end>"
EN: "This is no time for jokes! If we lose our nerve now, the world's future will be dark indeed! Especially since we're fighting the beings of darkness!<end>"
KO: "농담할 때가 아니야! 여기서 겁먹으면 세상 앞날이 캄캄해진다고! 적이 어둠의 자들뿐이니까!!<end>"

## 1250070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガル帝国首都、<line-break>エンシャントが…。<line-break>ゾフォル…許せない。<line-break>謁見の間に急ごう、<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Ancient, capital of the Dyneskal Empire... Zofor... I won't forgive this. Hurry to the audience chamber, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "딘갈 제국 수도 엔샨트가…. 조포르… 용서 못 해. 알현실로 서두르자, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1250071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の皇帝が、<line-break>闇の帝国が誕生しようとしている。<line-break>放ってはおけない…！<line-break>謁見の間へ急ぐぞ！<end>"
EN: "An emperor of darkness and an empire of darkness are about to be born. We cannot allow it! Hurry to the audience chamber!<end>"
KO: "어둠의 황제가, 어둠의 제국이 탄생하려 하고 있어. 내버려 둘 수는 없어…! 알현실로 서두르자!<end>"

## 1250072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、世の喧騒から遠ざかって<line-break>生きていこうと決めていた私ですが、<line-break>そうもいかないようですね。<line-break>あなたがやるというのなら、<line-break>精一杯のお手伝いを<line-break>させていただきますよ。<end>"
EN: "Good grief. I had resolved to live far from the clamor of the world, but that seems impossible now. If you mean to do this, I shall help in every way I can.<end>"
KO: "이런, 세상의 소란에서 벗어나 살아가기로 결심한 저였지만 뜻대로 되진 않는군요. 당신이 하겠다면 저도 힘껏 돕겠습니다.<end>"

## 1250073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "で、勝機はあんの、<line-break><value:$28>？<line-break><line-break>中途ハンパは一番危ない。<line-break>この死神サンが言うんだから、<line-break>間違いない、ってね。<end>"
EN: "So, do we stand a chance, <value:$28>? Half measures are the most dangerous. Take it from Mr. Reaper here: that's the truth.<end>"
KO: "그래서 승산은 있어, <value:$28>? 어중간한 게 제일 위험해. 이 사신님이 하는 말이니 틀림없다니까.<end>"

## 1250074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "怖くねえのかよ、姉ちゃん。<line-break>敵はエンシャントを死の町にして<line-break>なんとも思わねえ奴だぜ？<line-break>俺は、やっぱりちょっと怖いぜ。<end>"
EN: "Aren't you scared, Sis? We're up against someone who turned Ancient into a city of death without a second thought. I'm still kind of scared.<end>"
KO: "무섭지 않아, 누나? 적은 엔샨트를 죽음의 도시로 만들고도 아무렇지 않은 녀석이잖아. 난 역시 조금 무서워.<end>"

## 1250075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントをこんなにした<line-break>ゾフォルは、城の謁見の間にいる。<line-break>彼の野望をくじかなければ…。<end>"
EN: "Zofor, the man who did this to Ancient, is in the castle's audience chamber. We must thwart his ambitions...<end>"
KO: "엔샨트를 이렇게 만든 조포르는 성의 알현실에 있어. 그의 야망을 꺾어야 해….<end>"

## 1250076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の皇帝、闇の帝国、か。<line-break>そんなものが出現するとなると、<line-break>貴族同士の小競り合いなんぞは、<line-break>それこそ子供同士のケンカみたいな<line-break>もんだなあ。<line-break><line-break>ま、今ごろ気づいても遅いんだが。<end>"
EN: "An emperor of darkness and an empire of darkness, eh? If such things appear, squabbles between nobles will seem like children fighting. Not that realizing it now does us any good.<end>"
KO: "어둠의 황제, 어둠의 제국이라…. 그런 게 나타난다면 귀족끼리의 다툼 따윈 정말 애들 싸움이나 마찬가지겠군. 뭐, 이제 와 깨달아도 늦었지만.<end>"

## 1250077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォルを倒しに、この死の町を、<line-break>城の謁見の間まで進むか。<line-break>フッ、お前も趣味が悪いな。<end>"
EN: "So you'll cross this city of death to reach the castle's audience chamber and defeat Zofor? Hmph. You have dreadful taste.<end>"
KO: "조포르를 쓰러뜨리려고 이 죽음의 도시를 지나 성 알현실까지 가겠다고? 흥, 너도 취향 한번 나쁘군.<end>"

## 1250078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かつて栄華を誇った<line-break>エンシャントが、今や…。<line-break>いや、落ち込んだりはしておれん。<line-break>悪が血を流すために<line-break>手に取るのも剣なら、<line-break>悪を討てるものも、また剣じゃ！<end>"
EN: "Ancient once reveled in glory, and now... No, this is no time for despair. Evil may take up the sword to spill blood, but the sword can also strike evil down!<end>"
KO: "한때 영화를 자랑하던 엔샨트가 이제는…. 아니, 낙담하고 있을 때가 아니다. 악이 피를 흘리게 하려고 드는 것도 검이라면, 악을 벨 수 있는 것도 또한 검이다!<end>"

## 1250079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、<value:$28>、<line-break>キミは怖くはないの？<line-break><line-break>エンシャントの人は、<line-break>みんなゾフォルに殺されたんだよ？<line-break>僕、考えただけで吐き気がするよ…。<end>"
EN: "Aren't you afraid, <value:$28>? Zofor killed everyone in Ancient. Just thinking about it makes me sick...<end>"
KO: "있잖아, <value:$28>, 넌 무섭지 않아? 엔샨트 사람들은 모두 조포르에게 죽었어. 난 생각만 해도 토할 것 같아….<end>"

## 1250080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かつてのバイアシオン最大の都市が<line-break>ゾフォルひとりの手でここまで…。<line-break>この戦、負けるわけにはいかん。<end>"
EN: "To think one man, Zofor, could reduce what was once Vyashion's greatest city to this... We cannot lose this battle.<end>"
KO: "한때 바이아시온 최대의 도시가 조포르 한 사람의 손에 이 지경까지…. 이 싸움은 절대 질 수 없다.<end>"

## 1250081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、おぞましい！<line-break>破壊神を復活させるために<line-break>住民を生け贄に、ですって？<line-break>アタクシは、か、考えただけで…。<line-break>ううっ…。<line-break><line-break>なっ、何見てるのよ、ド下等生物！<line-break>清純無比なエルフのアタクシが<line-break>は、吐いたりするわけなくってよ！<end>"
EN: "H-how revolting! The residents were sacrificed to revive the God of Destruction? J-just thinking about it... Ugh... W-what are you looking at, you wretched inferior creature!? A perfectly pure Elf like me would n-never vomit!<end>"
KO: "으, 끔찍해! 파괴신을 부활시키려고 주민들을 제물로 바친다고? 이 몸은 생각만 해도…. 우욱…. 뭐, 뭘 보는 거야, 최하등 생물! 순결무구한 엘프인 이 몸이 토, 토할 리가 없잖아!<end>"

## 1250082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャント虐殺のボゲー<line-break>エンシャント虐殺のボゲー<line-break>エンシャント虐殺のボゲー<line-break>エンシャント虐殺のボゲー<line-break>エンシャント虐殺のボゲー<line-break>エンシャント虐殺のボゲー<end>"
EN: "The Ancient Massacre, Bogee! The Ancient Massacre, Bogee! The Ancient Massacre, Bogee! The Ancient Massacre, Bogee! The Ancient Massacre, Bogee! The Ancient Massacre, Bogee!<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 학살의 보게 엔샨트 학살의 보게 엔샨트 학살의 보게 엔샨트 학살의 보게 엔샨트 학살의 보게 엔샨트 학살의 보게<end>"

## 1250083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、怖いよぉ、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break><line-break>破壊神を蘇らせるために、<line-break>みんな生け贄にされちゃったなんて…。<line-break><line-break>ルルアンタ、<line-break>こわい夢見ちゃいそうだよぉ。<end>"
EN: "I'm scared, <value:$28>. Everyone was sacrificed to revive the God of Destruction... Lulluantha is going to have nightmares.<end>"
KO: "있지, 무서워, <value:$28>. 파괴신을 되살리려고 모두 제물이 됐다니…. 루루안타, 무서운 꿈을 꿀 것 같아.<end>"

## 1250084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "倒す…。<line-break>闇の円卓騎士が何者であろうと、<line-break><line-break>民を生け贄に蘇った破壊神が<line-break>どれほど強かろうとも…。<end>"
EN: "I will defeat them... Whoever the dark Knight of the Round Table may be, and however powerful the God of Destruction revived through the people's sacrifice...<end>"
KO: "쓰러뜨리겠다…. 어둠의 원탁기사가 누구든, 백성을 제물 삼아 되살아난 파괴신이 아무리 강하더라도….<end>"

## 1250085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の帝国が誕生する。<line-break>夜よりも濃い暗黒に塗られた、<line-break>おぞましい帝国が…。<end>"
EN: "An empire of darkness is being born. A hideous empire steeped in darkness deeper than night...<end>"
KO: "어둠의 제국이 탄생한다. 밤보다도 짙은 암흑으로 물든 끔찍한 제국이….<end>"

## 1250086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "嘘でしょ？　嘘って言ってよ…。<line-break>エンシャントの人たちの命を生け贄に<line-break>破壊神を復活させるだなんて…！<line-break>そんなの…そんなの、<line-break>ぜったい、キレイじゃない！<line-break>キレイの反対側にあることだよ！<end>"
EN: "This can't be true. Tell me it's a lie... Sacrificing the people of Ancient to revive the God of Destruction... That's... that's not beautiful at all! It's the exact opposite of beautiful!<end>"
KO: "거짓말이지? 거짓말이라고 해 줘…. 엔샨트 사람들의 목숨을 제물로 파괴신을 부활시키다니…! 그런 건… 그런 건 절대 아름답지 않아! 아름다움과 정반대에 있는 일이야!<end>"

## 1250087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我が都、エンシャントが…。<line-break><line-break><line-break>………………。<end>"
EN: "My city, Ancient... ...<end>"
KO: "우리의 도시, 엔샨트가…. ……………….<end>"

## 1250088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様。<line-break>謁見の間へ行かれるのですね。<line-break>お供させていただきますわ。<end>"
EN: "You're going to the audience chamber, <value:$28>. Please allow me to accompany you.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님. 알현실로 가시는군요. 함께 가겠습니다.<end>"

## 1250089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォルをやっつけに謁見の間に<line-break>行くんでしょ？<line-break>ねえ、ヴィアも連れてって！<end>"
EN: "You're going to the audience chamber to beat Zofor, right? Take Via with you!<end>"
KO: "조포르를 쓰러뜨리러 알현실에 가는 거지? 저기, 비아도 데려가 줘!<end>"

## 1250090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォルめ、想像以上に恐ろしい奴…。<line-break>エンシャントの人々を皆殺しにしてなお<line-break>平然と笑おうとは…。<line-break>謁見の間へ急ぐぞ、<value:$28>。<line-break>奴を討つのじゃ！<end>"
EN: "Zofor is even more terrible than I imagined... He massacred the people of Ancient and can still smile without a care... Hurry to the audience chamber, <value:$28>. We shall strike him down!<end>"
KO: "조포르, 상상 이상으로 무서운 놈이군…. 엔샨트 사람들을 몰살하고도 태연히 웃다니…. 알현실로 서두르자, <value:$28>. 녀석을 친다!<end>"

## 1250091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の中で闇を祓う…。<line-break>まるで、困難であることの<line-break>例え話のようだ。<line-break>しかし、私は信じているよ。<line-break><value:$28>になら、<line-break>必ずそれが成し遂げられると。<end>"
EN: "Dispelling darkness from within darkness... It sounds almost like a metaphor for difficulty itself. But I believe you can accomplish it, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "어둠 속에서 어둠을 몰아낸다…. 마치 어려운 일을비유하는 말 같군. 하지만 나는 믿고 있어.<value:$28>라면, 반드시 해낼 수 있다고.<end>"

## 1250092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "街の人たちを生贄に…<line-break>街の人たちの未来を奪ったあいつを<line-break>俺は許せない！<line-break><value:$28>！<line-break>謁見の間へ急ぐぞ！<end>"
EN: "He sacrificed the townspeople and stole their future. I'll never forgive him! Hurry to the audience chamber, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "마을 사람들을 제물로… 사람들의 미래를 빼앗은 그 녀석을 난 용서 못 해! <value:$28>! 알현실로 서두르자!<end>"

## 1250093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントが一瞬にして死の都ね。<line-break>住民を生贄だなんて、<line-break>ゾフォルって最低の男ね。<line-break>行きましょ、謁見の間に。<line-break>まだ、全部終わってしまった<line-break>わけじゃないわ。<end>"
EN: "Ancient became a city of death in an instant. Sacrificing its people... Zofor is the worst. Let's go to the audience chamber. It isn't all over yet.<end>"
KO: "엔샨트가 순식간에 죽음의 도시라니. 주민을 제물로 삼다니 조포르는 최악의 남자야. 가자, 알현실로. 아직 전부 끝난 건 아니야.<end>"

## 1250094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォルは<line-break>謁見の間です。<end>"
EN: "Zofor is in the audience chamber.<end>"
KO: "조포르은 알현실에 있습니다.<end>"

## 1250095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私怖いです、<value:$28>さん。<line-break>街をこんなにした人と戦わなくちゃって<line-break>思うと、ふるえちゃうんです…。<line-break>でもこんなんじゃダメですよね。<line-break>これ以上犠牲を出さないためにも、<line-break>勇気を出さないと！<end>"
EN: "I'm afraid, <value:$28>. The thought of fighting someone who did this to the city makes me tremble... But I can't stay like this. I have to find the courage to prevent any more victims!<end>"
KO: "저 무서워요, <value:$28> 씨. 도시를 이렇게 만든 사람과 싸워야 한다고 생각하면 몸이 떨려요…. 하지만 이래선 안 되겠죠. 더 이상 희생이 생기지 않게 하려면 용기를 내야 해요!<end>"

## 1250096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォル、か…。<line-break><line-break><line-break>彼は予言にとらわれた悲しい男だ。<line-break>彼が、変われないのなら<line-break>いつか、戦う時が来ると思っていた。<line-break>行こう、<value:$28>。<line-break>ゾフォルを倒しに、謁見の間へ。<end>"
EN: "Zofor... He is a sad man, trapped by prophecy. If he could not change, I knew the day would come when we had to fight. Come, <value:$28>. Let us go to the audience chamber and defeat Zofor.<end>"
KO: "조포르인가…. 그는 예언에 사로잡힌 슬픈 남자야. 그가 변할 수 없다면 언젠가 싸울 날이 올 거라고 생각했어. 가자, <value:$28>. 조포르를 쓰러뜨리러 알현실로.<end>"

## 1250097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（エンシャントを脱出する。<line-break>　次のソウルリープが起これば、<line-break>　助からない…。）<end>"
EN: "(Escape Ancient. If another Soul Leap occurs, there will be no surviving it...)<end>"
KO: "(엔샨트를 탈출한다. 다음 소울 리프가 일어나면 살아남지 못해….)<end>"

## 1250098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言いたいことはわかるわ、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break><line-break>でも、これが戦争。<line-break>戦争だったのよ！<line-break>少なくとも、途中までは…！<end>"
EN: "I know what you mean, <value:$28>. But this is war. It was war! At least until partway through...<end>"
KO: "무슨 말인지 알아, <value:$28>. 하지만 이게 전쟁이야. 전쟁이었던 거라고! 적어도 중간까지는…!<end>"

## 1250099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォルめ…。<line-break>二度目のソウルリープまでに、<line-break>何としても脱出するのじゃ！<end>"
EN: "Curse you, Zofor... We must escape at any cost before the second Soul Leap!<end>"
KO: "조포르 놈…. 두 번째 소울 리프 전까지 무슨 일이 있어도 탈출하는 거다!<end>"

## 1250100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "次のソウルリープが来たら<line-break>私たちももう助からないんですって！<line-break>きゃ！　ハラハラドキドキですねっ！<end>"
EN: "They say we won't survive if the next Soul Leap comes! Oh! How thrillingly suspenseful!<end>"
KO: "다음 소울 리프가 오면 우리도 이제 살아남지 못한대요! 꺅! 조마조마 두근두근하네요!<end>"

## 1250101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "革新なんざどうでもいいけど、<line-break>こんなトコで死ぬのはゴメンだ！<line-break><line-break>あのバチアタリ坊主！<line-break>いつか耳元で俺様のとっておきを<line-break>聞かせてやるぜっ！<end>"
EN: "I don't give a damn about Humanity's Evolution, but I'm not dying in a place like this! That blasphemous monk! Someday I'll whisper something special right into his ear!<end>"
KO: "혁신 따윈 아무래도 좋지만 이런 데서 죽는 건 사양이다! 그 벌 받을 꼬맹이! 언젠가 귓가에 내 비장의 한마디를 들려주겠어!<end>"

## 1250102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "は、早く逃げなくっちゃ！<line-break>次のソウルリープが来たら<line-break>ボクたちも助からないよ！<end>"
EN: "W-we have to get out of here! If the next Soul Leap comes, we won't survive either!<end>"
KO: "빠, 빨리 도망쳐야 해! 다음 소울 리프가 오면 우리도 못 살아남아!<end>"

## 1250103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悔しいけれど、<line-break>今は立ち止まっていられない。<line-break>帝都の門まで退くのよ！<end>"
EN: "It pains me, but we can't stop now. Fall back to the imperial capital's gates!<end>"
KO: "분하지만 지금은 멈춰 있을 때가 아니야. 제도의 문까지 물러나!<end>"

## 1250104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ソウルリープ…また厄介なものを<line-break>持ち出して来ましたね。<line-break>早く退きましょう。<end>"
EN: "A Soul Leap... Another troublesome thing to contend with. Let us withdraw quickly.<end>"
KO: "소울 리프… 또 성가신 걸 꺼내 들었군요. 어서 물러납시다.<end>"

## 1250105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうすんの、<value:$28>？<line-break>逃げなきゃやられちゃうよ～？<line-break>宰相サンの革新も台無しよん？<end>"
EN: "What are you going to do, <value:$28>? If we don't run, we're done for. Then the Chancellor's vision of Humanity's Evolution will come to nothing too.<end>"
KO: "어쩔 거야, <value:$28>? 도망치지 않으면 당한다고~? 재상님의 혁신도 물거품이야?<end>"

## 1250106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "出直しだ、姉ちゃん！<line-break>俺たちがやられちゃ、<line-break>誰がゾフォルを倒すんだ！<end>"
EN: "We'll come back and try again, Sis! If we get killed, who'll defeat Zofor?<end>"
KO: "다시 시작하자, 누나! 우리가 당해 버리면 누가 조포르를 쓰러뜨리겠어!<end>"

## 1250107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォル…。<line-break>なんということを…。<line-break>今はひとまず町の外へ！<end>"
EN: "Zofor... What have you done...? For now, get outside the city!<end>"
KO: "조포르…. 무슨 짓을…. 지금은 일단 도시 밖으로!<end>"

## 1250108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悔しいが、ここは一度退くぞ、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break><line-break>俺たちまでやられちゃ、<line-break>エンシャントの人々の<line-break>仇を討つこともできなくなる。<end>"
EN: "It pains me, but we have to withdraw for now, <value:$28>. If we're killed too, we won't be able to avenge the people of Ancient.<end>"
KO: "분하지만 여기선 일단 물러나자, <value:$28>. 우리까지 당하면 엔샨트 사람들의 원수를 갚을 수도 없어.<end>"

## 1250109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "踏みとどまるなら、１人でしろ。<line-break>自らの目的も果たせず、<line-break>こんなところで死ぬのは愚かだ。<end>"
EN: "If you insist on staying, do it alone. Dying here before accomplishing your purpose would be foolish.<end>"
KO: "남겠다면 혼자 남아. 자신의 목적도 이루지 못하고 이런 곳에서 죽는 건 어리석어.<end>"

## 1250110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここでわしらが命を失っては、<line-break>闇の者どもの思うツボじゃ。<line-break>悔しいが、退くのじゃ！<end>"
EN: "If we lose our lives here, those creatures of darkness will have exactly what they want. It pains me, but withdraw!<end>"
KO: "여기서 우리가 목숨을 잃으면 어둠의 무리만 좋아할 뿐이다. 분하지만 후퇴하자!<end>"

## 1250111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人類の革新なんて、<line-break>よくわからないけど、<line-break>死ぬのだけはゴメンだよ！<line-break>早くこの町を脱出しなくちゃ！<end>"
EN: "I don't really understand this talk of Humanity's Evolution, but I refuse to die! We have to escape this city, now!<end>"
KO: "인류의 혁신이니 뭐니, 잘 모르겠지만, 죽는 것만은 싫어! 빨리 이 도시를 빠져나가야 해!<end>"

## 1250112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガルの首都が<line-break>魔物の巣窟に変わり果てるとは…。<line-break>ここで死ぬわけには行かないぞ。<end>"
EN: "To think Dyneskal's capital would become a den of monsters... We cannot die here.<end>"
KO: "딩갈의 수도가 마물의 소굴로 변해 버리다니…. 여기서 죽을 순 없어.<end>"

## 1250113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何よ、下等生物。<line-break>アタクシに聞かなくちゃ、<line-break>何も決められないの？<line-break>…なんて言ってる暇もないのよー！<line-break>このままじゃ、革新どころか、<line-break>人類滅亡なのよー！！<end>"
EN: "What is it, inferior creature? Can't you decide anything without asking me? But there's no time for that now! At this rate, forget Humanity's Evolution―humanity will go extinct!<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 하등생물. 나한테 묻지 않으면 아무것도 못 정해? …라고 말하고 있을 때가 아니야! 이대로면 혁신은커녕 인류 멸망이라고!!<end>"

## 1250114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャント虐殺のボゲー<line-break>エンシャント虐殺のボゲー<line-break>エンシャント虐殺のボゲー<line-break>エンシャント虐殺のボゲー<line-break>エンシャント虐殺のボゲー<line-break>エンシャント虐殺のボゲー<end>"
EN: "The Ancient Massacre, Bogee! The Ancient Massacre, Bogee! The Ancient Massacre, Bogee! The Ancient Massacre, Bogee! The Ancient Massacre, Bogee! The Ancient Massacre, Bogee!<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 학살의 보게 엔샨트 학살의 보게 엔샨트 학살의 보게 엔샨트 학살의 보게 엔샨트 학살의 보게 엔샨트 학살의 보게<end>"

## 1250115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もうすぐ次のソウルリープが<line-break>来ちゃうんだよぉ！<line-break>早く逃げようよぉ、<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "The next Soul Leap will be here soon! Let's run, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "곧 다음 소울 리프가 와! 빨리 도망치자, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1250116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "退かねば…<line-break>生きねば…革新もなにもない。<end>"
EN: "We must retreat... We must live... Otherwise Humanity's Evolution means nothing.<end>"
KO: "물러나야 해… 살아야 해… 살아야 혁신도 뭐도 있지.<end>"

## 1250117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の帝国が誕生する。<line-break>夜よりも黒い暗黒に塗られた、<line-break>おぞましい帝国が…。<line-break>誰がそれを望んだというのだ…。<end>"
EN: "An empire of darkness is being born. A hideous empire steeped in darkness blacker than night... Who could have wished for such a thing...?<end>"
KO: "어둠의 제국이 태어난다. 밤보다 검은 암흑에 물든 끔찍한 제국이…. 누가 그런 걸 바랐다는 거지….<end>"

## 1250118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントの外まで逃げなくちゃ！<line-break>もうキレイなものを探しにも<line-break>行けなくなっちゃうよ！<end>"
EN: "We have to escape beyond Ancient! Otherwise I'll never get to search for beautiful things again!<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 밖까지 도망쳐야 해! 이제 아름다운 걸 찾으러도 못 가게 되잖아!<end>"

## 1250119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "急ぐのだ、<value:$28>！<line-break>人類の革新を諦めるわけにはいかん。<line-break>何としてもやり遂げねば…！<end>"
EN: "Hurry, <value:$28>! We cannot abandon Humanity's Evolution. We must see it through at any cost...!<end>"
KO: "서둘러라, <value:$28>! 인류의 혁신을 포기할 수는 없다. 어떻게든 해내야 해…!<end>"

## 1250120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>お早く都市の外へ！<line-break>ソウルリープが来ます！<end>"
EN: "Hurry outside the city, <value:$28>! The Soul Leap is coming!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 어서 도시 밖으로! 소울 리프가 옵니다!<end>"

## 1250121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "次のソウルリープが起きれば、<line-break>今度こそみんな死んじゃう！<line-break>エンシャントの外へ逃げよう！<end>"
EN: "If the next Soul Leap happens, everyone really will die this time! Let's escape Ancient!<end>"
KO: "다음 소울 리프가 일어나면 이번엔 모두 죽어! 엔샨트 밖으로 도망가자!<end>"

## 1250122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悔しいがここはひとまず退くぞ！<line-break>わしらまでやられては、<line-break>闇の勢力を止めることもできぬ！<end>"
EN: "It pains me, but we must withdraw for now! If we fall too, no one will be left to stop the forces of darkness!<end>"
KO: "분하지만 일단 여기서는 물러난다! 우리까지 당하면 어둠의 세력을 막을 수조차 없어!<end>"

## 1250123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "町の外まで退くぞ！<line-break>ここで私たちがやられては、<line-break>人々の仇を討つこともできなくなる！<end>"
EN: "Fall back beyond the city! If we're defeated here, we won't be able to avenge the people!<end>"
KO: "도시 밖까지 후퇴한다! 여기서 우리가 당하면 사람들의 원수도 갚을 수 없게 된다!<end>"

## 1250124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悔しいがここは退くんだ。<line-break>死んだらエンシャントの皆の<line-break>仇も討てなくなるぞ。<end>"
EN: "It pains me, but we have to retreat. If we die, we can't avenge the people of Ancient.<end>"
KO: "분하지만 여기선 물러나야 해. 죽으면 엔샨트 사람들의 원수도 갚지 못한다고.<end>"

## 1250125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすがにここは退かないと<line-break>命が危ないわ。<line-break>早く町を脱出しましょ！<end>"
EN: "If we don't retreat now, our lives really will be in danger. Let's get out of the city, quickly!<end>"
KO: "아무래도 여기선 물러나지 않으면 목숨이 위험해. 빨리 마을을 빠져나가자!<end>"

## 1250126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "次のソウルリープが来れば<line-break>私たちも助からないでしょう。<line-break>　<line-break>私は貴方を死なせたくありません。<line-break>ここは退きましょう。<end>"
EN: "If the next Soul Leap comes, we probably won't survive either. I do not want you to die. Let us withdraw.<end>"
KO: "다음 소울 리프가 오면 우리도 살아남지 못할 겁니다. 저는 당신을 죽게 하고 싶지 않습니다. 여기서는 물러나죠.<end>"

## 1250127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん、逃げましょう！<line-break>あなたが死んだら、<line-break>誰がゾフォルを倒すんですか！<end>"
EN: "Let's run, <value:$28>! If you die, who will defeat Zofor?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 도망쳐요! 당신이 죽으면 누가 조포르을 쓰러뜨리겠어요!<end>"

## 1250128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "早く退くんだ。<line-break>ここで死んだら元も子もない。<line-break>ソウルリープが来れば助からないぞ。<end>"
EN: "Hurry and withdraw. Dying here would make everything pointless. We won't survive if the Soul Leap comes.<end>"
KO: "어서 물러나. 여기서 죽으면 아무 소용도 없어. 소울 리프가 오면 살아남지 못해.<end>"

## 1250129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（ジラークが人間に宣戦布告をした。<line-break>　このままでは、人間にもコーンスにも<line-break>　大きな犠牲が出る…）<end>"
EN: "(Zsilak has declared war on humanity. If this continues, both humans and Konsu will suffer terrible losses...)<end>"
KO: "(지라크가 인간에게 선전포고했다. 이대로라면 인간에게도 콘스에게도 큰 희생이 생긴다….)<end>"

## 1250130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア様がいないからって、<line-break>反乱を起こすだなんて…。<line-break><line-break>ジラーク将軍はもっと冷静な方だと<line-break>思っていたのに。<end>"
EN: "To start a rebellion simply because Lord Nemea is gone... I thought General Zsilak was more levelheaded.<end>"
KO: "네메아 님이 없다고 반란을 일으키다니…. 지라크 장군은 좀 더 냉정한 분이라고 생각했는데.<end>"

## 1250131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まさか反旗を翻そうとは…。<line-break>そこまで思いつめていたのか、<line-break>ジラーク…。<line-break>しかし、人間であるワシらに<line-break>挟む言葉はないのかもしれんな。<line-break>アルノートゥンへ行こう。<end>"
EN: "I never imagined he would rebel... Were you truly driven that far, Zsilak? Yet perhaps we humans have no right to interfere. Let us go to Alnortun.<end>"
KO: "설마 반기를 들 줄이야…. 그렇게까지 몰려 있었던 건가, 지라크…. 하지만 인간인 우리가 끼어들 말은 없을지도 모르겠군. 알노툰으로 가자.<end>"

## 1250132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きゃ！　反乱だって！<line-break>勝ち目はあるのかしら！<line-break><end>"
EN: "Oh! A rebellion! I wonder if they have any chance of winning!<end>"
KO: "꺅! 반란이라고! 승산은 있는 거야?<end>"

## 1250133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人間に戦いを挑むだって？<line-break>そんなことしたら…<line-break>人間とコーンスは、なおさら…。<end>"
EN: "They're challenging humanity to war? If they do that, relations between humans and Konsu will only...<end>"
KO: "인간에게 싸움을 건다고? 그런 짓을 하면… 인간과 콘스는 더욱….<end>"

## 1250134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "物事を力で解決しても、<line-break>本当の解決にはならないよ。<line-break>そして、いつか…。<end>"
EN: "Solving things by force brings no true resolution. And someday...<end>"
KO: "무력으로 일을 해결해도 진정한 해결은 되지 않아. 그리고 언젠가….<end>"

## 1250135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私がエルフではなくコーンスだったら、<line-break>真っ先に駆けつけてやるところよ。<line-break>勝つことは必ずしも目的ではない…。<line-break>そうせねばならないコーンスの死に様に<line-break>あなたたち人間族がしてきたことを<line-break>映し見るがいいわ。<end>"
EN: "If I were a Konsu rather than an Elf, I would be the first to rush there. Victory is not necessarily the goal... Look at the way the Konsu are forced to die, and see reflected in it what you humans have done.<end>"
KO: "내가 엘프가 아니라 콘스였다면 가장 먼저 달려갔을 거야. 이기는 것만이 반드시 목적은 아니지…. 그렇게 해야만 하는 콘스의 죽음에서 너희 인간족이 해 온 일을 비춰 보라고.<end>"

## 1250136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "反乱とは思い切りましたね。<line-break>ナッジ君は考えがちですから、<line-break>考えすぎてわからなくなったんですね。<line-break>…種族の共存に必要なのは<line-break>憎しみではなく友情だということが。<line-break>彼に友情を伝えられるかがカギですね。<end>"
EN: "Rebellion is a bold move. Nadge tends to brood, so he must have thought so much that he lost sight of the truth: coexistence between races requires friendship, not hatred. The key is whether you can show him that friendship.<end>"
KO: "반란이라니 큰 결심을 했군요. 나지 군은 생각이 많은 편이니까 너무 생각하다가 알 수 없게 된 거겠죠. …종족의 공존에 필요한 건 증오가 아니라 우정이라는 걸. 그에게 우정을 전할 수 있느냐가 관건이겠네요.<end>"

## 1250137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "反乱とは、やってくれるじゃない、<line-break>あの白ヤギじーさんも！<line-break><line-break>このカルラさんが<line-break>駆け足で行ってあげるから、<line-break>ガッカリさせないで欲しいもんだわ！<end>"
EN: "So that old white goat has the nerve to rebel! Karula herself will come running, so he'd better not disappoint me!<end>"
KO: "반란이라니, 그 흰 염소 할아버지도 제법이네! 이 카를라 님이 달려가 줄 테니까 실망시키지 않았으면 좋겠어!<end>"

## 1250138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺も小さいころ、<line-break>コーンスの子をいじめて、<line-break>姉ちゃんにぶたれたよな…。<line-break>あのおとなしいコーンスが、<line-break>反乱だなんて…。<line-break>俺、信じたくねえよ…！<end>"
EN: "When I was little, I bullied a Konsu child and you hit me for it, remember, Sis? I can't believe those gentle Konsu would rebel... I don't want to believe it!<end>"
KO: "나도 어릴 때 콘스 아이를 괴롭혔다가 누나한테 맞았지…. 그 얌전한 콘스가 반란이라니…. 난 믿고 싶지 않아…!<end>"

## 1250139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "血迷ったな、ジラーク。<line-break><line-break><line-break>ネメア様の厚意をふいにして<line-break>一種族のための反乱に走るとは…<line-break>見損なったわ。<end>"
EN: "You've lost your senses, Zsilak. Squandering Lord Nemea's goodwill to launch a rebellion for a single race... I expected better of you.<end>"
KO: "정신이 나갔군, 지라크. 네메아 님의 호의를 저버리고 한 종족만을 위한 반란을 일으키다니… 실망했어.<end>"

## 1250140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "コーンスの立場を考えれば<line-break>反乱を起こす気持ちはわかる。<line-break>こいつはやっかいな問題だな…。<end>"
EN: "Given the position of the Konsu, I can understand why they would rebel. This is a difficult problem...<end>"
KO: "콘스의 처지를 생각하면 반란을 일으키는 심정은 이해돼. 이건 골치 아픈 문제군….<end>"

## 1250141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、馬鹿め、<line-break>かえって寿命を縮めたな。<line-break><line-break>コーンスだろうが人間だろうが、<line-break>道を踏み誤れば奈落へ落ちる。<line-break>そういうものだ。<end>"
EN: "Hmph, fool. You've only shortened your life. Konsu or human, anyone who strays from the path falls into the abyss. That is the way of things.<end>"
KO: "흥, 바보 같으니. 오히려 수명을 줄였군. 콘스든 인간이든 길을 잘못 들면 나락으로 떨어진다. 그런 법이지.<end>"

## 1250142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの温和なコーンスが反乱とは…。<line-break>いや、温和なだけに思いつめてしまう<line-break>ところがあるのか？<line-break>ワシらドワーフのように頑強な<line-break>肉体も持たず、それゆえに…？<line-break>哀しいな、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Those gentle Konsu, in rebellion... Or does their very gentleness make them brood too deeply? They lack the sturdy bodies we Dwarves possess, and perhaps because of that...? It is sad, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그 온순한 콘스가 반란이라니…. 아니, 온순하기에 더 궁지에 몰리는 면이 있는 건가? 우리 드워프처럼 튼튼한 육체도 없고, 그렇기에…? 슬프구나, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1250143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きみにわかってもらおうとは<line-break>思わないよ、<value:$28>。<line-break><line-break>きみは人間で、僕はコーンス。<line-break>生まれた時からそうだったんだもの…。<line-break><end>"
EN: "I don't expect you to understand, <value:$28>. You're human, and I'm a Konsu. That's how we were born...<end>"
KO: "네가 이해해 주길 바라진 않아, <value:$28>. 넌 인간이고 나는 콘스야. 태어날 때부터 그랬으니까….<end>"

## 1250144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待てなかったか、ジラーク…。<end>"
EN: "Could you not wait, Zsilak...?<end>"
KO: "기다리지 못했나, 질라크….<end>"

## 1250145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、下等生物、<line-break>エルフのアタクシが大人しくしてるのが<line-break>そんなに不思議？<line-break>何でも力で解決しようとするのは、<line-break>程度が低い証拠なのよ。<line-break>これだから…野蛮な下等生物は。<end>"
EN: "Why, inferior creature, is it so surprising to see an Elf like me staying quiet? Trying to solve everything by force only proves how crude you are. Honestly... you savage inferior creatures.<end>"
KO: "어머, 하등생물. 엘프인 내가 얌전히 있는 게 그렇게 신기해? 뭐든 힘으로 해결하려는 건 수준 낮다는 증거야. 이래서… 야만적인 하등생물이란.<end>"

## 1250146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "コ―ンスの反乱のボゲー<line-break>コ―ンスの反乱のボゲー<line-break>コ―ンスの反乱のボゲー<line-break>コ―ンスの反乱のボゲー<line-break>コ―ンスの反乱のボゲー<line-break>コ―ンスの反乱のボゲー<end>"
EN: "The Konsu Rebellion, Bogee! The Konsu Rebellion, Bogee! The Konsu Rebellion, Bogee! The Konsu Rebellion, Bogee! The Konsu Rebellion, Bogee! The Konsu Rebellion, Bogee!<end>"
KO: "콘스의 반란 보게ー콘스의 반란 보게ー콘스의 반란 보게ー콘스의 반란 보게ー콘스의 반란 보게ー콘스의 반란 보게ー<end>"

## 1250147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦争で解決するなんて、<line-break>力で勝てばいいだなんて…！<line-break>そんなの、間違ってるよぉ！<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>アルノートゥンへ行こう？<line-break>止めなくちゃいけないよぉ！<end>"
EN: "Settling this through war, saying victory by force is enough... That's wrong! Let's go to Alnortun and stop it, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "전쟁으로 해결한다니, 힘으로 이기면 된다니…! 그런 건 잘못됐어! <value:$28>, 알노툰으로 가자? 막아야 해!<end>"

## 1250148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>…友情でナッジを説得しろ。<line-break><end><end>"
EN: "... Persuade Nadge through friendship.<end><end>"
KO: "………………. …우정으로 나지를 설득해라.<end><end>"

## 1250149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつかこのような日が来るとは<line-break>予想していた。<line-break><line-break>それがたまたま今で、<line-break>たまたまコーンスだっただけだ。<line-break>それ以上、言うことはない。<end>"
EN: "I knew a day like this would come. It merely happened to be now, and the Konsu merely happened to be the ones. I have nothing more to say.<end>"
KO: "언젠가 이런 날이 올 거라 예상했다. 그게 우연히 지금이고 우연히 콘스였을 뿐이다. 그 이상 할 말은 없다.<end>"

## 1250150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>キミにまでわかってもらおうとは<line-break>思わないけれど…<line-break>リルビーのぼくには、<line-break>わかるような気がする。<line-break>ジラークの気持ちがね。<line-break>でも、このやり方はキレイじゃない。<line-break>これじゃ、コーンスは、<line-break>嫌われちゃうよ…。<end>"
EN: "I don't expect even you to understand, <value:$28>... but as a Lilubee, I think I understand how Zsilak feels. Still, this way isn't beautiful. This will only make people hate the Konsu...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 너까지 이해해 달라는 건 아니지만… 릴비인 나는 알 것 같아. 지라크의 마음을. 하지만 이 방식은 아름답지 않아. 이래선 콘스가 미움받을 거야….<end>"

## 1250151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "図に乗ったな、ジラークめ。<line-break>ネメア様の厚意に仇で報いるなど、<line-break>死して償ってもとても足りぬ！<end>"
EN: "You've grown arrogant, Zsilak. Repaying Lord Nemea's kindness with betrayal is a crime not even death could atone for!<end>"
KO: "기고만장해졌구나, 지라크! 네메아 님의 호의를 원수로 갚다니 죽음으로 갚아도 모자라다!<end>"

## 1250152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>コーンスという、<line-break>哀しき一族を助けなくては。<line-break>私も妹も、そのために助力を<line-break>惜しんだりはいたしません。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, we must help the tragic Konsu people. My sister and I will spare no effort in doing so.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 콘스라는 슬픈 일족을 도와야 합니다. 저도 제 여동생도 그 일을 위해 힘을 아끼지 않겠습니다.<end>"

## 1250153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>もちろん、アルノートゥンに<line-break>行くんだよね？<line-break>ヴァイにも聞いてみて、<line-break>私たちも力を貸すつもりだよ！<end>"
EN: "You're going to Alnortun, of course, right, <value:$28>? I'll ask Vai too. We intend to help!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 물론 알노툰에 갈 거지? 바위에게도 물어보고 우리도 힘을 보탤 생각이야!<end>"

## 1250154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "止めねばならぬ…！<line-break>聖光石の坑道には、<line-break>恐ろしい秘密があるのじゃ！<end>"
EN: "We must stop this...! The Holy Light Stone mine holds a terrible secret!<end>"
KO: "막아야 한다…! 성광석 갱도에는, 무서운 비밀이 있단다!<end>"

## 1250155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラーク将軍が反乱を起こすとは…。<line-break>何か悪い予感がする。<line-break>何も起きなければいいのだが。<end>"
EN: "General Zsilak, leading a rebellion... I have a terrible feeling about this. I only hope nothing happens.<end>"
KO: "지라크 장군이 반란을 일으키다니…. 왠지 안 좋은 예감이 들어. 아무 일도 없으면 좋겠는데.<end>"

## 1250156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "力ですべてを解決しようとするのは<line-break>間違っている。<line-break>コーンスの反乱を止めるぞ。<end>"
EN: "Trying to solve everything through force is wrong. We're stopping the Konsu Rebellion.<end>"
KO: "힘으로 모든 걸 해결하려는 건 잘못이야. 콘스의 반란을 막자.<end>"

## 1250157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このままじゃコーンスに未来はないわ。<line-break>彼らを止められるのは、<line-break>あなただけなのよ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "At this rate, the Konsu have no future. You're the only one who can stop them, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "이대로면 콘스에 미래는 없어. 그들을 막을 수 있는 건 너뿐이야, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1250158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私には彼らの気持ちがわかりません。<line-break>なぜ、反乱を起こしたのでしょう。<end>"
EN: "I don't understand how they feel. Why did they rebel?<end>"
KO: "저는 그들의 마음을 모르겠습니다. 왜 반란을 일으킨 걸까요.<end>"

## 1250159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "コーンスって優しい人ばかりなんです。<line-break>そんなコーンスが反乱を起こすなんて。<line-break>彼らを止めないと！<end>"
EN: "The Konsu are such kind people. I can't believe they would rebel. We have to stop them!<end>"
KO: "콘스는 상냥한 사람들뿐이에요. 그런 콘스가 반란을 일으키다니. 그들을 막아야 해요!<end>"

## 1250160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "コーンスは道を誤った。<line-break>力で解決しようとしても、<line-break>どこかで必ず亀裂が生じるのに。<end>"
EN: "The Konsu have chosen the wrong path. Trying to solve matters by force will inevitably create a rift somewhere.<end>"
KO: "콘스는 길을 잘못 들었다. 힘으로 해결하려 해도 어딘가에서 반드시 금이 가기 마련인데.<end>"

## 1250161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（思いつめたナッジが反乱に<line-break>　参加してしまった。<line-break>　このままでは…）<end>"
EN: "(Driven to desperation, Nadge has joined the rebellion. At this rate...)<end>"
KO: "(궁지에 몰린 나지가 반란에 참가해 버렸다. 이대로라면…)<end>"

## 1250162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラーク将軍はもっと冷静な方だと<line-break>思っていたのに…。<line-break>それに、ナッジまで…。<end>"
EN: "I thought General Zsilak was more levelheaded... And now even Nadge...<end>"
KO: "지라크 장군은 좀 더 냉정한 분이라고 생각했는데…. 게다가 나지까지….<end>"

## 1250163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まさか反旗を翻そうとは…。<line-break>そこまで思いつめていたのか、<line-break>ジラーク、ナッジ…。<line-break>しかし、人間であるワシらに<line-break>挟む言葉はないのかもしれんな。<end>"
EN: "I never imagined they would rebel... Were you truly driven that far, Zsilak, Nadge? Yet perhaps we humans have no right to interfere.<end>"
KO: "설마 반기를 들 줄이야…. 그렇게까지 몰렸던 건가, 지라크, 나지…. 하지만 인간인 우리가 끼어들 말은 없을지도 모르겠군.<end>"

## 1250164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きゃ！　反乱だって！<line-break>勝ち目はあるのかしら！<end>"
EN: "Oh! A rebellion! I wonder if they have any chance of winning!<end>"
KO: "꺅! 반란이라고! 승산은 있는 거야?<end>"

## 1250165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ナッジ！<line-break>ちくしょう！<line-break>あいつまで反乱に加わるなんて！<line-break>そんなことしたら…<line-break>人間とコーンスは、なおさら…。<end>"
EN: "Nadge! Damn it! Even he joined the rebellion! If he does this... relations between humans and Konsu will only...<end>"
KO: "나지! 젠장! 그 녀석까지 반란에 가담하다니! 그러면… 인간과 콘스는 더더욱….<end>"

## 1250166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの優しいナッジが、<line-break>あんなことに加わるなんて…。<line-break><line-break>アルノートゥンに行こうっ、<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "I can't believe gentle Nadge would join something like that... Let's go to Alnortun, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "그 착한 나지가 그런 일에 가담하다니…. 알노툰으로 가자, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1250167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ナッジの考えは痛いほどわかるわ。<line-break>私がエルフではなくコーンスだったら、<line-break>真っ先に駆けつけてやるところよ。<line-break>勝つことは必ずしも目的ではない…。<line-break><line-break><line-break>そうせねばならないコーンスの死に様に<line-break>あなたたち人間族がしてきたことを<line-break>映し見るがいいわ。<end>"
EN: "I understand Nadge's thinking painfully well. If I were a Konsu rather than an Elf, I would be the first to rush there. Victory is not necessarily the goal... Look at the way the Konsu are forced to die, and see reflected in it what you humans have done.<end>"
KO: "나지의 생각은 아플 만큼 이해해. 내가 엘프가 아니라 콘스였다면 가장 먼저 달려갔을 거야. 이기는 것만이 반드시 목적은 아니지…. 그렇게 해야만 하는 콘스의 죽음에서 너희 인간족이 해 온 일을 비춰 보라고.<end>"

## 1250168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "反乱とは、思い切ったことを…。<line-break>コーンスには魔力を秘めた角という<line-break>狙われる要素がありますからね。<line-break>エルフよりもつらい立場と言えます。<line-break>その辺をわかってあげてくださいね、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Rebellion is a bold choice... The Konsu are targeted for the magic hidden in their horns, so their position may be even harder than the Elves'. Please try to understand that, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "반란이라니, 과감한 선택을…. 콘스에게는 마력을 품은 뿔이라는 노림받을 요소가 있으니까요. 엘프보다도 힘든 처지라고 할 수 있습니다. 그 점을 이해해 주세요, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1250169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "反乱とは、やってくれるじゃない、<line-break>あの白ヤギじーさんたち！<line-break><line-break>このカルラさんが<line-break>駆け足で行ってあげるから、<line-break>ガッカリさせないで欲しいもんだね！<end>"
EN: "So those old white goats have the nerve to rebel! Karula herself will come running, so they had better not disappoint me!<end>"
KO: "반란이라니 제법인데, 그 흰 염소 영감들! 이 카를라 님이달려가 줄 테니까, 실망시키지 않았으면 좋겠네!<end>"

## 1250170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺も小さいころ、<line-break>コーンスの子をいじめて、<line-break>姉ちゃんにぶたれたよな…。<line-break>あのナッジたちが敵だなんて…。<line-break>俺、信じたくねえよ…！<end>"
EN: "When I was little, I bullied a Konsu child and you hit me for it, remember, Sis? I can't believe Nadge and the others are our enemies... I don't want to believe it!<end>"
KO: "나도 어릴 때 콘스 아이를 괴롭혔다가 누나한테 맞았었지… 그 나지 일행이 적이라니… 난 믿고 싶지 않아…!<end>"

## 1250171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア様の厚意をふいにして<line-break>反乱を起こすなんて…<line-break>見損なったわ。<end>"
EN: "Starting a rebellion and squandering Lord Nemea's goodwill... I expected better of them.<end>"
KO: "네메아 님의 호의를 저버리고 반란을 일으키다니… 실망했어.<end>"

## 1250172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "コーンスの立場を考えれば<line-break>反乱を起こす気持ちはわかる。<line-break><line-break>が、ネメアは種族平等をうたって<line-break>ジラークを将軍にしたんじゃ<line-break>なかったのかね？<line-break>敵ながら哀しいぜ、こいつは。<end>"
EN: "Given the position of the Konsu, I understand why they would rebel. But didn't Nemea proclaim racial equality and make Zsilak a general? He may be our enemy, but this is still sad.<end>"
KO: "콘스의 처지를 생각하면 반란을 일으킬 마음은 이해해. 하지만 네메아는 종족 평등을 내세워 지라크를 장군으로 삼은 거 아니었나? 적이지만 슬픈 일이군.<end>"

## 1250173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、馬鹿め、<line-break>かえって寿命を縮めたな。<line-break><line-break>コーンスだろうが人間だろうが、<line-break>道を踏み誤れば奈落へ落ちる。<line-break>そういうものだ。<end>"
EN: "Hmph, fool. You've only shortened your life. Konsu or human, anyone who strays from the path falls into the abyss. That is the way of things.<end>"
KO: "흥, 바보 같으니. 오히려 수명을 줄였군. 콘스든 인간이든 길을 잘못 들면 나락으로 떨어진다. 그런 법이지.<end>"

## 1250174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの温和なコーンスが反乱とは…。<line-break>いや、温和なだけに思いつめてしまう<line-break>ところがあるのか？<line-break>ワシらドワーフのように頑強な<line-break>肉体も持たず、それゆえに…？<line-break>哀しいな、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Those gentle Konsu, in rebellion... Or does their very gentleness make them brood too deeply? They lack the sturdy bodies we Dwarves possess, and perhaps because of that...? It is sad, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그 온순한 콘스가 반란이라니…. 아니, 온순하기에 더 궁지에 몰리는 면이 있는 건가? 우리 드워프처럼 튼튼한 육체도 없고, 그렇기에…? 슬프구나, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1250175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、ごめんよ。<line-break>でも、きみを裏切るつもりじゃない。<line-break>コーンスの未来のためなんだ！<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, <value:$28>. But I never meant to betray you. I'm doing this for the future of the Konsu!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 미안해. 하지만 널 배신할 생각은 없어. 콘스의 미래를 위해서야!<end>"

## 1250176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待てなかったか、ジラーク…。<end>"
EN: "Could you not wait, Zsilak...?<end>"
KO: "기다리지 못했나, 질라크….<end>"

## 1250177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、下等生物、<line-break>エルフのアタクシが大人しくしてるのが<line-break>そんなに不思議？<line-break>何でも力で解決しようとするのは、<line-break>程度が低い証拠なのよ。<line-break>これだから…野蛮な下等生物は。<end>"
EN: "Why, inferior creature, is it so surprising to see an Elf like me staying quiet? Trying to solve everything by force only proves how crude you are. Honestly... you savage inferior creatures.<end>"
KO: "어머, 하등생물. 엘프인 내가 얌전히 있는 게 그렇게 신기해? 뭐든 힘으로 해결하려는 건 수준 낮다는 증거야. 이래서… 야만적인 하등생물이란.<end>"

## 1250178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "コーンスの反乱のボゲー<line-break>コーンスの反乱のボゲー<line-break>コーンスの反乱のボゲー<line-break>コーンスの反乱のボゲー<line-break>コーンスの反乱のボゲー<line-break>コーンスの反乱のボゲー<end>"
EN: "The Konsu Rebellion, Bogee! The Konsu Rebellion, Bogee! The Konsu Rebellion, Bogee! The Konsu Rebellion, Bogee! The Konsu Rebellion, Bogee! The Konsu Rebellion, Bogee!<end>"
KO: "콘스의 반란 보게ー콘스의 반란 보게ー콘스의 반란 보게ー콘스의 반란 보게ー콘스의 반란 보게ー콘스의 반란 보게ー<end>"

## 1250179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦争で解決するなんて、<line-break>力で勝てばいいだなんて…！<line-break>そんなの、間違ってるよぉ！<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>お願い、やめさせて！<end>"
EN: "Settling this through war, saying victory by force is enough... That's wrong! Please stop it, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "전쟁으로 해결한다니, 힘으로 이기면 된다니…! 그런 건 잘못됐어! <value:$28>, 부탁이야, 막아 줘!<end>"

## 1250180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我は、人間もコーンスも、<line-break>区別したことはない。<line-break><line-break>強ければ勝つ、それが掟だ。<line-break>コーンスが人間より強く賢ければ、<line-break>反乱は、成功するだろう…。<end>"
EN: "I have never distinguished between humans and Konsu. The strong prevail; that is the law. If the Konsu are stronger and wiser than humans, the rebellion will succeed...<end>"
KO: "나는 인간과 콘스를 구별한 적이 없다. 강하면 이긴다, 그것이 규칙이다. 콘스가 인간보다 강하고 현명하다면 반란은 성공하겠지….<end>"

## 1250181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつかこのような日が来るとは<line-break>予想していた。<line-break><line-break>それがたまたま今で、<line-break>たまたまコーンスだっただけだ。<line-break>それ以上、言うことはない。<end>"
EN: "I knew a day like this would come. It merely happened to be now, and the Konsu merely happened to be the ones. I have nothing more to say.<end>"
KO: "언젠가 이런 날이 올 거라 예상했다. 그게 우연히 지금이고 우연히 콘스였을 뿐이다. 그 이상 할 말은 없다.<end>"

## 1250182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>キミにまでわかってもらおうとは<line-break>思わないけれど…<line-break>リルビーのぼくには、<line-break>わかるような気がする。<line-break>ジラークやナッジの気持ちがね。<line-break>でも、このやり方はキレイじゃない。<line-break>これじゃ、コーンスは、<line-break>嫌われちゃうよ…。<end>"
EN: "I don't expect even you to understand, <value:$28>... but as a Lilubee, I think I understand how Zsilak and Nadge feel. Still, this way isn't beautiful. This will only make people hate the Konsu...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 네가 이해해 주길 바라는 건 아니지만… 릴비인 나는 조금 알 것 같아. 지라크와 나지의 마음을 말이야. 하지만 이런 방식은 올바르지 않아. 이러면 콘스는 미움을 받게 될 거야….<end>"

## 1250183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "図に乗ったな、ジラークめ。<line-break>ネメア様の厚意に仇で報いるなど、<line-break>死して償ってもとても足りぬ！<end>"
EN: "You've grown arrogant, Zsilak. Repaying Lord Nemea's kindness with betrayal is a crime not even death could atone for!<end>"
KO: "기고만장해졌구나, 지라크! 네메아 님의 호의를 원수로 갚다니 죽음으로 갚아도 모자라다!<end>"

## 1250184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>コーンスという、<line-break>哀しき一族を助けなくては。<line-break>私も妹も、そのために助力は<line-break>惜しんだりはいたしません。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, we must help the tragic Konsu people. My sister and I will spare no effort in doing so.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 콘스라는 슬픈 일족을 도와야 합니다. 저도 제 여동생도 그 일을 위해 힘을 아끼지 않겠습니다.<end>"

## 1250185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>もちろん、アルノートゥンに<line-break>行くんだよね？<line-break>ヴァイにも聞いてみて、<line-break>私たちも力を貸すつもりだよ！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you're going to Alnortun, of course, right? Ask Vai too. We intend to help!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 물론 알노툰에 갈 거지? 바위에게도 물어보고 우리도 힘을 보탤 생각이야!<end>"

## 1250186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "止めねばならぬ…！<line-break>聖光石の坑道には、<line-break>恐ろしい秘密があるのじゃ！<end>"
EN: "We must stop this...! The Holy Light Stone mine holds a terrible secret!<end>"
KO: "막아야 한다…! 성광석 갱도에는, 무서운 비밀이 있단다!<end>"

## 1250187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ナッジが反乱に加わるとは…。<line-break>ディンガル帝国が黙っているとは<line-break>考えにくい。急がなければ！<end>"
EN: "Nadge has joined the rebellion... The Dyneskal Empire is unlikely to stand by and do nothing. We must hurry!<end>"
KO: "나지가 반란에 가담하다니…. 딘갈 제국이 가만있을 리 없어. 서둘러야 해!<end>"

## 1250188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まずいな…。<line-break>ナッジが大切なら<line-break>助けに行かないと。<end>"
EN: "This is bad... If Nadge matters to you, we have to go save him.<end>"
KO: "곤란하군…. 나지가 소중하다면 구하러 가야 해.<end>"

## 1250189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ナッジも反乱に参加したのね…。<line-break>彼の優しさが、仲間への思いが、<line-break>反乱へと駆り立てたのね。<line-break>でも、コーンスに未来はないわ。<line-break>彼を止めなくちゃ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "So Nadge joined the rebellion too... His kindness and concern for his comrades drove him to it. But there is no future for the Konsu. We have to stop him, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "나지도 반란에 참가했구나…. 그 상냥함과 동료를 향한 마음이 반란으로 몰아붙인 거야. 하지만 콘스에게 미래는 없어. 그를 막아야 해, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1250190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私には彼の気持ちがわかりません。<line-break>なぜ、反乱に参加したのでしょうか…。<end>"
EN: "I don't understand how he feels. Why did he join the rebellion...?<end>"
KO: "저는 그의 마음을 모르겠어요. 왜 반란에 가담한 걸까요….<end>"

## 1250191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まさかナッジさんまで<line-break>反乱に参加するなんて。<line-break>　<line-break>ナッジさんは仲間です。<line-break>それと戦うなんて…。<line-break>どうすればいいんでしょうか…。<end>"
EN: "I can't believe even Nadge joined the rebellion. Nadge is one of us. How can we fight him...? What are we supposed to do...?<end>"
KO: "설마 나지 씨까지 반란에 참가하다니. 나지 씨는 동료예요. 그 사람과 싸우다니…. 어떻게 해야 하죠….<end>"

## 1250192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "コーンスは道を誤った。<line-break>力で解決しようとしても、<line-break>どこかで必ず亀裂が生じるんだ。<line-break>ナッジもそれはわかってたはず。<line-break>それでも戦う道を選んだのか…。<end>"
EN: "The Konsu chose the wrong path. Trying to settle things by force will always create a rift somewhere. Nadge must have known that. Did he choose to fight anyway...?<end>"
KO: "콘스는 길을 잘못 들었어. 힘으로 해결하려 해도 어딘가에는 반드시 균열이 생겨. 나지도 그건 알고 있었을 텐데. 그래도 싸우는 길을 택한 건가….<end>"

## 1250193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（カルラの後を追わないと…）<end>"
EN: "(I have to follow Karula...)<end>"
KO: "(카를라의 뒤를 쫓아야 해…)<end>"

## 1250194
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "急いで追いかけてきなさいよ。<line-break>あてにしてるんだから。。<end>"
EN: "Hurry and catch up. I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "서둘러 쫓아가. 기대하고 있으니까.<end>"

## 1250195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神速の戦術家とはいえ、<line-break>このスピードは異常じゃな。<line-break>なにか、きな臭い物を感じるの。<end>"
EN: "Even for the Lightning-Fast Tactician, this pace is extraordinary. Something smells suspicious.<end>"
KO: "신속의 전술가라 해도 이 속도는 이상하구나. 뭔가 수상한 냄새가 난다.<end>"

## 1250196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "置いてきぼりなんて…<line-break>ちょっと酷いかも？<end>"
EN: "Leaving us behind... That's a little cruel, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "두고 가다니… 좀 너무한 거 아냐?<end>"

## 1250197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちっ…なんだってこんなに<line-break>急ぐんだよ。いくらなんでも<line-break>飛ばしすぎだぜ…。<end>"
EN: "Tch... Why the rush? This pace is ridiculous, no matter how you look at it...<end>"
KO: "쳇… 왜 이렇게 서두르는 거야. 아무리 그래도 너무 날아다니잖아….<end>"

## 1250198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　もう、行っちゃった？<line-break>急いで追いかけないと！<end>"
EN: "What? She's already gone? We have to hurry after her!<end>"
KO: "뭐? 벌써 가 버렸어? 서둘러 쫓아가야 해!<end>"

## 1250199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "好機を逃さないってことかしら。<line-break>カルラらしいわ。<line-break><line-break>でも、この強行軍についていける兵は<line-break>少ないはず。<line-break>追いかけた方が良さそうね。<end>"
EN: "I suppose she won't let an opportunity pass. That's Karula for you. But few soldiers can keep up with a forced march like this. We should follow her.<end>"
KO: "기회를 놓치지 않겠다는 건가. 카를라답네. 하지만 이 강행군을 따라갈 병사는 적을 거야. 쫓아가는 게 좋겠어.<end>"

## 1250200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アルノートゥンから邪悪な気配がします。<line-break>急いでいって上げてください。<end>"
EN: "I sense an evil presence from Alnortun. Please hurry after her.<end>"
KO: "알노툰에서 사악한 기운이 느껴집니다. 서둘러 가 주세요.<end>"

## 1250201
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お留守番しといてね。<end>"
EN: "Stay here and mind things for me.<end>"
KO: "집 잘 보고 있어.<end>"

## 1250202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ね、姉ちゃん。<line-break>もう、出発したって…。<line-break>急いで追いかけよう。<end>"
EN: "S-Sis. They say she's already left... Let's hurry after her.<end>"
KO: "누, 누나. 벌써 출발했대…. 서둘러 쫓아가자.<end>"

## 1250203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…早いわね。<line-break>ベルゼーヴァ様が何か仕掛けた？<end>"
EN: "That was fast... Has Lord Beelzeva set something in motion?<end>"
KO: "…빠르네. 벨제바 님이 뭔가 손을 쓴 건가?<end>"

## 1250204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "後を追うのか？<line-break>優秀な部下がいるの羨ましいぜ…。<line-break>わりぃ、愚痴が出ちまった。<end>"
EN: "You're going after her? I'm jealous that she has such capable subordinates... Sorry. I couldn't help complaining.<end>"
KO: "뒤를 쫓을 거야? 뛰어난 부하가 있어서 부럽군…. 미안, 푸념이 나왔네.<end>"

## 1250205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふん。巧遅は拙速に如かずのつもりか…<line-break>凡人の自信過剰は痛い目にあうだろうな。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. Does she think a rough job done quickly is better than a fine job done late? A mediocrity's overconfidence is sure to end painfully.<end>"
KO: "흥. 서투르더라도 빠른 게 낫다는 셈인가… 평범한 자의 과신은 호되게 당할 텐데.<end>"

## 1250206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしらを置いてきぼりにするとはな。<line-break>失礼な話じゃわい。<line-break>追いかける？　物好きじゃのう。<end>"
EN: "Leaving us behind like this is downright rude. You want to follow her? You do enjoy making trouble for yourself.<end>"
KO: "우릴 두고 가 버리다니 실례도 정도가 있구먼. 쫓아가겠다고? 별난 사람이군.<end>"

## 1250207
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "コーンスの世界…かぁ。<end>"
EN: "A world for the Konsu... huh.<end>"
KO: "콘스의 세계…인가.<end>"

## 1250209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "コーンスも人間も野蛮だわ！<line-break>高貴で優雅で知性あふれるエルフの<line-break>ように静かに生きれないの？<line-break>アタクシ、泥臭い戦争は嫌いよ！<end>"
EN: "Konsu and humans are both savages! Why can't they live quietly like noble, graceful, brilliant Elves? I despise grubby wars!<end>"
KO: "콘스도 인간도 야만적이야! 고귀하고 우아하며 지성 넘치는 엘프처럼 조용히 살 수 없어? 난 촌스러운 전쟁은 싫어!<end>"

## 1250210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "コーンスの反乱のボゲー<line-break>コーンスの反乱のボゲー<line-break>コーンスの反乱のボゲー<line-break>コーンスの反乱のボゲー<line-break>コーンスの反乱のボゲー<line-break>コーンスの反乱のボゲー<end>"
EN: "The Konsu Rebellion, Bogee! The Konsu Rebellion, Bogee! The Konsu Rebellion, Bogee! The Konsu Rebellion, Bogee! The Konsu Rebellion, Bogee! The Konsu Rebellion, Bogee!<end>"
