## 1250211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラさん、行っちゃったね。<line-break>追いかけないの？<end>"
EN: "Karula's gone. Aren't you going after her?<end>"
KO: "카를라 씨, 가 버렸네. 쫓아가지 않을 거야?<end>"

## 1250212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break>追わぬのか？<end>"
EN: "... Will you not follow?<end>"
KO: "………………. 쫓지 않는 건가?<end>"

## 1250213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "帝国内部で何かあったようだな。<line-break>いくらカルラでも、この間隔で<line-break>出陣するのは考えられん。<line-break>ロストールを放置するのも妙な話だ。<line-break>ディンガルも長くないかもしれんな。<end>"
EN: "Something must have happened within the Empire. Even Karula would not deploy again so soon. It is also strange to leave Rostorl alone. Dyneskal may not have long left.<end>"
KO: "제국 내부에서 무슨 일이 있었던 모양이군. 아무리 카를라라도 이 간격으로 출진하는 건 생각하기 어렵다. 로스톨을 방치하는 것도 이상하고. 딘갈도 오래 못 갈지도 모르겠군.<end>"

## 1250214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "種族のための戦いかぁ…。<line-break>僕は複雑な気持ちがするよ。<end>"
EN: "A battle for one's race... I have mixed feelings about it.<end>"
KO: "종족을 위한 싸움인가…. 난 복잡한 기분이 들어.<end>"

## 1250215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ…。<line-break>獅子身中の虫め…。<line-break>なかなか尻尾を出さんな。<end>"
EN: "Karula... You traitor in our midst... You hide your true colors well.<end>"
KO: "칼라… 내부의 배신자 같으니… 좀처럼 꼬리를 드러내지 않는군.<end>"

## 1250216
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この短期間の間に出陣するなんて。<line-break>帝国内で何かあったのかもしれません。<line-break>追いかけた方が良さそうですね。<end>"
EN: "Deploying again after so little time... Something may have happened within the Empire. We should probably follow.<end>"
KO: "이렇게 짧은 기간에 출진하다니. 제국 안에서 무슨 일이 있었을지도 모르겠네요. 뒤쫓는 편이 좋겠습니다.<end>"

## 1250217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お兄ちゃんを置いていくなんて！<line-break>それでも、お兄ちゃんはカルラを<line-break>助けにいくの？<end>"
EN: "She left you behind, Big Brother! Are you still going to help Karula?<end>"
KO: "오빠를 두고 가다니! 그래도 오빠는 카를라를 구하러 갈 거야?<end>"

## 1250218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アルノートゥンに攻め入るか…。<line-break>敵の手に落ちているとはいえ、<line-break>複雑な気分じゃ。<end>"
EN: "So we are to attack Alnortun... Even though it has fallen into enemy hands, I have mixed feelings.<end>"
KO: "알노툰에 쳐들어가는 건가…. 적의 손에 떨어졌다 해도 마음이 복잡하군.<end>"

## 1250219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…早すぎる。<line-break>これでは戦力を立てなおす暇すらない。<line-break>後を追った方が良さそうだな。<end>"
EN: "Too soon... There hasn't even been time to rebuild our forces. We should follow.<end>"
KO: "…너무 빠르다. 이래선 전력을 재정비할 틈도 없어. 뒤를 쫓는 게 좋겠군.<end>"

## 1250220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラの行動は拙速に過ぎる。<line-break>俺たちも急ぐぞ。<end>"
EN: "Karula is acting far too hastily. We must hurry too.<end>"
KO: "카를라의 행동은 너무 성급해. 우리도 서두르자.<end>"

## 1250221
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いくら何でも急ぎすぎよ。<line-break>でも、時間がないのは確かね。<end>"
EN: "She's rushing this far too much. Still, we truly are short on time.<end>"
KO: "아무리 그래도 너무 서두르잖아. 그래도 시간이 없는 건 확실하네.<end>"

## 1250222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラはすでに行ってしまいました。<line-break>追いかけるなら急ぐべきです。<end>"
EN: "Karula has already left. If you mean to follow, you should hurry.<end>"
KO: "칼라는 이미 떠났습니다. 뒤쫓으려면 서둘러야 합니다.<end>"

## 1250223
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行っちゃいましたね、カルラさん。<line-break>追いかけないんですか？<end>"
EN: "Karula's already gone. Aren't you going after her?<end>"
KO: "가 버렸네요, 카를라 씨. 쫓아가지 않으세요?<end>"

## 1250224
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何を急いでいるんだ、あの女は？<line-break>帝国で何かあったのか？<line-break>追いかけたほうがよさそうだな。<end>"
EN: "Why is that woman in such a hurry? Did something happen in the Empire? We should follow her.<end>"
KO: "저 여자는 뭘 그렇게 서두르는 거지? 제국에서 무슨 일이 있었나? 쫓아가는 게 좋겠군.<end>"

## 1250225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（湖に沈められた遺跡…<line-break>　どこにその湖があるのだろう？）<line-break><end>"
EN: "(Ruins submerged in a lake... Where could that lake be?)<end>"
KO: "(호수에 가라앉은 유적… 그 호수는 어디에 있는 걸까?)<end>"

## 1250226
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "湖に沈められた遺跡か。<line-break>ごめんなさい。見当がつかないわ。<end>"
EN: "Ruins submerged in a lake? I'm sorry. I have no idea where that could be.<end>"
KO: "호수에 가라앉은 유적인가. 미안해요. 짐작 가는 곳이 없네요.<end>"

## 1250227
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "湖と言われてものう…<line-break>そう言えば、夢幻の湖には水中遺跡が<line-break>あるという噂があったような…。<line-break>はっきりとは憶えておらんのじゃがな。<end>"
EN: "A lake, you say... Come to think of it, I seem to recall a rumor about underwater ruins in the Lake of Illusions. My memory is rather hazy, though.<end>"
KO: "호수라고 해도 말이지…. 그러고 보니 몽환의 호수에는 수중 유적이 있다는 소문이 있었던 것 같군…. 확실히 기억나진 않지만.<end>"

## 1250228
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "湖に沈められた遺跡？<line-break>禁断の呪文？<line-break>ちょっと、すごいかも？<line-break>…場所が分からない？<line-break>興奮して損しちゃったかも？<end>"
EN: "Ruins submerged in a lake? A forbidden spell? That sounds kind of amazing! ...You don't know where it is? I got excited for nothing.<end>"
KO: "호수에 가라앉은 유적? 금단의 주문? 좀 대단할지도? …장소를 모른다고? 괜히 흥분했나 봐.<end>"

## 1250229
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ベルゼーヴァがいるところ？<line-break>どうしてそれを俺が知ってるって<line-break>思うんだよ。<line-break>そうだな、ベルゼーヴァだけに…。<line-break><line-break><line-break>………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>逃げる準備しなかったか、今？<end>"
EN: "Where Beelzeva is? Why would you think I'd know? Well, since it's Beelzeva... ... Did you just get ready to run away?<end>"
KO: "벨제바가 있는 곳? 왜 내가 안다고 생각하는 거야. 그러게, 벨제바니까…. ………………. 방금 도망갈 준비한 거 아니지?<end>"

## 1250230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "消えたベルゼーヴァの行き先？<line-break>うーん、ちょっとわかんないや。<line-break><line-break>前にちょくちょくいなくなってた<line-break>このボクでもさ。<line-break><line-break>前に彼に会った場所とか、<line-break>どこか、なかったっけ？<end>"
EN: "Where did Beelzeva disappear to? Hmm, I don't really know, even though I used to disappear all the time myself. Wasn't there somewhere you met him before?<end>"
KO: "사라진 벨제바가 어디로 갔냐고? 으음, 잘 모르겠네. 예전에 종종 사라졌던 나도 말이야. 전에 그를 만난 장소 같은 데 없었나?<end>"

## 1250231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "禁呪の眠る遺跡…。<line-break><line-break><line-break>そういった場所は魔法的に保護されて<line-break>いることが多いから、発見するのは<line-break>難しいでしょうね。<end>"
EN: "Ruins where a Forbidden Spell lies dormant... Places like that are often protected by magic, so finding them will be difficult.<end>"
KO: "금주가 잠든 유적…. 그런 곳은 마법적으로 보호되는 경우가 많아서 발견하기 어려울 거예요.<end>"

## 1250232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "禁断の聖なる呪文ですか？<line-break>それなら、夢幻の湖に封じられたはず。<line-break><line-break>この戦いが終わったら、<line-break>行ってみてはいかがですか？<end>"
EN: "A forbidden sacred spell? If so, it should be sealed in the Lake of Illusions. Why not go there once this battle is over?<end>"
KO: "금단의 성스러운 주문 말입니까? 그거라면 몽환의 호수에 봉인되어 있을 겁니다. 이 싸움이 끝나면 가 보시는 건 어떻습니까?<end>"

## 1250233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "遺跡の沈んだ湖ねぇ。<line-break>たしか、南の方にそういう噂のある湖が<line-break>あったと思うけど？<end>"
EN: "A lake with sunken ruins... I think there was a lake to the south with rumors like that.<end>"
KO: "유적이 가라앉은 호수라…. 남쪽에 그런 소문이 있는 호수가 있었던 것 같은데?<end>"

## 1250234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくぞ聞いてくれたぜ、姉ちゃん！<line-break>ヤツなら、霊峰トールとか、魔導の塔とか…<line-break>…の…山か…。<line-break>怒ることないだろ！<line-break>俺の勘って、<line-break>そんなにアテにならないのかよ！<end>"
EN: "Glad you asked, Sis! He might be at Sacred Mount Thor, or the Arcane Tower, or... some... mountain... Hey, don't get mad! Is my intuition really that unreliable?<end>"
KO: "잘 물어봤다, 누나! 그 녀석이라면 영봉 토르라든가, 마도의 탑이라든가… …어느 산이었지…. 화내지 마! 내 감이 그렇게 못 미더워!?<end>"

## 1250235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ベルゼーヴァ様…。<line-break><line-break><line-break>………………。<end>"
EN: "Lord Beelzeva... ...<end>"
KO: "벨제바 님…. ……………….<end>"

## 1250236
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "湖に沈んだ遺跡？<line-break><line-break><line-break>そうだな…<line-break>夢幻の湖がそんな感じだな。<line-break>行ってみるかい？<end>"
EN: "Ruins submerged in a lake? Well... the Lake of Illusions sounds about right. Want to go there?<end>"
KO: "호수에 가라앉은 유적? 그렇군… 몽환의 호수가 그런 느낌이지. 가 볼래?<end>"

## 1250237
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "湖に沈んだ遺跡か…<line-break>魔法で守られているのであれば、<line-break>そう簡単には見つけることはできない。<line-break>面倒だが地道に探すしか<line-break>ないのかもしれん。<end>"
EN: "Ruins submerged in a lake... If magic protects them, they will not be easy to find. It may be tedious, but we might have no choice except to search carefully.<end>"
KO: "호수에 가라앉은 유적인가…. 마법으로 보호받고 있다면 쉽게 찾을 순 없겠지. 번거롭지만 차근차근 찾는 수밖에 없을지도 모르겠군.<end>"

## 1250238
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "湖に沈んだ遺跡？<line-break>見つけたとしても、どうやって<line-break>そこにいけばいいのかのう？<line-break>泳ぐのは苦手じゃしな。<end>"
EN: "Ruins submerged in a lake? Even if we find them, how would we reach them? I'm not a strong swimmer.<end>"
KO: "호수에 가라앉은 유적? 찾는다 해도 어떻게 거기까지 가지? 난 수영도 서툰데.<end>"

## 1250239
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "湖に沈んだ遺跡かぁ…<line-break>心辺りがないなぁ。<line-break><line-break>僕のあまり知らない大陸の南側に<line-break>あるのかもしれないね。<end>"
EN: "Ruins submerged in a lake... Nothing comes to mind. They may be somewhere in the south of the continent, which I don't know very well.<end>"
KO: "호수에 가라앉은 유적이라…. 짐작 가는 데가 없네. 내가 잘 모르는 대륙 남쪽에 있을지도 몰라.<end>"

## 1250241
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ベルゼーヴァが消えた先？<line-break>湖に沈んだ遺跡？<line-break><line-break>高貴で優雅で知性あふれるエルフには<line-break>下等生物の作った遺跡なんて<line-break>関係ないわ。<end>"
EN: "Where Beelzeva disappeared to? Ruins submerged in a lake? A noble, graceful, brilliant Elf has no interest in ruins built by inferior creatures.<end>"
KO: "벨제바가 사라진 곳? 호수에 가라앉은 유적? 고귀하고 우아하며 지성 넘치는 엘프에겐 하등생물이 만든 유적 따윈 상관없어.<end>"

## 1250242
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ベルゼ成功のボゲー<line-break>ベルゼ成功のボゲー<line-break>ベルゼ成功のボゲー<line-break>ベルゼ成功のボゲー<line-break>ベルゼ成功のボゲー<line-break>ベルゼ成功のボゲー<end>"
EN: "Beelze Success, Bogee! Beelze Success, Bogee! Beelze Success, Bogee! Beelze Success, Bogee! Beelze Success, Bogee! Beelze Success, Bogee!<end>"
KO: "벨제 성공 보게 벨제 성공 보게 벨제 성공 보게 벨제 성공 보게 벨제 성공 보게 벨제 성공 보게<end>"

## 1250243
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "湖に沈んだ遺跡？<line-break>ゴメンね。<value:$28>。<line-break>ルルアンタ、分からないよぉ。<end>"
EN: "Ruins submerged in a lake? Sorry, <value:$28>. Lulluantha doesn't know.<end>"
KO: "호수에 가라앉은 유적? 미안해, <value:$28>. 루루안타는 모르겠어.<end>"

## 1250244
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "………………. 모른다.<end>"

## 1250245
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "湖に沈んだ遺跡か…。<line-break>以前、エストから夢幻の湖の話を<line-break>聞いたことがある。<line-break>そこかもしれんな。<end>"
EN: "Ruins submerged in a lake... Est once told me about the Lake of Illusions. That may be the place.<end>"
KO: "호수에 가라앉은 유적인가…. 전에 에스트에게 몽환의 호수 이야기를 들은 적이 있다. 그곳일지도 모르겠군.<end>"

## 1250246
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "♪古き力は眠りについた…<line-break>♪古き闇は新月の下に…<line-break>♪古き光は夢幻の彼方…<line-break>この伝承が本当なら…<line-break>そこにいるような気がするんだ。<end>"
EN: "♪ Ancient power fell asleep... ♪ Ancient darkness lies beneath the new moon... ♪ Ancient light lies beyond illusion... If the legend is true... I have a feeling that's where he is.<end>"
KO: "♪오래된 힘은 잠들었다… ♪오래된 어둠은 신월 아래에… ♪오래된 빛은 몽환의 저편에… 이 전승이 사실이라면… 거기에 있는 것 같아.<end>"

## 1250247
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔導の塔、夢幻の湖…。<line-break><line-break><line-break>私は、そこへ禁呪を求めに行ったのだ。<line-break>人間の、さらなる進歩のために…！<end>"
EN: "The Arcane Tower, the Lake of Illusions... I went there in search of Forbidden Spells. For humanity's further advancement...!<end>"
KO: "마도의 탑, 몽환의 호수…. 나는 그곳에 금주를 찾으러 갔다. 인간의 더 큰 진보를 위해…!<end>"

## 1250248
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ベルゼーヴァの行方、ですか。<line-break>そうですね。<line-break>新月の塔、夢幻の湖、魔道の塔。<line-break>ベルゼーヴァは以前、人目を忍んで<line-break>これらの場所に出かけていたようです。<line-break>行かれてみてはいかがでしょう？<end>"
EN: "Beelzeva's whereabouts? Let's see. New Moon Tower, the Lake of Illusions, the Arcane Tower. It seems Beelzeva used to visit those places in secret. Why not investigate them?<end>"
KO: "벨제바의 행방 말입니까. 그렇군요. 신월의 탑, 몽환의 호수, 마도의 탑. 벨제바는 예전에 남의 눈을 피해 이곳들에 가곤 했던 모양입니다. 가 보시는 건 어떻습니까?<end>"

## 1250249
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "消えたベルゼーヴァの行方…。<line-break>むかし、ヴァイと調べてたんだけど、<line-break>新月の塔や夢幻の湖や魔道の塔。<line-break>こういったところに行ってたみたい。<line-break>行ってみる？　お兄ちゃん？<end>"
EN: "Where did Beelzeva disappear to...? Vai and I looked into it a long time ago. It seems he used to visit places like New Moon Tower, the Lake of Illusions, and the Arcane Tower. Want to check them out, Big Brother?<end>"
KO: "사라진 벨제바의 행방…. 옛날에 바이와 조사했는데 신월의 탑이나 몽환의 호수, 마도의 탑. 그런 곳에 갔던 것 같아. 가 볼래, 오빠?<end>"

## 1250250
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか…聖の禁呪が眠るとされる<line-break>遺跡は湖に眠っているのじゃな。<line-break>そうじゃな…。<line-break>長く旅をしているものなら知っている<line-break>かもしれん。<end>"
EN: "I see... So the ruins said to hold the Holy Forbidden Spell lie beneath a lake. Hmm... Perhaps a seasoned traveler would know something.<end>"
KO: "그렇군… 성의 금주가 잠들었다는 유적은 호수에 잠들어 있구나. 그래…. 오래 여행한 사람이라면 알고 있을지도 모르지.<end>"

## 1250251
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔導の塔や新月の塔には<line-break>強大無比な魔法が封じられたと<line-break>聞いているが…。湖か。<line-break>すまない。力になれそうもない。<end>"
EN: "I've heard that incomparably powerful magic was sealed within the Arcane Tower and New Moon Tower, but... a lake? I'm sorry. I don't think I can help.<end>"
KO: "마도의 탑이나 신월의 탑에는 강대한 마법이 봉인돼 있다고 들었지만…. 호수라. 미안해. 도움이 될 것 같진 않아.<end>"

## 1250252
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "夢幻の湖に水中遺跡があるが、<line-break>行ってみるか？<end>"
EN: "There are underwater ruins in the Lake of Illusions. Shall we go and see?<end>"
KO: "몽환의 호수에 수중 유적이 있는데, 가 볼래?<end>"

## 1250253
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "夢幻の湖の水中遺跡の話を<line-break>以前聞いたことがあるわ。<line-break>そこかもしれないわね。<end>"
EN: "I once heard about underwater ruins in the Lake of Illusions. That might be the place.<end>"
KO: "전에 몽환의 호수 수중 유적 이야기를 들은 적이 있어. 거기일지도 모르겠네.<end>"

## 1250254
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "湖に沈められた遺跡ですか…。<line-break>私の経験ではお役に立てません。<line-break>申しわけありません…。<end>"
EN: "Ruins sunk beneath a lake...? I'm afraid my experience is of no use here. My apologies...<end>"
KO: "호수에 가라앉은 유적입니까…. 제 경험으로는 도움이 되지 못하겠습니다. 죄송합니다….<end>"

## 1250255
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "湖に沈められた遺跡ですか？<line-break>夢幻の湖に水中遺跡があるって<line-break>お客さんから聞きましたけど。<end>"
EN: "Ruins sunk beneath a lake? A customer told me there are underwater ruins in the Lake of Illusions.<end>"

## 1250256
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "湖に沈められた遺跡なら、<line-break>夢幻の湖に水中遺跡がある。<line-break>おそらくそこにいるはずだ。<end>"
EN: "If you're looking for ruins beneath a lake, there are underwater ruins in the Lake of Illusions. That's probably where he is.<end>"
KO: "호수에 가라앉은 유적이라면 몽환의 호수에 수중 유적이 있다. 아마 거기에 있을 거야.<end>"

## 1250257

## 1250258
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ベルゼーヴァ様…。<line-break>あの方のことだから、<line-break>大丈夫だと思うけど…<end>"
EN: "Lord Beelzeva... Knowing him, I'm sure he'll be all right, but...<end>"
KO: "벨제바 님… 그분이라면 괜찮을 것 같긴 하지만…<end>"

## 1250259
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あれはお主のせいではない。<line-break>それに宰相殿のことじゃ。<line-break>あの男に屈したりはしないじゃろう。<line-break>そのうち、戻ってくるはずじゃ…。<line-break>きっとな…。<end>"
EN: "...That was not your fault. Besides, we are speaking of the Chancellor. He would never yield to that man. He will return before long... Surely...<end>"
KO: "…그건 네 탓이 아니다. 게다가 재상님이라면 그 남자에게 굴복하진 않을 거다. 머지않아 돌아올 게다…. 분명히….<end>"

## 1250260
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きゃっ、ベルゼーヴァさんの<line-break>消えた先を知ってるかって？<line-break><line-break>…新月の塔かも？<line-break>あそこには封印された呪文が<line-break>あるって話だし。<end>"
EN: "Oh! You're asking if I know where Beelzeva disappeared to? ...Maybe New Moon Tower? They say there's a spell sealed away there.<end>"
KO: "앗, 벨제바 씨가 사라진 곳을 아느냐고? …신월의 탑일지도? 거기엔 봉인된 주문이 있다는 얘기도 있고.<end>"

## 1250261
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ベルゼーヴァがいるところ？<line-break>どうしてそれを俺が知ってるって<line-break>思うんだよ。<line-break>そうだな、ベルゼーヴァだけに…。<line-break><line-break><line-break>………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>逃げる準備しなかったか、今？<end>"
EN: "Where Beelzeva is? Why would you think I'd know? Well, since it's Beelzeva... ... Did you just get ready to run away?<end>"
KO: "벨제바가 있는 곳? 왜 내가 안다고 생각하는 거야. 그러게, 벨제바니까…. ………………. 방금 도망갈 준비한 거 아니지?<end>"

## 1250262
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "消えたベルゼーヴァの行き先？<line-break>うーん、ちょっとわかんないや。<line-break><line-break>前に彼に会った場所とか、<line-break>どこか、なかったっけ？<end>"
EN: "Where did Beelzeva disappear to? Hmm, I really don't know. Wasn't there somewhere you'd met him before?<end>"
KO: "사라진 벨제바의 행방? 음, 잘 모르겠네. 전에 그를 만났던 장소 같은 데 없었어?<end>"

## 1250263
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのお方には、宰相としての他に、<line-break>考えてらいらしたことがあったわ。<line-break>…禁じられた呪文。<line-break>人類の革新に大きな力が必要になると<line-break>すればそういった場所を目指すのかも<line-break>しれないわね。<end>"
EN: "He had matters on his mind beyond his duties as Chancellor. ...Forbidden spells. If great power is needed for Humanity's Evolution, he may have gone to a place where such spells lie.<end>"
KO: "그분은 재상으로서의 일 외에도 생각하고 계신 게 있었어요. …금지된 주문. 인류 혁신에 큰 힘이 필요하다면 그런 장소를 찾을지도 모르겠네요.<end>"

## 1250264
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "不死の王シャロームですね…。<line-break>彼の次の標的は…<line-break>おそらくあなたでしょう。<line-break>気をつけてください。<line-break>深遠の闇に囚われないよう…。<end>"
EN: "Shalom, the Immortal King... His next target will probably be you. Please be careful. Do not let the profound darkness claim you...<end>"
KO: "불사의 왕 샬롬이군요…. 그의 다음 표적은… 아마 당신일 겁니다. 조심하세요. 심연의 어둠에 사로잡히지 않도록….<end>"

## 1250265
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人類の革新…。<line-break>これを成し遂げるには力が必要よ。<line-break><line-break>そういった力が眠る場所、<line-break>古の樹海や、新月の塔、精霊神の座所。<line-break>そういった場所にいるんじゃない？<end>"
EN: "Humanity's Evolution... Achieving it will require power. He may be somewhere such power lies dormant: the Ancient Forest, New Moon Tower, or the Spirit God's seat.<end>"
KO: "인류의 혁신…. 그걸 이루려면 힘이 필요해. 그런 힘이 잠든 곳, 고대 수해나 신월의 탑, 정령신의 좌소. 그런 곳에 있지 않을까?<end>"

## 1250266
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくぞ聞いてくれたぜ、姉ちゃん！<line-break>ヤツなら、霊峰トールとか、魔導の塔とか…<line-break>…の…山か…。<line-break>怒ることないだろ！<line-break>俺の勘って、<line-break>そんなにアテにならないのかよ！<end>"
EN: "Glad you asked, Sis! He might be at Sacred Mount Thor, or the Arcane Tower, or... some... mountain... Hey, don't get mad! Is my intuition really that unreliable?<end>"
KO: "잘 물어봤다, 누나! 그 녀석이라면 영봉 토르라든가, 마도의 탑이라든가… …어느 산이었지…. 화내지 마! 내 감이 그렇게 못 미더워!?<end>"

## 1250267
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ベルゼーヴァ様…。<line-break><line-break><line-break>………………。<end>"
EN: "Lord Beelzeva... ...<end>"
KO: "벨제바 님…. ……………….<end>"

## 1250268
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガルの宰相の行きそうな場所？<line-break>奴の執務室以外でか？<line-break><line-break>…そうだな。少し前に、<line-break>奴を新月の塔で見かけたって<line-break>話を聞いたことがあるぜ。<end>"
EN: "Where might Dyneskal's Chancellor go? Besides his office? ...Let's see. Not long ago, I heard someone had spotted him at New Moon Tower.<end>"
KO: "딘갈 재상이 갈 법한 곳? 집무실 말고? …그러고 보니 얼마 전 그 녀석을 신월의 탑에서 봤다는 이야기를 들은 적 있어.<end>"

## 1250269
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴らの言う人類の革新を進めるには<line-break>強大な力が必要だ。<line-break>それを求めに行ったのではないか？<line-break>新月の塔、魔道の塔…<line-break>ここには、特殊な魔法が封じられたと<line-break>耳に挟んだことがある。<line-break>気になるなら行け。他の連中になど聞くだけ無駄だ。<end>"
EN: "Advancing what they call Humanity's Evolution would require tremendous power. Perhaps he went in search of it. New Moon Tower, the Arcane Tower... I've heard that unusual magic was sealed in those places. If you're curious, go. Asking anyone else is a waste of time.<end>"
KO: "놈들이 말하는 인류의 혁신을 추진하려면 강대한 힘이 필요하다. 그걸 구하러 간 것 아닐까? 신월의 탑, 마도의 탑… 이곳들에는 특수한 마법이 봉인되어 있다는 말을 들은 적이 있다. 신경 쓰이면 가 봐라. 다른 녀석들에게 물어봤자 소용없다.<end>"

## 1250270
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "キーワードは人類の革新じゃな。<line-break>やはり、強い力が必要ではないかの？<line-break><line-break>昔、新月の塔に恐ろしい魔法が<line-break>封印されていると聞いたことがある。<line-break>行ってみるのも手じゃな。<end>"
EN: "The key is Humanity's Evolution. Would that not require great power? Long ago, I heard that terrifying magic was sealed in New Moon Tower. It may be worth investigating.<end>"
KO: "키워드는 인류의 혁신이군. 역시 강한 힘이 필요한 것 아니겠나? 예전에 신월의 탑에 무서운 마법이 봉인되어 있다고 들은 적이 있어. 가 보는 것도 방법이겠지.<end>"

## 1250271
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ベルゼーヴァにしろ、シャロームにしろ<line-break>人類の革新を進めるには大きな力が<line-break>必要になると思うんだ。<line-break>…新月の塔。<line-break>ここには恐ろしい魔法が封じられたと<line-break>確か、聞いたことがあるよ。<end>"
EN: "Whether it's Beelzeva or Shalom, I think bringing about Humanity's Evolution would require immense power. ...New Moon Tower. If I remember correctly, I heard that terrifying magic was sealed there.<end>"
KO: "벨제바든 샬롬이든 인류의 혁신을 추진하려면 큰 힘이 필요하다고 생각해. …신월의 탑. 거기에는 무서운 마법이 봉인되어 있다고 들은 기억이 있어.<end>"

## 1250273
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ベルゼーヴァが消えた先、ですって？<line-break>どうして、アタクシが下等生物の行方を<line-break>言わなきゃいけないの！<line-break>下等生物の考えなんて<line-break>高貴で優雅で知性あふれる<line-break>アタクシたちには関係ないわ。<end>"
EN: "Where Beelzeva disappeared to? Why should I tell you the whereabouts of some inferior creature? What inferior creatures think is no concern of noble, graceful, brilliant beings like us.<end>"
KO: "벨제바가 사라진 곳이라고? 왜 내가 하등생물의 행방을 말해 줘야 하는데! 하등생물 생각 따윈 고귀하고 우아하며 지성 넘치는 우리와 관계없어.<end>"

## 1250274
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ベルゼ失敗のボゲー<line-break>ベルゼ失敗のボゲー<line-break>ベルゼ失敗のボゲー<line-break>ベルゼ失敗のボゲー<line-break>ベルゼ失敗のボゲー<line-break>ベルゼ失敗のボゲー<end>"
EN: "Beelze Failure, Bogee! Beelze Failure, Bogee! Beelze Failure, Bogee! Beelze Failure, Bogee! Beelze Failure, Bogee! Beelze Failure, Bogee!<end>"
KO: "벨제 실패 보게 벨제 실패 보게 벨제 실패 보게 벨제 실패 보게 벨제 실패 보게 벨제 실패 보게<end>"

## 1250275
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "前に見たあの人の目…怖かったよぉ…。<line-break>あの人、ぜったい怖いことを考えてる…。<line-break>前にギルドで、こんな話を聞いたんだ。<line-break>魔導の塔と新月の塔には、<line-break>むかし、怖い魔法が封じられんだって…。<line-break>行ってみる？　<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "The look in his eyes when I saw him before... It was scary... He's definitely planning something frightening... I heard something at the Guild once: terrifying magic was sealed long ago in the Arcane Tower and New Moon Tower... Want to check them out, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "전에 봤던 그 사람 눈… 무서웠어…. 분명 무서운 일을 생각하고 있어…. 전에 길드에서 들었는데 마도의 탑과 신월의 탑에는 옛날 무서운 마법이 봉인됐대…. 가 볼래, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1250276
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…新月の塔…。<line-break>闇の力が眠るという…。<line-break>行ってみるがいい。<end>"
EN: "...New Moon Tower... They say the power of darkness sleeps there... You should go.<end>"
KO: "…신월의 탑…. 어둠의 힘이 잠들어 있다는…. 가 보는 게 좋겠군.<end>"

## 1250277
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ベルゼーヴァの消えた先を<line-break>探していると？<line-break><line-break>新月の塔か、夢幻の湖か…。<line-break>奴は以前からこの二箇所を<line-break>調査していたらしい。<end>"
EN: "You're searching for where Beelzeva disappeared to? New Moon Tower or the Lake of Illusions... Apparently, he'd been investigating those two places for some time.<end>"
KO: "벨제바가 사라진 곳을 찾고 있다고요? 신월의 탑이나 몽환의 호수…. 녀석은 예전부터 이 두 곳을 조사했던 모양이에요.<end>"

## 1250278
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "♪古き力は眠りについた…<line-break>♪古き闇は新月の下に…<line-break>♪古き光は夢幻の彼方…<line-break>この伝承が本当なら…<line-break>そこにいるような気がするんだ。<end>"
EN: "♪ Ancient power fell asleep... ♪ Ancient darkness lies beneath the new moon... ♪ Ancient light lies beyond illusion... If the legend is true... I have a feeling that's where he is.<end>"
KO: "♪오래된 힘은 잠들었다… ♪오래된 어둠은 신월 아래에… ♪오래된 빛은 몽환의 저편에… 이 전승이 사실이라면… 거기에 있는 것 같아.<end>"

## 1250279
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔導の塔、夢幻の湖…。<line-break><line-break><line-break>私は、そこへ禁呪を求めに行ったのだ。<line-break>人間の、さらなる進歩のために…！<end>"
EN: "The Arcane Tower, the Lake of Illusions... I went there in search of Forbidden Spells. For humanity's further advancement...!<end>"
KO: "마도의 탑, 몽환의 호수…. 나는 그곳에 금주를 찾으러 갔다. 인간의 더 큰 진보를 위해…!<end>"

## 1250280
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ベルゼーヴァの行方、ですか。<line-break>そうですね。<line-break>新月の塔、夢幻の湖、魔道の塔。<line-break>ベルゼーヴァは以前、人目を忍んで<line-break>これらの場所に出かけていたようです。<line-break>行かれてみてはいかがでしょう？<end>"
EN: "Beelzeva's whereabouts? Let's see. New Moon Tower, the Lake of Illusions, the Arcane Tower. It seems Beelzeva used to visit those places in secret. Why not investigate them?<end>"
KO: "벨제바의 행방 말입니까. 그렇군요. 신월의 탑, 몽환의 호수, 마도의 탑. 벨제바는 예전에 남의 눈을 피해 이곳들에 가곤 했던 모양입니다. 가 보시는 건 어떻습니까?<end>"

## 1250281
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "消えたベルゼーヴァの行方…。<line-break>むかし、ヴァイと調べてたんだけど、<line-break>新月の塔や夢幻の湖や魔道の塔…<line-break>こういったところに行ってたみたい。<line-break>行ってみる？　お兄ちゃん？<end>"
EN: "Where did Beelzeva disappear to...? Vai and I looked into it a long time ago. It seems he used to visit places like New Moon Tower, the Lake of Illusions, and the Arcane Tower... Want to check them out, Big Brother?<end>"
KO: "사라진 벨제바의 행방…. 옛날에 바이와 조사했는데 신월의 탑이나 몽환의 호수, 마도의 탑…. 그런 곳에 갔던 것 같아. 가 볼래, 오빠?<end>"

## 1250282
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴らが求めていたものは力じゃ。<line-break>とすれば、やばり禁呪じゃろう。<line-break><line-break>しかし、聖の禁呪が眠るとされる<line-break>遺跡は発見されていない。となれば、<line-break>残るは新月の塔しかあるまい。<end>"
EN: "What they sought was power. That would point to a Forbidden Spell. Yet the ruins said to hold the Holy Forbidden Spell have never been discovered. That leaves only New Moon Tower.<end>"
KO: "녀석들이 원한 건 힘이었지. 그렇다면 역시 금주일 게야. 하지만 성의 금주가 잠들어 있다는 유적은 발견되지 않았어. 그렇다면 남은 건 신월의 탑뿐이겠지.<end>"

## 1250283
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人類の革新には大きな力が必要だ。<line-break>とすれば、魔道の塔や新月の塔は<line-break>どうだろう？<line-break>あそこにはかつて、<line-break>強大無比な魔法が封じられたと聞く。<end>"
EN: "Humanity's Evolution requires immense power. With that in mind, what about the Arcane Tower or New Moon Tower? I hear that incomparably powerful magic was once sealed there.<end>"
KO: "인류의 혁신에는 큰 힘이 필요하다. 그렇다면 마도의 탑이나 신월의 탑은 어떨까? 그곳에는 과거 강대무비한 마법이 봉인되었다고 들었다.<end>"

## 1250284
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまないが心当たりがない。<line-break>ベルゼーヴァの近くにいた人間なら<line-break>何か知っているかもしれない。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, but nothing comes to mind. Someone who spent time around Beelzeva might know something.<end>"
KO: "미안하지만 짚이는 곳이 없어. 벨제바 곁에 있던 사람이라면 뭔가 알지도 모르겠군.<end>"

## 1250285
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっとわからないわ。<line-break>でも革新って言うくらいだし<line-break>強力な力が必要よね。<line-break>どこか強力な力が封じられた場所に<line-break>いるんじゃない？<end>"
EN: "I'm not really sure. But if they call it Humanity's Evolution, it must require tremendous power. Don't you think he might be somewhere that such power has been sealed away?<end>"
KO: "잘 모르겠어. 그래도 혁신이라고 할 만큼 강한 힘이 필요하겠지. 어디 강력한 힘이 봉인된 곳에 있는 거 아닐까?<end>"

## 1250286
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ベルゼーヴァの行方…ですか。<line-break>私では…お役に立てません。<line-break>申しわけありません…。<end>"
EN: "Beelzeva's whereabouts...? I'm afraid... I can't help you. My apologies...<end>"
KO: "벨제바의 행방…입니까. 저는… 도움이 되지 못하겠습니다. 죄송합니다….<end>"

## 1250287
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ベルゼーヴァさんの行き先ですか？<line-break>ちょっと思いつきません。<line-break>　<line-break>もしかしたら今までベルゼーヴァさんに<line-break>会ったことがある場所にいるのかも。<end>"
EN: "Where did Beelzeva go? Nothing comes to mind. Maybe he's somewhere you've encountered him before.<end>"
KO: "벨제바 씨가 어디로 갔냐고요? 잘 떠오르지 않네요. 어쩌면 지금까지 벨제바 씨를 만났던 장소 중 어딘가에 있을지도 몰라요.<end>"

## 1250288
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ベルゼーヴァの消えた先か。<line-break>奴は力を求めているし<line-break>それを得られる所に行ったはずだ。<line-break>新月の塔に<line-break>強力な魔法が封印されているけど、<line-break>もしかしたら…。<end>"
EN: "Where did Beelzeva disappear to? He's seeking power, so he must have gone somewhere he could obtain it. Powerful magic is sealed in New Moon Tower, so perhaps...<end>"
KO: "벨제바가 사라진 곳인가. 녀석은 힘을 원하고 있으니 그걸 얻을 수 있는 곳으로 갔을 거야. 신월의 탑에 강력한 마법이 봉인되어 있다는데, 혹시…<end>"

## 1250289
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（地の精霊神グラジェオンが、<line-break>　転送装置の先の聖なる座所で<line-break>　エステルを呼んでいる）<line-break>（グラジェオンはエステルに<line-break>　試練を受けさせ、<line-break>　巫女として目覚めさせるつもりだ）<line-break>（エステルは行きたいと言っている…）<end>"
EN: "(Grajeon, the Spirit God of Earth, is calling Esther to the sacred seat beyond the teleportation device.) (Grajeon intends to put Esther through a trial and awaken her as a priestess.) (Esther says she wants to go...)<end>"
KO: "(땅의 정령신 그라제온이 전송장치 너머의 성스러운 자리에서 에스텔을 부르고 있다) (그라제온은 에스텔에게 시련을 내리고 무녀로 각성시키려 한다) (에스텔은 가고 싶다고 말하고 있다…)<end>"

## 1250290
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "転送装置の先には、グラジェオンがいる<line-break>聖なる座所があるのね。<line-break><line-break>エステルはそこで試練を受けて、<line-break>グラジェオンに仕える巫女になる。<line-break><line-break>エステルに聞いてみて決めれば？<line-break>これは彼女自身の問題なんだし。<end>"
EN: "Beyond the teleportation device is the sacred seat where Grajeon awaits. Esther will undergo a trial there and become a priestess who serves Grajeon. Why not ask Esther and then decide? This concerns her personally, after all.<end>"
KO: "전송장치 너머에는 그라제온이 있는 성스러운 자리가 있는 거네. 에스텔은 거기서 시련을 받고 그라제온을 섬기는 무녀가 되는 거고. 에스텔에게 물어보고 정하면 어때? 이건 그녀 자신의 문제잖아.<end>"

## 1250291
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "地の精霊神グラジェオンが<line-break>エステルを呼んでおるのか。<line-break><line-break>エステル自身がグラジェオンに<line-break>仕えたいと言うのなら、<line-break>止めはしまい。<line-break>しかし、シャリは何と言った？<line-break>ワシはそれが気にかかってならん…。<end>"
EN: "Grajeon, the Spirit God of Earth, is calling Esther? If Esther herself wishes to serve Grajeon, I will not stop her. But what did Shalli say? I cannot help worrying about that...<end>"
KO: "땅의 정령신 그라제온이 에스텔을 부르고 있는 건가. 에스텔 자신이 그라제온을 섬기고 싶다고 한다면 막지는 않겠다. 하지만 샤리는 뭐라고 했지? 나는 그게 마음에 걸려 견딜 수가 없구나….<end>"

## 1250293
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エステルがそんなにたのミコむんなら、<line-break>巫女でも何でもやらせてやりゃいいと<line-break>俺は思うぜ。<line-break>冗談言ってる場合じゃねえ？<line-break>俺はいつだって大マジだ！<line-break>さっさと転送装置に乗ろうぜ！<end>"
EN: "If Esther's begging that hard, I say let her become a priestess or whatever else she wants. This is no time for jokes? I'm always completely serious! Let's get on that teleportation device already!<end>"
KO: "에스텔이 그렇게 부탁한다면 무녀든 뭐든 시키면 된다고 나는 생각해. 농담할 때가 아니라고? 난 언제나 진지해! 얼른 전송장치에 타자!<end>"

## 1250294
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願い、<value:$28>。<line-break>ボク、みんなの足手まといには<line-break>なりたくないんだよ。<line-break>地の精霊神グラジェオンの試練を<line-break>受けて、巫女になれれば、<line-break>きっともっと強くなれる！<line-break>…ボク、<value:$28>を<line-break>守りたいんだよ。<end>"
EN: "Please, <value:$28>. I don't want to hold everyone back. If I can undergo the trial of Grajeon, the Spirit God of Earth, and become a priestess, I'm sure I'll grow stronger! ...I want to protect <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "부탁이야, <value:$28>. 난 모두의 짐이 되고 싶지 않아. 땅의 정령신 그라제온의 시련을 받아 무녀가 되면 분명 더 강해질 수 있어! …난 <value:$28>을 지키고 싶어.<end>"

## 1250295
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エステルが地の精霊神グラジェオンに<line-break>仕える、魔導文明の巫女のひとり…。<line-break><line-break>例えそうだとして、お前は、エステルを<line-break>巫女にさせるつもりなのか？<line-break><line-break>結果として、それがシャリの思惑と重なる<line-break>ということを忘れるな。<end>"
EN: "Esther may be one of the priestesses of the arcane civilization who serve Grajeon, the Spirit God of Earth... Even if that is true, do you intend to let Esther become a priestess? Do not forget that the outcome would align with Shalli's designs.<end>"
KO: "에스텔이 대지의 정령신 그라제온을 섬기는 마도문명의 무녀 중 하나라…. 그렇다 치고, 너는 에스텔을 무녀로 만들 생각인가? 결과적으로 그것이 샤리의 의도와 맞아떨어진다는 걸 잊지 마라.<end>"

## 1250296
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "太古の魔導文明に目をつけてくるとは…<line-break>シャリは相当厄介な相手ですね。<line-break><line-break>それでも、決断するのはあなた…<line-break>そしてエステル自身です。<line-break>重々、お気をつけなさい。<end>"
EN: "For Shalli to set his sights on the ancient arcane civilization... He is a formidable adversary indeed. Even so, the decision belongs to you and to Esther herself. Exercise the utmost caution.<end>"
KO: "태고의 마도문명에 눈을 돌리다니… 샤리는 상당히 성가신 상대군요. 그래도 결정하는 건 당신… 그리고 에스텔 자신입니다. 부디 조심하세요.<end>"

## 1250297
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へえ～、エステルちゃんが<line-break>太古の魔導文明の巫女かもしれない<line-break>とはね～。<line-break>で、カノジョは、正式に巫女に<line-break>なりたいって言ったんだよね？<line-break>だったら、何を迷ってることがあんのさ。<line-break>かわいい女の子の願いは聞いてやるために<line-break>あるようなもんだよ？<line-break>それが世界のハメツにつながってることも<line-break>けっこう、あるんだけどね。<end>"
EN: "Well, well. So Esther might be a priestess of the ancient arcane civilization. And she said she wants to become a proper priestess, right? Then what is there to hesitate over? Pretty girls' wishes are practically made to be granted. Though they do lead to the end of the world surprisingly often.<end>"
KO: "헤에~ 에스텔이 태고의 마도문명 무녀일지도 모른다니~. 그래서 그 애가 정식으로 무녀가 되고 싶다고 한 거지? 그럼 뭘 망설여? 귀여운 여자애의 소원은 들어주라고 있는 거잖아? 뭐, 그게 세계 멸망으로 이어지는 경우도 꽤 있긴 하지만.<end>"

## 1250298
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エステルさんがあんなに頼んでんのに、<line-break>迷ってんのかよ、姉ちゃん！<line-break><line-break>わかってる。シャリが言ってたことが<line-break>気になってるってんだろ？<line-break><line-break>でも、だから姉ちゃんが一緒に<line-break>行くんじゃないかよ。<line-break>あんなチビ、姉ちゃんの怪力で…<line-break>い、痛えっ！<line-break>頭の形が変わったらどうすんだ！<end>"
EN: "Esther begged you that hard, and you're still hesitating, Sis? I know. You're worried about what Shalli said, right? But that's exactly why you're going with her. A little runt like that is no match for your monstrous strength... Ow! What if you change the shape of my skull?!<end>"
KO: "에스텔 씨가 저렇게까지 부탁하는데도 망설이는 거야, 누나! 알아. 샤리가 했던 말이 마음에 걸리는 거지? 하지만 그래서 누나가 같이 가는 거잖아. 저 꼬맹이 정도는 누나의 괴력으로… 아, 아파! 머리 모양 바뀌면 어쩌려고 그래!<end>"

## 1250299
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エステルが何を望んでいるか、<line-break>そんなことは問題ではない。<line-break><line-break>出現する新たな力によって、<line-break>歴史は間違いなく混乱する。<line-break><line-break>それを恐れないというのなら、<line-break>言う通りにしてやるといいわ。<line-break>エステルと、あのシャリの言う通りに。<end>"
EN: "What Esther wants is irrelevant. The emergence of a new power will unquestionably throw history into turmoil. If that does not frighten you, then grant the request. Do as Esther and that Shalli say.<end>"
KO: "에스텔이 무엇을 원하는지는 문제가 아니야. 새롭게 나타날 힘 때문에 역사는 틀림없이 혼란에 빠질 거야. 그걸 두려워하지 않겠다면 말하는 대로 해 주면 되지. 에스텔과 그 샤리가 말하는 대로.<end>"

## 1250300
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔導文明復活の鍵を握る地の巫女、<line-break>エステルはそれになるため、精霊神の<line-break>座所に行きたいってんだろ。<line-break><value:$28>、お人よしの<line-break>お前さんが、エステルの望む通りに<line-break>してやりたいって気持ちはわかるぜ。<line-break>ただ、この件に関しちゃ、もっと<line-break>慎重に考えた方がいいかもしれねえ。<line-break><line-break>ロストールの酒場の看板娘になりたい…<line-break>なんてえ話とは、<line-break>あまりにもスケールが違うからな。<end>"
EN: "Esther wants to go to the Spirit God's seat so she can become the Priestess of Earth who holds the key to reviving the arcane civilization, right? <value:$28>, I know you're softhearted and want to give Esther what she wants. But this may be something you ought to consider more carefully. It's on a completely different scale from wanting to become the tavern's poster girl in Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "마도문명 부활의 열쇠를 쥔 땅의 무녀 에스텔이 그 자리에 오르기 위해 정령신의 자리로 가고 싶다는 거지. <value:$28>, 착해 빠진 네가 에스텔이 원하는 대로 해 주고 싶은 마음은 알아. 다만 이 일만큼은 좀 더 신중하게 생각하는 게 좋을지도 몰라. 로스톨 술집의 간판 아가씨가 되고 싶다는 얘기와는 규모부터 너무 다르니까.<end>"

## 1250301
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "精霊神グラジェオンの座所を訪れ、<line-break>試練を受けたエステルが<line-break>魔導文明の地の巫女となる…？<line-break>面白い。<line-break>お前が行くというなら、<line-break>付き合ってもいいぞ。<end>"
EN: "Esther will visit the seat of Grajeon the Spirit God, undergo the trial there, and become the Priestess of Earth of the arcane civilization...? Interesting. If you're going, I wouldn't mind accompanying you.<end>"
KO: "정령신 그라제온의 처소를 찾아 시련을 받은 에스텔이 마도문명의 땅의 무녀가 된다…? 재미있군. 네가 간다면 함께해도 좋아.<end>"

## 1250302
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エステルが魔導文明復活の鍵を握る<line-break>巫女のひとりかもしれんとはなあ…。<line-break>本当なら、これは大変なことじゃぞ。<line-break>エステル自身も求めていることじゃ、<line-break>精霊神グラジェオンの聖座とやらに、<line-break>真偽を確かめに赴こうではないか。<end>"
EN: "To think Esther might be one of the priestesses who hold the key to reviving the arcane civilization... If true, this is momentous indeed. Esther herself desires it, so let us visit what they call Grajeon the Spirit God's Holy Seat and discover the truth.<end>"
KO: "에스텔이 마도문명 부활의 열쇠를 쥔 무녀 중 하나일지도 모른다니…. 사실이라면 큰일이야. 에스텔 자신도 원하는 일이니 정령신 그라제온의 성좌라는 곳에 가서 진위를 확인해 보자.<end>"

## 1250303
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "精霊神グラジェオンの聖座に行くことで<line-break>エステルは地の巫女になれる…。<line-break><line-break>エステルがそうなりたって思うのは<line-break>いいと思うよ。でも、そのせいで、<line-break>魔導文明とかが復活するんでしょ？<line-break>それが悪い結果を呼びはしないか、<line-break>僕、それがちょっと心配だよ。<end>"
EN: "Esther can become the Priestess of Earth by going to Grajeon the Spirit God's Holy Seat... I think it's fine for Esther to want that. But it would revive the arcane civilization or something, wouldn't it? I'm a little worried that might have bad consequences.<end>"
KO: "정령신 그라제온의 성좌에 가면 에스텔은 땅의 무녀가 될 수 있어…. 에스텔이 그렇게 되고 싶어 하는 건 괜찮다고 생각해. 하지만 그 때문에 마도문명 같은 게 부활하는 거지? 그게 나쁜 결과를 부르진 않을지, 난 그게 조금 걱정돼.<end>"

## 1250304
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "精霊神グラジェオンに連なる巫女の<line-break>魔力も、それを鍵に蘇るという<line-break>魔導文明の力も…<line-break>問題は、それが正しき方向に<line-break>使われるか否か、というところだ。<line-break><line-break>そして、さらに言えば、<line-break>何が正しいのかを、誰が判断するのか？<line-break>それこそが最大の問題だ。<end>"
EN: "The magical power of a priestess linked to Grajeon the Spirit God, and the power of the arcane civilization said to be revived through her... The question is whether that power will be used for the right purpose. More fundamentally, who decides what is right? That is the greatest question of all.<end>"
KO: "정령신 그라제온과 이어진 무녀의 마력도, 그것을 열쇠로 되살아난다는 마도문명의 힘도…. 문제는 그것이 올바른 방향으로 쓰이느냐 아니냐다. 그리고 더 말하자면 무엇이 옳은지를 누가 판단하느냐? 바로 그것이 가장 큰 문제다.<end>"

## 1250305
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エステルが精霊神の聖座へ行って、<line-break>選ばれし地の巫女になるですって？<line-break><line-break>ふ、ふん、悔しくなんかないわよ。<line-break>高雅なアタクシは、土臭い民族信仰の<line-break>巫女なんかに憧れたりしなくてよ。<line-break>え？　超高度な魔導文明ですって？<line-break>そ、そんなことをアタクシに<line-break>聞かせてどーーしようってのよ！<end>"
EN: "Esther is going to the Spirit God's Holy Seat to become the chosen Priestess of Earth? H-hmph! I'm not jealous. A refined lady like me would never envy a priestess of some dirt-reeking folk religion. What? An extraordinarily advanced arcane civilization? W-why would you tell me thaaat?!<end>"
KO: "에스텔이 정령신의 성좌로 가서 선택받은 땅의 무녀가 된다고요? 흐, 흥, 분한 거 아니거든요. 고아한 이 몸은 흙내 나는 민간신앙의 무녀 따위 부러워하지 않아요. 네? 초고도의 마도 문명이라고요? 그, 그런 얘길 나한테 들려줘서 어쩌자는 거예요!<end>"

## 1250306
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聖座のボゲー<line-break>聖座のボゲー<line-break>聖座のボゲー<line-break>聖座のボゲー<line-break>聖座のボゲー<line-break>聖座のボゲー<end>"
EN: "Holy Seat Bogee! Holy Seat Bogee! Holy Seat Bogee! Holy Seat Bogee! Holy Seat Bogee! Holy Seat Bogee!<end>"
KO: "성좌의 보게 성좌의 보게 성좌의 보게 성좌의 보게 성좌의 보게 성좌의 보게<end>"

## 1250307
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "グラジェオンの聖座ってとこに行けば<line-break>エステルは地の巫女になれるんだって。<line-break><line-break>エステルがなりたいっていうんなら<line-break>それがいいと思うけどぉ、<line-break>前のエステルじゃなくなっちゃったら、<line-break>ルルアンタ、さびしいな…。<end>"
EN: "They say Esther can become the Priestess of Earth if she goes to a place called Grajeon's Holy Seat. If Esther wants to become one, I think that's fine, but... Lulluantha would be sad if Esther wasn't the same anymore...<end>"
KO: "그라제온의 성좌라는 곳에 가면 에스텔은 대지의 무녀가 될 수 있대. 에스텔이 되고 싶다면 그게 좋다고 생각하지만… 예전 에스텔이 아니게 되면 루루안타는 쓸쓸할 것 같아….<end>"

## 1250308
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>重大なのは、<line-break>地の巫女が現れるということより、<line-break>それにより魔導文明とやらが、<line-break>復活するかもしれぬ、<line-break>ということではないのか。<line-break><line-break>………………。<end>"
EN: "... What matters is not so much the appearance of the Priestess of Earth as the possibility that this may revive the so-called arcane civilization. ...<end>"
KO: "………………. 중요한 건 땅의 무녀가 나타난다는 것보다 그로 인해 마도문명이라는 것이 부활할지도 모른다는 점 아닌가. ……………….<end>"

## 1250309
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "精霊神グラジェオンの聖座へ行き、<line-break>その試練を受けることで、<line-break>エステルが地の巫女となる…。<line-break>エステルがお前を頼っている以上、<line-break>決断する権利はお前にある。<line-break><line-break>滅んだ魔導文明がにわかに復活し、<line-break>ディンガルを、そして世界を焼くか、<line-break>それとも…。<line-break>それが、お前にかかっている。<end>"
EN: "Esther will go to Grajeon the Spirit God's Holy Seat, undergo the trial there, and become the Priestess of Earth... Since Esther is relying on you, the decision is yours to make. The fallen arcane civilization may suddenly return and burn Dyneskal, and then the world, or else... It all depends on you.<end>"
KO: "정령신 그라제온의 성좌로 가서 시련을 받으면 에스텔은 땅의 무녀가 된다…. 에스텔이 너를 의지하고 있는 이상 결정할 권리는 네게 있다. 멸망한 마도 문명이 갑자기 부활해 딩갈을, 그리고 세계를 불태울지, 아니면…. 모든 것은 네게 달렸다.<end>"

## 1250310
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "グラジェオンの座所に行けば、<line-break>エステルは地の巫女になれるんだって。<line-break>エステルはそれを望んでるんだよね？<line-break>だったら、一緒に行ってあげるのが、<line-break>スマートってものじゃないかな。<line-break><line-break>もちろん行くよ。<line-break>精霊の神様なんてものは、<line-break>めったに見られるもんじゃないからね。<end>"
EN: "They say Esther can become the Priestess of Earth if she goes to Grajeon's seat. That's what Esther wants, right? Then going with her would be the smart thing to do. Of course I'm coming. You rarely get a chance to see a god of the spirits.<end>"
KO: "그라제온의 성좌에 가면 에스텔은 땅의 무녀가 될 수 있대. 에스텔은 그걸 바라고 있지? 그렇다면 같이 가 주는 게 멋진 일 아닐까. 물론 나도 갈 거야. 정령신 같은 건 좀처럼 볼 수 없으니까.<end>"

## 1250311
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "精霊神が巫女をその聖座へと<line-break>招いているだと…。<line-break><line-break>聞けば、巫女は強力な魔力を秘め、<line-break>なおかつ魔導文明復活の鍵も<line-break>握っているというではないか。<line-break>おお、そこにこそ、<line-break>私が夢見る人間進歩の次なる段階が<line-break>含有されているのではないか…！<end>"
EN: "A Spirit God is inviting a priestess to its Holy Seat...? I hear the priestess harbors tremendous magical power and also holds the key to reviving the arcane civilization. Ah! Could that contain the next stage in human progress that I have dreamed of...?!<end>"
KO: "정령신이 무녀를 자신의 성좌로 부르고 있다니…. 듣자 하니 무녀는 강력한 마력을 지녔고 마도문명 부활의 열쇠까지 쥐고 있다 하지 않는가. 오오, 바로 거기에 내가 꿈꾸는 인간 진보의 다음 단계가 담겨 있는 게 아닐까…!<end>"

## 1250312
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "精霊神グラジェオンの聖座にて、<line-break>エステル様をその巫女とする…。<line-break><line-break>どうなるのであれ、ヴァイは<line-break><value:$28>の決断に従います。<line-break><line-break>が、結果的にそれが、シャリの思惑と<line-break>重なることもお忘れなきように…。<end>"
EN: "Lady Esther is to become Grajeon the Spirit God's priestess at the Holy Seat... Whatever may come, Vai will abide by <value:$28>'s decision. But please do not forget that the outcome may align with Shalli's designs...<end>"
KO: "정령신 그라제온의 성좌에서 에스텔 님을 그 무녀로 삼는다…. 어떤 결과가 되든 바이는 <value:$28>의 결정을 따르겠습니다. 다만 결과적으로 그것이 샤리의 의도와 겹칠 수도 있다는 점을 잊지 마십시오….<end>"

## 1250314
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イオンズせりふ追加予定！！！<end>"
EN: "Ions dialogue to be added!!!<end>"
KO: "이온즈 대사 추가 예정!!!<end>"

## 1250315
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "精霊神グラジェオンの聖座へ行き、<line-break>その試練を受けることで、<line-break>エステルが地の巫女となる、か…。<line-break>まだ真偽もわからないことではあるが、<line-break>その背後にシャリの思惑があることは<line-break>間違いがない。<line-break>そのことを忘れず、くれぐれも<line-break>気を抜かないようにするんだぞ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "So Esther will go to Grajeon the Spirit God's Holy Seat, undergo the trial there, and become the Priestess of Earth... The truth of that remains uncertain, but there can be no doubt that Shalli's designs lie behind it. Never forget that, and do not let your guard down for an instant, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "정령신 그라제온의 성좌에 가서 그 시련을 받으면 에스텔이 땅의 무녀가 된다, 라…. 아직 사실인지도 알 수 없지만 그 뒤에 샤리의 의도가 있다는 건 틀림없다. 그걸 잊지 말고 절대로 방심하지 마라, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1250321
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（世界を救おうという想いが<line-break>　エルファスを別のものに変えた。<line-break>　あれが本当に神なのだろうか？）<end>"
EN: "(The desire to save the world transformed Elfas into something else. Was that truly a god?)<end>"
KO: "(세계를 구하고 싶다는 마음이 엘파스를 다른 무언가로 바꿨다. 저것이 정말 신인 걸까?)<end>"

## 1250322
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれは、何だったのかしら？<line-break>エルファスのことを思えば、<line-break>神と呼んであげたいけれど…<line-break>…でも、あれは、神と呼ぶには<line-break>いびつすぎ、醜すぎたわ…。<end>"
EN: "What was that thing? For Elfas's sake, I want to call it a god... But it was too misshapen, too hideous to be called one...<end>"
KO: "그건 대체 뭐였을까? 엘파스를 생각하면 신이라고 불러 주고 싶지만… 그래도 신이라기엔 너무 일그러지고 추악했어…<end>"

## 1250323
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス…。<line-break>結局は奴もシャリの手のひらで<line-break>踊らされていたのじゃな…。<line-break>むなしいことじゃ…。<end>"
EN: "Elfas... In the end, Shalli was pulling his strings too... How empty it all is...<end>"
KO: "엘파스…. 결국 그자도 샤리의 손바닥 위에서 놀아났던 거구나…. 허무한 일이야….<end>"

## 1250324
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きゃっ！　<line-break>神様って初めてみたかも。<line-break><line-break>どうせなら、私の願いを<line-break>かなえて欲しかったかも。<end>"
EN: "Oh! I think that might be the first god I've ever seen. It could at least have granted my wish while it was at it.<end>"
KO: "꺅! 신은 처음 보는 것 같아. 이왕이면 내 소원도 들어 줬으면 좋았을 텐데.<end>"

## 1250325
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス…。<line-break>いけすかない奴だったが、<line-break>あいつもシャリに操られてたんだな。<line-break>可哀想だが、弱ってるうちに<line-break>倒すしかないよな。<line-break>正義の味方ってのつらいよな。<end>"
EN: "Elfas... I couldn't stand him, but Shalli was manipulating him too. I feel sorry for him, but we have to defeat him while he's weakened. Being a champion of justice is tough.<end>"
KO: "엘파스…. 마음에 안 드는 녀석이었지만 그 녀석도 샤리에게 조종당했던 거구나. 불쌍하지만 약해졌을 때 쓰러뜨릴 수밖에 없겠지. 정의의 편도 힘드네.<end>"

## 1250326
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何が何だか、ボクにはわからないよ…。<line-break>でも、悲しいことだってのは、<line-break>すっごくよくわかる。感じる！<line-break>エルファスのためにも、彼を信じた<line-break>人たちのためにも、ボクはシャリを<line-break>許さない！<end>"
EN: "I don't understand any of this... But I know, really know, that it's sad. I can feel it! For Elfas's sake, and for everyone who believed in him, I will never forgive Shalli!<end>"
KO: "뭐가 뭔지 나는 모르겠어…. 그래도 슬픈 일이라는 건 정말 잘 알아. 느껴져! 엘파스를 위해서도, 그를 믿었던 사람들을 위해서도 난 샤리를 용서하지 않을 거야!<end>"

## 1250327
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれは神などではなかった…！<line-break>断じて…私は、あんなものを、<line-break>エルファスを神とは認めないぞ！<end>"
EN: "That was no god...! Never... I will never acknowledge that thing, Elfas, as a god!<end>"
KO: "저건 신 따위가 아니었어…! 절대로… 나는 저런 것을, 엘파스를 신으로 인정하지 않겠다!<end>"

## 1250328
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの神も虚無の子も我々とは<line-break>相容れぬ存在のようです。<line-break><line-break>倒す必要があります。<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>あなたの力をお借りしますよ。<end>"
EN: "It seems both that god and the Child of the Void are beings with whom we cannot coexist. They must be defeated. <value:$28>, I will require your strength.<end>"
KO: "그 신도 허무의 아이도 우리와 양립할 수 없는 존재인 듯합니다. 쓰러뜨려야 합니다. <value:$28>. 당신의 힘을 빌리겠습니다.<end>"

## 1250329
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャリはあれを神なんて呼んだけど<line-break>…冗談じゃないわ！<line-break>人は、神様になんてなれやしない。<line-break>全知全能でパーフェクトなんて、<line-break>どう背伸びしたって無理なのよ。<line-break>だから、人なんじゃない…。<end>"
EN: "Shalli called that thing a god, but... What a joke! A person can never become a god. No matter how hard one tries, no human can be omniscient, omnipotent, and perfect. That's what makes us human...<end>"
KO: "샤리는 저걸 신이라고 불렀지만… 웃기지 마! 사람은 신이 될 수 없어. 전지전능하고 완벽하다니 아무리 발돋움해도 무리야. 그래서 인간인 거잖아….<end>"

## 1250330
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃん、あれは何だったんだろうな。<line-break>エルファスは世界を救おうとしたのか？<line-break>…俺には、何が何だかわかんねえよ。<end>"
EN: "Sis, what was that thing? Was Elfas trying to save the world? ...I don't understand any of this.<end>"
KO: "누나, 저건 대체 뭐였을까. 엘파스는 세상을 구하려 했던 걸까? …난 뭐가 뭔지 모르겠어.<end>"

## 1250331
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャリはあれを神などと呼んだけど…<line-break>あれは絶対に神などではないわ。<line-break>あれは、醜い怪物に他ならない。<end>"
EN: "Shalli called it a god, but... It is absolutely no god. It is nothing more than a hideous monster.<end>"
KO: "샤리는 저걸 신이라고 불렀지만… 저건 절대 신 같은 게 아니야. 그저 추악한 괴물일 뿐이야.<end>"

## 1250332
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まいったね…。<line-break>相手は神様ときたか。<line-break>まったく…退屈しない人生だな。<line-break>行こうか、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Well, this is a fine mess... Now we're up against a god. Honestly... My life is never dull. Shall we go, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "곤란하군…. 상대가 신이라니. 정말… 지루할 틈 없는 인생이야. 가자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1250333
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれは、神などではない。<line-break>俺には分かる。<line-break><line-break>やつらはエンシャントのどこかに<line-break>いるはずだ。<line-break>油断するな。<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "That is no god. I know it. They must be somewhere in Ancient. Stay alert, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "저건 신 같은 게 아니야. 난 알아. 녀석들은 엔샨트 어딘가에 있을 거다. 방심하지 마, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1250334
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大きすぎる力を手にすれば<line-break>それと同じくらい大切なものを失う。<line-break><line-break>あの神は大切なものを失った<line-break>悲しい存在じゃ。とすれば、<line-break>わしらにできることは１つ…。<end>"
EN: "When one gains power beyond measure, one loses something equally precious. That god is a tragic being that lost something precious. That leaves only one thing for us to do...<end>"
KO: "너무 큰 힘을 손에 넣으면 그만큼 소중한 것도 잃는다. 그 신은 소중한 것을 잃은 슬픈 존재다. 그렇다면 우리가 할 수 있는 건 하나….<end>"

## 1250335
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界中の人たちが、エルファスの<line-break>呼びかけに応えた。<line-break>そして、強大な力が生まれた。<line-break>善にしろ、悪にしろ、人間って<line-break>すごい力を持ってるよね。<line-break>僕は本当にそう思うよ。<end>"
EN: "People all over the world answered Elfas's call, and tremendous power was born. For good or evil, human beings possess incredible power. I truly believe that.<end>"
KO: "세계의 사람들이 엘파스의 호소에 응했어. 그리고 강대한 힘이 태어났지. 선이든 악이든 인간은 엄청난 힘을 지니고 있어. 난 정말 그렇게 생각해.<end>"

## 1250336
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この大地を闇の眷属の好きにさせる<line-break>訳にはいけない。<line-break>行くぞ！　<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "We cannot let the minions of darkness do as they please with this land. Let's go, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "이 대지를 어둠의 권속들 마음대로 하게 둘 수는 없다. 가자, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1250337
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんなの、絶対、神様じゃないわ！<line-break>博識なアタクシは、ちゃ、ちゃんと<line-break>本物の神様を見たこともあってよ！<end>"
EN: "That thing is absolutely not a god! A learned lady like me has a-actually seen a real god, you know!<end>"
KO: "저런 건 절대 신이 아니야! 박식한 이 몸은 지, 진짜 신도 제대로 본 적 있거든!<end>"

## 1250338
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス神になるのボゲー<line-break>エルファス神になるのボゲー<line-break>エルファス神になるのボゲー<line-break>エルファス神になるのボゲー<line-break>エルファス神になるのボゲー<line-break>エルファス神になるのボゲー<end>"
EN: "God Elfas, Bogee! God Elfas, Bogee! God Elfas, Bogee! God Elfas, Bogee! God Elfas, Bogee! God Elfas, Bogee!<end>"
KO: "엘파스 신이 되는 보게~ 엘파스 신이 되는 보게~ 엘파스 신이 되는 보게~ 엘파스 신이 되는 보게~ 엘파스 신이 되는 보게~ 엘파스 신이 되는 보게~<end>"

## 1250339
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの神様をやっつけないと<line-break>みんなが戻ってこないんだよね。<line-break>ルルアンタ、悲しいよぉ。<end>"
EN: "Everyone won't come back unless we beat that god, right? Lulluantha is sad...<end>"
KO: "저 신을 쓰러뜨려야 모두 돌아오는 거지? 루루안타 슬퍼….<end>"

## 1250340
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break>神か…面白い。<line-break>その力…見せてもらおう。<end>"
EN: "... A god... Interesting. Let me see its power.<end>"
KO: "………………. 신인가… 재미있군. 그 힘… 보여 다오.<end>"

## 1250341
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人の弱さが産んだ<line-break>造られし神…。<line-break>悲しい存在だな。<line-break>しかし…倒さねばなるまい。<line-break>やつらは闇の深いところにいる。<line-break>逃がすな。<end>"
EN: "A manufactured god born of human weakness... A tragic being. Yet it must be defeated. They are deep within the darkness. Do not let them escape.<end>"
KO: "사람의 나약함이 낳은 만들어진 신…. 슬픈 존재군. 하지만… 쓰러뜨려야 한다. 놈들은 깊은 어둠 속에 있다. 놓치지 마.<end>"

## 1250342
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確かに輝いてた。確かに、みんなの<line-break>祈りが生んだものだった。<line-break>でも、ボクは認めたくないよ！<line-break>あれが神様だなんて…。<line-break>あんなキレイじゃない神様が、<line-break>生まれてしまっただなんて…！<end>"
EN: "It truly shone. It truly was born from everyone's prayers. But I don't want to accept it! I don't want to believe that was a god... that such an ugly god could have been born...!<end>"
KO: "분명 빛나고 있었어. 분명 모두의 기도가 낳은 것이었어. 하지만 난 인정하고 싶지 않아! 그게 신이라니…. 저렇게 아름답지 않은 신이 태어났다니…!<end>"

## 1250343
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人間は自らの力で未来を切り開く。<line-break>神に…造られた神などに<line-break>未来を委ねる訳にはいかない。<line-break>我々は人類の未来を取り戻すのだ！<end>"
EN: "Humanity must forge its own future. We cannot entrust that future to a god... to some manufactured god. We will reclaim humanity's future!<end>"
KO: "인간은 자신의 힘으로 미래를 개척한다. 신에게… 만들어진 신 따위에게 미래를 맡길 수는 없다. 우리는 인류의 미래를 되찾는 것이다!<end>"

## 1250344
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様…。<line-break>あれが神なのでしょうか？<line-break>…そうですね。<line-break>あれがどのような存在でも<line-break>世界の破滅を認めることはできません。<line-break>つまらないことを申しました。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Is that truly a god? ...You're right. Whatever it may be, we cannot allow it to destroy the world. Forgive my foolish question.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님…. 저것이 신인 걸까요? …그렇군요. 저것이 어떤 존재라 해도 세계의 파멸을 용납할 수는 없습니다. 쓸데없는 말을 했습니다.<end>"

## 1250345
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人の願いから産まれた神…。<line-break>そんなものに勝てるのかな…。<line-break>お兄ちゃん…。<end>"
EN: "A god born from human wishes... Can we really defeat something like that...? Big Brother...<end>"
KO: "사람의 소원에서 태어난 신…. 그런 걸 이길 수 있을까…. 오빠….<end>"

## 1250346
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれが神じゃと？<line-break>あんなもの断じて神ではない！<line-break><line-break>人の願いを利用しとるだけの存在を<line-break>神などと…わしは断じて認めん！<line-break><end>"
EN: "That is a god? That thing is no god! I will never acknowledge as a god a being that merely exploits people's wishes!<end>"
KO: "저게 신이라고? 저런 것은 결코 신이 아니다! 사람의 소원을 이용하기만 하는 존재를신이라니… 나는 결코 인정 못 한다!<end>"

## 1250347
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "救世主であるということは、<line-break>まず人であるということではないか。<line-break>私はそう思う。<line-break>もし、エルファスが神になりたいと<line-break>シャリに願ったとすれば…その願いが<line-break>過ちの元だったのだろうな。<end>"
EN: "To be a Savior, must one not first be human? That is what I believe. If Elfas asked Shalli to make him a god... then that wish was likely the root of his error.<end>"
KO: "구세주라는 건 우선 인간이어야 한다는 뜻 아닐까. 나는 그렇게 생각해. 만약 엘파스가 신이 되고 싶다고 샤리에게 빌었다면… 그 소원이 잘못의 시작이었겠지.<end>"

## 1250348
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれが神だとしても、あんなものに<line-break>俺たちの未来を決められるのは<line-break>ごめんだ！<line-break>俺たちの未来は俺たちが決める！<end>"
EN: "Even if that thing is a god, I'll be damned if I let it decide our future! We decide our own future!<end>"
KO: "저게 신이라 해도 저런 것에게 우리 미래를 정하게 둘 순 없어! 우리 미래는 우리가 정한다!<end>"

## 1250349
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんなのが神なわけがないわ。<line-break>もしあれが神だとしても<line-break>世界を滅ぼす神なんていらない！<line-break><value:$28>、あいつを倒して<line-break>あたしたちの…いえ、この世界の明日を<line-break>取り戻すのよ！<end>"
EN: "There's no way that thing is a god. And even if it is, we don't need a god that destroys the world! <value:$28>, defeat it and take back our... no, this world's tomorrow!<end>"
KO: "저런 게 신일 리 없어. 설령 신이라 해도 세계를 멸망시키는 신 따윈 필요 없어! <value:$28>, 저 녀석을 쓰러뜨리고 우리… 아니, 이 세계의 내일을 되찾는 거야!<end>"

## 1250350
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人の思いとはこれほどのモノを<line-break>作り出してしまうのですね。<line-break>しかし何故でしょう、胸が痛むのです。<end>"
EN: "To think that human thoughts could create such a thing... Yet why does my heart ache so?<end>"
KO: "사람의 마음이란 이 정도의 것을 만들어 내기도 하는군요. 그런데 왜일까요, 가슴이 아픕니다.<end>"

## 1250351
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんなの神様じゃありません！<line-break>人の犠牲の上に存在するものが、<line-break>神様なわけがありません。<end>"
EN: "That thing is no god! Nothing that exists through the sacrifice of human lives could ever be a god.<end>"
KO: "저런 건 신이 아닙니다! 사람의 희생 위에 존재하는 것이 신일 리가 없습니다.<end>"

## 1250353
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（イーシャは<line-break>　ほほに傷のある男を<line-break>　捜しているらしい）<line-break>（アルノートゥンのイオンズを<line-break>　訪ねてみよう）<end>"
EN: "(Iria appears to be searching for a man with a scar on his cheek.) (Visit Ions in Alnortun.)<end>"
KO: "(이샤는 뺨에 흉터가 있는 남자를 찾고 있는 모양이다) (알노툰의 이온즈를 찾아가 보자)<end>"

## 1250354
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほほに傷のある男？<line-break>ゴメン、心当たりがないわ。<line-break>他の人に聞いてみて。<end>"
EN: "A man with a scar on his cheek? Sorry, no one comes to mind. Try asking someone else.<end>"
KO: "뺨에 상처가 있는 남자? 미안, 짐작 가는 사람이 없어. 다른 사람한테 물어봐.<end>"

## 1250355
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほほに傷のある男？<line-break>心当たりがありすぎて<line-break>見当もつかんな。<line-break>傭兵や冒険者をしておれば、<line-break>顔に傷がつくのも<line-break>珍しくないのでな。<end>"
EN: "A man with a scar on his cheek? Far too many men fit that description for me to guess. Facial scars are hardly uncommon among mercenaries and adventurers.<end>"
KO: "뺨에 상처가 있는 남자? 짚이는 사람이 너무 많아서 모르겠군. 용병이나 모험자를 하다 보면 얼굴에 상처가 생기는 것도 드문 일이 아니니까.<end>"

## 1250356
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほほに傷のある男の人？<line-break>それなら、アルノートゥンに、<line-break>そんな神官さんがいたかも。<line-break>あのおじさんと古い知り合いなんて<line-break>案外、イーシャってオバサンかも！<line-break>きゃっ！<end>"
EN: "A man with a scar on his cheek? I think there might be a priest like that in Alnortun. If she's an old friend of that man, maybe this Iria is actually an old lady! Eek!<end>"
KO: "뺨에 상처가 있는 남자? 그렇다면 알노툰에 그런 신관님이 있었던 것 같아. 그 아저씨랑 오래된 사이였다니, 의외로 이샤라는 아줌마일지도! 꺅!<end>"

## 1250357
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほほに傷のある男を捜してる？<line-break>お前ってつくづくお人好しだよな。<line-break>うーん…ほほに傷のある男……<line-break>ダメだ！<line-break>ぜんっぜん思い浮かばねえ！<line-break>今回は俺のシャレはナシな。<end>"
EN: "You're looking for a man with a scar on his cheek? You're such a soft touch. Hmm... a man with a scar on his cheek... Nope! I can't think of anyone at all! No pun from me this time.<end>"
KO: "뺨에 상처가 있는 남자를 찾는다고? 넌 정말이지 너무 착해 빠졌어. 음… 뺨에 상처 있는 남자…… 안 돼! 전혀 생각이 안 나! 이번엔 내 말장난도 패스다.<end>"

## 1250358
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほほに傷のある人…。<line-break>そういえば、アルノートゥンの<line-break>イオンズさんにもあるよね。<line-break>なんかわかるかもしれないし、<line-break>ダメもとで行ってみたら、<line-break><value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "Someone with a scar on his cheek... Come to think of it, Ions in Alnortun has one too. He might know something, so why not go there and ask, <value:$28>? You've got nothing to lose.<end>"
KO: "뺨에 상처가 있는 사람…. 그러고 보니 알노툰의 이온즈 씨에게도 있지. 뭔가 알지도 모르니까 밑져야 본전으로 가 볼래, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1250360
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほほに傷のある男ですか。<line-break>もちろん知っていますよ。<line-break>アルノートゥンに行ってみてください。<end>"
EN: "A man with a scar on his cheek? Of course I know him. Try going to Alnortun.<end>"
KO: "뺨에 흉터가 있는 남자요? 물론 알고 있습니다. 알노툰에 가 보세요.<end>"

## 1250361
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほほに傷のある男…？<line-break>うーん、<line-break>なんか心当たりあるような…。<line-break>うう…ダメ…。<line-break>頭が…なんか割れそうに痛い…。<line-break>他あたって…ゴメン…。<end>"
EN: "A man with a scar on his cheek...? Hmm, that does sound familiar... Ugh... No... My head... It feels like it's splitting open... Ask someone else... Sorry...<end>"
KO: "뺨에 상처가 있는 남자…? 음, 뭔가 짚이는 게 있는 듯한데…. 으으… 안 돼…. 머리가 깨질 듯 아파…. 다른 데 알아봐… 미안….<end>"

## 1250362
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃん、他に特徴はないのか？<line-break>それだけじゃわかんねえよ。<end>"
EN: "Sis, don't you have any other details? That's not enough to go on.<end>"
KO: "누나, 다른 특징은 없어? 그것만으론 모르겠어.<end>"

## 1250364
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イーシャがほほに傷のある男をねぇ。<line-break>誰を捜してるのか見当はつくんだが、<line-break>居場所まではわからないな。<end>"
EN: "So Iria is looking for a man with a scar on his cheek. I have an idea who she means, but I don't know where he is.<end>"
KO: "이샤가 뺨에 상처가 있는 남자를 찾는다고? 누굴 찾는지는 짐작이 가지만, 어디 있는지는 모르겠군.<end>"

## 1250365
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…アルノートゥンに<line-break>ほほの傷が目立つ、<line-break>イオンズという神官がいる。<line-break>捜すなら、さっさと済ませるんだな。<end>"
EN: "...There's a priest named Ions in Alnortun with a prominent cheek scar. If you're going to look, get it over with.<end>"
KO: "…알노툰에 뺨의 흉터가 눈에 띄는 이온즈라는 신관이 있다. 찾을 거면 빨리 끝내.<end>"

## 1250366
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほほに傷のある男とな。<line-break>ふむ、聞いたことがあるぞ。<line-break> <line-break>アルノートゥンの地に、<line-break>優れた神官がおってな。<line-break>ほほの傷が特徴だという話じゃ。<end>"
EN: "A man with a scar on his cheek, you say? Hmm. I have heard of someone like that. There's an accomplished priest in Alnortun said to be known for the scar on his cheek.<end>"
KO: "볼에 상처가 있는 남자라. 흠, 들은 적이 있네. 알노툰에 뛰어난 신관이 있는데 볼의 상처가 특징이라고 하더군.<end>"

## 1250367
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その人かどうかわからないけど、<line-break>ほほの傷のある男って<line-break>アルノートゥンのイオンズさんかな。<line-break>イオンズさんなら、<line-break>上門の広場によくいらっしゃるよ。<end>"
EN: "I don't know if he's the man you're looking for, but could it be Ions in Alnortun? Ions is often at the Upper Gate plaza.<end>"
KO: "그 사람인지는 모르겠지만 뺨에 상처가 있는 남자라면 알노툰의 이온즈 씨 아닐까. 이온즈 씨라면 상문 광장에 자주 계셔.<end>"

## 1250369
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はぁ！？<line-break>知らないわよ。そんな男！<line-break>　<line-break>仮に見たことがあったとして、<line-break>下等生物の顔がどんなだったかなんて<line-break>覚えているわけないでしょ！<end>"
EN: "What?! I don't know any man like that! Even if I had seen him, why would I remember what some inferior creature's face looked like?!<end>"
KO: "하아!? 몰라, 그런 남자! 설령 본 적이 있다 해도 하등생물 얼굴이 어떻게 생겼는지 기억할 리 없잖아!<end>"

## 1250371
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーん…。<line-break>アルノートゥンのイオンズさんかな。<line-break>ほほにキズがあったよ。<line-break>よく、しょうこうきを出たとこの<line-break>かいだんに座ってるやさしい人だよ。<line-break>ね、あいにいってみよ？<end>"
EN: "Hmm... Maybe it's Ions in Alnortun. He had a scar on his cheek. He's a nice man who often sits on the stairs just outside the elevator. Hey, let's go see him!<end>"
KO: "음…. 알노툰의 이온즈 씨일까. 뺨에 흉터가 있었어. 자주 상공기를 나온 계단에 앉아 있는 다정한 사람이야. 있지, 만나러 가 볼까?<end>"

## 1250373
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほほに傷のある男か。<line-break>心当たりがないな。<line-break>　<line-break>旅なれた者なら<line-break>何か知っているかもしれんが。<end>"
EN: "A man with a scar on his cheek? No one comes to mind. A seasoned traveler might know something, though.<end>"
KO: "뺨에 상처가 있는 남자인가. 짐작 가는 사람이 없군. 여행에 익숙한 사람이라면 뭔가 알지도 모르지.<end>"

## 1250374
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、彼のことなら知ってるけど…<line-break>どうなったか知らせないほうがいいよ。<line-break>力になれなくて、ごめんね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, I know him, but... it's probably better not to say what happened to him. Sorry I can't help.<end>"
KO: "아, 그 사람이라면 알지만… 어떻게 됐는지는 알려주지 않는 편이 좋아. 도움이 못 돼서 미안해.<end>"

## 1250381
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしても彼を見つけたいの。<line-break>詳しいことは言えないけど…<line-break>あたしの大切な人だから…。<end>"
EN: "I have to find him, no matter what. I can't tell you the details, but... he's someone very dear to me...<end>"
KO: "어떻게든 그 사람을 찾고 싶어. 자세한 건 말할 수 없지만… 내 소중한 사람이니까….<end>"

## 1250383
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec125-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すみません、ちょっとわからないです。<line-break>お役に立てなくてすみません。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, but I don't know. Sorry I couldn't help.<end>"
KO: "죄송합니다, 잘 모르겠어요. 도움이 못 돼서 죄송합니다.<end>"

## 1260004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec126-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "酒の臭いをさせ、無精ひげをはやし、だらしなく<line-break>胸をはだけて歩くという、無頼の男。<line-break>生まれつき身分の上下が存在し、<line-break>それを仕方がないとするロストールの貴族制度に<line-break>反発している。<line-break>その明るい人柄とざっくばらんなところから<line-break>冒険者や傭兵の間で特に人気がある。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "A roguish man who reeks of alcohol and goes about unshaven, with his shirt carelessly open at the chest. He rebels against Rostorl's aristocratic system, which treats inequalities of birth as natural and unavoidable. His cheerful, forthright nature makes him especially popular among adventurers and mercenaries.<end>"
KO: "술 냄새를 풍기고 수염도 제대로 깎지 않은 채 흐트러진 차림으로 가슴을 드러내고 다니는 무뢰한. 태어나면서부터 신분의 높고 낮음이 존재하고 그것을 당연시하는 로스톨의 귀족 제도에 반발하고 있다. 밝고 소탈한 성격 덕분에 모험가와 용병 사이에서 특히 인기가 많다.<end>"

## 1260009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec126-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール攻略のため南下するディンガル軍を<line-break>迎撃すべく、総司令ノヴィン率いるロストール<line-break>軍は出撃する。その中にゼネテスの姿もあった。<line-break>ロストール軍はアンギルダンの山越え作戦の前に<line-break>敗れるが、ゼネテスは奇襲に備えて無傷であった<line-break>自分の部隊を中心に敗残兵を立て直し反撃する。<line-break>勝利に油断し陣形を崩していたディンガル軍は、<line-break>中枢を打ち破られ敗走する。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes serves in Rostorl's army during the Rostorl Campaign. After Angeerdan defeats the army at the mountain crossing, he rallies the survivors around his unscathed unit and counterattacks. The Dyneskal Army breaks formation and retreats.<end>"
KO: "로스톨 공략을 위해 남하하는 딩갈군을 맞아 총사령 노빈이 이끄는 로스톨군이 출격했고 그 안에는 제네테스도 있었다. 로스톨군은 앙길단의 산 넘기 작전에 패하지만 제네테스는 기습에 대비해 무사했던 자신의 부대를 중심으로 패잔병을 수습해 반격한다. 승리에 취해 진형을 흐트러뜨린 딩갈군은 중추를 돌파당해 패주한다.<end>"

## 1260014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec126-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "朱雀将軍アンギルダンに続いて、青竜将軍カルラ<line-break>までも撃退し、ロストールを守ったゼネテスは<line-break>救国の英雄としてたたえられる。<line-break>しかし、貴族の領袖エリエナイ公レムオンは、<line-break>ファーロス家の権力が、このことで決定的になる<line-break>ことに危機感を抱き、リューガの変を起こす。<line-break>王妃エリスはレムオン派の兵士に殺害され、<line-break>ゼネテスも命を落とした。<end>"
EN: "After defeating Angeerdan and Karula, Xenetes is hailed as Rostorl's hero. Duke Lemghon fears House Pharos will become unassailable and instigates the Ryuga Incident. Lemghon's soldiers kill Queen Eris and Xenetes.<end>"
KO: "주작장군 앙길단에 이어 청룡장군 카를라마저 격퇴해 로스톨을 지킨 제네테스는 구국의 영웅으로 칭송받는다. 그러나 귀족의 영수 엘리에나이 공 레무온은 파로스 가문의 권력이 결정적으로 커질 것을 우려해 류가의 변을 일으킨다. 왕비 에리스는 레무온파 병사에게 살해되고 제네테스도 목숨을 잃었다.<end>"

## 1260019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec126-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "剣狼の異名をとるほどの凄腕の剣士。兵を<line-break>操っては、朱雀将軍アンギルダンに続いて、青竜<line-break>将軍カルラまでも撃退したほどの名将である。<line-break>ロストールのクーデターで処刑されかけたが、<line-break><value:$28>に救出され、行動を共にする。<line-break>ロストールの守護神ともてはやされていたが、<line-break>ゼネテスはあい変わらず、酒と女と自由を愛する<line-break>ざっくばらんな冒険者のままである。<end>"
EN: "The Sword Wolf is a peerless swordsman and commander. Condemned in Rostorl's coup, he is saved by <value:$28> and travels with his rescuer. Though called Rostorl's Guardian, Xenetes remains an easygoing adventurer devoted to drink, women, and freedom.<end>"
KO: "'검랑'이라는 별명을 얻을 만큼 뛰어난 검사. 병력을 지휘해 주작장군 앙길단에 이어 청룡장군 카를라마저 격퇴한 명장이다. 로스톨 쿠데타에서 처형될 뻔했으나 <value:$28>에게 구출되어 함께 행동한다. 로스톨의 수호신이라 칭송받았지만 제네테스는 여전히 술과 여자와 자유를 사랑하는 소탈한 모험자 그대로다.<end>"

## 1260024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec126-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "精神を斬る妖刀月光を操る剣士。<line-break>かつて自分が唯一認めた男ロイと冒険し、<line-break>妖刀月光と聖剣日光を発見する。<line-break>ふたりは友情の証としてセラは月光を<line-break>ロイは日光をそれぞれ所有することにする。<line-break>行方不明の姉シェスターを捜すうちに、<line-break><value:$28>に出会い、ともに旅をすること<line-break>になる。<end>"
EN: "Sera wields Moonlight, a Demon Blade that cuts the spirit. He and Roy found Moonlight and the Holy Sword Sunlight, then each took one as a token of friendship. Seeking his missing sister Shester, Sera meets <value:$28> and becomes a companion.<end>"
KO: "정신을 베는 요도 월광을 다루는 검사. 과거 자신이 유일하게 인정한 남자 로이와 모험하며 요도 월광과 성검 일광을 발견한다. 두 사람은 우정의 증표로 세라는 월광, 로이는 일광을 각각 갖기로 한다. 행방불명된 언니 셰스타를 찾던 중 <value:$28>을 만나 함께 여행하게 된다.<end>"

## 1260029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec126-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "力の求道者。強い者と戦い、それを打ち破ること<line-break>を生きる目的としている。ネメアと出会い、<line-break>その強さにひかれ彼に従う。ネメアが闇の神器<line-break>探索の旅に出ると、オイフェ、ドルドラムと共に<line-break>ネメアに従い、<value:$28>と闇の神器の<line-break>争奪戦を行う。<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "A seeker of strength whose purpose in life is to fight the strong and defeat them. Drawn to Nemea's strength upon meeting him, Zelig enters his service. When Nemea sets out in search of the Artifacts of Darkness, Zelig follows alongside Aoife and Doldrum, competing with <value:$28> to claim them.<end>"
KO: "힘을 추구하는 자. 강한 자와 싸워 쓰러뜨리는 것을 삶의 목적으로 삼고 있다. 네메아를 만나 그 강함에 이끌려 그를 따른다. 네메아가 어둠의 신기를 찾는 여행에 나서자 오이페, 돌드람과 함께 네메아를 따르며 <value:$28>과 어둠의 신기를 놓고 쟁탈전을 벌인다.<end>"

## 1260034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec126-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールの王。妻はファーロス家のエリス。<line-break>その間に娘ティアナをもうける。彼の兄である<line-break>先王が死んだとき、先王の子であるアトレイアは<line-break>まだ生まれていなかった。彼は、名家の娘エリス<line-break>を娶る事で強力な後ろ盾を得、王になる。<line-break>その時、愛のない結婚をしたことが彼に罪悪感を<line-break>抱かせ、それがエリスやティアナに対する冷たい<line-break>態度や政治への無関心に結び付いている。<end>"
EN: "King of Rostorl, husband of Eris, and father of Tiana. He became king with House Pharos's backing after marrying Eris. Guilty over their loveless marriage, he is cold toward Eris and Tiana and indifferent to politics.<end>"
KO: "로스톨의 왕. 아내는 파로스 가문의 에리스이며 딸 티아나가 있다. 그의 형인 선왕이 죽었을 때 선왕의 아이 아트레이아는 아직 태어나지 않았다. 명문가의 딸 에리스와 결혼해 강력한 배경을 얻고 왕이 되었다. 사랑 없는 결혼을 했다는 죄책감이 에리스와 티아나에게 냉담한 태도를 보이고 정치에 무관심하게 된 원인이 되었다.<end>"

## 1260039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec126-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール先王。ファーロス家の娘エリスと結婚<line-break>することで強力な後ろ盾を得、王になる。この時<line-break>愛のない結婚をしたことが彼に罪悪感を抱かせ<line-break>それがエリスや娘ティアナへの冷たい態度となり<line-break>また、その罪悪感で自縄自縛状態に陥っていた。<line-break>リューガの変でエリスを殺害させると、<line-break>この罪悪感がこうじて、破壊神の円卓騎士である<line-break>縛られしものサムスンに取りつかれてしまう。<end>"
EN: "Former king of Rostorl. His loveless marriage to Eris left him cold toward her and Tiana. After having Eris killed in the Ryuga Incident, his guilt deepened until Samson, a Knight of the Round Table, possessed him.<end>"
KO: "로스톨 선왕. 파로스 가문의 딸 에리스와 결혼해 강력한 배경을 얻고 왕이 되었다. 사랑 없는 결혼을 했다는 죄책감은 에리스와 딸 티아나를 냉정하게 대하는 태도로 이어졌고, 그 죄책감 때문에 스스로를 옭아매고 있었다. 류가의 변에서 에리스를 살해하게 한 뒤 죄책감이 심해져 파괴신의 원탁기사 '속박된 자' 삼슨에게 빙의되고 만다.<end>"

## 1260044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec126-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神の復活を目論むシスティーナの伝道師の<line-break>ひとり。ディンガル帝国のバロル帝に闇の力で<line-break>統治するように勧め、バロルを闇におとした。<line-break>バロルに与えた以下の予言、バロルは娘の子に<line-break>滅ぼされる。死より生まれし獅子が槍の主を<line-break>殺す。死の獅子に我らの主は降り神を倒す。<line-break>ザギヴがディンガルを統治する。の４つは有名。<line-break>バロルがネメアに倒されると逃亡する。<end>"
EN: "A Sistina missionary seeking to revive the God of Destruction. He led Emperor Balor into darkness and gave him four famous prophecies, including that Balor would fall to his daughter's child. When Nemea defeats Balor, Zofor flees.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 부활을 꾀하는 시스티나의 전도사 중 하나. 딩갈 제국 발로르 황제에게 어둠의 힘으로 통치하라 권해 발로르를 어둠에 빠뜨렸다. 발로르에게 전한 네 예언, '발로르는 딸의 자식에게 멸망한다', '죽음에서 태어난 사자가 창의 주인을 죽인다', '죽음의 사자에게 우리의 주가 내려와 신을 쓰러뜨린다', '자기브가 딩갈을 다스린다'가 유명하다. 발로르가 네메아에게 쓰러지자 도망쳤다.<end>"

## 1260049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec126-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールの貴族でルブルグ伯爵。王家の分家<line-break>リューガ家の血筋で、レムオンの従弟にあたる。<line-break>無能だが、それ故にプライドだけは異常に高い。<line-break>弱い者いじめが好きで、イヤなことがあると<line-break>自分に決して逆らえないスラムの住人に乱暴を<line-break>働き、時にはリベルダムから買い求めた<line-break>戦闘用モンスターをけしかけるなど、<line-break>非道の限りをつくす最低な男。<end>"
EN: "A Rostorl noble and Count of Ruburg, descended from House Ryuga. Incompetent yet proud, Tartuba bullies the weak and brutalizes Slums residents, even setting Liberdam combat monsters on them. He is thoroughly vile.<end>"
KO: "로스톨의 귀족이자 루브르그 백작. 왕가의 분가 류가 가문 혈통으로 레무온의 사촌이다. 무능하지만 그래서 자존심만 비정상적으로 높다. 약자를 괴롭히는 것을 좋아하며 기분 나쁜 일이 있으면 자신에게 절대 맞설 수 없는 슬럼 주민에게 폭력을 쓰고, 때로는 리벨덤에서 산 전투용 몬스터까지 풀어 놓는 악행을 일삼는 최악의 남자.<end>"
